# Phase 3 Exam Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.
> Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Measure exam readiness honestly before November 14: a 260-item exam pool under `content/exam/pool/` written to `quotas.yaml`, mock forms A to D built by `pnpm content:forms` and frozen, module bosses, the full 65-question mock in focus mode with a wall-clock timer, error review with cause tags, readiness with the go/no-go line, ranks, badges and the full Progress overlay, plus the Phase 2 deferrals (boss-ready dot, focus quiz, extras line, module page mastery and weakest-first concepts, lesson recall dots), with R10 `examQuotas` and R12 switched on and the leakage test green.

**Architecture:** Everything Phase 3 shows is still derived from the one event log: `derive` gains exam results, error causes and recorded ranks, `deriveMemory` applies boss and mock answers on submit and tracks the 12 badges, and new pure engine files compute the boss draw, mock scores, readiness, ranks and the extras line for a given moment.
Exam answers live in a kv draft while the timer runs and become ordinary `answer` events at submit, so no event schema changes and the storage schema stays at version 1, while `ENGINE_VERSION` moves to 3.
Content adds a second pool: `pnpm content:quotas` turns the registry and blueprint into module by task cells, exam-writer agents fill them in a dedicated worktree, the existing check rounds run with `--pool exam` and ids-only output, and `pnpm content:forms` splits the live pool into four frozen forms that rule R12 guards.
The build ships each form in its own lazy chunk that only its mock (and, once taken, practice) loads, so no learn-mode selector can reach an untaken form.

**Tech Stack:** Node 24.21.0, pnpm 12.8.1, TypeScript 7.0.2, Vite 8.3.2, Preact 10.29.8 with @preact/signals 2.11.3, zod 4.6.5 (classic for content, `zod/mini` for events and backups), ts-fsrs 5.4.2 (imported only by `src/engine/srs.ts`), marked 18.0.14, yaml 2.9.1, Vitest 5.0.3, fast-check 4.10.2, Playwright 1.63.0 with @axe-core/playwright 4.13.0, Biome 2.5.15, IndexedDB, the Cache Storage and Service Worker APIs; no new dependencies.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-aws-cpe-study-site-design.md`, sections 2.6, 2.7, 2.9 (R2, R10, R12), 2.10, 3.1, 3.2, 3.4, 3.5, 3.8 to 3.11, 4.1 to 4.4, 4.6, 7.2 to 7.6, 8, 9, 10.1 (Phase 3 row) and 10.2.
**Target:** Saturday, October 24, 2026, with forms A to D frozen by Friday, October 23, so Mock A can run on Tuesday, October 27 (spec 10.2), for the exam on Saturday, November 14, 2026, and the go/no-go check on Saturday, November 7.

---

## Conventions for every task

- Run every command from the repository root, `/Users/danielchahine/Programs/AWS Cloud Practitioner Essentials` (or your worktree of it), where `node --version` must print `v24.21.0`; in a fresh shell start with `eval "$(fnm env)"; fnm use`.
- Never write the em dash character (U+2014) anywhere, and keep one sentence per line in every Markdown file outside code blocks and tables (rule R8 checks both).
- Commit messages are plain imperative sentences with a short body; never add a `Co-Authored-By` line (Dani's global instructions forbid agent co-author lines).
- Run `pnpm lint:fix` before each commit so Biome formats the code; the formatter has the last word.
- The pre-commit hook runs `pnpm check`, so a commit fails while any check fails; fix the cause rather than bypassing the hook, and never use `--no-verify`.
- Stage files by path, never with `git add -A` or `git add .`.
- Never hand-edit generated files: `content/fingerprints/*` comes only from `pnpm content:fingerprint`, `content/exam/quotas.yaml` only from `pnpm content:quotas`, `content/exam/forms.yaml` only from `pnpm content:forms`, the `verified` and `review` blocks and exam retirements only from `pnpm content:stamp`, and every `*.snapshot.json` and journey `.jsonl` under `tests/fixtures/` only from its test helper (delete it and rerun the test to regenerate it).
- Course text stays outside the repo in `~/Library/Application Support/ccp-study/`; no file, fixture, test, agent prompt or agent output in the repo may contain it, and test fixtures use made-up bakery text.
- Exam-pool writers never read the course source text, `content/fingerprints/`, AWS exam questions or `content/questions/`; checker agents read only their own batch file and never `.results/checks/keys.json`.
- Dani must never see exam-pool item text before a mock: plans, tool output, test output, agent returns, logs and commit bodies name exam items by id and count only, and exam end-to-end tests run on synthetic forms served by `page.route`, with `trace`, `screenshot` and `video` off.
- Agent results and check batches live under `.results/` (git-ignored) and are never committed.
- Code under `src/engine/` is pure: it imports no Preact, signals, `src/app`, `src/ui` or `src/store` code, touches no DOM, storage, `crypto` or `globalThis`, and takes the current moment and the UTC offset as arguments; Biome enforces the boundary.
- Raw question and concept ids appear only in the runner's report panel and in tool output, never elsewhere in the UI (spec 2.8).
- UI copy in this plan is exact: tests match it word for word, so change it only together with its test.
- Every bug found while building gets `tests/regressions/YYYY-MM-DD-<slug>.test.ts`, written and failing before the fix (spec 9).

## Task order and dependencies

- Tasks 1 and 16 depend on nothing; Task 2 needs Task 1; Task 3 needs Task 1; Task 4 needs Tasks 1, 2 and 3; Task 5 needs Task 4; Task 6 starts its writers once Task 4 is on `master` and its first check round once Task 5 is on `master` and merged into its branch; Task 7 needs Tasks 5 and 6.
- Task 8 needs Task 1, whose `schemas/ids.ts` gives it `FORM_IDS`; Task 9 needs Task 8; Task 10 needs Tasks 1 and 9; Task 11 needs Tasks 8 and 9; Task 12 needs Tasks 3 and 8; Task 13 needs Tasks 9 and 12; Task 14 needs Tasks 11, 12 and 13.
- Task 15 needs Tasks 1, 2 and 9; Task 16 depends on nothing; Task 17 needs Tasks 9 to 16; Task 18 needs Tasks 16 and 17; Task 19 needs Task 18.
- Task 20 needs Tasks 17, 18 and 19; Task 21 needs Tasks 17, 19 and 20; Task 22 needs Tasks 17 and 21; Task 23 needs Tasks 18 to 22 (its exam tests run on synthetic forms, so it never waits for Task 7); Task 24 needs every other task, with Task 6's branch merged.
- Tasks that edit one file run in a fixed order: `src/engine/replay.ts`, `tests/fixtures/memory.ts` and `tests/unit/replay.test.ts` Task 8 then 9; `src/engine/memory.ts` and the three journey `.snapshot.json` files Task 9 then 10 (Task 10 only regenerates the snapshots); `tests/unit/anti-grind.test.ts` Tasks 9, 11, 14; `scripts/content/rules/index.ts` and `tests/unit/checks.test.ts` Task 2 then 5; `scripts/content/lib/draft.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/style.ts`, `tests/fixtures/questions.ts` and `tests/unit/r10.test.ts` Task 2 then 4; `tests/unit/exam-redaction.test.ts` Tasks 2, 4, 5; `package.json` Tasks 4, 5, 24; `tests/unit/home.test.ts` Tasks 8, 9, 15, 17, 20; `src/app/home.ts` Tasks 9, 17, 20; `src/app/keyboard.ts` Task 16 then 17; `src/app/exam.ts` and `tests/unit/exam.test.ts` Task 16 then 18; `src/app/screens/Home.tsx` Task 17 then 20; `src/app/screens/Screen.tsx` Tasks 16, 18, 19, 21; `src/app/screens/Pending.tsx` created by Task 16 and deleted by Task 21; `src/app/progress.ts` Task 15 then 22; `TopBar.tsx`, `topbar.ts` and `App.tsx` Task 18 then 20; `Runner.tsx` Task 11 then 21; `tests/e2e/reader.spec.ts` Task 16 then 23; `src/ui/base.css` Tasks 18, 19, 20, 21, 22.
- Builders work in parallel git worktrees: Tasks 1 and 16 start at once, and Task 8 as soon as Task 1 is on `master`; Tasks 2, 3, 4 and 5 follow in the content-code worktree (Task 3 beside Task 2); Tasks 9 to 11 and Tasks 12 to 15 follow their prerequisites in the engine worktrees; Tasks 17 to 22 follow parts 04 and 05, or start earlier against the exact signatures in their tasks as borrowed stubs that the integrator replaces with the owner's code.
- The content fan-out (Task 6) is the long pole: the controller starts its writers with the Workflow tool as soon as Task 4 is on `master`, in its own worktree `<scratchpad>/p3/wt-exam` on branch `content/phase-3-exam`; no regenerate round starts after Wednesday, October 21, and Task 7 freezes the forms there by Friday, October 23.
- The controller merges `content/phase-3-exam` into `master` at the end of Task 7 and copies back only `.results/checks/passes.json`; Task 24 removes the content worktree with its exam results, runs `pnpm verify`, and has Dani deploy.

## Task 1: Exam, quota, form, rank and badge schemas, task short names and the game files

**Files:**
- Create: `schemas/ids.ts`, `schemas/exam.ts`, `schemas/game.ts`, `content/game/ranks.yaml`, `content/game/badges.yaml`, `tests/fixtures/content-root/content/game/ranks.yaml`, `tests/fixtures/content-root/content/game/badges.yaml`, `tests/unit/game-content.test.ts`
- Modify: `schemas/blueprint.ts`, `content/exam/blueprint.yaml`, `tests/fixtures/content-root/content/exam/blueprint.yaml`, `tests/content/bad/r10-missing-term.yaml`, `tests/unit/schemas.test.ts`, `tests/unit/blueprint.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/schemas.test.ts`, `tests/unit/game-content.test.ts`, `tests/unit/blueprint.test.ts`

Design notes:
- Changes to existing files are unified diffs against the tree this task starts from: save a block to a file and run `git apply <file>` from the repository root, or make the same edit by hand; new files are shown whole.
- `schemas/ids.ts` holds the domain, form and badge ids as plain `as const` arrays with no zod import, so `src/engine` can import them; the zod schemas build `z.enum` from them.
- `Quotas.form.tasks` is `z.record(TaskId, ...)`, which zod 4 makes exhaustive, so a missing task fails the parse on its own; the refinement adds the sums: tasks to 65, each domain's tasks to its domain count, each task's cells to 4 times its per-form count, `pool.multi` to 4 times `form.multi`, and each domain's `multiByModule` shares to `pool.multi`.
- `Forms` holds exactly four forms whose ids run A, B, C, D in order, with no qid twice inside a form; a qid in two forms is R12's finding (Task 5), not the schema's.
- `Ranks` needs `basic` first with `requires: {}` and no facts, unique ids, and two facts on every later rank; `Badges` needs the 12 ids in `BADGE_IDS` order.
- The blueprint task `short` is 3 to 40 characters with no final period; it is not forced to lower case, because `AI/ML and analytics` keeps its capitals.
- The fixture blueprint puts `short` right after `id` in each one-line task map, because `updates.test.ts`, `r10.test.ts`, `coverage.test.ts` and `r13.test.ts` replace the text `text: Fixture task statement 1.1, terms: []` and must still find it; `tests/content/bad/r10-missing-term.yaml`, a full copy of the fixture blueprint, gets the same shorts or its R10 fixture would fail R1 first.
- The fixture tree gets `content/game/ranks.yaml` with `basic` only, so R13's rank-fact check (Task 2) has nothing to compare there, and 12 made-up badge rules, so every test built on `makeFixtureRoot` still loads both files once Task 2 makes them required.
- `content/game/ranks.yaml` is spec 3.4's block word for word; its six facts are the six "Exam guide update" sentences of `resources/11-pricing-and-support/04-aws-support-plans.md`, which the new test checks.
- Covers P3-029, P3-030, P3-031 (the test), P3-032 and P3-033 (the schemas).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/unit/game-content.test.ts`:

```ts
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { parse } from 'yaml';
import { Badges, Ranks } from '../../schemas/game.ts';
import { parseUpdateLine } from '../../scripts/content/lib/update-line.ts';

const ROOT = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../', import.meta.url));
const ranksText = readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'content/game/ranks.yaml'), 'utf8');
const ranks = Ranks.parse(parse(ranksText));
const badges = Badges.parse(parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'content/game/badges.yaml'), 'utf8')));

/** The sentence of every Exam guide update line in the Module 11 notes. */
function module11Sentences(): Set<string> {
  const [dir] = readdirSync(join(ROOT, 'resources')).filter((d) => d.startsWith('11-'));
  if (dir === undefined) throw new Error('no Module 11 folder under resources/');
  const sentences = new Set<string>();
  for (const file of readdirSync(join(ROOT, 'resources', dir)).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.md'))) {
    for (const line of readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'resources', dir, file), 'utf8').split('\n')) {
      const update = parseUpdateLine(line);
      if (update !== null) sentences.add(update.sentence);
    }
  }
  return sentences;
}

describe('content/game/ranks.yaml (spec 3.4)', () => {
  it('holds the four Support-plan ranks in order', () => {
    expect(ranks.map((r) => [r.id, r.name, r.short])).toEqual([
      ['basic', 'Basic', 'Basic'],
      ['business-support-plus', 'Business Support+', 'Business+'],
      ['enterprise', 'Enterprise', 'Enterprise'],
      ['unified-operations', 'Unified Operations', 'Unified Ops'],
    ]);
    expect(ranks.map((r) => r.requires)).toEqual([
      {},
      { bosses: 5, hexesAt60: 5 },
      { bosses: 13, hexesAt60: 13 },
      { unseenMocksAt80: 2, latestMockWithinDays: 14, ready: true },
    ]);
  });

  it('each rank fact equals, word for word, the sentence of a Module 11 Exam guide update line', () => {
    const sentences = module11Sentences();
    const facts = ranks.flatMap((r) => r.facts ?? []);
    expect(facts).toHaveLength(6);
    for (const fact of facts) expect(sentences.has(fact), fact).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rank text never contains the five-plan list or the January 1, 2027 notice', () => {
    for (const banned of ['Developer', 'Enterprise On-Ramp', 'January 1, 2027', '2027']) {
      expect(ranksText.includes(banned), banned).toBe(false);
    }
  });
});

describe('content/game/badges.yaml (spec 3.10)', () => {
  it('lists the 12 badges by their spec names, in spec order', () => {
    expect(badges.map((b) => b.name)).toEqual([
      'First Brew',
      'Clean Queue',
      'Changed My Mind',
      'Long Memory',
      'Well Calibrated',
      'Hands On',
      'Lab Complete',
      'Leave No Trace',
      'Full Dress Rehearsal',
      'Pass Mark',
      'Even Keel',
      'Architect',
    ]);
  });

  it('marks the badges that wait for labs, missions and the capstone', () => {
    expect(badges.filter((b) => b.arrives !== undefined).map((b) => [b.id, b.arrives])).toEqual([
      ['hands-on', 'labs'],
      ['lab-complete', 'labs'],
      ['leave-no-trace', 'missions'],
      ['architect', 'capstone'],
    ]);
  });

  it('ends every rule with a period', () => {
    for (const badge of badges) expect(badge.rule.endsWith('.'), badge.id).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

In `tests/unit/schemas.test.ts`, import the new schemas, give the `blueprint()` helper's tasks a `short`, and add the `Quotas and Forms` and `Ranks and Badges` describes at the end:

```diff
+      { module: 1, domain: '2', target: 8 },
+      { module: 1, domain: '3', target: 12 },

+    ],
+  };
+}

+    expect(messages(Quotas.safeParse(quotas()))).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it('rejects task counts that do not sum to 65', () => {
+    const tasks = { ...FORM_TASKS, '2.2': 11 };
+    const result = quotas(tasks);
+    expect(messages(Quotas.safeParse({ ...result, cells: quotas().cells }))).toEqual([
+      'form.tasks: tasks sum to 66, not 65',
+      'form.tasks: tasks of domain 2 sum to 20, not 19',
+      'cells: cells of task 2.2 sum to 40, not 44',
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it('rejects quotas with a task missing', () => {
+    const { '4.3': _gone, ...tasks } = FORM_TASKS;
+    expect(Quotas.safeParse(quotas(tasks)).success).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it('accepts four forms A to D and rejects three, a wrong order and a repeated qid', () => {
+    const forms = { seed: 20261024, forms: ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'].map((id) => form(id)) };
+    expect(messages(Forms.safeParse(forms))).toEqual([]);
+    expect(Forms.safeParse({ ...forms, forms: forms.forms.slice(0, 3) }).success).toBe(false);
+    expect(
+      messages(Forms.safeParse({ ...forms, forms: ['B', 'A', 'C', 'D'].map((id) => form(id)) })),
+    ).toEqual(['forms.0.id: form 1 must be A', 'forms.1.id: form 2 must be B']);
+    const repeated = { ...forms, forms: [form('A', ['q-aaaaaa', 'q-aaaaaa']), ...forms.forms.slice(1)] };
+    expect(messages(Forms.safeParse(repeated))).toEqual(['forms.0.qids: form A lists q-aaaaaa twice']);
+  });
+});
+
+describe('Ranks and Badges', () => {

+  const plus = {

+    name: 'Business Support+',
+    short: 'Business+',
+    requires: { bosses: 5, hexesAt60: 5 },
+    facts: ['A first made-up fact about the plan.', 'A second made-up fact about the plan.'],
+  };
+
+  it('accepts basic first and later ranks with two facts', () => {
+    expect(messages(Ranks.safeParse([basic, plus]))).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it('rejects a first rank other than basic and a rank with one fact', () => {
+    expect(messages(Ranks.safeParse([plus]))).toEqual([
+      '0: the first rank must be basic, with requires {} and no facts',
+    ]);
+    expect(Ranks.safeParse([basic, { ...plus, facts: plus.facts.slice(0, 1) }]).success).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it('accepts the 12 badges in order and rejects 11', () => {
+    const badges = BADGE_IDS.map((id) => ({ id, name: `Badge ${id}`, rule: 'A made-up rule for the test.' }));
+    expect(messages(Badges.safeParse(badges))).toEqual([]);
+    expect(Badges.safeParse(badges.slice(0, 11)).success).toBe(false);
+    expect(messages(Badges.safeParse([badges[1], badges[0], ...badges.slice(2)]))).toEqual([
+      '0.id: badge 1 must be first-brew',
+      '1.id: badge 2 must be clean-queue',
+    ]);
+  });
+});
```

Create `schemas/ids.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Zod-free id lists shared by the content schemas and the engine (spec 3.10, 4.3).
 * `src/engine` imports these arrays, so this file must never import zod.
 */

/** The four CLF-C02 domains, in blueprint order. */
export const DOMAIN_IDS = ['1', '2', '3', '4'] as const;
export type DomainIdValue = (typeof DOMAIN_IDS)[number];

/** The four frozen mock forms (spec 4.3). */
export const FORM_IDS = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'] as const;
export type FormIdValue = (typeof FORM_IDS)[number];

/** The 12 badges in spec 3.10 order. */
export const BADGE_IDS = [
  'first-brew',
  'clean-queue',
  'changed-my-mind',
  'long-memory',
  'well-calibrated',
  'hands-on',
  'lab-complete',
  'leave-no-trace',
  'full-dress-rehearsal',
  'pass-mark',
  'even-keel',
  'architect',
] as const;
export type BadgeIdValue = (typeof BADGE_IDS)[number];
```

Create `schemas/exam.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { ModuleNumber, TASK_IDS, TaskId } from './common.ts';
import { DOMAIN_IDS, FORM_IDS } from './ids.ts';
import { Qid } from './question.ts';

export const DomainId = z.enum(DOMAIN_IDS);
export const FormId = z.enum(FORM_IDS);

const Count = z.int().min(0);
const DomainCounts = z.strictObject({ '1': Count, '2': Count, '3': Count, '4': Count });
const total = (values: Iterable<number>): number => {
  let sum = 0;
  for (const v of values) sum += v;
  return sum;
};
const pad = (module: number): string => String(module).padStart(2, '0');

/**
 * content/exam/quotas.yaml, written only by pnpm content:quotas (spec 2.6, 4.3): the per-form domain, task and
 * multi-response counts, the pool's module by task cells and each module's share of the multi-response target.
 */
export const Quotas = z
  .strictObject({
    form: z.strictObject({
      size: z.literal(65),
      domains: DomainCounts,
      tasks: z.record(TaskId, Count),
      multi: DomainCounts,
    }),
    pool: z.strictObject({
      size: z.literal(260),
      forms: z.literal(4),
      maxPerConcept: z.literal(4),
      multi: DomainCounts,
    }),
    cells: z.array(z.strictObject({ module: ModuleNumber, task: TaskId, target: z.int().min(1) })),
    multiByModule: z.array(
      z.strictObject({ module: ModuleNumber, domain: DomainId, target: z.int().min(1) }),
    ),
  })
  .superRefine((q, ctx) => {
    const issue = (path: (string | number)[], message: string): void => {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path, message });
    };
    const taskSum = total(Object.values(q.form.tasks));
    if (taskSum !== q.form.size) issue(['form', 'tasks'], `tasks sum to ${taskSum}, not ${q.form.size}`);
    const domainSum = total(Object.values(q.form.domains));
    if (domainSum !== q.form.size)
      issue(['form', 'domains'], `domains sum to ${domainSum}, not ${q.form.size}`);
    for (const d of DOMAIN_IDS) {
      const inDomain = total(TASK_IDS.filter((t) => t.startsWith(`${d}.`)).map((t) => q.form.tasks[t]));
      if (inDomain !== q.form.domains[d]) {
        issue(['form', 'tasks'], `tasks of domain ${d} sum to ${inDomain}, not ${q.form.domains[d]}`);
      }
      if (q.pool.multi[d] !== q.pool.forms * q.form.multi[d]) {
        issue(
          ['pool', 'multi', d],
          `domain ${d} needs ${q.pool.forms * q.form.multi[d]}, not ${q.pool.multi[d]}`,
        );
      }
      const shares = total(q.multiByModule.filter((m) => m.domain === d).map((m) => m.target));
      if (shares !== q.pool.multi[d]) {
        issue(['multiByModule'], `domain ${d} shares sum to ${shares}, not ${q.pool.multi[d]}`);
      }
    }
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    for (const [i, cell] of q.cells.entries()) {
      const key = `${cell.module}|${cell.task}`;
      if (seen.has(key)) issue(['cells', i], `cell m${pad(cell.module)} task ${cell.task} is listed twice`);
      seen.add(key);
    }
    for (const t of TASK_IDS) {
      const cells = total(q.cells.filter((c) => c.task === t).map((c) => c.target));
      const want = q.pool.forms * q.form.tasks[t];
      if (cells !== want) issue(['cells'], `cells of task ${t} sum to ${cells}, not ${want}`);
    }
  });
export type Quotas = z.infer<typeof Quotas>;

/** content/exam/forms.yaml, written only by pnpm content:forms (spec 4.3): forms A to D by qid, frozen once committed. */
export const Forms = z
  .strictObject({
    seed: z.int().min(0),
    forms: z
      .array(z.strictObject({ id: FormId, frozen: z.boolean(), qids: z.array(Qid).min(1).max(65) }))
      .length(4),
  })
  .superRefine((f, ctx) => {
    for (const [i, form] of f.forms.entries()) {
      if (form.id !== FORM_IDS[i]) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['forms', i, 'id'],
          message: `form ${i + 1} must be ${FORM_IDS[i]}`,
        });
      }
      const seen = new Set<string>();
      for (const qid of form.qids) {
        if (seen.has(qid)) {
          ctx.addIssue({
            code: 'custom',
            path: ['forms', i, 'qids'],
            message: `form ${form.id} lists ${qid} twice`,
          });
        }
        seen.add(qid);
      }
    }
  });
export type Forms = z.infer<typeof Forms>;
```

Create `schemas/game.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { Slug } from './common.ts';
import { BADGE_IDS } from './ids.ts';

/** What a rank needs, every condition at the same moment (spec 3.4). */
export const RankRequires = z.strictObject({
  bosses: z.int().min(1).optional(),
  hexesAt60: z.int().min(1).optional(),
  unseenMocksAt80: z.int().min(1).optional(),
  latestMockWithinDays: z.int().min(1).optional(),
  ready: z.literal(true).optional(),
});
export type RankRequires = z.infer<typeof RankRequires>;

export const Rank = z.strictObject({
  id: Slug,
  name: z.string().min(2),
  short: z.string().min(2).max(12),
  requires: RankRequires,
  facts: z.array(z.string().min(10)).length(2).optional(),
});
export type Rank = z.infer<typeof Rank>;

/** content/game/ranks.yaml: the Support-plan ranks, lowest first (spec 3.4). */
export const Ranks = z
  .array(Rank)
  .min(1)
  .superRefine((ranks, ctx) => {
    const [first] = ranks;
    if (
      first !== undefined &&
      (first.id !== 'basic' || Object.keys(first.requires).length > 0 || first.facts !== undefined)
    ) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: 'custom',
        path: [0],
        message: 'the first rank must be basic, with requires {} and no facts',
      });
    }
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    for (const [i, rank] of ranks.entries()) {
      if (seen.has(rank.id))
        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [i, 'id'], message: `rank ${rank.id} is listed twice` });
      seen.add(rank.id);
      if (i > 0 && rank.facts === undefined) {
        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [i, 'facts'], message: `rank ${rank.id} needs two facts` });
      }
    }
  });

export const BadgeId = z.enum(BADGE_IDS);
export const Badge = z.strictObject({
  id: BadgeId,
  name: z.string().min(2),
  rule: z.string().min(10),
  arrives: z.enum(['labs', 'missions', 'capstone']).optional(),
});
export type Badge = z.infer<typeof Badge>;

/** content/game/badges.yaml: the 12 badges in spec 3.10 order. */
export const Badges = z
  .array(Badge)
  .length(12)
  .superRefine((badges, ctx) => {
    for (const [i, badge] of badges.entries()) {
      if (badge.id !== BADGE_IDS[i]) {
        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [i, 'id'], message: `badge ${i + 1} must be ${BADGE_IDS[i]}` });
      }
    }
  });
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the task short names and the game files**

Run this command once from the repository root; its Python script adds a `short` line after each task's `text` in the real blueprint, and a `short` key right after `id` in the fixture blueprint and in its R10 bad-fixture copy:

```bash
python3 - <<'PY'
import re
SHORT = {
    "1.1": "cloud benefits", "1.2": "design principles", "1.3": "migration", "1.4": "cloud economics",
    "2.1": "shared responsibility", "2.2": "security and compliance", "2.3": "access management",
    "2.4": "security resources", "3.1": "deploying and operating", "3.2": "global infrastructure",
    "3.3": "compute", "3.4": "databases", "3.5": "networking", "3.6": "storage",
    "3.7": "AI/ML and analytics", "3.8": "other services", "4.1": "pricing models",
    "4.2": "billing and budgets", "4.3": "support and resources",
}
for path in ["content/exam/blueprint.yaml", "tests/fixtures/content-root/content/exam/blueprint.yaml", "tests/content/bad/r10-missing-term.yaml"]:
    lines = open(path, encoding="utf8").read().split("\n")
    out, task = [], None
    for line in lines:
        m = re.match(r'^  - \{ id: "(\d\.\d)", ', line)
        if m:
            line = line.replace(f'id: "{m.group(1)}", ', f'id: "{m.group(1)}", short: {SHORT[m.group(1)]}, ', 1)
        out.append(line)
        m = re.match(r'^  - id: "(\d\.\d)"$', line)
        if m:
            task = m.group(1)
        elif task and line.startswith("    text: "):
            out.append(f"    short: {SHORT[task]}")
            task = None
    open(path, "w", encoding="utf8").write("\n".join(out))
PY
```

```yaml
# Badges (spec 3.10): derived from the event log, never stored, and worth no XP.
- id: first-brew
  name: First Brew
  rule: First lesson check submitted.
- id: clean-queue
  name: Clean Queue
  rule: Due queue reached 0 after at least 10 reviews, on 7 distinct study days.
- id: changed-my-mind
  name: Changed My Mind
  rule: 5 distinct misconceptions cleared, each by the rule in 3.1 (a correct Sure answer on a different question at least 1 study day later).
- id: long-memory
  name: Long Memory
  rule: 50 concepts with stability of 21 days or more.
- id: well-calibrated
  name: Well Calibrated
  rule: "Over the last 150 counted answers, with at least 40 Sure and 40 Not sure: Sure accuracy at least 85% and Not sure accuracy at most 75%."
- id: hands-on
  name: Hands On
  rule: Every core lab in 5 modules completed.
  arrives: labs
- id: lab-complete
  name: Lab Complete
  rule: All 26 core labs completed.
  arrives: labs
- id: leave-no-trace
  name: Leave No Trace
  rule: 3 missions closed, each with cleanup and the next-day usage check confirmed (the kept Mission 1 does not count).
  arrives: missions
- id: full-dress-rehearsal
  name: Full Dress Rehearsal
  rule: All 65 questions of an unseen form answered before the timer ran out.
- id: pass-mark
  name: Pass Mark
  rule: An unseen form scored at 80% or more.
- id: even-keel
  name: Even Keel
  rule: 75% or more in every domain of one unseen form.
- id: architect
  name: Architect
  rule: All 13 capstone decisions with a final grade of 8/12 or more and no criterion at 0.
  arrives: capstone
```

```bash
pnpm lint:fix
git add schemas/ids.ts schemas/exam.ts schemas/game.ts schemas/blueprint.ts content/exam/blueprint.yaml content/game/ranks.yaml content/game/badges.yaml tests/fixtures/content-root/content/exam/blueprint.yaml tests/fixtures/content-root/content/game/ranks.yaml tests/fixtures/content-root/content/game/badges.yaml tests/content/bad/r10-missing-term.yaml tests/unit/game-content.test.ts tests/unit/schemas.test.ts tests/unit/blueprint.test.ts
git commit -m "Add the exam, quota, form, rank and badge schemas" -m "Ranks and badges live in content/game; blueprint tasks gain short names for Home lines."
```

Expected: the pre-commit hook runs `pnpm check` and the commit succeeds.


## Task 2: Load pool, quota, form, rank and badge files, keep exam output to ids, guard frozen forms

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/content/bad/r13-rank-fact.yaml`, `tests/unit/exam-redaction.test.ts`
- Modify: `scripts/content/lib/context.ts`, `scripts/content/rules/index.ts`, `scripts/content/rules/r02-ids.ts`, `scripts/content/rules/r06-references.ts`, `scripts/content/rules/r13-sources.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/draft.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/style.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/qid.ts`, `scripts/content/new-question.ts`, `scripts/content/vite-plugin.ts`, `scripts/content/validate.ts`, `tests/fixtures/questions.ts`, `tests/unit/checks.test.ts`, `tests/unit/r10.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/context.test.ts`, `tests/unit/r02.test.ts`, `tests/unit/r03.test.ts`, `tests/unit/r06.test.ts`, `tests/unit/r13.test.ts`, `tests/unit/qid.test.ts`, `tests/unit/style.test.ts`, `tests/unit/exam-redaction.test.ts`, `tests/content/bad.test.ts` (unchanged; it picks up the new R13 fixture)

Design notes:
- Changes to existing files are unified diffs against the tree this task starts from: save a block to a file and run `git apply <file>` from the repository root, or make the same edit by hand; new files are shown whole.
- `QuestionEntry` gains `module`, the file's `NN` for both kinds of file; `noteId` stays the lesson note for a practice file and becomes the item's own `note` for a pool file, which R1 checks against the file's module.
- One loader serves both kinds: `loadQuestions(path, module, pool, lessonNote?)` rejects an item of the wrong pool with an R1 finding at its line and leaves it out of `ctx.questions`, so a practice item in a pool file can never reach a form and an exam item under `content/questions/` can never reach a module chunk.
- `quotas.yaml` and `forms.yaml` are optional (`null` when absent) and parsed with their schemas; `ranks.yaml` and `badges.yaml` are required, so a deleted game file is an R1 finding.
- Ids-only output is one `redact` step in `runRules`, applied to every finding whose file is under `content/exam/pool/` or is `content/exam/forms.yaml`: a YAML error keeps only its line and column, and any other message is cut at its first double quote, where R3, R5, R7 and the style number check quote text; R1 schema messages carry paths, and R2, R4, R6, R9 and R10 messages carry ids and counts only.
- `redactMessage` and `isExamFile` are exported beside `redact`, so `draftCheck` can cut its style lines too, and the Vite plugin and `pnpm content:validate` apply `redact` to the load errors they print when `validate.yaml` itself is broken, the two paths that bypass `runRules`.
- `style.ts` now quotes the number it reports (`the number "7777" is not in m01/...`), so the redaction cuts it like every other quoted fragment; `style.test.ts`'s three exact messages change with it.
- R2 claims pool qids with the rest, walks `content/exam/pool/` in the working tree and in HEAD so a live or retired exam id never disappears, and compares every form with `frozen: true` in `HEAD:content/exam/forms.yaml` with the working tree: same qids in the same order and still `frozen: true`, or `form <F> is frozen in HEAD and may not change`; a deleted `forms.yaml` breaks every frozen form, and a working file that fails its schema is left to R1.
- R6 skips the "keeps its questions in" check for pool items, because a pool file holds a whole module, and keeps every other reference check.
- R13 collects the sentence of every Module 11 update line while it checks them, then reports each rank fact that is not one of them; the fixture tree's `basic` rank has no facts, and the bad fixture adds a made-up rank.
- `qidSourceTexts(root)` reads every YAML text under `content/questions/` and `content/exam/pool/` in the working tree and in HEAD, so `pnpm content:new-question` never hands out an id a pool file uses.
- `tests/fixtures/questions.ts` gains `POOL_FILE` and `writePool`, which Tasks 4 and 5 reuse; `checks.test.ts` and `r10.test.ts` build `QuestionEntry` literals, so each gains `module: 1` to compile.
- `r03.test.ts`'s case "a match in the exam pool" used to flip a practice item to `pool: exam` in place, which the loader now rejects under R1; it is split, and the exam half writes the match into a pool file instead.
- The sentinel test puts the token `ZZLEAKZZ` into every text field of a pool item and checks the output of `runRules`, `draftCheck` and the Vite plugin case-insensitively, because R3 lowercases the option text it quotes.
- Covers P3-001, P3-004, P3-005, P3-013, P3-015, P3-024, P3-026, P3-031 (the rule), P3-033, P3-034, P3-037 (R1 and R2), P3-040 and P3-046.

```diff
diff --git a/tests/fixtures/questions.ts b/tests/fixtures/questions.ts
--- a/tests/fixtures/questions.ts
+++ b/tests/fixtures/questions.ts
@@ -1,5 +1,5 @@
-import { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
+import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
 import { stringify } from 'yaml';
 import type { MatchQuestion, MultiQuestion, Question, SingleQuestion } from '../../schemas/question.ts';
 
@@ -145,3 +145,12 @@ export function matchQuestion(over: Partial<MatchQuestion> = {}): MatchQuestion
 export function writeQuestions(root: string, questions: readonly Question[], file = QUESTION_FILE): void {
   writeFileSync(join(root, file), stringify({ questions }, { lineWidth: 0 }));
 }
+
+/** The exam-pool file of the fixture tree's one module; the tree ships without it. */
+export const POOL_FILE = 'content/exam/pool/01-baking-basics.yaml';
+
+/** Writes the given questions as an exam-pool file, creating content/exam/pool/ when needed. */
+export function writePool(root: string, questions: readonly unknown[], file = POOL_FILE): void {
+  mkdirSync(dirname(join(root, file)), { recursive: true });
+  writeFileSync(join(root, file), stringify({ questions }, { lineWidth: 0 }));
+}
```

Add the pool and frozen-form cases to the end of `tests/unit/r02.test.ts`:

```diff
diff --git a/tests/unit/r02.test.ts b/tests/unit/r02.test.ts
--- a/tests/unit/r02.test.ts
+++ b/tests/unit/r02.test.ts
@@ -1,9 +1,11 @@
 import { copyFileSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
 import { join } from 'node:path';
 import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { stringify } from 'yaml';
 import { type Context, loadContext } from '../../scripts/content/lib/context.ts';
 import { runRules } from '../../scripts/content/rules/index.ts';
 import { FIXTURE_TODAY, makeFixtureRoot, replaceIn } from '../fixtures/content-root.ts';
+import { POOL_FILE, singleQuestion, writePool } from '../fixtures/questions.ts';
 
 const CONCEPTS = 'content/concepts.yaml';
 
@@ -207,3 +209,69 @@ describe('R2 questions', () => {
     ]);
   });
 });
+
+describe('R2 exam pool and frozen forms (spec 4.3)', () => {
+  const FORMS = 'content/exam/forms.yaml';
+  const formsText = (a: { frozen: boolean; qids: string[] }): string =>
+    stringify({
+      seed: 20261024,
+      forms: [
+        { id: 'A', ...a },
+        { id: 'B', frozen: false, qids: ['q-b00001'] },
+        { id: 'C', frozen: false, qids: ['q-c00001'] },
+        { id: 'D', frozen: false, qids: ['q-d00001'] },
+      ],
+    });
+  const FROZEN = formsText({ frozen: true, qids: ['q-a00001', 'q-a00002'] });
+
+  /** R2 messages with `headFiles` as HEAD, after `edit` changes the working tree. */
+  function r02Head(headFiles: Record<string, string>, edit: (root: string) => void): string[] {
+    const root = makeFixtureRoot();
+    edit(root);
+    const ctx = loadContext(root, { today: FIXTURE_TODAY });
+    const withHead: Context = {
+      ...ctx,
+      head: (path) => headFiles[path] ?? null,
+      headPaths: (prefix) => Object.keys(headFiles).filter((path) => path.startsWith(prefix)),
+    };
+    return runRules(withHead, ['R2']).findings.map((f) => `${f.file}  ${f.message}`);
+  }
+  const writeForms = (text: string) => (root: string) => writeFileSync(join(root, FORMS), text);
+
+  it('keeps a frozen form unchanged against HEAD', () => {
+    const changed = formsText({ frozen: true, qids: ['q-a00001', 'q-a00003'] });
+    const reordered = formsText({ frozen: true, qids: ['q-a00002', 'q-a00001'] });
+    const thawed = formsText({ frozen: false, qids: ['q-a00001', 'q-a00002'] });
+    const message = `${FORMS}  form A is frozen in HEAD and may not change`;
+    expect(r02Head({ [FORMS]: FROZEN }, writeForms(FROZEN))).toEqual([]);
+    expect(r02Head({ [FORMS]: FROZEN }, writeForms(changed))).toEqual([message]);
+    expect(r02Head({ [FORMS]: FROZEN }, writeForms(reordered))).toEqual([message]);
+    expect(r02Head({ [FORMS]: FROZEN }, writeForms(thawed))).toEqual([message]);
+    expect(r02Head({ [FORMS]: FROZEN }, () => {})).toEqual([message]);
+  });
+
+  it('lets a form that is not frozen in HEAD change, and leaves a forms file that breaks its schema to R1', () => {
+    const open = formsText({ frozen: false, qids: ['q-a00001'] });
+    expect(r02Head({ [FORMS]: open }, writeForms(formsText({ frozen: true, qids: ['q-a00009'] })))).toEqual(
+      [],
+    );
+    expect(r02Head({ [FORMS]: FROZEN }, writeForms('seed: 1\nforms: []\n'))).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it('reports a live exam id that HEAD has and the pool lost', () => {
+    const live = singleQuestion({ id: 'q-9d4r6t', pool: 'exam', status: 'live' });
+    const headPool = stringify({ questions: [live] });
+    expect(r02Head({ [POOL_FILE]: headPool }, (root) => writePool(root, [live]))).toEqual([]);
+    expect(r02Head({ [POOL_FILE]: headPool }, () => {})).toEqual([
+      `${POOL_FILE}  question 'q-9d4r6t' is live or retired in HEAD but is gone; retire it in place with status: retired`,
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it('reports an exam id that repeats a practice id', () => {
+    expect(
+      r02Head({}, (root) => writePool(root, [singleQuestion({ id: 'q-b4k7m2', pool: 'exam' })])),
+    ).toEqual([
+      `content/questions/01-baking-basics/01-what-a-bakery-cloud-is.yaml  id 'q-b4k7m2' (question) is already used by question in ${POOL_FILE}`,
+    ]);
+  });
+});
```

Create `tests/unit/exam-redaction.test.ts`:

```ts
import { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import type { Plugin } from 'vite';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { type Context, loadContext } from '../../scripts/content/lib/context.ts';
import { draftCheck } from '../../scripts/content/lib/draft.ts';
import { formatFinding, RULES, runRules } from '../../scripts/content/rules/index.ts';
import { contentPlugin } from '../../scripts/content/vite-plugin.ts';
import { FIXTURE_TODAY, makeFixtureRoot } from '../fixtures/content-root.ts';
import {
  POOL_FILE,
  QUESTION_FILE,
  singleQuestion,
  writePool,
  writeQuestions,
} from '../fixtures/questions.ts';

/** A made-up token in every text field of the sentinel items: output that shows it has leaked item text. */
const TOKEN = 'ZZLEAKZZ';
/** Whether output shows the token in any case; R3 lowercases the option text it quotes. */
const leaks = (lines: readonly string[]): boolean =>
  lines.join('\n').toLowerCase().includes(TOKEN.toLowerCase());
const BROKEN_FILE = 'content/exam/pool/01-broken.yaml';
/** A 9-word run of the fixture course, so R7 fires on the item (see r07.test.ts). */
const COURSE_RUN = 'bakers rent proofing cabinets by the minute and hand them back';

/** A live item that trips R3 (repeated option), R5 (evidence), R7 (course run), R9 (no stamp) and the number check. */
function leaky(pool: 'practice' | 'exam') {
  return singleQuestion({
    id: 'q-zz0001',
    pool,
    status: 'live',
    stem: `${TOKEN} The harbour branch bakes 7777 trays on market days. Which arrangement gives it capacity?`,
    options: [
      {
        id: 'a',
        text: `${TOKEN} borrow central ovens`,
        correct: true,
        why: `${TOKEN} central ovens are lent out.`,
      },
      {
        id: 'b',
        text: `${TOKEN} borrow central ovens`,
        correct: false,
        why: `${TOKEN} ${COURSE_RUN} later.`,
      },
      { id: 'c', text: `${TOKEN} order ahead`, correct: false, why: `${TOKEN} orders add no ovens at all.` },
      {
        id: 'd',
        text: `${TOKEN} bake at night`,
        correct: false,
        why: `${TOKEN} night baking adds no ovens.`,
      },
    ],
    evidence: [`${TOKEN} is not a line of the note.`],
    takeaway: `${TOKEN} borrow ovens for peaks.`,
  });
}

/** The fixture tree with the sentinel items in an exam-pool file, a schema-breaking item and a file that does not parse. */
function examRoot(): string {
  const root = makeFixtureRoot();
  writePool(root, [leaky('exam'), { ...singleQuestion({ id: 'q-zz0002', pool: 'exam' }), [TOKEN]: TOKEN }]);
  writeFileSync(join(root, BROKEN_FILE), `questions:\n  - id: q-zz0003\n    stem: ${TOKEN}: [unclosed\n`);
  return root;
}

const ruleOutput = (ctx: Context): string[] =>
  runRules(
    ctx,
    RULES.map((r) => r.id),
  ).findings.map(formatFinding);

/** The message the Vite overlay and `vite build` show for the tree. */
function overlayMessage(root: string): string {
  const plugin: Plugin = contentPlugin(root);
  const call = (name: 'configResolved' | 'buildStart', ...args: unknown[]): void => {
    const hook: unknown = plugin[name];
    const fn = typeof hook === 'object' && hook !== null && 'handler' in hook ? hook.handler : hook;
    if (typeof fn !== 'function') throw new Error(`the plugin has no ${name} hook`);
    fn.apply(
      {
        error(message: string): never {
          throw new Error(message);
        },
      },
      args,
    );
  };
  call('configResolved', { command: 'build' });
  try {
    call('buildStart');
  } catch (error) {
    return error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  }
  return '';
}

describe('ids-only output for exam-pool files (spec 2.6)', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date(2026, 9, 10, 12));
  });
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('fires R1, R3, R5, R7 and R9 on the sentinel items without showing their text', () => {
    const output = ruleOutput(loadContext(examRoot(), { today: FIXTURE_TODAY }));
    expect(leaks(output)).toBe(false);
    expect(output.filter((line) => line.includes('content/exam/pool/'))).toEqual([
      `R1 ${POOL_FILE}:33  questions[1] (q-zz0002): Unrecognized key: "..."`,
      `R1 ${BROKEN_FILE}:3  YAML: parse error at line 3, column 11`,
      `R3 ${POOL_FILE}:2  q-zz0001: two options share the text "..."`,
      `R5 ${POOL_FILE}:2  q-zz0001: evidence is not a whole line of m01/what-a-bakery-cloud-is: "..."`,
      `R7 ${POOL_FILE}:2  q-zz0001 copies a 9-word run from the course: "..."`,
      `R9 ${POOL_FILE}:2  q-zz0001: live but not verified; run the checks and pnpm content:stamp`,
    ]);
  });

  it('keeps draftCheck output for a pool file to ids, the number check included', () => {
    const problems = draftCheck(loadContext(examRoot(), { today: FIXTURE_TODAY }), POOL_FILE).problems;
    expect(leaks(problems)).toBe(false);
    expect(problems).toContain(`${POOL_FILE}:2  q-zz0001: the number "..."`);
  });

  it('keeps the Vite overlay message to ids', () => {
    const message = overlayMessage(examRoot());
    expect(message).toContain(`R3 ${POOL_FILE}:2  q-zz0001: two options share the text "..."`);
    expect(leaks([message])).toBe(false);
  });

  it('keeps the load errors to ids when validate.yaml is broken', () => {
    const root = examRoot();
    writeFileSync(join(root, 'content/validate.yaml'), 'rules: [\n');
    const message = overlayMessage(root);
    expect(message).toContain(`R1 ${POOL_FILE}:33  questions[1] (q-zz0002): Unrecognized key: "..."`);
    expect(leaks([message])).toBe(false);
  });

  it('keeps the text of the same findings on a practice file', () => {
    const root = makeFixtureRoot();
    writeQuestions(root, [leaky('practice')], QUESTION_FILE);
    const output = ruleOutput(loadContext(root, { today: FIXTURE_TODAY }));
    expect(output).toContain(
      `R3 ${QUESTION_FILE}:2  q-zz0001: two options share the text "${TOKEN.toLowerCase()} borrow central ovens"`,
    );
  });
});
```

In `scripts/content/rules/index.ts`, add `isExamFile`, `redactMessage` and `redact`, and apply `redact` in `runRules`:

```diff
diff --git a/scripts/content/rules/index.ts b/scripts/content/rules/index.ts
--- a/scripts/content/rules/index.ts
+++ b/scripts/content/rules/index.ts
@@ -1,5 +1,5 @@
 import type { RuleId } from '../../../schemas/validate-config.ts';
-import type { Context, Finding } from '../lib/context.ts';
+import { type Context, type Finding, FORMS_FILE, POOL_DIR } from '../lib/context.ts';
 import { r01 } from './r01-schema.ts';
 import { r02 } from './r02-ids.ts';
 import { r03 } from './r03-answer-counts.ts';
@@ -18,10 +18,35 @@ export const RULES: readonly Rule[] = [r01, r02, r03, r04, r05, r06, r07, r08, r
 
 const rank = (id: RuleId): number => Number(id.slice(1));
 
-/** Runs the given rules and reports any id that has no implementation yet. */
+/** Whether a finding's file holds exam-pool text, so its messages must carry ids and counts only (spec 2.6). */
+export function isExamFile(file: string): boolean {
+  return file.startsWith(`${POOL_DIR}/`) || file === FORMS_FILE;
+}
+
+/**
+ * A message cut down to what may be shown for an exam file: a YAML error keeps only its position, and any other
+ * message keeps the part before its first double quote, where rule messages quote item text.
+ */
+export function redactMessage(message: string): string {
+  if (message.startsWith('YAML:')) {
+    const at = /at line (\d+), column (\d+)/.exec(message);
+    return at === null ? 'YAML: parse error' : `YAML: parse error at line ${at[1]}, column ${at[2]}`;
+  }
+  const quote = message.indexOf('"');
+  return quote === -1 ? message : `${message.slice(0, quote)}"..."`;
+}
+
+/** The finding as it may be shown: ids and counts only for exam-pool and form files, unchanged for every other file. */
+export function redact(f: Finding): Finding {
+  return isExamFile(f.file) ? { ...f, message: redactMessage(f.message) } : f;
+}
+
+/** Runs the given rules and reports any id that has no implementation yet; exam findings come back redacted. */
 export function runRules(ctx: Context, ids: readonly RuleId[]): { findings: Finding[]; missing: RuleId[] } {
   const missing = ids.filter((id) => !RULES.some((rule) => rule.id === id));
-  const findings = RULES.filter((rule) => ids.includes(rule.id)).flatMap((rule) => rule.run(ctx));
+  const findings = RULES.filter((rule) => ids.includes(rule.id))
+    .flatMap((rule) => rule.run(ctx))
+    .map(redact);
   findings.sort(
     (a, b) => rank(a.rule) - rank(b.rule) || a.file.localeCompare(b.file) || (a.line ?? 0) - (b.line ?? 0),
   );
```

## Task 3: Quota and form math in the engine

**Files:**
- Create: `src/engine/quotas.ts`, `src/engine/forms.ts`, `tests/unit/quotas.test.ts`, `tests/unit/forms.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/quotas.test.ts`, `tests/unit/forms.test.ts`

Design notes:
- Both files are pure engine code: `pnpm content:quotas` (Task 4), `pnpm content:forms` and R12 (Task 5) and the remix (Task 12) import them, and they take the domain list from `schemas/ids.ts`, never from a second literal.
- `largestRemainder` works in integers (each share's remainder is `(total * w) mod sum`), so ties are exact; ties go to the larger remainder, then the larger weight, then the earlier index, the one rule shared by quotas, cells, multi-response splits and the mock.
- `taskCounts` counts a concept once in every task it is tagged with (spec 4.3 "in proportion to concept counts"); a covered task that rounds to 0 takes one seat from the task in its domain with the most seats, ties to the later task id, and only from a task that keeps at least one seat.
- On the real registry this gives 1.1:4 1.2:2 1.3:7 1.4:3, 2.1:3 2.2:10 2.3:3 2.4:3, 3.1:2 3.2:2 3.3:4 3.4:3 3.5:2 3.6:4 3.7:3 3.8:2, 4.1:3 4.2:1 4.3:4, which `pnpm content:quotas` prints in Task 4.
- `buildForms` first runs up to 2,000 seeded greedy attempts, each ordering items by concept size, largest first, after a `mulberry32(seedFor(seed, 'attempt-' + a))` shuffle, and giving each item to the form that needs its task most among those without its concept and with room for its type in its domain.
- When every greedy attempt fails, a seeded depth-first search takes over: it first places each form's multi-response items domain by domain, then fills every task with single items, always taking the open slot with the fewest fitting candidates, prunes a slot that can no longer be filled, enumerates each slot's items as a set rather than a list, and stops after `SEARCH_STEPS` placements.
- A plain seat-by-seat search ran out of 1,000,000 steps on a made-up pool whose concepts cross tasks between forms, while this two-phase search solves it in about 20 ms, so a feasible pool builds.
- The blocker is `precheck`'s first counting problem, or else the first short form and task of the greedy attempt with the fewest open seats; tests pin each message.
- `forms.test.ts` builds its pool in memory: four made-up forms that meet the real per-form counts, where seat `k` of form `f` in a domain holds concept `c-<d>-<(k + 3f) mod n>`, so every concept sits once in each form but under different tasks; that defeats the greedy pass on every seed tried, which is what the search test needs.
- Covers P3-007, P3-008, P3-009 (the math), P3-010, P3-028, P3-069, P3-070, P3-071, P3-072, P3-084 and P3-172.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/unit/quotas.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { TASK_IDS } from '../../schemas/common.ts';
import {
  domainCounts,
  domainOf,
  FORM_COUNT,
  largestRemainder,
  MULTI_PER_FORM,
  multiByModule,
  poolCells,
  type QuotaConcept,
  taskCounts,
} from '../../src/engine/quotas.ts';

const DOMAINS = domainCounts({ '1': 24, '2': 30, '3': 34, '4': 12 });

/** Made-up tested concepts: `n` concepts in `module` tagged with `tasks`. */
function concepts(module: number, tasks: readonly string[], n: number): QuotaConcept[] {
  return Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ id: `m${module}-${tasks.join('-')}-${i}`, module, tasks }));
}
const REGISTRY: QuotaConcept[] = [
  ...concepts(1, ['1.1'], 10),
  ...concepts(1, ['1.1', '1.4'], 5),
  ...concepts(2, ['1.2'], 1),
  ...concepts(2, ['1.3'], 30),
  ...concepts(3, ['2.1'], 4),
  ...concepts(3, ['2.2'], 40),
  ...concepts(4, ['2.2', '2.3'], 10),
  ...concepts(4, ['2.4'], 8),
  ...TASK_IDS.filter((t) => t.startsWith('3.')).flatMap((t, i) => concepts(5 + (i % 3), [t], 6 + i)),
  ...concepts(9, ['4.1'], 12),
  ...concepts(9, ['4.2'], 2),
  ...concepts(10, ['4.3'], 20),
];

describe('largestRemainder', () => {
  it('splits 65 by the CLF-C02 weights into 16, 19, 22 and 8', () => {
    expect(largestRemainder(65, [24, 30, 34, 12])).toEqual([16, 19, 22, 8]);
    expect(DOMAINS).toEqual({ '1': 16, '2': 19, '3': 22, '4': 8 });
  });

  it('breaks ties by the larger remainder, then the larger weight, then the earlier index', () => {
    expect(largestRemainder(3, [1, 1, 1, 1])).toEqual([1, 1, 1, 0]);
    expect(largestRemainder(1, [1, 3])).toEqual([0, 1]);
    expect(largestRemainder(2, [2, 1, 2, 1])).toEqual([1, 0, 1, 0]);
    expect(largestRemainder(2, [1, 3])).toEqual([0, 2]);
  });

  it('gives nothing to all-zero weights', () => {
    expect(largestRemainder(5, [0, 0])).toEqual([0, 0]);
  });
});

describe('taskCounts', () => {
  const counts = taskCounts(REGISTRY, DOMAINS, TASK_IDS);

  it('splits each domain over its tasks by the concepts tagged with each task', () => {
    // Domain 1 weights 15, 1, 30, 5 of 51: 4.71, 0.31, 9.41, 1.57 floor to 4, 0, 9, 1, then 1.4 and 1.1 take the 2 left.
    expect([counts['1.1'], counts['1.2'], counts['1.3'], counts['1.4']]).toEqual([5, 1, 8, 2]);
    // Domain 2 weights 4, 50, 10, 8 of 72 give 1.06, 13.19, 2.64, 2.11, so 1, 13, 3, 2.
    expect([counts['2.1'], counts['2.2'], counts['2.3'], counts['2.4']]).toEqual([1, 13, 3, 2]);
    expect([counts['4.1'], counts['4.2'], counts['4.3']]).toEqual([3, 1, 4]);
  });

  it('raises a covered task from 0 to 1 by taking a seat from the task with the most', () => {
    // Without the raise, 1.2 (1 concept of 51) would round to 0 and 1.3 would keep 9.
    expect(largestRemainder(16, [15, 1, 30, 5])).toEqual([5, 0, 9, 2]);
    expect(counts['1.2']).toBe(1);
    expect(counts['1.3']).toBe(8);
  });

  it('takes the raised seat from the later task when two tie for the most', () => {
    // Weights 20, 1, 20 of 41 split 8 seats into 4, 0, 4; 4.2 then takes its seat from 4.3, not 4.1.
    const tied = [...concepts(1, ['4.1'], 20), ...concepts(1, ['4.2'], 1), ...concepts(1, ['4.3'], 20)];
    const seats = taskCounts(tied, { '1': 0, '2': 0, '3': 0, '4': 8 }, ['4.1', '4.2', '4.3']);
    expect(seats).toEqual({ '4.1': 4, '4.2': 1, '4.3': 3 });
  });

  it('sums to 65 over the 19 tasks and to each domain count within it', () => {
    expect(Object.values(counts).reduce((a, n) => a + n, 0)).toBe(65);
    for (const d of ['1', '2', '3', '4'] as const) {
      const inDomain = TASK_IDS.filter((t) => domainOf(t) === d).reduce((a, t) => a + (counts[t] ?? 0), 0);
      expect(inDomain).toBe(DOMAINS[d]);
    }
  });
});

describe('poolCells and multiByModule', () => {
  const tasks = taskCounts(REGISTRY, DOMAINS, TASK_IDS);
  const cells = poolCells(REGISTRY, tasks);

  it('gives each task 4 x its per-form count, split over the modules of its tagged concepts', () => {
    for (const t of TASK_IDS) {
      const sum = cells.filter((c) => c.task === t).reduce((a, c) => a + c.target, 0);
      expect(sum, t).toBe(FORM_COUNT * (tasks[t] ?? 0));
    }
    expect(cells.filter((c) => c.task === '2.2')).toEqual([
      { module: 3, task: '2.2', target: 42 },
      { module: 4, task: '2.2', target: 10 },
    ]);
    expect(cells.reduce((a, c) => a + c.target, 0)).toBe(260);
  });

  it('sorts cells by module, then task, and drops empty ones', () => {
    const keys = cells.map((c) => [c.module, c.task] as const);
    expect(keys).toEqual([...keys].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0] || a[1].localeCompare(b[1])));
    expect(cells.every((c) => c.target > 0)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('splits 8, 8, 12 and 4 multi-response items over the modules by their cells in each domain', () => {
    const shares = multiByModule(cells);
    for (const d of ['1', '2', '3', '4'] as const) {
      const sum = shares.filter((s) => s.domain === d).reduce((a, s) => a + s.target, 0);
      expect(sum, d).toBe(FORM_COUNT * MULTI_PER_FORM[d]);
    }
    // Domain 2 cells: module 3 holds 4 + 42 = 46 seats and module 4 holds 10 + 12 + 8 = 30, so 4.84 and 3.16.
    expect(shares.filter((s) => s.domain === '2')).toEqual([
      { module: 3, domain: '2', target: 5 },
      { module: 4, domain: '2', target: 3 },
    ]);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/unit/forms.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { buildForms, type FormItem, type FormTargets, formProblems } from '../../src/engine/forms.ts';
import { domainOf } from '../../src/engine/quotas.ts';
import { mulberry32, shuffle } from '../../src/engine/rng.ts';

/** The per-form counts of the real registry (spec 4.3). */
const TARGETS: FormTargets = {
  tasks: {
    '1.1': 4,
    '1.2': 2,
    '1.3': 7,
    '1.4': 3,
    '2.1': 3,
    '2.2': 10,
    '2.3': 3,
    '2.4': 3,
    '3.1': 2,
    '3.2': 2,
    '3.3': 4,
    '3.4': 3,
    '3.5': 2,
    '3.6': 4,
    '3.7': 3,
    '3.8': 2,
    '4.1': 3,
    '4.2': 1,
    '4.3': 4,
  },
  multi: { '1': 2, '2': 2, '3': 3, '4': 1 },
};

/**
 * Four made-up forms that meet TARGETS, merged and shuffled. Within a domain, seat k of form f holds concept
 * c-<d>-<(k + 3f) mod n>, so each concept sits once in every form but under different tasks, which defeats the
 * greedy pass on most seeds; ids are f<form>-<domain>-<seat>, and seat k is multi-response when (k + f) mod n is
 * below the domain's multi count.
 */
function exactPool(): FormItem[] {
  const items: FormItem[] = [];
  for (let f = 0; f < 4; f += 1) {
    for (const d of ['1', '2', '3', '4'] as const) {
      const seats = Object.entries(TARGETS.tasks)
        .filter(([task]) => domainOf(task) === d)
        .flatMap(([task, n]) => Array.from({ length: n }, () => task));
      for (const [k, task] of seats.entries()) {
        const type = (k + f) % seats.length < TARGETS.multi[d] ? 'multi' : 'single';
        items.push({ id: `f${f}-${d}-${k}`, concept: `c-${d}-${(k + 3 * f) % seats.length}`, task, type });
      }
    }
  }
  return shuffle(items, mulberry32(11));
}

/** The exact pool plus about 10% spare single items per task, each on a concept of its own. */
function sparePool(): FormItem[] {
  const spare = Object.entries(TARGETS.tasks).flatMap(([task, n]) =>
    Array.from(
      { length: Math.ceil(0.4 * n) },
      (_, k): FormItem => ({
        id: `spare-${task}-${k}`,
        concept: `spare-${task}-${k}`,
        task,
        type: 'single',
      }),
    ),
  );
  return shuffle([...exactPool(), ...spare], mulberry32(12));
}

function built(
  items: readonly FormItem[],
  seed = 20261024,
  extra: { attempts?: number; steps?: number } = {},
) {
  const result = buildForms({ items, targets: TARGETS, count: 4, seed, ...extra });
  if (!result.ok) throw new Error(result.blocker);
  const byId = new Map(items.map((i) => [i.id, i] as const));
  return result.forms.map((qids) => qids.map((id) => byId.get(id) as FormItem));
}

describe('buildForms (spec 4.3)', () => {
  const forms = built(sparePool());

  it('builds four forms of exactly 16, 19, 22 and 8 items by domain', () => {
    expect(forms).toHaveLength(4);
    for (const form of forms) {
      expect(form).toHaveLength(65);
      expect(['1', '2', '3', '4'].map((d) => form.filter((i) => domainOf(i.task) === d).length)).toEqual([
        16, 19, 22, 8,
      ]);
    }
  });

  it('meets every per-task count', () => {
    for (const form of forms) {
      for (const [task, n] of Object.entries(TARGETS.tasks)) {
        expect(form.filter((i) => i.task === task).length, task).toBe(n);
      }
    }
  });

  it('puts 8 multi-response items in each form, 2, 2, 3 and 1 by domain', () => {
    for (const form of forms) {
      const multi = form.filter((i) => i.type === 'multi');
      expect(multi).toHaveLength(8);
      expect(['1', '2', '3', '4'].map((d) => multi.filter((i) => domainOf(i.task) === d).length)).toEqual([
        2, 2, 3, 1,
      ]);
    }
  });

  it('never repeats a concept inside a form', () => {
    for (const form of forms) expect(new Set(form.map((i) => i.concept)).size).toBe(form.length);
  });

  it('never puts one item in two forms', () => {
    const ids = forms.flat().map((i) => i.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
  });

  it('gives forms that formProblems passes', () => {
    for (const form of forms) expect(formProblems(form, TARGETS)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('gives the same forms for the same seed and different forms for another seed', () => {
    const ids = (fs: FormItem[][]) => fs.map((f) => f.map((i) => i.id));
    expect(ids(built(sparePool()))).toEqual(ids(forms));
    expect(ids(built(sparePool(), 7))).not.toEqual(ids(forms));
  });

  it('builds four forms from a pool of exactly 4 x targets', () => {
    const exact = built(exactPool());
    expect(exact.flat()).toHaveLength(260);
    for (const form of exact) expect(formProblems(form, TARGETS)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('finds the forms by search when every greedy attempt fails', () => {
    const greedyOnly = buildForms({
      items: exactPool(),
      targets: TARGETS,
      count: 4,
      seed: 3,
      attempts: 1,
      steps: 0,
    });
    expect(greedyOnly.ok).toBe(false);
    const forms = built(exactPool(), 3, { attempts: 1 });
    expect(forms.flat()).toHaveLength(260);
    for (const form of forms) expect(formProblems(form, TARGETS)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('names the concept that holds five items', () => {
    const items: FormItem[] = [
      ...exactPool(),
      { id: 'extra', concept: 'c-1-0', task: '1.1', type: 'single' },
    ];
    expect(buildForms({ items, targets: TARGETS, count: 4, seed: 1 })).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      blocker: 'concept c-1-0 has 5 items; a concept can sit in at most 4 forms',
    });
  });

  it('names the task that is one item short', () => {
    const items = exactPool().filter((i) => i.id !== 'f0-4-3');
    expect(buildForms({ items, targets: TARGETS, count: 4, seed: 1 })).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      blocker: 'task 4.2 has 3 live items; 4 forms need 4',
    });
  });

  it('names the domain that lacks multi-response items', () => {
    const items = exactPool().map((i): FormItem => (i.id === 'f0-4-0' ? { ...i, type: 'single' } : i));
    expect(buildForms({ items, targets: TARGETS, count: 4, seed: 1 })).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      blocker: 'domain 4 has 3 multi-response items; 4 forms need 4',
    });
  });

  it('names the first short form and task of the closest greedy attempt when the search runs out of steps', () => {
    const result = buildForms({
      items: exactPool(),
      targets: TARGETS,
      count: 4,
      seed: 1,
      attempts: 5,
      steps: 0,
    });
    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, blocker: 'form A is short of task 1.1 by 1' });
  });
});

describe('formProblems', () => {
  const small: FormTargets = { tasks: { '1.1': 2, '2.1': 1 }, multi: { '1': 1, '2': 0, '3': 0, '4': 0 } };

  it('passes a form that meets its targets', () => {
    const form: FormItem[] = [
      { id: 'a', concept: 'c1', task: '1.1', type: 'multi' },
      { id: 'b', concept: 'c2', task: '1.1', type: 'single' },
      { id: 'c', concept: 'c3', task: '2.1', type: 'single' },
    ];
    expect(formProblems(form, small)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('names every count a form misses and every repeated concept, in that order', () => {
    const form: FormItem[] = [
      { id: 'a', concept: 'c1', task: '1.1', type: 'single' },
      { id: 'b', concept: 'c1', task: '1.1', type: 'single' },
    ];
    expect(formProblems(form, small)).toEqual([
      'has 2 items; it needs 3',
      'domain 2 has 0 items; it needs 1',
      'task 2.1 has 0 items; it needs 1',
      'domain 1 has 0 multi-response items; it needs 1',
      'concept c1 appears 2 times',
    ]);
  });
});
```
