- [ ] **Step 3: Add the quota math**

Create `src/engine/quotas.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Exam-pool quota math (spec 2.6 and 4.3): domain counts per form, per-task counts, the pool's module by task
 * cells and each module's share of the multi-response target. Pure; `pnpm content:quotas` and the engine share it.
 */
import { DOMAIN_IDS, type DomainIdValue } from '../../schemas/ids.ts';

export type Domain = DomainIdValue;

/** Questions in one mock form (spec 4.3). */
export const FORM_SIZE = 65;
/** Mock forms A to D (spec 4.3). */
export const FORM_COUNT = 4;
/** No concept holds more exam-pool items than there are forms (spec 2.6). */
export const MAX_PER_CONCEPT = 4;
/** Multi-response items per form, by domain (spec 4.3). */
export const MULTI_PER_FORM: Readonly<Record<Domain, number>> = { '1': 2, '2': 2, '3': 3, '4': 1 };

/** The domain of a task id: its first character. */
export function domainOf(task: string): Domain {
  const domain = DOMAIN_IDS.find((d) => task.startsWith(`${d}.`));
  if (domain === undefined) throw new Error(`task ${task} is in no domain`);
  return domain;
}

/**
 * Splits `total` in proportion to `weights`, flooring each share and giving the seats left over one by one to the
 * largest remainder; ties go to the larger weight, then to the earlier index. All-zero weights get nothing.
 */
export function largestRemainder(total: number, weights: readonly number[]): number[] {
  const sum = weights.reduce((a, w) => a + w, 0);
  if (sum === 0) return weights.map(() => 0);
  // Integer arithmetic: share i is floor(total * w / sum) and its remainder is (total * w) mod sum.
  const floors = weights.map((w) => Math.floor((total * w) / sum));
  const remainders = weights.map((w, i) => total * w - (floors[i] ?? 0) * sum);
  let left = total - floors.reduce((a, f) => a + f, 0);
  const order = weights
    .map((w, i) => ({ w, i, r: remainders[i] ?? 0 }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.r - a.r || b.w - a.w || a.i - b.i);
  for (const { i } of order) {
    if (left === 0) break;
    floors[i] = (floors[i] ?? 0) + 1;
    left -= 1;
  }
  return floors;
}

/** A tested concept as quota math sees it: its home module and every task it is tagged with. */
export interface QuotaConcept {
  id: string;
  module: number;
  tasks: readonly string[];
}

/** Questions per domain in one form: 65 x weights / 100 by largest remainder (16, 19, 22 and 8 for CLF-C02). */
export function domainCounts(weights: Readonly<Record<Domain, number>>): Record<Domain, number> {
  const seats = largestRemainder(
    FORM_SIZE,
    DOMAIN_IDS.map((d) => weights[d]),
  );
  return { '1': seats[0] ?? 0, '2': seats[1] ?? 0, '3': seats[2] ?? 0, '4': seats[3] ?? 0 };
}

/**
 * Questions per task in one form: each domain's seats split over its tasks in proportion to the tested concepts
 * tagged with each task (a concept counts once in every task it carries); a covered task that rounds to 0 takes
 * one seat from the task in its domain with the most seats (ties: the later task id).
 */
export function taskCounts(
  concepts: readonly QuotaConcept[],
  domains: Readonly<Record<Domain, number>>,
  taskIds: readonly string[],
): Record<string, number> {
  const counts: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const d of DOMAIN_IDS) {
    const tasks = taskIds.filter((t) => domainOf(t) === d);
    const tagged = tasks.map((t) => concepts.filter((c) => c.tasks.includes(t)).length);
    const seats = largestRemainder(domains[d], tagged);
    for (const [i, t] of tasks.entries()) counts[t] = seats[i] ?? 0;
    for (const [i, t] of tasks.entries()) {
      if ((tagged[i] ?? 0) === 0 || counts[t] !== 0) continue;
      let donor: string | undefined;
      for (const other of tasks) {
        if ((counts[other] ?? 0) >= (donor === undefined ? 2 : (counts[donor] ?? 0))) donor = other;
      }
      if (donor === undefined) continue;
      counts[donor] = (counts[donor] ?? 0) - 1;
      counts[t] = 1;
    }
  }
  return counts;
}

/** One module by task cell of the exam pool: how many live items the module's writer owes that task. */
export interface Cell {
  module: number;
  task: string;
  target: number;
}

/**
 * The pool's cells: each task's pool target (4 x its per-form count) split over the home modules of the concepts
 * tagged with it, by largest remainder; empty cells are dropped, and cells sort by module, then task.
 */
export function poolCells(
  concepts: readonly QuotaConcept[],
  tasks: Readonly<Record<string, number>>,
): Cell[] {
  const cells: Cell[] = [];
  for (const [task, perForm] of Object.entries(tasks)) {
    const target = FORM_COUNT * perForm;
    const modules = [...new Set(concepts.filter((c) => c.tasks.includes(task)).map((c) => c.module))].sort(
      (a, b) => a - b,
    );
    const weights = modules.map(
      (m) => concepts.filter((c) => c.module === m && c.tasks.includes(task)).length,
    );
    const split = largestRemainder(target, weights);
    for (const [i, module] of modules.entries()) {
      const n = split[i] ?? 0;
      if (n > 0) cells.push({ module, task, target: n });
    }
  }
  return cells.sort((a, b) => a.module - b.module || a.task.localeCompare(b.task));
}

/** Each module's share of a domain's multi-response target (4 x MULTI_PER_FORM), in proportion to its cells there. */
export function multiByModule(cells: readonly Cell[]): { module: number; domain: Domain; target: number }[] {
  const out: { module: number; domain: Domain; target: number }[] = [];
  for (const d of DOMAIN_IDS) {
    const inDomain = cells.filter((c) => domainOf(c.task) === d);
    const modules = [...new Set(inDomain.map((c) => c.module))].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const weights = modules.map((m) =>
      inDomain.filter((c) => c.module === m).reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.target, 0),
    );
    const split = largestRemainder(FORM_COUNT * MULTI_PER_FORM[d], weights);
    for (const [i, module] of modules.entries()) {
      const n = split[i] ?? 0;
      if (n > 0) out.push({ module, domain: d, target: n });
    }
  }
  return out.sort((a, b) => a.module - b.module || a.domain.localeCompare(b.domain));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the form builder**

Create `src/engine/forms.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The mock form builder (spec 4.3): splits live exam-pool items into forms that each meet the per-task and
 * multi-response counts, with no concept twice in a form and no item in two forms, or names what blocks the split.
 * Pure and seeded, so the same pool and seed always give the same forms (spec 9).
 */
import { FORM_IDS } from '../../schemas/ids.ts';
import { type Domain, domainOf } from './quotas.ts';
import { mulberry32, seedFor, shuffle } from './rng.ts';

/** One live exam-pool item as the builder sees it: ids and shape only, never text. */
export interface FormItem {
  id: string;
  concept: string;
  task: string;
  type: 'single' | 'multi';
}

/** What one form must hold: items per task and multi-response items per domain. */
export interface FormTargets {
  tasks: Readonly<Record<string, number>>;
  multi: Readonly<Record<Domain, number>>;
}

export type FormBuild = { ok: true; forms: string[][] } | { ok: false; blocker: string };

/** Seeded greedy attempts before the search takes over. */
export const FORM_ATTEMPTS = 2000;
/** Placements the backtracking search may try before it gives up. */
export const SEARCH_STEPS = 5_000_000;

const formName = (i: number): string => FORM_IDS[i] ?? String(i + 1);

/** Items per domain in one form: the sum of its tasks' counts. */
function domainTotals(targets: FormTargets): Map<Domain, number> {
  const totals = new Map<Domain, number>();
  for (const [task, n] of Object.entries(targets.tasks)) {
    const d = domainOf(task);
    totals.set(d, (totals.get(d) ?? 0) + n);
  }
  return totals;
}

const domainList = (targets: FormTargets): Domain[] => [...domainTotals(targets).keys()];

/** The first reason `count` forms cannot be built from `items` by counting alone, or null. */
export function precheck(items: readonly FormItem[], targets: FormTargets, count: number): string | null {
  const perConcept = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const item of items) perConcept.set(item.concept, (perConcept.get(item.concept) ?? 0) + 1);
  for (const [concept, n] of [...perConcept].sort((a, b) => a[0].localeCompare(b[0]))) {
    if (n > count) return `concept ${concept} has ${n} items; a concept can sit in at most ${count} forms`;
  }
  for (const [task, target] of Object.entries(targets.tasks)) {
    const n = items.filter((i) => i.task === task).length;
    if (n < count * target) return `task ${task} has ${n} live items; ${count} forms need ${count * target}`;
  }
  const totals = domainTotals(targets);
  for (const d of domainList(targets)) {
    const multi = items.filter((i) => i.type === 'multi' && domainOf(i.task) === d).length;
    const needMulti = count * (targets.multi[d] ?? 0);
    if (multi < needMulti)
      return `domain ${d} has ${multi} multi-response items; ${count} forms need ${needMulti}`;
    const single = items.filter((i) => i.type === 'single' && domainOf(i.task) === d).length;
    const needSingle = count * ((totals.get(d) ?? 0) - (targets.multi[d] ?? 0));
    if (single < needSingle) {
      return `domain ${d} has ${single} single-answer items; ${count} forms need ${needSingle}`;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/** Open seats while forms are filled: per form, per task and per domain and item type. */
class Needs {
  readonly task: Map<string, number>[];
  readonly multi: Map<Domain, number>[];
  readonly single: Map<Domain, number>[];
  readonly concepts: Set<string>[];

  constructor(targets: FormTargets, count: number) {
    const totals = domainTotals(targets);
    this.task = Array.from({ length: count }, () => new Map(Object.entries(targets.tasks)));
    this.multi = Array.from(
      { length: count },
      () => new Map(domainList(targets).map((d) => [d, targets.multi[d] ?? 0] as const)),
    );
    this.single = Array.from(
      { length: count },
      () =>
        new Map(domainList(targets).map((d) => [d, (totals.get(d) ?? 0) - (targets.multi[d] ?? 0)] as const)),
    );
    this.concepts = Array.from({ length: count }, () => new Set<string>());
  }

  /** Whether form `f` can take `item` now. */
  fits(f: number, item: FormItem): boolean {
    const d = domainOf(item.task);
    const kind = item.type === 'multi' ? this.multi : this.single;
    return (
      (this.task[f]?.get(item.task) ?? 0) > 0 &&
      (kind[f]?.get(d) ?? 0) > 0 &&
      !(this.concepts[f]?.has(item.concept) ?? true)
    );
  }

  /** Places (`step` 1) or removes (`step` -1) `item` in form `f`. */
  move(f: number, item: FormItem, step: 1 | -1): void {
    const d = domainOf(item.task);
    const kind = item.type === 'multi' ? this.multi : this.single;
    this.task[f]?.set(item.task, (this.task[f]?.get(item.task) ?? 0) - step);
    kind[f]?.set(d, (kind[f]?.get(d) ?? 0) - step);
    if (step === 1) this.concepts[f]?.add(item.concept);
    else this.concepts[f]?.delete(item.concept);
  }

  /** Seats still open over every form and task. */
  missing(): number {
    return this.task.reduce((sum, m) => sum + [...m.values()].reduce((a, n) => a + n, 0), 0);
  }

  /** The first form and task still short, as a blocker sentence. */
  shortfall(): string {
    for (const [f, m] of this.task.entries()) {
      for (const [task, n] of m) if (n > 0) return `form ${formName(f)} is short of task ${task} by ${n}`;
    }
    return 'every seat is filled';
  }
}

/** One seeded greedy attempt: the biggest concepts first, each item to the form that needs its task most. */
function greedy(
  items: readonly FormItem[],
  targets: FormTargets,
  count: number,
  seed: number,
  attempt: number,
): { forms: string[][]; needs: Needs } {
  const size = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const item of items) size.set(item.concept, (size.get(item.concept) ?? 0) + 1);
  const order = shuffle(items, mulberry32(seedFor(seed, `attempt-${attempt}`))).sort(
    (a, b) => (size.get(b.concept) ?? 0) - (size.get(a.concept) ?? 0),
  );
  const needs = new Needs(targets, count);
  const forms: string[][] = Array.from({ length: count }, () => []);
  for (const item of order) {
    let best = -1;
    for (let f = 0; f < count; f += 1) {
      if (!needs.fits(f, item)) continue;
      if (best === -1 || (needs.task[f]?.get(item.task) ?? 0) > (needs.task[best]?.get(item.task) ?? 0))
        best = f;
    }
    if (best === -1) continue;
    needs.move(best, item, 1);
    forms[best]?.push(item.id);
  }
  return { forms, needs };
}

/** One open choice of the search: the multi-response items of a form's domain, or the single items of a form's task. */
interface Slot {
  form: number;
  need: () => number;
  candidates: readonly FormItem[];
  /** The candidate index chosen last for this slot; later picks take higher indexes, so a slot is a set, not a list. */
  last: number;
}

/**
 * Seeded depth-first search: it first places each form's multi-response items by domain, then fills every task with
 * single items, always taking the open slot with the fewest fitting candidates (tried in seeded order), pruning a
 * slot that can no longer be filled and backtracking on a concept clash; returns null after `steps` placements.
 */
function search(
  items: readonly FormItem[],
  targets: FormTargets,
  count: number,
  seed: number,
  steps: number,
): string[][] | null {
  const order = shuffle(items, mulberry32(seedFor(seed, 'search')));
  const needs = new Needs(targets, count);
  const multiSlots: Slot[] = [];
  const singleSlots: Slot[] = [];
  for (let f = 0; f < count; f += 1) {
    for (const d of domainList(targets)) {
      multiSlots.push({
        form: f,
        need: () => needs.multi[f]?.get(d) ?? 0,
        candidates: order.filter((i) => i.type === 'multi' && domainOf(i.task) === d),
        last: -1,
      });
    }
    for (const task of Object.keys(targets.tasks)) {
      singleSlots.push({
        form: f,
        need: () => needs.task[f]?.get(task) ?? 0,
        candidates: order.filter((i) => i.type === 'single' && i.task === task),
        last: -1,
      });
    }
  }
  const used = new Set<string>();
  const forms: string[][] = Array.from({ length: count }, () => []);
  let budget = steps;

  /** The open slot with the fewest fitting candidates, null when every slot is full, or 'dead' when one cannot fill. */
  const choose = (slots: readonly Slot[]): { slot: Slot; fitting: number[] } | null | 'dead' => {
    let pick: { slot: Slot; fitting: number[] } | null = null;
    for (const slot of slots) {
      const need = slot.need();
      if (need === 0) continue;
      const fitting: number[] = [];
      for (const [c, item] of slot.candidates.entries()) {
        if (c > slot.last && !used.has(item.id) && needs.fits(slot.form, item)) fitting.push(c);
      }
      if (fitting.length < need) return 'dead';
      if (pick === null || fitting.length < pick.fitting.length) pick = { slot, fitting };
    }
    return pick;
  };

  const fill = (): boolean => {
    let pick = choose(multiSlots);
    if (pick === null) pick = choose(singleSlots);
    if (pick === 'dead') return false;
    if (pick === null) return true;
    const { slot, fitting } = pick;
    const last = slot.last;
    for (const c of fitting) {
      const item = slot.candidates[c];
      if (item === undefined || used.has(item.id) || !needs.fits(slot.form, item)) continue;
      if (budget <= 0) return false;
      budget -= 1;
      needs.move(slot.form, item, 1);
      used.add(item.id);
      forms[slot.form]?.push(item.id);
      slot.last = c;
      if (fill()) return true;
      slot.last = last;
      forms[slot.form]?.pop();
      used.delete(item.id);
      needs.move(slot.form, item, -1);
    }
    return false;
  };
  return fill() ? forms : null;
}

/**
 * Builds `count` forms from `items`: up to `attempts` seeded greedy attempts, then a seeded backtracking search
 * of up to `steps` placements. Spare items stay unused. On failure the blocker names the shortest greedy attempt's
 * first missing seat, or the counting problem that makes the split impossible.
 */
export function buildForms(args: {
  items: readonly FormItem[];
  targets: FormTargets;
  count: number;
  seed: number;
  attempts?: number;
  steps?: number;
}): FormBuild {
  const { items, targets, count, seed } = args;
  const blocked = precheck(items, targets, count);
  if (blocked !== null) return { ok: false, blocker: blocked };
  let best: Needs | null = null;
  for (let a = 0; a < (args.attempts ?? FORM_ATTEMPTS); a += 1) {
    const { forms, needs } = greedy(items, targets, count, seed, a);
    if (needs.missing() === 0) return { ok: true, forms };
    if (best === null || needs.missing() < best.missing()) best = needs;
  }
  const found = search(items, targets, count, seed, args.steps ?? SEARCH_STEPS);
  if (found !== null) return { ok: true, forms: found };
  return { ok: false, blocker: best?.shortfall() ?? 'the search ran out of steps' };
}

/** Every way one form breaks its targets; [] when it meets them all. Shared by R12 and the tests. */
export function formProblems(form: readonly FormItem[], targets: FormTargets): string[] {
  const problems: string[] = [];
  const totals = domainTotals(targets);
  const size = [...totals.values()].reduce((a, n) => a + n, 0);
  if (form.length !== size) problems.push(`has ${form.length} items; it needs ${size}`);
  for (const [d, need] of totals) {
    const n = form.filter((i) => domainOf(i.task) === d).length;
    if (n !== need) problems.push(`domain ${d} has ${n} items; it needs ${need}`);
  }
  for (const [task, need] of Object.entries(targets.tasks)) {
    const n = form.filter((i) => i.task === task).length;
    if (n !== need) problems.push(`task ${task} has ${n} items; it needs ${need}`);
  }
  for (const d of totals.keys()) {
    const n = form.filter((i) => i.type === 'multi' && domainOf(i.task) === d).length;
    const need = targets.multi[d] ?? 0;
    if (n !== need) problems.push(`domain ${d} has ${n} multi-response items; it needs ${need}`);
  }
  const perConcept = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const item of form) perConcept.set(item.concept, (perConcept.get(item.concept) ?? 0) + 1);
  for (const [concept, n] of perConcept) if (n > 1) problems.push(`concept ${concept} appears ${n} times`);
  return problems;
}
```

## Task 4: `content:quotas`, R10 exam quotas, `--exam-targets` and the exam writer

**Files:**
- Create: `scripts/content/quotas.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/quotas-command.ts`, `content/exam/quotas.yaml` (generated), `agents/exam-writer.md`, `tests/unit/quotas-command.test.ts`
- Modify: `scripts/content/rules/r10-coverage.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/coverage.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/report.ts`, `scripts/content/report.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/draft.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/style.ts`, `content/STYLE.md`, `package.json`, `tests/fixtures/questions.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/r10.test.ts`, `tests/unit/quotas-command.test.ts`, `tests/unit/coverage.test.ts`, `tests/unit/draft.test.ts`, `tests/unit/agents.test.ts`, `tests/unit/exam-redaction.test.ts`, `tests/unit/style.test.ts` (unchanged; it still passes)

Design notes:
- Changes to existing files are unified diffs against the tree this task starts from: save a block to a file and run `git apply <file>` from the repository root, or make the same edit by hand; new files are shown whole.
- This task holds everything the exam-pool writers need, so Task 6 can start its writers as soon as it lands on `master`; the checks, the stamp, `content:forms` and R12 follow in Task 5.
- `quotasOf(ctx)` turns the tested concepts and the blueprint weights into `Quotas` with the Task 3 math and parses the result with the schema, so a sum that does not hold fails before anything is written; `quotasText` writes compact flow maps per cell and quotes every task and domain id, because YAML would read `1.1` as a number.
- `examQuotaProblems(ctx)` is exported for `pnpm content:forms --freeze` (Task 5); it counts live items only, by the pool file's module and the item's task, and never reports surplus, so the writers' spare items are harmless.
- `report(ctx)` now counts practice items only in its `questions:` and `mNN:` lines, adds one counts-only `mNN exam:` line per module with exam items, and groups by `entry.module` instead of slicing `noteId`, which for a pool item is the item's own note.
- `homed` and `targets` filter `pool === 'practice'` before they read `noteId`, because a pool item's `noteId` is its note: without the filter, a pool file would count as the lesson's question file (`content/exam/` sorts before `content/questions/`); Task 2 made that possible and this task owns `report.ts`, so the fix lands here.
- `moduleStyle` counts every non-retired question it is given, so its callers pick the pool; `report` is its only caller, and `style.test.ts` passes unchanged.
- `--exam-targets` prints, per module with quota cells, its pool file (the existing one, or `content/exam/pool/<module folder>.yaml`), each cell and multi-response share with `target`, `spare`, `live`, `open`, `need` and `liveNeed`, and the module's tested concepts with fewer than 4 open exam items; `spare` is `max(1, ceil(0.1 x target))` for a cell and 1 for a multi-response share, and `liveNeed` is the number the fan-out loop and R10 both end on.
- For a pool file, `--draft` swaps the practice coverage check for the 4-item cap (`concept <id> would hold <n> exam items; at most 4`, counted over the whole pool, drafts included); a task outside the concept's tasks is already R6's finding in `--draft`, so it is not reported twice.
- `agents/exam-writer.md` is a separate prompt at version 1 that never reads `content/questions/`; it takes the YAML layout from the "The file format" section of `agents/question-writer.md` instead of repeating it, and `agents/critic.md` stays at version 1, so practice passes stay valid.
- `tests/fixtures/questions.ts` gains `fixtureQuotas()`, made-up quotas for the fixture's two concepts that tests set on `ctx.quotas` directly; it skips the schema's sums on purpose, because the fixture registry is too small for real ones.
- The real config keeps `examQuotas: false` until Task 7, so `pnpm content:validate` still passes with an empty pool.
- Covers P3-002, P3-003, P3-005 (the report), P3-006, P3-009, P3-011, P3-012, P3-016, P3-017, P3-035, P3-037 (R10) and P3-041.

```diff
   mkdirSync(dirname(join(root, file)), { recursive: true });
   writeFileSync(join(root, file), stringify({ questions }, { lineWidth: 0 }));
 }
+
+/**
+ * Made-up quotas for the fixture tree's two concepts, for tests that set `ctx.quotas` directly: cells m01 1.1
+ * (target 2) and m01 4.1 (target 1), and 1 multi-response item in domain 1. Only R10 and the targets read it, so it
+ * skips the schema's sums.
+ */
+export function fixtureQuotas(): Quotas {
+  const tasks = Object.fromEntries(TASK_IDS.map((t) => [t, 0])) as Record<TaskId, number>;
+  return {
+    form: {
+      size: 65,
+      domains: { '1': 16, '2': 19, '3': 22, '4': 8 },
+      tasks,
+      multi: { '1': 2, '2': 2, '3': 3, '4': 1 },
+    },
+    pool: { size: 260, forms: 4, maxPerConcept: 4, multi: { '1': 1, '2': 0, '3': 0, '4': 0 } },
+    cells: [
+      { module: 1, task: '1.1', target: 2 },
+      { module: 1, task: '4.1', target: 1 },
+    ],
+    multiByModule: [{ module: 1, domain: '1', target: 1 }],
+  };
+}
```

Create `tests/unit/quotas-command.test.ts`:

```ts
import { appendFileSync, readFileSync, unlinkSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { parse } from 'yaml';
import { TASK_IDS } from '../../schemas/common.ts';
import { Quotas } from '../../schemas/exam.ts';
import { loadContext, QUOTAS_FILE } from '../../scripts/content/lib/context.ts';
import {
  QUOTAS_HEADER,
  QUOTAS_USAGE,
  quotasOf,
  runQuotas,
} from '../../scripts/content/lib/quotas-command.ts';
import { FIXTURE_TODAY, makeFixtureRoot } from '../fixtures/content-root.ts';

/** A fixture root whose registry tags one tested concept per task in Module 1 and one more in Module 2. */
function quotaRoot(): string {
  const root = makeFixtureRoot();
  const entries = [1, 2].flatMap((module) =>
    TASK_IDS.map((task) => {
      const id = `m${module}-task-${task.replace('.', '-')}`;
      return `- { id: ${id}, name: Concept ${id}, home: m0${module}/what-a-bakery-cloud-is, anchor: ${id}, tasks: ["${task}"] }`;
    }),
  );
  writeFileSync(join(root, 'content/concepts.yaml'), `${entries.join('\n')}\n`);
  return root;
}

/** Per-form counts when every task of a domain has the same number of concepts: largest remainder, earlier task first. */
const PER_FORM: Record<string, number> = {
  '1.1': 4,
  '1.2': 4,
  '1.3': 4,
  '1.4': 4,
  '2.1': 5,
  '2.2': 5,
  '2.3': 5,
  '2.4': 4,
  '3.1': 3,
  '3.2': 3,
  '3.3': 3,
  '3.4': 3,
  '3.5': 3,
  '3.6': 3,
  '3.7': 2,
  '3.8': 2,
  '4.1': 3,
  '4.2': 3,
  '4.3': 2,
};

describe('pnpm content:quotas', () => {
  it('writes quotas.yaml and prints the pool size and each task count', () => {
    const root = quotaRoot();
    expect(runQuotas(root, [])).toEqual({
      code: 0,
      out: [
        'quotas: 260 items in 38 cells over 2 modules',
        ...TASK_IDS.map((t) => `${t} per form ${PER_FORM[t]}, pool ${4 * (PER_FORM[t] ?? 0)}`),
      ],
    });
    const text = readFileSync(join(root, QUOTAS_FILE), 'utf8');
    expect(text.split('\n')[0]).toBe(QUOTAS_HEADER);
    const written = Quotas.parse(parse(text));
    expect(written).toEqual(quotasOf(loadContext(root, { today: FIXTURE_TODAY })));
    expect(written.cells.filter((c) => c.task === '2.1')).toEqual([
      { module: 1, task: '2.1', target: 10 },
      { module: 2, task: '2.1', target: 10 },
    ]);
    expect(loadContext(root, { today: FIXTURE_TODAY }).loadErrors).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('passes --check after a write and fails it after a hand edit, writing nothing', () => {
    const root = quotaRoot();
    runQuotas(root, []);
    expect(runQuotas(root, ['--check']).out.at(-1)).toBe('quotas.yaml is up to date');
    appendFileSync(join(root, QUOTAS_FILE), '# edited by hand\n');
    const before = readFileSync(join(root, QUOTAS_FILE), 'utf8');
    expect(runQuotas(root, ['--check'])).toEqual({
      code: 1,
      out: ['quotas.yaml is out of date; run pnpm content:quotas'],
    });
    expect(readFileSync(join(root, QUOTAS_FILE), 'utf8')).toBe(before);
  });

  it('prints the usage for an unknown flag and names a missing blueprint', () => {
    const root = quotaRoot();
    expect(runQuotas(root, ['--write'])).toEqual({ code: 1, out: [QUOTAS_USAGE] });
    unlinkSync(join(root, 'content/exam/blueprint.yaml'));
    expect(runQuotas(root, [])).toEqual({
      code: 1,
      out: ['quotas: content/exam/blueprint.yaml is missing or invalid'],
    });
  });
});
```

```diff

-import { BLUEPRINT_FILE, CONCEPTS_FILE, CONFIG_FILE, type Finding } from '../lib/context.ts';

+import { DOMAIN_IDS } from '../../../schemas/ids.ts';
+import {
+  BLUEPRINT_FILE,
+  CONCEPTS_FILE,
+  CONFIG_FILE,
+  type Context,
+  type Finding,
+  QUOTAS_FILE,

+import {
+  examConceptCounts,
+  examCounts,
+  examMultiByDomain,
+  missingTerms,
+  practiceCounts,
+} from '../lib/coverage.ts';
 import { finding, type Rule } from './rule.ts';
 
+const live = (q: { status: string }): boolean => q.status === 'live';
+
+/**
+ * The exam quota check of R10 (spec 2.9), also run by `pnpm content:forms --freeze`: every quotas.yaml cell and
+ * domain multi-response target met by live exam-pool items, and no concept above 4 of them; surplus is fine.
+ */
+export function examQuotaProblems(ctx: Context): Finding[] {
+  const quotas = ctx.quotas;
+  if (quotas === null) {
+    return [finding('R10', CONFIG_FILE, `examQuotas is on, but ${QUOTAS_FILE} is missing`)];
+  }
+  const out: Finding[] = [];
+  const cells = examCounts(ctx, live);
+  for (const cell of quotas.cells) {
+    const n = cells.get(`${cell.module}|${cell.task}`) ?? 0;
+    if (n >= cell.target) continue;
+    const nn = String(cell.module).padStart(2, '0');
+    out.push(
+      finding(
+        'R10',
+        QUOTAS_FILE,
+        `cell m${nn} task ${cell.task} has ${n} live exam items; it needs ${cell.target}`,
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+  const multi = examMultiByDomain(ctx, live);
+  for (const d of DOMAIN_IDS) {
+    const n = multi.get(d) ?? 0;
+    const target = quotas.pool.multi[d];
+    if (n < target) {
+      out.push(
+        finding(
+          'R10',
+          QUOTAS_FILE,
+          `domain ${d} has ${n} live multi-response exam items; it needs ${target}`,
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+  const perConcept = examConceptCounts(ctx, live);
+  for (const [id, n] of [...perConcept].sort((a, b) => a[0].localeCompare(b[0]))) {
+    if (n > quotas.pool.maxPerConcept) {
+      out.push(
+        finding(
+          'R10',
+          QUOTAS_FILE,
+          `concept ${id} has ${n} live exam items; at most ${quotas.pool.maxPerConcept}`,
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+  return out;
+}
+

   id: 'R10',
```

```diff
+
+/** One cell or multi-response share of `--exam-targets`; `liveNeed` 0 is the only sign that it is met. */
+interface ExamNeed {
+  target: number;
+  /** Extra items asked of the writers, so one retired or stuck item never blocks the forms. */
+  spare: number;
+  /** Live items. */
+  live: number;
+  /** Live items plus drafts below 3 failed check rounds. */
+  open: number;
+  /** Items to write now: target + spare - open, never below 0. */
+  need: number;
+  /** Live items still missing: target - live, never below 0; the test R10 and the fan-out loop share. */
+  liveNeed: number;
+}
+export interface ExamTarget {
+  module: number;
+  file: string;
+  cells: (ExamNeed & { task: string })[];
+  multi: (ExamNeed & { domain: DomainIdValue })[];
+  /** The module's tested concepts that can still take an exam item, with their open exam items. */
+  concepts: { id: string; note: string; tasks: string[]; open: number }[];
+}
+
+const examNeed = (target: number, spare: number, live: number, open: number): ExamNeed => ({
+  target,
+  spare,
+  live,
+  open,
+  need: Math.max(0, target + spare - open),
+  liveNeed: Math.max(0, target - live),
+});
+
+/** A cell's spare: one item per started ten of its target, never below one. */
+export const cellSpare = (target: number): number => Math.max(1, Math.ceil(0.1 * target));
+
+/** `--exam-targets`: per module with quota cells, its pool file, cell and multi-response needs, and open concepts. */
+export function examTargets(ctx: Context): ExamTarget[] {
+  const quotas = ctx.quotas;
+  if (quotas === null) throw new Error(`${QUOTAS_FILE} is missing; run pnpm content:quotas`);
+  const isLive = (q: Question): boolean => q.status === 'live';
+  const live = examCounts(ctx, isLive);
+  const open = examCounts(ctx, isOpen);
+  const conceptOpen = examConceptCounts(ctx, isOpen);
+  const multiOf = (module: number, d: DomainIdValue, keep: (q: Question) => boolean): number =>
+    ctx.questions.filter(
+      (e) =>
+        e.module === module &&
+        e.q.pool === 'exam' &&
+        e.q.type === 'multi' &&
+        domainOf(e.q.task) === d &&
+        keep(e.q),
+    ).length;
+  const modules = [...new Set(quotas.cells.map((c) => c.module))].sort((a, b) => a - b);
+  return modules.map((module) => {
+    const readme = ctx.notes.find((n) => n.kind === 'module' && n.module === module);
+    const dir = readme?.path.split('/')[1] ?? pad2(module);
+    const file =
+      ctx.questions.find((e) => e.q.pool === 'exam' && e.module === module)?.path ??
+      `${POOL_DIR}/${dir}.yaml`;
+    const cells = quotas.cells
+      .filter((c) => c.module === module)
+      .map((c) => {
+        const key = `${module}|${c.task}`;
+        const spare = cellSpare(c.target);
+        return { task: c.task, ...examNeed(c.target, spare, live.get(key) ?? 0, open.get(key) ?? 0) };
+      });
+    const multi = quotas.multiByModule
+      .filter((m) => m.module === module)
+      .map((m) => ({
+        domain: m.domain,
+        ...examNeed(m.target, 1, multiOf(module, m.domain, isLive), multiOf(module, m.domain, isOpen)),
+      }));
+    const concepts = ctx.concepts
+      .filter((c) => c.tested !== false && Number(c.home.slice(1, 3)) === module)
+      .map((c) => ({ id: c.id, note: c.home, tasks: [...c.tasks], open: conceptOpen.get(c.id) ?? 0 }))
+      .filter((c) => c.open < MAX_PER_CONCEPT);
+    return { module, file, cells, multi, concepts };
+  });
+}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Add `pnpm content:quotas`**

Create `scripts/content/lib/quotas-command.ts`:

```ts
/** pnpm content:quotas: writes content/exam/quotas.yaml from the registry and the blueprint (spec 2.6, 4.3). */
import { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { TASK_IDS } from '../../../schemas/common.ts';
import { Quotas } from '../../../schemas/exam.ts';
import { DOMAIN_IDS } from '../../../schemas/ids.ts';
import {
  domainCounts,
  FORM_COUNT,
  FORM_SIZE,
  MAX_PER_CONCEPT,
  MULTI_PER_FORM,
  multiByModule,
  poolCells,
  taskCounts,
} from '../../../src/engine/quotas.ts';
import { type Context, loadContext, QUOTAS_FILE } from './context.ts';

export const QUOTAS_USAGE = 'usage: pnpm content:quotas [--check]';
export const QUOTAS_HEADER =
  '# Generated by pnpm content:quotas from concepts.yaml and blueprint.yaml; never edit by hand.';

/** The quotas of a context's tested concepts and blueprint weights, checked against the schema. */
export function quotasOf(ctx: Context): Quotas {
  const blueprint = ctx.blueprint;
  if (blueprint === null) throw new Error('content/exam/blueprint.yaml is missing or invalid');
  const concepts = ctx.concepts
    .filter((c) => c.tested !== false)
    .map((c) => ({ id: c.id, module: Number(c.home.slice(1, 3)), tasks: c.tasks }));
  const weight = (d: string): number => blueprint.domains.find((x) => x.id === d)?.weight ?? 0;
  const domains = domainCounts({ '1': weight('1'), '2': weight('2'), '3': weight('3'), '4': weight('4') });
  const tasks = taskCounts(concepts, domains, TASK_IDS);
  const cells = poolCells(concepts, tasks);
  const times = (n: number): number => FORM_COUNT * n;
  return Quotas.parse({
    form: { size: FORM_SIZE, domains, tasks, multi: { ...MULTI_PER_FORM } },
    pool: {
      size: FORM_COUNT * FORM_SIZE,
      forms: FORM_COUNT,
      maxPerConcept: MAX_PER_CONCEPT,
      multi: {
        '1': times(MULTI_PER_FORM['1']),
        '2': times(MULTI_PER_FORM['2']),
        '3': times(MULTI_PER_FORM['3']),
        '4': times(MULTI_PER_FORM['4']),
      },
    },
    cells,
    multiByModule: multiByModule(cells),
  });
}

/** quotas.yaml text: compact flow maps per cell, and task and domain ids quoted so YAML keeps them strings. */
export function quotasText(q: Quotas): string {
  const counts = (record: Readonly<Record<string, number>>, keys: readonly string[]): string =>
    `{ ${keys.map((k) => `"${k}": ${record[k]}`).join(', ')} }`;
  return [
    QUOTAS_HEADER,
    'form:',
    `  size: ${q.form.size}`,
    `  domains: ${counts(q.form.domains, DOMAIN_IDS)}`,
    '  tasks:',
    ...TASK_IDS.map((t) => `    "${t}": ${q.form.tasks[t]}`),
    `  multi: ${counts(q.form.multi, DOMAIN_IDS)}`,
    'pool:',
    `  size: ${q.pool.size}`,
    `  forms: ${q.pool.forms}`,
    `  maxPerConcept: ${q.pool.maxPerConcept}`,
    `  multi: ${counts(q.pool.multi, DOMAIN_IDS)}`,
    'cells:',
    ...q.cells.map((c) => `  - { module: ${c.module}, task: "${c.task}", target: ${c.target} }`),
    'multiByModule:',
    ...q.multiByModule.map((m) => `  - { module: ${m.module}, domain: "${m.domain}", target: ${m.target} }`),
    '',
  ].join('\n');
}

/** The summary lines: the pool size, cells and modules, then each task's per-form and pool count. */
function summary(q: Quotas): string[] {
  const modules = new Set(q.cells.map((c) => c.module)).size;
  return [
    `quotas: ${q.pool.size} items in ${q.cells.length} cells over ${modules} modules`,
    ...TASK_IDS.map((t) => `${t} per form ${q.form.tasks[t]}, pool ${q.pool.forms * q.form.tasks[t]}`),
  ];
}

/** Writes quotas.yaml, or with --check only compares it; exit code 1 on bad arguments, bad sources or a stale file. */
export function runQuotas(root: string, argv: readonly string[]): { code: number; out: string[] } {
  if (argv.some((a) => a !== '--check')) return { code: 1, out: [QUOTAS_USAGE] };
  let quotas: Quotas;
  const ctx = loadContext(root);
  try {
    quotas = quotasOf(ctx);
  } catch (error) {
    return { code: 1, out: [`quotas: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`] };
  }
  const text = quotasText(quotas);
  if (argv.includes('--check')) {
    if (ctx.read(QUOTAS_FILE) !== text) {
      return { code: 1, out: ['quotas.yaml is out of date; run pnpm content:quotas'] };
    }
    return { code: 0, out: [...summary(quotas), 'quotas.yaml is up to date'] };
  }
  writeFileSync(join(root, QUOTAS_FILE), text);
  return { code: 0, out: summary(quotas) };
}
```

Create `scripts/content/quotas.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * pnpm content:quotas: writes content/exam/quotas.yaml from content/concepts.yaml and content/exam/blueprint.yaml,
 * the per-form domain, task and multi-response counts and the pool's module by task cells (spec 2.6, 4.3).
 *   --check   write nothing; exit 1 when the committed file is out of date
 */
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { runQuotas } from './lib/quotas-command.ts';

const { code, out } = runQuotas(resolve(import.meta.dirname, '..', '..'), process.argv.slice(2));
for (const line of out) (code === 0 ? console.log : console.error)(line);
process.exitCode = code;
```

```diff
+
+## Exam pool
+
+For an item with pool: exam, the cafe rules above do not apply, and this section replaces them.
+Exam-pool items live in `content/exam/pool/NN-<module-slug>.yaml` and fill the mock forms, so they read like the real exam rather than like the course.
+
+- Use `single` or `multi` only; `match` stays in the practice pool.
+- Set scenario stems in neutral made-up businesses, such as a bike rental shop, a regional clinic or an online bookstore, and never in the cafe.
+- Vary the business from item to item, so no two items of a file share one.
+- Make about 60% of stems scenarios, and ask about the concept directly in the rest.
+- Every other rule in this guide applies unchanged: shapes, levels, one concept per item, distractors, parallel options, banned phrasings, negative stems, numbers, current names, whys, evidence and the takeaway.
+
+- Good: `A regional clinic stores scanned patient forms that staff open often in the first month and rarely after that.`
+- Bad: `The cafe's loyalty app stores receipts.` sets an exam item in the cafe.
```

Create `agents/exam-writer.md`:

````markdown
---
name: exam-writer
version: 1
---

# Exam writer

You write the exam-pool items of one module into one pool file (spec 2.6 and 2.7).
These items fill the four mock forms, which measure readiness on questions Dani has never seen.
Checker agents then verify each item, and `pnpm content:stamp` makes it live, so you only ever write drafts.

## Inputs

- Your module number `NN` and your module's entry of `pnpm --silent content:report --exam-targets`, run from the repository root.
- The entry gives your target `file`, under `content/exam/pool/`, its `cells`, its `multi` shares and its `concepts`.
- Each cell names a `task` and counts `target`, `spare`, `live`, `open`, `need` and `liveNeed`; `open` counts live items plus drafts below 3 failed check rounds.
- Each `multi` share names a `domain` and carries the same counts for multi-response items.
- Each concept names its `id`, its home `note`, its `tasks` and `open`, the exam items it already holds over the whole pool.
- For each concept you write on: its home note under `resources/`, and its entry in `content/concepts.yaml`, which you only read.
- `content/exam/blueprint.yaml`, for the wording of each task statement.
- `content/STYLE.md`, the rules every item follows, with its section `Exam pool`; read it in full before you write.
- `resources/glossary.md`, the only services a distractor may name besides those in an "Exam guide update" line.
- The section "The file format" of `agents/question-writer.md`, for the YAML layout of an item.
- That section's example is a practice item set in the cafe, so take only its layout: your items set `pool: exam`, use `single` or `multi` only, and follow the `Exam pool` section of `content/STYLE.md`.

Never read the course source text, `~/Library/Application Support/ccp-study/`, AWS exam questions, anything under `content/fingerprints/`, anything under `.results/`, or any file under `content/questions/`.
Your items must come from the notes in your own words and stay independent of the practice questions Dani has seen, so nothing can echo the course, the exam or the practice pool.

## What to write

- For each cell, write exactly its `need` new items on its `task`, and none for a cell whose `need` is 0.
- Spread a cell's items round-robin over the concepts that list its `task`, taking the concept with the fewest `open` items first.
- Never take a concept past 4 open exam items, counting the items you write; when a cell's concepts are full, write fewer and say so in `problems`.
- For each `multi` share, make exactly its `need` of your items `multi`, on tasks of that domain; every other item is `single`.
- Each item tests its own concept only, with `concept` set to that concept's id, `note` set to its home note, and `task` set to the cell's task, which must be one of the concept's `tasks`.
- Pick `level` 1, 2 or 3 by what the reader must do in that stem, as `content/STYLE.md` defines them.
- Copy every `evidence` line as one whole line of the concept's home note, without its list marker; for an "Exam guide update" line, copy only the sentence after the colon.
- Get fresh ids with `pnpm --silent content:new-question N`, one per item, and never invent or reuse an id.
- Every new item starts with `version: 1`, `status: draft`, `pool: exam` and `history: []`, and has no `verified` or `review` block, because only the tools write those.
- Add your items to the end of the file's `questions` list, or create the file with one `questions` list when it does not exist.
- Never change or remove a `live` or `retired` item, or a draft whose `review.attempts` is 3 or more.

## Checking your file

Run `pnpm --silent content:validate --draft <file>` and fix every problem it prints, until its last line ends with `0 problems`, then stop.
It checks the schema, the answer counts, the whys, the evidence lines, the references, originality against the course, the mechanical style rules and the 4-item cap per concept, and it prints ids only.

## Revising after the checks

When your task says to revise, read each draft's `review.lastFailure`, which names the failed check and its reason.

- Fix that cause in the item, keep its `id`, and leave its `review` block for the tools to update.
- A `solve-closed` failure is tied to the stem, the option texts and which options are correct, so its fix must change at least one of them.
- For a cue failure with a `tell` basis, remove the tell; with a `knowledge` basis, rewrite the distractors as statements that are also true about AWS but do not answer the stem.
- Revise only drafts whose `review.attempts` is below 3; the tools retire an exam item at its third failed round, and a later round writes a new one.
- Loop on `pnpm --silent content:validate --draft <file>` until it ends with `0 problems` again.

## What you return

Return one JSON object and nothing else: `{ "file": "<your file>", "written": <new items>, "problems": ["<id or cell>: <short reason>"] }`.
Never quote a stem, an option, a why, a takeaway, an evidence line or a failure reason in your return, in a log or anywhere outside your file; name items by id and cells by module and task only.

## Rules

- Write only your one target file, and never edit a note, `content/concepts.yaml`, `content/exam/quotas.yaml`, `content/exam/forms.yaml`, another pool file or any practice file.
- Never write course wording: if a phrase comes to mind as something you read in the course, say it differently.
- Follow `content/STYLE.md` in every item, with its `Exam pool` section in place of the cafe rules.
````

## Task 5: Checks, stamp and `content:forms` over the exam pool, and R12

**Files:**
- Create: `scripts/content/forms.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/forms-command.ts`, `scripts/content/rules/r12-forms.ts`, `tests/content/bad/r12-repeated-concept.yaml`, `tests/fixtures/exam-pool.ts`, `tests/unit/forms-command.test.ts`, `tests/unit/r12.test.ts`
- Modify: `scripts/content/lib/checks.ts`, `scripts/content/checks.ts`, `scripts/content/stamp.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/question-files.ts`, `scripts/content/rules/index.ts`, `package.json`, `tests/fixtures/content-root/content/validate.yaml`, `tests/unit/checks.test.ts`, `tests/unit/exam-redaction.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/checks.test.ts`, `tests/unit/r12.test.ts`, `tests/unit/forms-command.test.ts`, `tests/unit/exam-redaction.test.ts`, `tests/content/bad.test.ts`

Design notes:
- The check pipeline gains a pool selector and nothing else: `candidates` and `prepare` take `pool` (default `practice`), `moduleOf` reads `QuestionEntry.module` (Task 2), and the critic payload carries `pool` so the critic can apply STYLE's exam section; `agents/critic.md` stays at version 1 because its text does not change, so no practice pass goes stale.
- `fullHash` is unchanged (spec 2.7 lists its fields, and `pool` is not one), so the extra payload field never re-runs a stamped item.
- `grade` now returns the lines the CLI prints: an exam failure prints as `FAIL <id> <check>`, because a checker's reason may paraphrase the item.
- `stampQuestions` stamps both pools; an exam draft's failure logs `review <id> attempts <n> <check>`, and its third failed round sets `status: retired` in place and logs `retire <id>` (spec 2.7), so no exam item ever reaches `--needs-dani`; practice drafts keep their Phase 2 path.
- The stamp summary gains the total of retired exam items: `stamp: <L> live, <R> drafts to revise, <D> need Dani, <X> retired`.
- `pnpm content:forms` keeps every frozen form as it is and builds the others with `buildForms` (Task 3) from the live items in no frozen form, with seed `20261024`, and writes `frozen: false`.
- `pnpm content:forms --freeze` never builds: it freezes the forms already in `forms.yaml` (the ones a person reviewed with R12), and only when `examQuotaProblems` (Task 4) and R12's form checks find nothing; `--allow-short-cells` waives only the quota half and is used only after Dani answers yes to the short-cell question.
- R12 and the freeze guard share `formSetProblems` in `r12-forms.ts`, so they can never disagree.
- The made-up pool in `tests/fixtures/exam-pool.ts` holds exactly 4 x 65 live items over the decided per-form task counts, one task per concept and four items per concept, so each form must take one item of every concept; the brief names no fixture file for it, and R12, `content:forms` and the redaction test all need the same pool.
- Every line these tools print is a form id, a count, a task, domain or concept id, a module number or a qid; the redaction sentinel proves it with the `ZZLEAKZZ` token.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the made-up exam pool fixture**

Create `tests/fixtures/exam-pool.ts`:

```ts
import { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { stringify } from 'yaml';
import { TASK_IDS } from '../../schemas/common.ts';
import { DOMAIN_IDS } from '../../schemas/ids.ts';
import type { Question } from '../../schemas/question.ts';
import { multiQuestion, singleQuestion, writePool } from './questions.ts';

/** Per-form task counts of the Phase 3 quota decision; they sum to 65. */
export const FORM_TASKS: Readonly<Record<string, number>> = {
  '1.1': 4,
  '1.2': 2,
  '1.3': 7,
  '1.4': 3,
  '2.1': 3,
  '2.2': 10,
  '2.3': 3,
  '2.4': 3,
  '3.1': 2,
  '3.2': 2,
  '3.3': 4,
  '3.4': 3,
  '3.5': 2,
  '3.6': 4,
  '3.7': 3,
  '3.8': 2,
  '4.1': 3,
  '4.2': 1,
  '4.3': 4,
};
const FORM_MULTI = { '1': 2, '2': 2, '3': 3, '4': 1 } as const;
const CROCKFORD = '0123456789abcdefghjkmnpqrstvwxyz';

/** A made-up exam id: `q-e` and the number in five Crockford digits, so it never meets a practice fixture id. */
export function examQid(n: number): string {
  let digits = '';
  for (let rest = n, i = 0; i < 5; i++, rest = Math.floor(rest / 32))
    digits = CROCKFORD.charAt(rest % 32) + digits;
  return `q-e${digits}`;
}

/** The concept of the j-th seat (from 1) of a task: `exam-1-3-2` for task 1.3. */
export const examConcept = (task: string, j: number): string => `exam-${task.replace('.', '-')}-${j}`;

/**
 * Exactly 4 x 65 live exam items: each task holds as many concepts as its per-form count, each concept 4 items, and the
 * first concepts of each domain are multi-response until the domain's per-form multi count is met. `text` is added to
 * every stem, why and takeaway, so a test can prove that no tool prints item text; `status` sets every item's status.
 */
export function examPoolQuestions(text = '', status: 'draft' | 'live' = 'live'): Question[] {
  const out: Question[] = [];
  const multiLeft: Record<string, number> = { ...FORM_MULTI };
  let n = 0;
  for (const task of TASK_IDS) {
    for (let j = 1; j <= (FORM_TASKS[task] ?? 0); j++) {
      const domain = task.charAt(0);
      const multi = (multiLeft[domain] ?? 0) > 0;
      if (multi) multiLeft[domain] = (multiLeft[domain] ?? 0) - 1;
      for (let copy = 0; copy < 4; copy++) {
        const id = examQid(n++);
        const base = { id, status, pool: 'exam' as const, concept: examConcept(task, j), task };
        if (multi) {
          const q = multiQuestion(base);
          out.push({ ...q, stem: `${q.stem} ${text}`.trim(), takeaway: `${q.takeaway} ${text}`.trim() });
        } else {
          const q = singleQuestion(base);
          out.push({
            ...q,
            stem: `${q.stem} ${text}`.trim(),
            options: q.options.map((o) => ({ ...o, why: `${o.why} ${text}`.trim() })),
            takeaway: `${q.takeaway} ${text}`.trim(),
          });
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * A quotas.yaml for the made-up pool. With `splitTask`, that task's pool target is split between Modules 1 and 2, so
 * the Module 2 cell stays short while the forms still build from the Module 1 items.
 */
export function examQuotas(splitTask?: string): object {
  const domains = { '1': 16, '2': 19, '3': 22, '4': 8 };
  const cells = TASK_IDS.flatMap((task) => {
    const target = 4 * (FORM_TASKS[task] ?? 0);
    return task === splitTask
      ? [
          { module: 1, task, target: target / 2 },
          { module: 2, task, target: target / 2 },
        ]
      : [{ module: 1, task, target }];
  }).sort((a, b) => a.module - b.module || TASK_IDS.indexOf(a.task) - TASK_IDS.indexOf(b.task));
  return {
    form: { size: 65, domains, tasks: { ...FORM_TASKS }, multi: { ...FORM_MULTI } },
    pool: { size: 260, forms: 4, maxPerConcept: 4, multi: { '1': 8, '2': 8, '3': 12, '4': 4 } },
    cells,
    multiByModule: DOMAIN_IDS.map((domain) => ({ module: 1, domain, target: 4 * FORM_MULTI[domain] })),
  };
}

/** Writes the made-up pool and its quotas.yaml into a fixture root; returns the questions it wrote. */
export function writeExamPool(
  root: string,
  options: { text?: string; status?: 'draft' | 'live'; splitTask?: string } = {},
): Question[] {
  const questions = examPoolQuestions(options.text, options.status);
  writePool(root, questions);
  writeFileSync(join(root, 'content/exam/quotas.yaml'), stringify(examQuotas(options.splitTask)));
  return questions;
}
```

The stamp summary gains its retired count, so the three summary strings of the `stampQuestions` test change:

```ts
    expect(first.summary).toBe('stamp: 2 live, 1 drafts to revise, 0 need Dani, 0 retired');
```

```ts
    expect(stampQuestions(load(root), FIXTURE_TODAY).summary).toBe(
      'stamp: 2 live, 1 drafts to revise, 0 need Dani, 0 retired',
    );
```

```ts
    expect(stampQuestions(load(root), FIXTURE_TODAY).summary).toBe(
      'stamp: 2 live, 0 drafts to revise, 1 need Dani, 0 retired',
    );
```

The last of the three still proves that a practice draft at 3 attempts goes to needs Dani.
Append to the end of `tests/unit/checks.test.ts`:

```ts
describe('the exam pool', () => {
  const conceptOf = new Map([
    ['q-e1a2b3', 'shared-kitchen'],
    ['q-e4c5d6', 'shared-kitchen'],
    ['q-e7f8g9', 'per-minute-rental'],
  ]);
  /** The practice single and match in their lesson file, and three exam items in Module 1's pool file. */
  function examSetup(): string {
    const root = setup([singleQuestion(), matchQuestion()]);
    writePool(root, [
      singleQuestion({ id: 'q-e1a2b3', pool: 'exam' }),
      singleQuestion({ id: 'q-e4c5d6', pool: 'exam', stem: `${singleQuestion().stem} At the mill.` }),
      multiQuestion({ id: 'q-e7f8g9', pool: 'exam', concept: 'per-minute-rental' }),
    ]);
    return root;
  }
  const failCritic = (root: string, target: string, problem: string): void =>
    answerAll(root, (batch, id, answer) =>
      id === target && batch.startsWith('critic') && !batch.startsWith('critic-cue')
        ? { ...answer, noTell: false, problems: [problem] }
        : answer,
    );

  it('batches only exam items with --pool exam, at most one per concept, and tells the critic the pool', () => {
    const root = examSetup();
    expect(prepare(load(root), [1], 1).questions).toBe(2);
    expect(
      batchFiles(root)
        .filter((b) => b.kind === 'critic')
        .flatMap((b) => b.questions.map((q) => q.pool)),
    ).toEqual(['practice', 'practice']);

    expect(prepare(load(root), [1], 1, 'exam').questions).toBe(3);
    for (const b of batchFiles(root)) {
      const ids = b.questions.map((q) => String(q.id));
      expect(ids.every((id) => conceptOf.has(id))).toBe(true);
      const concepts = ids.map((id) => conceptOf.get(id));
      expect(new Set(concepts).size).toBe(concepts.length);
    }
    expect(
      batchFiles(root)
        .filter((b) => b.kind === 'critic')
        .flatMap((b) => b.questions.map((q) => q.pool)),
    ).toEqual(['exam', 'exam', 'exam']);
  });

  it('prints an exam failure as its id and check only, and a practice failure with its reason', () => {
    const root = examSetup();
    prepare(load(root), [1], 1, 'exam');
    failCritic(root, 'q-e1a2b3', 'The stem names the oven.');
    expect(grade(root, FIXTURE_TODAY).lines).toEqual(['FAIL q-e1a2b3 critic']);

    prepare(load(root), [1], 2);
    failCritic(root, 'q-7k2m9p', 'The stem names the oven.');
    expect(grade(root, FIXTURE_TODAY).lines).toEqual(['FAIL q-7k2m9p critic: The stem names the oven.']);
  });

  it('retires an exam draft at its third failed round and logs ids only', () => {
    const root = examSetup();
    const logs: string[][] = [];
    const summaries: string[] = [];
    for (const attempt of [1, 2, 3]) {
      if (attempt > 1) replaceIn(root, POOL_FILE, 'on market days', `on market days (take ${attempt})`);
      prepare(load(root), [1], attempt, 'exam');
      failCritic(root, 'q-e1a2b3', 'The stem names the oven.');
      grade(root, FIXTURE_TODAY);
      const stamp = stampQuestions(load(root), FIXTURE_TODAY);
      logs.push(stamp.log);
      summaries.push(stamp.summary);
    }
    expect(logs).toEqual([
      ['review q-e1a2b3 attempts 1 critic', `live q-e4c5d6 (${POOL_FILE})`, `live q-e7f8g9 (${POOL_FILE})`],
      ['review q-e1a2b3 attempts 2 critic'],
      ['retire q-e1a2b3'],
    ]);
    expect(summaries).toEqual([
      'stamp: 2 live, 1 drafts to revise, 0 need Dani, 0 retired',
      'stamp: 2 live, 1 drafts to revise, 0 need Dani, 0 retired',
      'stamp: 2 live, 0 drafts to revise, 0 need Dani, 1 retired',
    ]);
    const retired = load(root).questions.find((e) => e.q.id === 'q-e1a2b3')?.q;
    expect(retired?.status).toBe('retired');
    expect(retired?.review).toEqual({ attempts: 3, lastFailure: 'critic: The stem names the oven.' });
    expect(stampQuestions(load(root), FIXTURE_TODAY).revise).toEqual([]);
    expect(prepare(load(root), [1], 4, 'exam').questions).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing R12 and `content:forms` tests**

Create `tests/unit/r12.test.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { parse, stringify } from 'yaml';
import type { Forms } from '../../schemas/exam.ts';
import { FORMS_FILE, loadContext } from '../../scripts/content/lib/context.ts';
import { runForms } from '../../scripts/content/lib/forms-command.ts';
import { r12 } from '../../scripts/content/rules/r12-forms.ts';
import { FIXTURE_TODAY, makeFixtureRoot, replaceIn } from '../fixtures/content-root.ts';
import { examConcept, writeExamPool } from '../fixtures/exam-pool.ts';
import { POOL_FILE } from '../fixtures/questions.ts';

const run = (root: string) => r12.run(loadContext(root, { today: FIXTURE_TODAY }));
const messages = (root: string): string[] => run(root).map((f) => f.message);

/** A fixture root with the made-up pool and forms A to D built by content:forms. */
function built(): { root: string; forms: Forms; conceptOf: Map<string, string> } {
  const root = makeFixtureRoot();
  const questions = writeExamPool(root);
  expect(runForms(root, []).code).toBe(0);
  const forms = parse(readFileSync(join(root, FORMS_FILE), 'utf8')) as Forms;
  return { root, forms, conceptOf: new Map(questions.map((q) => [q.id, q.concept])) };
}
const writeForms = (root: string, forms: Forms): void =>
  writeFileSync(join(root, FORMS_FILE), stringify(forms));
const setStatus = (root: string, id: string, status: string): void => {
  const text = readFileSync(join(root, POOL_FILE), 'utf8');
  const at = text.indexOf(`id: ${id}`);
  writeFileSync(
    join(root, POOL_FILE),
    text.slice(0, at) + text.slice(at).replace('status: live', `status: ${status}`),
  );
};

describe('R12 Mock forms', () => {
  it('passes without forms.yaml while examQuotas is off, and fires once it is on', () => {
    const root = makeFixtureRoot();
    expect(run(root)).toEqual([]);
    replaceIn(root, 'content/validate.yaml', 'examQuotas: false', 'examQuotas: true');
    expect(run(root)).toEqual([
      { rule: 'R12', file: FORMS_FILE, message: 'forms.yaml is missing; run pnpm content:forms' },
    ]);
  });

  it('passes forms that content:forms built', () => {
    expect(run(built().root)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('fires on a draft item in a form that is not frozen', () => {
    const { root, forms } = built();
    const id = forms.forms[0]?.qids[0] ?? '';
    setStatus(root, id, 'draft');
    expect(messages(root)).toContain(`form A holds ${id}, which is not a live exam item`);
  });

  it('fires on a form of 64 items', () => {
    const { root, forms } = built();
    const formA = forms.forms[0];
    if (formA === undefined) throw new Error('no form A');
    formA.qids.pop();
    writeForms(root, forms);
    expect(messages(root)).toContain('form A has 64 items; it needs 65');
  });

  it('fires on a concept repeated inside a form', () => {
    const { root, forms, conceptOf } = built();
    const [a, b] = forms.forms;
    if (a === undefined || b === undefined) throw new Error('no forms A and B');
    const x = examConcept('2.2', 1);
    const y = examConcept('2.2', 2);
    const ax = a.qids.findIndex((id) => conceptOf.get(id) === x);
    const ay = a.qids.findIndex((id) => conceptOf.get(id) === y);
    const bx = b.qids.findIndex((id) => conceptOf.get(id) === x);
    const swapped = a.qids[ay] ?? '';
    a.qids[ay] = b.qids[bx] ?? '';
    b.qids[bx] = swapped;
    writeForms(root, forms);
    expect(ax).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    expect(messages(root)).toContain(`form A concept ${x} appears 2 times`);
    expect(messages(root)).toContain(`form B concept ${y} appears 2 times`);
  });

  it('fires on a wrong domain split', () => {
    const { root, forms, conceptOf } = built();
    const [a, b] = forms.forms;
    if (a === undefined || b === undefined) throw new Error('no forms A and B');
    const fromA = a.qids.findIndex((id) => conceptOf.get(id) === examConcept('1.3', 1));
    const fromB = b.qids.findIndex((id) => conceptOf.get(id) === examConcept('3.3', 1));
    const swapped = a.qids[fromA] ?? '';
    a.qids[fromA] = b.qids[fromB] ?? '';
    b.qids[fromB] = swapped;
    writeForms(root, forms);
    expect(messages(root)).toContain('form A domain 1 has 15 items; it needs 16');
    expect(messages(root)).toContain('form A domain 3 has 23 items; it needs 22');
  });

  it('skips the live and count checks on a frozen form', () => {
    const { root, forms } = built();
    const formA = forms.forms[0];
    if (formA === undefined) throw new Error('no form A');
    formA.frozen = true;
    writeForms(root, forms);
    setStatus(root, formA.qids[0] ?? '', 'retired');
    expect(run(root)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('fires on one qid in two frozen forms', () => {
    const { root, forms } = built();
    const [a, b] = forms.forms;
    if (a === undefined || b === undefined) throw new Error('no forms A and B');
    a.frozen = true;
    b.frozen = true;
    const shared = a.qids[0] ?? '';
    b.qids[0] = shared;
    writeForms(root, forms);
    expect(messages(root)).toEqual([`qid ${shared} sits in forms A and B`]);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/unit/forms-command.test.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { parse, stringify } from 'yaml';
import { Forms } from '../../schemas/exam.ts';
import { FORMS_FILE, loadContext } from '../../scripts/content/lib/context.ts';
import { FORMS_SEED, FORMS_USAGE, runForms } from '../../scripts/content/lib/forms-command.ts';
import { r12 } from '../../scripts/content/rules/r12-forms.ts';
import { FIXTURE_TODAY, makeFixtureRoot } from '../fixtures/content-root.ts';
import { writeExamPool } from '../fixtures/exam-pool.ts';
import { POOL_FILE } from '../fixtures/questions.ts';

const formsOf = (root: string): Forms => Forms.parse(parse(readFileSync(join(root, FORMS_FILE), 'utf8')));
/** Drops the last qid of form A, as a hand edit would. */
function dropFromFormA(root: string): void {
  const forms = formsOf(root);
  forms.forms[0]?.qids.pop();
  writeFileSync(join(root, FORMS_FILE), stringify(forms));
}

describe('pnpm content:forms', () => {
  it('writes four forms of 65 that pass R12, and the same file on a second run', () => {
    const root = makeFixtureRoot();
    writeExamPool(root);
    expect(runForms(root, [])).toEqual({ code: 0, out: ['forms: A 65, B 65, C 65, D 65 (seed 20261024)'] });
    const first = readFileSync(join(root, FORMS_FILE), 'utf8');
    expect(first.startsWith('# Generated by pnpm content:forms')).toBe(true);
    const forms = formsOf(root);
    expect(forms.seed).toBe(FORMS_SEED);
    expect(forms.forms.map((f) => [f.id, f.frozen, f.qids.length])).toEqual([
      ['A', false, 65],
      ['B', false, 65],
      ['C', false, 65],
      ['D', false, 65],
    ]);
    expect(r12.run(loadContext(root, { today: FIXTURE_TODAY }))).toEqual([]);
    expect(runForms(root, []).code).toBe(0);
    expect(readFileSync(join(root, FORMS_FILE), 'utf8')).toBe(first);
  });

  it('names the task that blocks a pool one item short, and writes nothing', () => {
    const root = makeFixtureRoot();
    const questions = writeExamPool(root);
    const last = questions.filter((q) => q.task === '4.2').at(-1)?.id ?? '';
    const text = readFileSync(join(root, POOL_FILE), 'utf8');
    const at = text.indexOf(`id: ${last}`);
    writeFileSync(
      join(root, POOL_FILE),
      text.slice(0, at) + text.slice(at).replace('status: live', 'status: draft'),
    );
    expect(runForms(root, [])).toEqual({
      code: 1,
      out: ['forms: blocked: task 4.2 has 3 live items; 4 forms need 4'],
    });
    expect(loadContext(root, { today: FIXTURE_TODAY }).forms).toBeNull();
  });

  it('refuses to freeze while a quota cell is short', () => {
    const root = makeFixtureRoot();
    writeExamPool(root, { splitTask: '4.2' });
    expect(runForms(root, []).code).toBe(0);
    expect(runForms(root, ['--freeze'])).toEqual({
      code: 1,
      out: [
        'freeze refused: 1 quota problems, 0 form problems',
        'cell m02 task 4.2 has 0 live exam items; it needs 2',
      ],
    });
    expect(formsOf(root).forms.map((f) => f.frozen)).toEqual([false, false, false, false]);
  });

  it('freezes every form of a pool that meets its quotas', () => {
    const root = makeFixtureRoot();
    writeExamPool(root);
    runForms(root, []);
    const built = formsOf(root);
    expect(runForms(root, ['--freeze'])).toEqual({ code: 0, out: ['frozen: A, B, C, D'] });
    const frozen = formsOf(root);
    expect(frozen.forms.map((f) => f.frozen)).toEqual([true, true, true, true]);
    expect(frozen.forms.map((f) => f.qids)).toEqual(built.forms.map((f) => f.qids));
    expect(runForms(root, []).out).toEqual(['forms: A 65, B 65, C 65, D 65 (seed 20261024)']);
    expect(formsOf(root)).toEqual(frozen);
  });

  it('freezes a pool with a short cell only with --allow-short-cells, and never a form with a problem', () => {
    const root = makeFixtureRoot();
    writeExamPool(root, { splitTask: '4.2' });
    runForms(root, []);
    dropFromFormA(root);
    const refused = runForms(root, ['--freeze', '--allow-short-cells']);
    expect(refused.code).toBe(1);
    expect(refused.out[0]).toMatch(/^freeze refused: 1 quota problems, [1-9]\d* form problems$/);
    expect(refused.out).toContain('form A has 64 items; it needs 65');
    expect(formsOf(root).forms.map((f) => f.frozen)).toEqual([false, false, false, false]);

    runForms(root, ['--seed', String(FORMS_SEED)]);
    expect(runForms(root, ['--freeze', '--allow-short-cells'])).toEqual({
      code: 0,
      out: ['allowed: cell m02 task 4.2 has 0 live exam items; it needs 2', 'frozen: A, B, C, D'],
    });
  });

  it('prints its usage for an unknown flag, --allow-short-cells without --freeze, or --seed with --freeze', () => {
    const root = makeFixtureRoot();
    for (const argv of [
      ['--frozen'],
      ['--allow-short-cells'],
      ['--freeze', '--seed', '7'],
      ['--seed', 'x'],
    ]) {
      expect(runForms(root, argv)).toEqual({ code: 1, out: [FORMS_USAGE] });
    }
    expect(FORMS_USAGE).toBe('usage: pnpm content:forms [--seed N] [--freeze [--allow-short-cells]]');
  });

  it('is blocked without quotas.yaml and refuses to freeze without forms.yaml', () => {
    const root = makeFixtureRoot();
    expect(runForms(root, [])).toEqual({
```

Replace `stampQuestions` from its doc comment to the end of the file:

```ts
/**
 * pnpm content:stamp: stamps and promotes every question with all three passes at its current hashes, and records each
 * new failure at a draft's current hashes in its review block (spec 2.7). An exam draft that reaches
 * NEEDS_DANI_ATTEMPTS is retired in place, and exam lines carry ids, attempts and check names only.
 */
export function stampQuestions(
  ctx: Context,
  today = localDate(),
): { log: string[]; live: number; revise: string[]; needsDani: number; retired: number; summary: string } {
  const root = ctx.root;
  const passes = readPasses(root);
  const failures = readFailures(root);
  const edits = new Map<string, Map<string, QuestionEdit>>();
  const edit = (path: string, id: string, e: QuestionEdit): void => {
    edits.set(path, (edits.get(path) ?? new Map()).set(id, e));
  };
  const log: string[] = [];
  const state = new Map<string, { status: string; attempts: number; failed: boolean; path: string }>();

  for (const entry of ctx.questions) {
    const { q } = entry;
    if (q.status === 'retired') continue;
    const exam = q.pool === 'exam';
    const kh = keyHash(q);
    const fh = fullHash(q);
    const versionOf = (check: CheckName): number | null => {
      const hash = check === 'solve-closed' ? kh : fh;
      return passedVersion(passes, q.id, check, hash) ?? stampedVersion(q.verified, check, kh, fh);
    };
    const versions = CHECK_NAMES.map(versionOf);
    const current = failures.filter(
      (f) => f.id === q.id && f.hash === hashFor(f.check, { keyHash: kh, fullHash: fh }),
    );
    if (versions.every((v) => v !== null)) {
      const checks = CHECK_NAMES.map((c, i) => `${c}@${versions[i]}`);
      const valid =
        q.status === 'live' &&
        q.review === undefined &&
        q.verified?.keyHash === kh &&
        q.verified.fullHash === fh &&
        q.verified.checks.join() === checks.join();
      if (!valid) {
        edit(entry.path, q.id, {
          status: 'live',
          verified: { keyHash: kh, fullHash: fh, checks, on: today },
          review: null,
        });
        log.push(`live ${q.id} (${entry.path})`);
      }
      state.set(q.id, { status: 'live', attempts: 0, failed: false, path: entry.path });
      continue;
    }
    let attempts = q.review?.attempts ?? 0;
    let review = q.review;
    const fresh = current.filter((f) => f.stamped !== true);
    if (q.status === 'draft' && fresh.length > 0) {
      const first = fresh[0] as Failure;
      attempts += 1;
      review = { attempts, lastFailure: `${first.check}: ${first.reason}` };
      for (const f of fresh) f.stamped = true;
      if (!exam || attempts < NEEDS_DANI_ATTEMPTS) {
        edit(entry.path, q.id, { review });
        log.push(
          exam
            ? `review ${q.id} attempts ${attempts} ${first.check}`
            : `review ${q.id} (${entry.path}) attempts ${attempts}: ${first.check}: ${first.reason}`,
        );
      }
    }
    if (exam && q.status === 'draft' && attempts >= NEEDS_DANI_ATTEMPTS) {
      edit(entry.path, q.id, review === undefined ? { status: 'retired' } : { status: 'retired', review });
      log.push(`retire ${q.id}`);
      state.set(q.id, { status: 'retired', attempts, failed: false, path: entry.path });
      continue;
    }
    state.set(q.id, { status: q.status, attempts, failed: current.length > 0, path: entry.path });
  }

  for (const [path, byId] of edits) {
    writeFileSync(join(root, path), editQuestionFile(ctx.read(path) ?? '', byId));
  }
  if (existsSync(paths(root).failures)) writeJson(paths(root).failures, failures);

  const all = [...state.values()];
  const live = all.filter((s) => s.status === 'live').length;
  const drafts = all.filter((s) => s.status === 'draft');
  const reviseDrafts = drafts.filter((s) => s.failed && s.attempts < NEEDS_DANI_ATTEMPTS);
  const needsDani = drafts.filter((s) => s.attempts >= NEEDS_DANI_ATTEMPTS).length;
  const retired =
    ctx.questions.filter((e) => e.q.pool === 'exam' && e.q.status === 'retired').length +
    all.filter((s) => s.status === 'retired').length;
  const revise = [...new Set(reviseDrafts.map((s) => s.path))].sort();
  const summary = `stamp: ${live} live, ${reviseDrafts.length} drafts to revise, ${needsDani} need Dani, ${retired} retired`;
  return { log, live, revise, needsDani, retired, summary };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Add `--pool exam` to the `content:checks` CLI and ids-only lines to its `grade`**

Replace `scripts/content/checks.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * pnpm content:checks <command>: the agent check pipeline (spec 2.7).
 *   prepare --modules 01,03 [--seed N] [--pool exam]  write keyless batches for every question of the pool (practice
 *                                                     by default) that needs a check, and keys.json
 *   lint <answers file>                               check one answer file against its batch until it prints OK
 *   grade                                             grade every answer file and record the passes and failures;
 *                                                     an exam item's failure prints as its id and check only
 */
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { grade, lintAnswers, prepare } from './lib/checks.ts';
import { loadContext } from './lib/context.ts';

const root = resolve(import.meta.dirname, '..', '..');
const [command, ...rest] = process.argv.slice(2);
const option = (name: string): string | undefined => {
  const at = rest.indexOf(name);
  return at < 0 ? undefined : rest[at + 1];
};
const fail = (message: string): never => {
  console.error(`content:checks: ${message}`);
  process.exit(1);
};

switch (command) {
  case 'prepare': {
    const modules = (option('--modules') ?? fail('prepare needs --modules, like --modules 01,03'))
      .split(',')
      .map(Number);
    if (modules.some((m) => !Number.isInteger(m) || m < 1 || m > 13))
      fail('--modules lists module numbers 01 to 13');
    const seed = Number(option('--seed') ?? '1');
    if (!Number.isInteger(seed)) fail('--seed must be a whole number');
    const poolFlag = option('--pool') ?? 'practice';
    if (poolFlag !== 'practice' && poolFlag !== 'exam') fail('--pool is practice or exam');
    const pool = poolFlag === 'exam' ? 'exam' : 'practice';
    const { paths, questions } = prepare(loadContext(root), modules, seed, pool);
    for (const path of paths) console.log(path);
    console.log(`prepared ${paths.length} batches for ${questions} questions`);
    break;
  }
  case 'lint': {
    const file = rest[0] ?? fail('lint needs an answers file');
    const problems = lintAnswers(root, resolve(file));
    if (problems.length > 0) {
      for (const problem of problems) console.error(problem);
      process.exit(1);
    }
    const count = (JSON.parse(readFileSync(resolve(file), 'utf8')) as { answers: unknown[] }).answers.length;
    console.log(`OK ${file}: ${count} answers`);
    break;
  }
  case 'grade': {
    const { passes, failures, lines } = grade(root);
    for (const line of lines) console.log(line);
    console.log(`graded ${passes + failures.length} answers: ${passes} passed, ${failures.length} failed`);
    break;
  }
  default:
    fail(
      'usage: pnpm content:checks prepare --modules 01,03 [--seed N] [--pool exam] | lint <answers file> | grade',
    );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write R12**

Create `scripts/content/rules/r12-forms.ts`:

```ts
import type { Forms, Quotas } from '../../../schemas/exam.ts';
import { type FormItem, type FormTargets, formProblems } from '../../../src/engine/forms.ts';
import { type Context, FORMS_FILE } from '../lib/context.ts';
import { finding, type Rule } from './rule.ts';

/** Every live exam item as the form builder sees it, by qid; an alias resolves to its registered concept id. */
export function liveFormItems(ctx: Context): Map<string, FormItem> {
  const canonical = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const c of ctx.concepts) {
    canonical.set(c.id, c.id);
    for (const alias of c.aliases ?? []) canonical.set(alias, c.id);
  }
  const out = new Map<string, FormItem>();
  for (const { q } of ctx.questions) {
    if (q.pool !== 'exam' || q.status !== 'live' || q.type === 'match') continue;
    out.set(q.id, { id: q.id, concept: canonical.get(q.concept) ?? q.concept, task: q.task, type: q.type });
  }
  return out;
}

/** The per-form targets of quotas.yaml. */
export function formTargets(quotas: Quotas): FormTargets {
  return { tasks: quotas.form.tasks, multi: quotas.form.multi };
}

/**
 * The problems of a set of forms, ids and counts only: across all forms no qid twice; in each form that is not frozen,
 * every qid a live exam item and, when the targets are known, no formProblems.
 */
export function formSetProblems(
  forms: Forms,
  live: ReadonlyMap<string, FormItem>,
  targets: FormTargets | null,
): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const form of forms.forms) {
    if (form.frozen) continue;
    const items: FormItem[] = [];
    for (const qid of form.qids) {
      const item = live.get(qid);
      if (item === undefined) out.push(`form ${form.id} holds ${qid}, which is not a live exam item`);
      else items.push(item);
    }
    if (targets !== null)
      for (const problem of formProblems(items, targets)) out.push(`form ${form.id} ${problem}`);
  }
  const first = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const form of forms.forms) {
    for (const qid of form.qids) {
      const seen = first.get(qid);
      if (seen === undefined) first.set(qid, form.id);
      else out.push(`qid ${qid} sits in forms ${seen} and ${form.id}`);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * R12 Mock forms (spec 2.9, 4.3): each form that is not frozen holds 65 live exam items that meet the per-form domain,
 * task and multi-response counts with no concept twice, and no qid sits in two forms, frozen or not. A missing
 * forms.yaml passes until examQuotas is on, so deleting the file can never switch the mocks off unnoticed.
 */
export const r12: Rule = {
  id: 'R12',
  title: 'Mock forms',
  run(ctx) {
    if (ctx.forms === null) {
      // A forms.yaml that is there but does not parse is an R1 finding already.
      if (ctx.read(FORMS_FILE) !== null || ctx.config?.examQuotas !== true) return [];
      return [finding('R12', FORMS_FILE, 'forms.yaml is missing; run pnpm content:forms')];
    }
    const targets = ctx.quotas === null ? null : formTargets(ctx.quotas);
    const out = formSetProblems(ctx.forms, liveFormItems(ctx), targets).map((m) =>
      finding('R12', FORMS_FILE, m),
    );
    if (targets === null)
      out.push(finding('R12', FORMS_FILE, 'quotas.yaml is missing; run pnpm content:quotas'));
    return out;
  },
};
```

In `scripts/content/rules/index.ts`, import it after `r10` and add it to `RULES`:
