```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { NewEvent, StudyEvent } from '../../schemas/events.ts';
import {
  BADGES,
  type BadgeId,
  CALIBRATION,
  calibrationOf,
  decisionPasses,
  domainScores,
  LONG_MEMORY,
  wellCalibrated,
} from '../../src/engine/badges.ts';
import { addDays } from '../../src/engine/day.ts';
import { deriveMemory, type MemoryContent } from '../../src/engine/memory.ts';
import { review, type SrsCard } from '../../src/engine/srs.ts';
import { SID, SID2, timelineOf } from '../fixtures/events.ts';
import { answerOf, at, contentOf, examStartOf, startOf, submitOf } from '../fixtures/memory.ts';

/** One fixture per badge (spec 3.10 and 9): the last event crosses the threshold, and without it no badge. */
type Log = (readonly [string, NewEvent])[];

const ids = (prefix: string, n: number): string[] =>
  Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => `${prefix}-${String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`);

/** The badge earned by `log` and its absence without the last event, with the event that earned it. */
function crossing(log: Log, content: MemoryContent, badge: BadgeId) {
  const events = timelineOf(log);
  const before = deriveMemory(events.slice(0, -1), content).badges.get(badge);
  const after = deriveMemory(events, content).badges.get(badge);
  return { before, after, last: events.at(-1) as StudyEvent };
}

const TEN = ids('c', 10);
const BASIC = contentOf(TEN.map((id) => ({ id, module: 1 })));

describe('badge helpers (spec 3.10)', () => {
  it('lists the 12 badges in spec order', () => {
    expect(BADGES).toEqual([
      'first-brew',
      'clean-queue',
      'changed-my-mind',
      'long-memory',
      'well-calibrated',
      'hands-on',
      'lab-complete',
      'leave-no-trace',
      'full-dress-rehearsal',
      'pass-mark',
      'even-keel',
      'architect',
    ]);
  });

  it('counts Sure and Not sure accuracy and needs 40 of each', () => {
    const window = [
      ...Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => ({ sure: true, correct: i < 34 })),
      ...Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => ({ sure: false, correct: i < 30 })),
    ];
    const c = calibrationOf(window);
    expect(c).toEqual({ sure: { right: 34, total: 40 }, notSure: { right: 30, total: 40 } });
    expect(wellCalibrated(c)).toBe(true);
    expect(wellCalibrated(calibrationOf(window.slice(1)))).toBe(false);
    expect(wellCalibrated({ sure: { right: 33, total: 40 }, notSure: { right: 30, total: 40 } })).toBe(false);
    expect(wellCalibrated({ sure: { right: 34, total: 40 }, notSure: { right: 31, total: 40 } })).toBe(false);
  });

  it('passes a decision at 8 of 12 with no criterion at 0', () => {
    expect(decisionPasses([2, 2, 2, 2])).toBe(true);
    expect(decisionPasses([3, 3, 1, 0])).toBe(false);
    expect(decisionPasses([2, 2, 2, 1])).toBe(false);
  });

  it('scores each domain by the task of each item', () => {
    const taskOf = new Map([
      ['x1', '1.2'],
      ['x2', '2.3'],
      ['x3', '2.1'],
    ]);
    const scores = domainScores(
      [
        { qid: 'x1', correct: true },
        { qid: 'x2', correct: false },
        { qid: 'x3', correct: true },
        { qid: 'x-unknown', correct: true },
      ],
      taskOf,
    );
    expect([...scores]).toEqual([
      ['1', { correct: 1, total: 1 }],
      ['2', { correct: 1, total: 2 }],
      ['3', { correct: 0, total: 0 }],
      ['4', { correct: 0, total: 0 }],
    ]);
  });
});

describe('badges in the memory replay (spec 3.10)', () => {
  it('First Brew: the first submitted lesson check', () => {
    const { before, after, last } = crossing(
      [
        [at('2026-10-10', 9), startOf(SID, 'check', ['q-c-01-1'], 'm01/a')],
        [at('2026-10-10', 9, 1), answerOf({ concept: 'c-01', correct: true, sure: true })],
        [at('2026-10-10', 9, 2), submitOf(SID, 'check')],
      ],
      BASIC,
      'first-brew',
    );
    expect(before).toBeUndefined();
    expect(after).toEqual({ day: '2026-10-10', eventId: last.id });
  });

  const reviewDay = (day: string, count = 10, extra: Log = []): Log => [
    ...TEN.slice(0, count).map(
      (c, i) =>
        [
          at(day, 18, i),
          answerOf({ concept: c, correct: true, sure: true, mode: 'review', qid: `q-${c}-${day}` }),
        ] as const,
    ),
    ...extra,
  ];

  it('Clean Queue: the seventh day with 10 reviews that ends with nothing due', () => {
    const days = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => addDays('2026-10-01', i));
    const { before, after, last } = crossing(
      days.flatMap((d) => reviewDay(d)),
      BASIC,
      'clean-queue',
    );
    expect(before).toBeUndefined();
    expect(after).toEqual({ day: '2026-10-07', eventId: last.id });
  });

  it('counts a Clean Queue day that is also a completion day', () => {
    const days = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => addDays('2026-10-01', i));
    const log = days.flatMap((d) => reviewDay(d, 10, [[at(d, 19), { t: 'lesson.done', note: `m01/${d}` }]]));
    expect(deriveMemory(timelineOf(log), BASIC).badges.get('clean-queue')?.day).toBe('2026-10-07');
  });

  it('never counts a day with 9 reviews toward Clean Queue', () => {
    const days = Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => addDays('2026-10-01', i));
    const log = days.flatMap((d, i) => reviewDay(d, i === 3 ? 9 : 10));
    expect(deriveMemory(timelineOf(log), BASIC).badges.get('clean-queue')?.day).toBe('2026-10-08');
  });

  const markAndClear = (concept: string, day: string, n: number): Log => [
    [at(day, 9, n), answerOf({ concept, correct: false, sure: true, qid: `q-${concept}-1` })],
    [at(addDays(day, 1), 9, n), answerOf({ concept, correct: true, sure: true, qid: `q-${concept}-2` })],
  ];

  it('Changed My Mind: the fifth distinct misconception cleared', () => {
    const log = TEN.slice(0, 5).flatMap((c, i) => markAndClear(c, addDays('2026-10-01', 2 * i), i));
    const { before, after, last } = crossing(log, BASIC, 'changed-my-mind');
    expect(before).toBeUndefined();
    expect(after).toEqual({ day: '2026-10-10', eventId: last.id });
  });

  it('never counts the same misconception twice', () => {
    const log = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5].flatMap((i) => markAndClear('c-01', addDays('2026-10-01', 2 * i), i));
    const m = deriveMemory(timelineOf(log), BASIC);
    expect(m.concepts.get('c-01')?.misconception).toBeNull();
    expect(m.badges.has('changed-my-mind')).toBe(false);
  });

  /** Good answers to one concept at each due time from `from` until its stability reaches 21 days. */
  function grow(concept: string, from: string): Log {
    const log: Log = [];
    let t = new Date(from);
    let card: SrsCard | null = null;
    for (let n = 0; card === null || card.stability < LONG_MEMORY.stabilityDays; n++) {
      log.push([
        t.toISOString(),
        answerOf({ concept, correct: true, sure: true, mode: 'review', qid: `q-${concept}-${n}` }),
      ]);
      card = review(card, 'good', t);
      t = new Date(card.due);
    }
    return log;
  }
  const FIFTY = ids('lm', 50);
  const LONG = contentOf(FIFTY.map((id) => ({ id, module: 1 })));
  const sortLog = (log: Log): Log => [...log].sort((a, b) => (a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : a[0] > b[0] ? 1 : 0));
  const longLog = sortLog([
    ...FIFTY.slice(0, 49).flatMap((c, i) => grow(c, `2026-09-01T10:${String(i).padStart(2, '0')}:00.000Z`)),
    ...grow('lm-50', '2026-12-01T10:00:00.000Z'),
  ]);

  it('Long Memory: 50 tested cards at a stability of 21 days or more', () => {
    const { before, after, last } = crossing(longLog, LONG, 'long-memory');
    expect(before).toBeUndefined();
    expect(after?.eventId).toBe(last.id);
  });

  it('keeps Long Memory after a lapse drops a card below 21 days', () => {
    const lapse: Log = [
      [
        '2027-06-01T10:00:00.000Z',
        answerOf({ concept: 'lm-01', correct: false, sure: true, mode: 'review' }),
      ],
    ];
    const m = deriveMemory(timelineOf([...longLog, ...lapse]), LONG);
    expect(m.concepts.get('lm-01')?.card?.stability).toBeLessThan(LONG_MEMORY.stabilityDays);
    expect(m.badges.has('long-memory')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('Well Calibrated: 40 Not sure answers at most 75% right beside 50 Sure at 86%', () => {
    const log: Log = [
      ...Array.from(
        { length: 50 },
        (_, i) =>
          [at('2026-10-10', 8, i), answerOf({ concept: 'c-01', correct: i < 43, sure: true })] as const,
      ),
      ...Array.from(
        { length: 40 },

          [at('2026-10-10', 9, i), answerOf({ concept: 'c-02', correct: i < 30, sure: false })] as const,
      ),
    ];
    const { before, after, last } = crossing(log, BASIC, 'well-calibrated');
    expect(before).toBeUndefined();
    expect(after?.eventId).toBe(last.id);
    expect(deriveMemory(timelineOf(log), BASIC).calibration).toEqual({
      sure: { right: 43, total: 50 },
      notSure: { right: 30, total: 40 },
    });
  });

  it('keeps the calibration window to the last 150 counted answers', () => {
    const log: Log = Array.from(
      { length: CALIBRATION.window + 10 },
      (_, i) =>
        [
          new Date(Date.parse(at('2026-10-10', 8)) + i * 1000).toISOString(),
          answerOf({ concept: 'c-01', correct: i >= 10, sure: i < 10 }),
        ] as const,
    );
    const m = deriveMemory(timelineOf(log), BASIC);
    expect(m.calibration).toEqual({ sure: { right: 0, total: 0 }, notSure: { right: 150, total: 150 } });
  });

  const labContent = (modules: number, perModule: number) =>
    contentOf([{ id: 'c-01', module: 1 }], new Map(), {
      coreLabs: new Map(
        Array.from({ length: modules }, (_, m) => [m + 1, ids(`lab-${m + 1}`, perModule)] as const),

  const labsDone = (content: MemoryContent, modules: number): Log =>
    [...content.coreLabs]
      .filter(([m]) => m <= modules)
      .flatMap(([, labs]) => labs)

  it('Hands On: every core lab done in 5 modules', () => {
    const content = labContent(6, 2);
    const { before, after, last } = crossing(labsDone(content, 5), content, 'hands-on');
    expect(before).toBeUndefined();
    expect(after?.eventId).toBe(last.id);
  });

  it('Lab Complete: all 26 core labs done', () => {
    const content = labContent(13, 2);
    const { before, after, last } = crossing(labsDone(content, 13), content, 'lab-complete');
    expect(before).toBeUndefined();
    expect(after?.eventId).toBe(last.id);

  });

      ...closed('mission-01', '2026-10-02', 0),
      ...closed('mission-02', '2026-10-04', 1),
      ...closed('mission-03', '2026-10-06', 2),
      ...closed('mission-04', '2026-10-08', 3),
    ];
    const { before, after, last } = crossing(log, content, 'leave-no-trace');
    expect(before).toBeUndefined();
    expect(after).toEqual({ day: '2026-10-09', eventId: last.id });
  });

  /** A Form A mock over `items` (`[qid, task, correct, picked]`), submitted unless `open`. */
  function mock(items: readonly (readonly [string, string, boolean, boolean])[], form = 'A') {
    const content = contentOf([{ id: 'c-01', module: 1 }], new Map(), {
      examTasks: new Map(items.map(([qid, task]) => [qid, task])),
    });
    const log: Log = [
      [

        ),
      ],
      ...items.map(

          [

            answerOf({
              concept: 'c-01',
              qid,
              correct,
              sure: false,
              mode: 'mock',
              sid: SID2,
              picked: picked ? ['a'] : [],
            }),
          ] as const,
      ),
      [at('2026-10-20', 10, 1), submitOf(SID2, 'mock')],
    ];
    return { content, log };
  }
  const item = (n: number, task: string, correct: boolean, picked = true) =>
    [`qx-${String(n).padStart(2, '0')}`, task, correct, picked] as const;

  it('Full Dress Rehearsal: every item of an unseen form answered', () => {
    const { content, log } = mock([item(1, '1.1', true), item(2, '2.1', false), item(3, '3.1', false)]);
    const { before, after, last } = crossing(log, content, 'full-dress-rehearsal');
    expect(before).toBeUndefined();
    expect(after?.eventId).toBe(last.id);
    expect(deriveMemory(timelineOf(log), content).badges.has('pass-mark')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('Pass Mark: an unseen form at 80%', () => {
    const { content, log } = mock([
      item(1, '1.1', true),
      item(2, '2.1', true),
      item(3, '3.1', true),
      item(4, '4.1', true),
      item(5, '3.2', false, false),
    ]);
    const { before, after, last } = crossing(log, content, 'pass-mark');
    expect(before).toBeUndefined();
    expect(after?.eventId).toBe(last.id);
    expect(deriveMemory(timelineOf(log), content).badges.has('full-dress-rehearsal')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('Even Keel: 75% in every domain of one unseen form', () => {
    const items = ['1', '2', '3', '4'].flatMap((d, k) =>
      [0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => item(4 * k + i + 1, `${d}.1`, i < 3)),
    );
    const { content, log } = mock(items);
    const { before, after, last } = crossing(log, content, 'even-keel');
    expect(before).toBeUndefined();
    expect(after?.eventId).toBe(last.id);
    expect(deriveMemory(timelineOf(log), content).badges.has('pass-mark')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('never earns a badge from a remix mock', () => {
    const { content, log } = mock(
      ['1', '2', '3', '4'].map((d, k) => item(k + 1, `${d}.1`, true)),
      'remix',
    );
    expect(deriveMemory(timelineOf(log), content).badges.size).toBe(0);
  });

  it('never earns a mock badge on a second attempt of a form', () => {
    const first = mock([item(1, '1.1', false)]);
    const second = mock(['1', '2', '3', '4'].map((d, k) => item(k + 1, `${d}.1`, true)));
    const retake: Log = second.log.map(([t, e]) => {
      const later = t.replace('2026-10-20', '2026-10-25');
      return [later, 'sid' in e ? { ...e, sid: SID } : e] as const;
    });
    const m = deriveMemory(timelineOf([...first.log, ...retake]), second.content);
    expect(m.badges.has('pass-mark')).toBe(false);
    expect(m.badges.has('even-keel')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('Architect: the latest final decision of all 13 modules passes', () => {
    const decision = (module: number, stage: 'self' | 'final', scores: [number, number, number, number]) =>
      [at('2026-10-20', 8, module), { t: 'decision', module, stage, scores }] as const;
    const log: Log = [
      [at('2026-10-19', 8), { t: 'decision', module: 13, stage: 'final', scores: [3, 3, 3, 0] }],
      ...Array.from({ length: 13 }, (_, i) => decision(i + 1, 'self', [1, 1, 1, 1])),
      ...Array.from(
        { length: 13 },
        (_, i) => [at('2026-10-20', 9, i), decision(i + 1, 'final', [2, 2, 2, 2])[1]] as const,
      ),
    ];
    const { before, after, last } = crossing(log, BASIC, 'architect');
    expect(before).toBeUndefined();
    expect(after?.eventId).toBe(last.id);
  });

  it('pays no XP for a badge', () => {
    const log: Log = [
      [at('2026-10-10', 9), startOf(SID, 'check', ['q-c-01-1'], 'm01/a')],
      [at('2026-10-10', 9, 1), answerOf({ concept: 'c-01', correct: true, sure: true })],
      [at('2026-10-10', 9, 2), submitOf(SID, 'check')],
    ];
    const events = timelineOf(log);
    const m = deriveMemory(events, BASIC);
    expect(m.badges.has('first-brew')).toBe(true);
    expect(m.xp).toBe(deriveMemory(events.slice(0, -1), BASIC).xp);
    expect(m.xpByEvent.has(events[2]?.id ?? '')).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the badge rules**

Create `src/engine/badges.ts`:

```ts
import { BADGE_IDS, type BadgeIdValue, DOMAIN_IDS, type DomainIdValue } from '../../schemas/ids.ts';

/**
 * Badges (spec 3.10): derived inside `deriveMemory` as the first event at which each rule held, so a badge
 * never disappears and pays no XP. Lab, mission and capstone badges become earnable when their content ships.
 */
export type BadgeId = BadgeIdValue;

/** The 12 badges in spec 3.10 order. */
export const BADGES: readonly BadgeId[] = BADGE_IDS;

export interface BadgeEarned {
  /** The study day of the event that earned it. */
  day: string;
  eventId: string;
}

export const CLEAN_QUEUE = { reviews: 10, days: 7 } as const;
export const CHANGED_MIND = 5;
export const LONG_MEMORY = { concepts: 50, stabilityDays: 21 } as const;
export const CALIBRATION = { window: 150, minEach: 40, sure: 0.85, notSure: 0.75 } as const;
export const HANDS_ON_MODULES = 5;
export const CORE_LABS = 26;
export const LEAVE_NO_TRACE = 3;
export const PASS_MARK = 0.8;
export const EVEN_KEEL = 0.75;
export const ARCHITECT = { decisions: 13, minTotal: 8 } as const;

export interface Calibration {
  sure: { right: number; total: number };
  notSure: { right: number; total: number };
}

/** Sure and Not sure accuracy over a window of counted answers. */
export function calibrationOf(window: readonly { correct: boolean; sure: boolean }[]): Calibration {
  const c: Calibration = { sure: { right: 0, total: 0 }, notSure: { right: 0, total: 0 } };
  for (const a of window) {
    const side = a.sure ? c.sure : c.notSure;
    side.total++;
    if (a.correct) side.right++;
  }
  return c;
}

/** At least 40 of each, Sure right at least 85% of the time and Not sure right at most 75%. */
export function wellCalibrated(c: Calibration): boolean {
  return (
    c.sure.total >= CALIBRATION.minEach &&
    c.notSure.total >= CALIBRATION.minEach &&
    c.sure.right / c.sure.total >= CALIBRATION.sure &&
    c.notSure.right / c.notSure.total <= CALIBRATION.notSure
  );
}

/** A capstone decision passes with a total of at least 8 of 12 and no criterion at 0. */
export function decisionPasses(scores: readonly number[]): boolean {
  const total = scores.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s, 0);
  return total >= ARCHITECT.minTotal && scores.every((s) => s > 0);
}

/** Right and total per exam domain, in domain order; an item's domain is its task's first digit. */
export function domainScores(
  answers: readonly { qid: string; correct: boolean }[],
  taskOf: ReadonlyMap<string, string>,
): Map<DomainIdValue, { correct: number; total: number }> {
  const scores = new Map<DomainIdValue, { correct: number; total: number }>(
    DOMAIN_IDS.map((d) => [d, { correct: 0, total: 0 }]),
  );
  for (const a of answers) {
    const domain = scores.get((taskOf.get(a.qid) ?? '').split('.')[0] as DomainIdValue);
    if (domain === undefined) continue;
    domain.total++;
    if (a.correct) domain.correct++;
  }
  return scores;
}
```

```ts

  /** Full Dress Rehearsal, Pass Mark and Even Keel, judged at the submit of a form's first attempt. */
  const firstAttemptBadges = (
    e: SessionEvent,
    qids: readonly string[],
    held: ReadonlyMap<string, AnswerEvent>,
    correct: number,
  ) => {
    if (qids.length === 0) return;
    if (qids.every((qid) => (held.get(qid)?.picked.length ?? 0) > 0)) grant('full-dress-rehearsal', e);
    if (correct / qids.length >= PASS_MARK) grant('pass-mark', e);
    const answers = qids.map((qid) => ({ qid, correct: held.get(qid)?.correct === true }));
    const domains = [...domainScores(answers, content.examTasks).values()];
    if (domains.every((d) => d.total > 0 && d.correct / d.total >= EVEN_KEEL)) grant('even-keel', e);
  };

  const applyLab = (e: Extract<StudyEvent, { t: 'lab.done' }>) => {
    labsDone.add(e.lab);
    const modules = [...content.coreLabs.values()];
    const done = (labs: readonly string[]) => labs.every((lab) => labsDone.has(lab));
    if (modules.filter((labs) => labs.length > 0 && done(labs)).length >= HANDS_ON_MODULES)
      grant('hands-on', e);
    const all = modules.flat();
    if (all.length >= CORE_LABS && done(all)) grant('lab-complete', e);
  };

  const applyMission = (e: Extract<StudyEvent, { t: 'mission' }>) => {
    if (e.step === 'cleaned' && !missionCleaned.has(e.mission)) missionCleaned.set(e.mission, e.day);
    if (e.step !== 'usage-ok' || content.keptMissions.has(e.mission)) return;
    const cleaned = missionCleaned.get(e.mission);
    if (cleaned === undefined || e.day <= cleaned) return;
    missionsClosed.add(e.mission);
    if (missionsClosed.size >= LEAVE_NO_TRACE) grant('leave-no-trace', e);
  };

  const applyDecision = (e: Extract<StudyEvent, { t: 'decision' }>) => {
    if (e.stage !== 'final') return;
    finalDecisions.set(e.module, e.scores);
    const all = [...finalDecisions.values()];
    if (all.length >= ARCHITECT.decisions && all.every(decisionPasses)) grant('architect', e);
  };

  /** Opens a boss or mock on start; closes it on its first submit or abandon, applying a submit. */
```

## Task 11: The boss draw, the learn pool and the leakage test

**Files:**
- Create: `src/engine/boss.ts`, `tests/unit/boss.test.ts`, `tests/unit/leakage.test.ts`, `tests/property/leakage.property.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/engine/select.ts`, `src/app/screens/Runner.tsx` (the two pool builders), `tests/unit/select.test.ts`, `tests/unit/anti-grind.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/boss.test.ts`, `tests/unit/leakage.test.ts`, `tests/property/leakage.property.test.ts`, `tests/unit/select.test.ts`, `tests/unit/anti-grind.test.ts`

Design notes:
- `PoolQuestion` gains `type` and `module`; `Runner.tsx` builds pools in two places (`poolOf` and the check and quiz start), and both pass `q.type` and the chunk's `module`.
- `learnPool(practice, exam, taken)` is the only door from the exam pool to learn modes: it adds the items of taken forms, without `form`, so every selector stays pool-driven and the leakage test checks one boundary (spec 4.3, P3-079).
- `pickVariant` takes the first item of the module-private `ranked()` with never-answered items first, then the oldest last answer, then a seeded tie, so a draw never fails and needs no separate 7-day tier (spec 4.2).
- `bossDraw` keeps `single` and `multi` items of the module and earlier modules, keys each module concept `u ** (1 / (1.5 - R))` with `u` from `mulberry32(seed)` in concept id order (seeded weighted sampling toward low recall), takes the highest-keyed concept of each lesson first (the 12 best when a module has more lessons), fills to 12 new concepts by key and then second variants by key, takes 3 studied concepts of earlier modules by lowest recall with seeded ties, fills any shortfall from the module, and shuffles the result by the same stream.
- Module 1 fills to 15 from the module; a boss holds `min(15, eligible)` items whenever its module has at least 12 eligible items, which every shipped module has many times over; a smaller module gives its own items plus at most 3 from earlier modules.
- Bosses draw only ids of the pool they are given, and the build ships only `live` practice items (`scripts/content/build.ts`), so a retired question never reaches a boss (P3-170).
- `bossDraw` keeps the brief's `now` argument for its callers, but reads recall only through `recall`, so the draw is a pure function of the seed and the progress.
- `bossStatus` is ready when every lesson is read, mastery is at least 60 and no submitted boss of the module passed; the 20 h cooldown runs from the latest submitted boss, passed or not (spec 3.11), and ends exactly at 20 h.
- `bossScope(module)` (`m03`) and `BOSS_READY_MASTERY` (60) are exported so the app writes and reads one scope and one threshold.

```ts
describe('pickVariant', () => {
  const variants = [q('a1', 'a'), q('a2', 'a'), q('a3', 'a')];

  it('prefers a never-answered variant, then the one answered longest ago', () => {
    const someSeen = derive(eventsOf(answer(variants, 'a1', true), answer(variants, 'a3', true)), null);
    for (let seed = 0; seed < 10; seed++) {
      expect(pickVariant(variants, someSeen, () => seed / 10).id).toBe('a2');
    }
    const allSeen = derive(
      eventsOf(answer(variants, 'a2', true), answer(variants, 'a1', true), answer(variants, 'a3', true)),
      null,
    );
    expect(pickVariant(variants, allSeen, () => 0.5).id).toBe('a2');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing boss, leakage and anti-grind tests**

Create `tests/unit/boss.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { NewEvent } from '../../schemas/events.ts';
import {
  BOSS_COOLDOWN_MS,
  BOSS_FROM_EARLIER,
  BOSS_MINUTES,
  BOSS_SIZE,
  bossDeadline,
  bossDraw,
  bossScope,
  bossStatus,
} from '../../src/engine/boss.ts';
import { derive } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import { type ExamPoolQuestion, learnPool, type PoolQuestion } from '../../src/engine/select.ts';
import { eventsOf, SID, SID2 } from '../fixtures/events.ts';
import { answerOf, examStartOf, submitOf } from '../fixtures/memory.ts';

/** Made-up bakery pools: module `m` has `concepts` concepts over `lessons` lessons, `variants` items each. */
function modulePool(m: number, concepts: number, lessons: number, variants = 2): PoolQuestion[] {
  const mm = String(m).padStart(2, '0');
  return Array.from({ length: concepts }, (_, c) => {
    const concept = `bake-${mm}-${String(c + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    const note = `m${mm}/lesson-${(c % lessons) + 1}`;
    return Array.from({ length: variants }, (_, v) => ({
      id: `q-${concept}-${v + 1}`,
      concept,
      note,
      type: v === 2 ? ('multi' as const) : ('single' as const),
      module: m,
    }));
  }).flat();
}
const lessonsOf = (m: number, n: number) =>
  Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => `m${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}/lesson-${i + 1}`);
const EMPTY = derive([], null);
const NOW = new Date('2026-10-20T10:00:00.000Z');
const draw = (over: Partial<Parameters<typeof bossDraw>[0]> = {}) =>
  bossDraw({
    module: 3,
    pool: [...modulePool(1, 10, 2), ...modulePool(2, 10, 2), ...modulePool(3, 14, 3)],
    lessons: lessonsOf(3, 3),
    progress: EMPTY,
    recall: () => 0.5,
    studied: () => true,
    now: NOW,
    seed: 11,
    ...over,
  });
const moduleOf = (qid: string) => Number(qid.slice(7, 9));

describe('bossDraw (spec 4.2)', () => {
  it('times a boss at 21 minutes for 15 items', () => {
    expect(BOSS_SIZE).toBe(15);
    expect(BOSS_MINUTES).toBe(21);
    expect(bossDeadline(NOW)).toBe('2026-10-20T10:21:00.000Z');
  });

  it('never draws from the exam pool', () => {
    const practice = modulePool(3, 14, 3);
    const exam: ExamPoolQuestion[] = modulePool(3, 14, 3).map((p) => ({ ...p, id: `x${p.id}`, form: 'A' }));
    for (let seed = 0; seed < 50; seed++) {
      const ids = draw({ pool: learnPool(practice, exam, new Set()), seed });
      expect(ids).toHaveLength(BOSS_SIZE);
      expect(ids.filter((id) => id.startsWith('x'))).toEqual([]);
    }
  });

  it('still draws 15 when every variant was seen within 7 days', () => {
    const pool = modulePool(1, 8, 2);
    const seen = derive(
      eventsOf(...pool.map((p) => answerOf({ concept: p.concept, qid: p.id, correct: true, sure: true }))),
      null,
    );
    const ids = draw({ module: 1, pool, lessons: lessonsOf(1, 2), progress: seen });
    expect(ids).toHaveLength(BOSS_SIZE);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(BOSS_SIZE);
  });

  it('prefers a never-seen variant, then the least recently seen', () => {
    const pool = modulePool(1, 15, 3);
    const answered = (ids: readonly string[]) =>
      derive(
        eventsOf(
          ...ids.map((id) => answerOf({ concept: id.slice(2, 12), qid: id, correct: true, sure: true })),
        ),
        null,
      );
    const firsts = pool.filter((p) => p.id.endsWith('-1')).map((p) => p.id);
    const seconds = pool.filter((p) => p.id.endsWith('-2')).map((p) => p.id);
    const fresh = draw({ module: 1, pool, lessons: lessonsOf(1, 3), progress: answered(firsts) });
    expect(fresh.every((id) => id.endsWith('-2'))).toBe(true);
    const stale = draw({
      module: 1,
      pool,
      lessons: lessonsOf(1, 3),
      progress: answered([...seconds, ...firsts]),
    });
    expect(stale.every((id) => id.endsWith('-2'))).toBe(true);
    expect(stale).toHaveLength(BOSS_SIZE);
  });

  it('covers every lesson of the module', () => {
    const lessons = lessonsOf(3, 3);
    const pool = [...modulePool(2, 10, 2), ...modulePool(3, 14, 3)];
    const lowLesson3 = (concept: string) =>
      pool.find((p) => p.concept === concept)?.note === lessons[2] ? 1 : 0;
    for (let seed = 0; seed < 100; seed++) {
      const ids = draw({ pool, lessons, recall: lowLesson3, seed });
      const notes = new Set(ids.map((id) => pool.find((p) => p.id === id)?.note));
      for (const lesson of lessons) expect(notes.has(lesson)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('draws all 15 from Module 1 for the Module 1 boss', () => {
    const pool = modulePool(1, 10, 2);
    const ids = draw({ module: 1, pool, lessons: lessonsOf(1, 2) });
    expect(ids).toHaveLength(BOSS_SIZE);
    expect(ids.every((id) => moduleOf(id) === 1)).toBe(true);
    expect(new Set(ids.map((id) => id.slice(2, 12))).size).toBe(10);
  });

  it('takes 3 items from studied concepts of earlier modules, lowest recall first', () => {
    const studied = (c: string) =>
      c.endsWith('-01') || c.endsWith('-02') || c.endsWith('-03') || c.endsWith('-04');
    const recall = (c: string) => (c === 'bake-01-04' ? 0.9 : c.startsWith('bake-03') ? 0.5 : 0.1);
    const ids = draw({ studied, recall });
    const earlier = ids.filter((id) => moduleOf(id) < 3);
    expect(earlier).toHaveLength(BOSS_FROM_EARLIER);
    for (const id of earlier) {
      const concept = id.slice(2, 12);
      expect(studied(concept)).toBe(true);
      expect(concept).not.toBe('bake-01-04');
    }
    expect(ids.filter((id) => moduleOf(id) === 3)).toHaveLength(12);
  });

  it('fills a shortfall of earlier concepts from the module', () => {
    const ids = draw({ studied: (c) => c === 'bake-01-01' });
    expect(ids.filter((id) => moduleOf(id) < 3)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(ids).toHaveLength(BOSS_SIZE);
  });

  it('draws single and multi items only', () => {
    const pool = modulePool(1, 10, 2, 3).map((p) =>
      p.id.endsWith('-1') ? { ...p, type: 'match' as const } : p,
    );
    for (let seed = 0; seed < 20; seed++) {
      const ids = draw({ module: 1, pool, lessons: lessonsOf(1, 2), seed });
      expect(ids).toHaveLength(BOSS_SIZE);
      expect(ids.some((id) => id.endsWith('-1'))).toBe(false);
    }
    const small = modulePool(1, 4, 2).map((p) =>
      p.id.endsWith('-1') ? { ...p, type: 'match' as const } : p,
    );
    expect(draw({ module: 1, pool: small, lessons: lessonsOf(1, 2) })).toHaveLength(4);
  });

  it('gives the same draw for the same seed and a fresh draw after a fail', () => {
    expect(draw({ seed: 5 })).toEqual(draw({ seed: 5 }));
    const draws = new Set(Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, seed) => [...draw({ seed })].sort().join(' ')));
    expect(draws.size).toBeGreaterThan(10);
  });

  it('favours concepts with low recall over 1,000 seeds', () => {
    const pool = modulePool(3, 24, 2, 1);
    const low = (c: string) => Number(c.slice(-2)) % 2 === 0;
    let lowCount = 0;
    let highCount = 0;
    for (let seed = 0; seed < 1000; seed++) {
      for (const id of draw({ pool, lessons: lessonsOf(3, 2), recall: (c) => (low(c) ? 0 : 1), seed })) {
        if (low(id.slice(2, 12))) lowCount++;
        else highCount++;
      }
    }
    expect(lowCount).toBeGreaterThan(1.5 * highCount);
  });
});

describe('bossStatus (spec 3.11, 4.2)', () => {
  const qids = Array.from({ length: 15 }, (_, i) => `qb-${i}`);
  const boss = (sid: string, right: number): NewEvent[] => [
    examStartOf(sid, 'boss', qids, { scope: 'm03', deadline: '2026-10-10T09:00:00.000Z' }),
    ...qids.map((qid, i) =>
      answerOf({ concept: 'oven', qid, correct: i < right, sure: false, mode: 'boss', sid }),
    ),
    submitOf(sid, 'boss'),
  ];
  const status = (events: NewEvent[], over: Partial<Parameters<typeof bossStatus>[0]> = {}) =>
    bossStatus({
      module: 3,
      progress: derive(eventsOf(...events), null),
      lessonsRead: true,
      mastery: 60,
      now: NOW,
      ...over,
    });

  it('names the boss scope after its module', () => {
    expect(bossScope(3)).toBe('m03');
    expect(bossScope(12)).toBe('m12');
  });

  it('stays locked until every lesson of the module is read', () => {
    expect(status([], { lessonsRead: false })).toMatchObject({ unlocked: false, ready: false, attempts: 0 });
  });

  it('is ready at mastery 60 with the lessons read and no pass', () => {
    expect(status([], { mastery: 59 }).ready).toBe(false);
    expect(status([])).toEqual({
      unlocked: true,
      passed: false,
      passedAt: null,
      ready: true,
      cooldownUntil: null,
      open: null,
      attempts: 0,
    });
  });

  it('records a pass at 12 of 15 and is then no longer ready', () => {
    const events = eventsOf(...boss(SID, 12));
    const s = bossStatus({
      module: 3,
      progress: derive(events, null),
      lessonsRead: true,
      mastery: 80,
      now: NOW,
    });
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ passed: true, ready: false, attempts: 1, passedAt: events.at(-1)?.at });
    expect(status(boss(SID, 11)).passed).toBe(false);
  });

  it('holds a 20 h cooldown after any submitted boss: on at 19 h 59 min, over at 20 h', () => {
    const events = eventsOf(...boss(SID, 3));
    const submitted = Date.parse(events.at(-1)?.at ?? '');
    const at = (ms: number) =>
      bossStatus({
        module: 3,
        progress: derive(events, null),
        lessonsRead: true,
        mastery: 70,
        now: new Date(submitted + ms),
      });
    expect(at(BOSS_COOLDOWN_MS - 60_000).cooldownUntil).toBe(
      new Date(submitted + BOSS_COOLDOWN_MS).toISOString(),
    );
    expect(at(BOSS_COOLDOWN_MS).cooldownUntil).toBeNull();
    const passed = eventsOf(...boss(SID, 15));
    const passedAt = Date.parse(passed.at(-1)?.at ?? '');
    const after = bossStatus({
      module: 3,
      progress: derive(passed, null),
      lessonsRead: true,
      mastery: 90,
      now: new Date(passedAt + 60_000),
    });
    expect(after.cooldownUntil).toBe(new Date(passedAt + BOSS_COOLDOWN_MS).toISOString());
  });

  it('finds the open boss of the module', () => {
    const open = examStartOf(SID2, 'boss', qids, { scope: 'm03', deadline: '2026-10-10T09:00:00.000Z' });
    expect(status([open]).open?.sid).toBe(SID2);
    expect(status([open], { module: 4 }).open).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

Create `tests/unit/leakage.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { bossDraw } from '../../src/engine/boss.ts';
import { derive, type Progress, takenForms } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import {
  type ExamPoolQuestion,
  learnPool,
  type PoolQuestion,
  relearnVariant,
  selectCheck,
  selectQuiz,
} from '../../src/engine/select.ts';
import { eventsOf, SID } from '../fixtures/events.ts';
import { examStartOf } from '../fixtures/memory.ts';

/**
 * The leakage test (spec 4.3 and 9, an exit criterion of Phase 3): no learn-mode selector ever returns an item
 * of a form whose mock has not started. Made-up bakery pools: 2 modules of 6 concepts, and 4 forms of 12 items
 * on the same concepts.
 */
const concepts = (m: number) => Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => `bake-0${m}-0${i + 1}`);
const PRACTICE: PoolQuestion[] = [1, 2].flatMap((m) =>
  concepts(m).flatMap((concept, i) =>
    [1, 2].map((v) => ({
      id: `q-${concept}-${v}`,
      concept,
      note: `m0${m}/lesson-${(i % 2) + 1}`,
      type: 'single' as const,
      module: m,
    })),
  ),
);
const EXAM: ExamPoolQuestion[] = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'].flatMap((form) =>
  [1, 2].flatMap((m) =>
    concepts(m).map((concept, i) => ({
      id: `x${form.toLowerCase()}-${concept}`,
      concept,
      note: `m0${m}/lesson-${(i % 2) + 1}`,
      type: 'multi' as const,
      module: m,
      form,
    })),
  ),
);
const ALL_CONCEPTS = [...concepts(1), ...concepts(2)];

/** Every learn-mode selection over the learn pool of `progress`, for a few seeds. */
function everySelection(progress: Progress): string[] {
  const pool = learnPool(PRACTICE, EXAM, takenForms(progress));
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (let seed = 0; seed < 25; seed++) {
    out.push(
      ...selectCheck({ concepts: concepts(1), pool: pool.filter((q) => q.module === 1), progress, seed }),
    );
    out.push(...selectQuiz({ pool, progress, seed, recall: () => 0.3 }));
    out.push(...selectQuiz({ pool, progress, seed, size: 10, recall: () => 0.3 }));
    out.push(...selectCheck({ concepts: ALL_CONCEPTS, pool, progress, seed }));
    for (const concept of ALL_CONCEPTS) {
      const variant = relearnVariant({ concept, avoid: `q-${concept}-1`, pool, seed });
      if (variant !== null) out.push(variant);
    }
    out.push(
      ...bossDraw({
        module: 2,
        pool,
        lessons: ['m02/lesson-1', 'm02/lesson-2'],
        progress,
        recall: () => 0.3,
        studied: () => true,
        now: new Date('2026-10-27T10:00:00.000Z'),
        seed,
      }),
    );
  }
  return out;
}
const formOf = (id: string) => (id.startsWith('x') ? id.slice(1, 2).toUpperCase() : null);

describe('leakage (spec 4.3)', () => {
  it('never returns an exam item from any learn-mode selector while no form is taken', () => {
    const picked = everySelection(derive([], null));
    expect(picked.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(picked.filter((id) => formOf(id) !== null)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('lets only Form A items in once Mock A has started', () => {
    const progress = derive(
      eventsOf(
        examStartOf(SID, 'mock', ['xa-bake-01-01'], { form: 'A', deadline: '2026-10-27T11:30:00.000Z' }),
      ),
      null,
    );
    const forms = new Set(everySelection(progress).map(formOf));
    expect(forms.has('A')).toBe(true);
    expect([...forms].filter((f) => f !== null && f !== 'A')).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('a form counts as taken once its mock starts', () => {
    const started = eventsOf(
      examStartOf(SID, 'mock', ['xb-bake-01-01'], { form: 'B', deadline: '2026-10-27T11:30:00.000Z' }),
    );
    expect([...takenForms(derive([], null))]).toEqual([]);
    expect([...takenForms(derive(started, null))]).toEqual(['B']);
    const remix = eventsOf(
      examStartOf(SID, 'mock', ['xb-bake-01-01'], { form: 'remix', deadline: '2026-10-27T11:30:00.000Z' }),
    );
    expect([...takenForms(derive(remix, null))]).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/property/leakage.property.test.ts`:

```ts
import fc from 'fast-check';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { bossDraw } from '../../src/engine/boss.ts';
import { derive, takenForms } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import {
  type ExamPoolQuestion,
  learnPool,
  type PoolQuestion,
  relearnVariant,
  selectCheck,
  selectQuiz,
} from '../../src/engine/select.ts';
import { eventLogArb } from './arbitraries.ts';

/** For any log and seed, no learn-mode selector returns an item of a form whose mock never started (spec 4.3). */
const CONCEPTS = ['shared-kitchen', 'per-minute-rental'];
const PRACTICE: PoolQuestion[] = ['q-aaaaaa', 'q-bbbbbb', 'q-cccccc'].map((id, i) => ({
  id,
  concept: CONCEPTS[i % 2] as string,
  note: 'm01/a',
  type: 'single',
  module: 1,
}));
const EXAM: ExamPoolQuestion[] = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'].flatMap((form) =>
  CONCEPTS.map((concept) => ({
    id: `x-${form}-${concept}`,
    concept,
    note: 'm01/a',
    type: 'single' as const,
    module: 1,
    form,
  })),
);

describe('leakage property (spec 4.3)', () => {
  it('never selects an item of an untaken form, whatever the log and seed', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(eventLogArb, fc.nat(), (events, seed) => {
        const progress = derive(events, null);
        const taken = takenForms(progress);
        const pool = learnPool(PRACTICE, EXAM, taken);
        const picked = [
          ...selectCheck({ concepts: CONCEPTS, pool, progress, seed }),
          ...selectQuiz({ pool, progress, seed, recall: () => 0.5 }),
          ...selectQuiz({ pool, progress, seed, size: 10, recall: () => 0.5 }),
          ...CONCEPTS.flatMap((concept) => relearnVariant({ concept, avoid: 'q-aaaaaa', pool, seed }) ?? []),
          ...bossDraw({
            module: 1,
            pool,
            lessons: ['m01/a'],
            progress,
            recall: () => 0.5,
            studied: () => true,
            now: new Date('2026-10-27T10:00:00.000Z'),
            seed,
          }),
        ];
        for (const id of picked) {
          const item = EXAM.find((x) => x.id === id);
          if (item) expect(taken.has(item.form)).toBe(true);
        }
      }),
    );
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the learn pool and the variant pick**

In `src/engine/select.ts`, replace:

```ts
  note: string;
```

```ts
/** An item of the exam pool with the form (`A` to `D`) it sits in. */
export interface ExamPoolQuestion extends PoolQuestion {
  form: string;
}

/**
 * The pool every learn mode selects from (spec 4.3): the practice items plus the items of each taken form.
 * Items of a form whose mock never started stay out, so no selector can return them.
 */
export function learnPool(
  practice: readonly PoolQuestion[],
  exam: readonly ExamPoolQuestion[],
  taken: ReadonlySet<string>,
): PoolQuestion[] {
  const joined = exam
    .filter((q) => taken.has(q.form))
    .map(({ id, concept, note, type, module }) => ({ id, concept, note, type, module }));
  return [...practice, ...joined];
}
```

with:

```ts
    );

/** The variant to show: never answered first, then the one answered longest ago, then a seeded tie (spec 4.2). */
export function pickVariant(
  candidates: readonly PoolQuestion[],
  progress: Progress,
  random: () => number,
): PoolQuestion {
  const seed = Math.floor(random() * 2 ** 32);
  const first = ranked(candidates, progress, seed, (_q, answered) => [answered ? 1 : 0, 0])[0];
  if (!first) throw new Error('pickVariant needs at least one candidate');
  return first.q;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the boss draw and status**

Create `src/engine/boss.ts`:

```ts
import { bossPassMark } from './memory.ts';
import { examsOf, openSession, type Progress, type SessionState } from './replay.ts';
import { mulberry32, shuffle } from './rng.ts';
import { type PoolQuestion, pickVariant } from './select.ts';

/**
 * Module bosses (spec 4.2): 15 questions in 21 minutes, 12 from the module with at least one per lesson and
 * 3 from studied concepts of earlier modules (all 15 from Module 1 for its boss), single and multi only.
 * Concepts are drawn by seeded weighted sampling toward low recall, so a fresh seed gives a fresh draw.
 */
export const BOSS_SIZE = 15;
export const BOSS_FROM_MODULE = 12;
export const BOSS_FROM_EARLIER = 3;
export const BOSS_MINUTES = 21;
export const BOSS_COOLDOWN_MS = 20 * 3_600_000;
/** The module mastery at which a boss becomes ready (spec 4.6). */
export const BOSS_READY_MASTERY = 60;

const byText = (a: string, b: string): number => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0);

/** The session scope of a module's boss, `m03`. */
export function bossScope(module: number): string {
  return `m${String(module).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

/** The deadline of a boss started at `start`: 21 minutes later, as an ISO string. */
export function bossDeadline(start: Date): string {
  return new Date(start.getTime() + BOSS_MINUTES * 60_000).toISOString();
}

/** The question ids of a boss, `min(15, eligible)` of them, shuffled by the seed. */
export function bossDraw(args: {
  module: number;
  pool: readonly PoolQuestion[];
  lessons: readonly string[];
  progress: Progress;
  recall: (concept: string) => number;
  studied: (concept: string) => boolean;
  now: Date;
  seed: number;
}): string[] {
  const { module, pool, lessons, progress, recall, studied, seed } = args;
  const random = mulberry32(seed);
  const eligible = pool.filter((q) => (q.type === 'single' || q.type === 'multi') && q.module <= module);
  const own = eligible.filter((q) => q.module === module);
  const earlier = eligible.filter((q) => q.module < module);

  const keys = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const concept of [...new Set(own.map((q) => q.concept))].sort(byText)) {
    const r = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, recall(concept)));
    keys.set(concept, random() ** (1 / (1.5 - r)));
  }
  const byKey = [...keys.keys()].sort((a, b) => (keys.get(b) ?? 0) - (keys.get(a) ?? 0) || byText(a, b));
  const rank = new Map(byKey.map((c, i) => [c, i]));

  const out: string[] = [];
  const used = new Set<string>();
  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
  const take = (candidates: readonly PoolQuestion[]): boolean => {
    const free = candidates.filter((q) => !used.has(q.id));
    if (free.length === 0) return false;
    const pick = pickVariant(free, progress, random);
    used.add(pick.id);
    counts.set(pick.concept, (counts.get(pick.concept) ?? 0) + 1);
    out.push(pick.id);
    return true;
  };
  const ownOf = (concept: string) => own.filter((q) => q.concept === concept);

  const fromModule = module === 1 ? BOSS_SIZE : BOSS_FROM_MODULE;
  const perLesson: string[] = [];
  for (const lesson of lessons) {
    const top = byKey.find(
      (c) => !perLesson.includes(c) && own.some((q) => q.note === lesson && q.concept === c),
    );
    if (top !== undefined) perLesson.push(top);
  }
  perLesson.sort((a, b) => (rank.get(a) ?? 0) - (rank.get(b) ?? 0));
  for (const concept of perLesson.slice(0, fromModule)) take(ownOf(concept));

  /** Fills from the module up to `limit`: new concepts by key first, then second variants by key, and so on. */
  const fillModule = (limit: number) => {
    for (let pass = 0; out.length < limit; pass++) {
      let grew = false;
      for (const concept of byKey) {
        if (out.length >= limit) return;
        if ((counts.get(concept) ?? 0) === pass && take(ownOf(concept))) grew = true;
      }
      if (!grew && pass >= Math.max(0, ...counts.values())) return;
    }
  };
  fillModule(fromModule);

  if (module > 1) {
    const ties = new Map(
      shuffle([...new Set(earlier.map((q) => q.concept))].sort(byText), random).map((c, i) => [c, i]),
    );
    const lowest = [...ties.keys()]
      .filter(studied)
      .sort((a, b) => recall(a) - recall(b) || (ties.get(a) ?? 0) - (ties.get(b) ?? 0));
    let fromEarlier = 0;
    for (const concept of lowest) {
      if (fromEarlier >= BOSS_FROM_EARLIER || out.length >= BOSS_SIZE) break;
      if (take(earlier.filter((q) => q.concept === concept))) fromEarlier++;
    }
  }
  fillModule(BOSS_SIZE);
  return shuffle(out, random);
}

export interface BossStatus {
  /** Every lesson of the module is read (spec 4.2). */
  unlocked: boolean;
  passed: boolean;
  passedAt: string | null;
  /** Unlocked, mastery at 60 or more, and not passed: the boss-ready dot (spec 4.6). */
  ready: boolean;
  /** The end of the 20 h cooldown after the latest submitted boss, while it runs. */
  cooldownUntil: string | null;
  open: SessionState | null;
  attempts: number;
}

/** Where a module's boss stands at `now`; `mastery` is the module's 0 to 100 mastery. */
export function bossStatus(args: {
  module: number;
  progress: Progress;
  lessonsRead: boolean;
  mastery: number;
  now: Date;
}): BossStatus {
  const { module, progress, lessonsRead, mastery, now } = args;
  const scope = bossScope(module);
  const results = examsOf(progress, 'boss', scope);
  const pass = results.find((r) => r.total > 0 && r.correct >= bossPassMark(r.total));
  const last = results.at(-1);
  const until = last === undefined ? null : Date.parse(last.submittedAt) + BOSS_COOLDOWN_MS;
  return {
    unlocked: lessonsRead,
    passed: pass !== undefined,
    passedAt: pass?.submittedAt ?? null,
    ready: lessonsRead && mastery >= BOSS_READY_MASTERY && pass === undefined,
    cooldownUntil: until !== null && until > now.getTime() ? new Date(until).toISOString() : null,
    open: openSession(progress, 'boss', scope),
    attempts: results.length,
  };
}
```

## Task 12: Mock sessions and scores

**Files:**
- Create: `src/engine/mock.ts`, `src/engine/remix.ts`, `tests/unit/mock.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/mock.test.ts`

Design notes:
- A mock start stores only the form's live qids, shuffled by the session seed (`mockQids`), so the score is over the stored qids and replay never reads `forms.yaml` (spec 4.3 and 8.1).
- `MockAttempt` is read from Task 8's `ExamResult`: `percent` is `correct / total` (raw, never scaled), `at` is the submit time and `day` is `ExamResult.day`, the submit event's study day, which Task 8 records from the event's `day`.
- `firstAttempts` keeps the first submitted session of each form A to D in submit order, so a later session of the same form and every remix (`form: 'remix'`) never count toward readiness or a rank (P3-081).
- `mockHistory` lists every submitted mock, remixes included, newest first, for the mock list and the extras line's 3-day gap.
- `nextForm` walks `FORMS` and skips every form `takenForms` reports, so a form counts as taken once its mock starts.
- `remixQids` lives alone in `src/engine/remix.ts`, the only app-side importer of `buildForms`, so the eager bundle never carries the form builder; it builds one form from the items it is given (the caller passes seen items) and shuffles it by the seed.
- `domainBars` splits a mock by domain from the session's stored qids and first answers, so the four bars always sum to the result; a qid with no task in `taskOf` is left out.
- Exam items in these tests are made up (`q-mock00` and so on), so no exam-pool text appears in a test or its output.
- Covers P3-068, P3-073, P3-075, P3-076, P3-080, P3-081, P3-084 and P3-085 (pay and count part).

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { NewEvent } from '../../schemas/events.ts';
import type { FormItem, FormTargets } from '../../src/engine/forms.ts';
import {
  domainBars,
  FORMS,
  firstAttempts,
  MOCK_MINUTES,
  MOCK_WINDOW_DAYS,
  mockDeadline,
  mockHistory,
  mockQids,
  nextForm,
  RANK_MOCK_DAYS,
  REMIX,
} from '../../src/engine/mock.ts';
import { domainOf } from '../../src/engine/quotas.ts';
import { remixQids } from '../../src/engine/remix.ts';
import { derive } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import { timelineOf } from '../fixtures/events.ts';
import { answerOf, at, examStartOf, submitOf } from '../fixtures/memory.ts';

type Item = readonly [string, NewEvent];

const FORM = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => `q-mock${String(i).padStart(2, '0')}`);
const SID_A = '01JA00000000000000000000MA';
const SID_A2 = '01JA00000000000000000000MB';
const SID_B = '01JA00000000000000000000MC';
const SID_R = '01JA00000000000000000000MD';

/** A mock session on `day` at `hour`: its first `right` qids answered right, the next `wrong` wrong, the rest blank. */
function mockLog(args: {
  sid: string;
  form: string;
  qids: readonly string[];
  right: number;
  wrong?: number;
  day: string;
  hour?: number;
  submit?: boolean;
}): Item[] {
  const hour = args.hour ?? 9;
  const start = at(args.day, hour);
  const answered = args.qids.slice(0, args.right + (args.wrong ?? 0));
  return [
    [
      start,
      examStartOf(args.sid, 'mock', args.qids, { form: args.form, deadline: mockDeadline(new Date(start)) }),
    ],
    ...answered.map(
      (qid, i): Item => [
        at(args.day, hour, i + 1),
        answerOf({
          concept: `c-${qid}`,
          qid,
          mode: 'mock',
          sid: args.sid,
          correct: i < args.right,
          sure: true,
        }),
      ],
    ),
    ...(args.submit === false ? [] : [[at(args.day, hour, 59), submitOf(args.sid, 'mock')] as Item]),
  ];
}

const progressOf = (...logs: Item[][]) => derive(timelineOf(logs.flat()), null);

/** Per-form task counts (spec 4.3): 16, 19, 22 and 8 by domain, 2, 2, 3 and 1 multi-response items. */
const TARGETS: FormTargets = {
  tasks: {
    '1.1': 4,
    '1.2': 2,
    '1.3': 7,
    '1.4': 3,
    '2.1': 3,
    '2.2': 10,
    '2.3': 3,
    '2.4': 3,
    '3.1': 2,
    '3.2': 2,
    '3.3': 4,
    '3.4': 3,
    '3.5': 2,
    '3.6': 4,
    '3.7': 3,
    '3.8': 2,
    '4.1': 3,
    '4.2': 1,
    '4.3': 4,
  },
  multi: { '1': 2, '2': 2, '3': 3, '4': 1 },
};

/** Seen items with one spare per task: one concept each, and the multi-response items on each domain's largest task. */
function seenItems(): FormItem[] {
  const items: FormItem[] = [];
  for (const [task, seats] of Object.entries(TARGETS.tasks)) {
    for (let i = 0; i <= seats; i++) {
      items.push({ id: `q-${task}-s${i}`, concept: `bun-${task}-s${i}`, task, type: 'single' });
    }
  }
  for (const [domain, count] of Object.entries(TARGETS.multi)) {
    const [task = ''] = Object.entries(TARGETS.tasks)
      .filter(([t]) => domainOf(t) === domain)
      .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
      .map(([t]) => t);
    for (let i = 0; i <= count; i++) {
      items.push({ id: `q-${task}-m${i}`, concept: `bun-${task}-m${i}`, task, type: 'multi' });
    }
  }
  return items;
}

describe('mock constants (spec 4.3)', () => {
  it('runs 90 minutes, reads 21 days for readiness and 14 for the rank, and names forms A to D', () => {
    expect(MOCK_MINUTES).toBe(90);
    expect(MOCK_WINDOW_DAYS).toBe(21);
    expect(RANK_MOCK_DAYS).toBe(14);
    expect(FORMS).toEqual(['A', 'B', 'C', 'D']);
    expect(REMIX).toBe('remix');
  });

  it('sets the deadline 90 minutes after the start on the wall clock', () => {
    expect(mockDeadline(new Date('2026-10-27T18:00:00.000Z'))).toBe('2026-10-27T19:30:00.000Z');
  });
});

describe('mockQids', () => {
  it('shuffles the live items of a form by the session seed and drops retired ones', () => {
    const live = new Set(FORM.filter((qid) => qid !== 'q-mock03'));
    const order = mockQids(FORM, live, 7);
    expect(order).toHaveLength(9);
    expect(order).not.toContain('q-mock03');
    expect([...order].sort()).toEqual([...live].sort());
    expect(order).not.toEqual(FORM.filter((qid) => live.has(qid)));
    expect(mockQids(FORM, live, 7)).toEqual(order);
    expect(mockQids(FORM, live, 8)).not.toEqual(order);
  });
});

describe('firstAttempts', () => {
  it('scores a frozen form over its live items and reports the count', () => {
    const qids = mockQids(FORM, new Set(FORM.filter((qid) => qid !== 'q-mock03')), 7);
    const p = progressOf(mockLog({ sid: SID_A, form: 'A', qids, right: 6, wrong: 1, day: '2026-10-27' }));
    expect(p.exams.get(SID_A)?.total).toBe(9);
    expect(firstAttempts(p)).toEqual([
      {
        form: 'A',
        sid: SID_A,
        percent: 6 / 9,
        day: '2026-10-27',
        at: at('2026-10-27', 9, 59),
        correct: 6,
        total: 9,
      },
    ]);
  });

  it('counts unanswered items as wrong', () => {
    const p = progressOf(mockLog({ sid: SID_A, form: 'A', qids: FORM, right: 4, day: '2026-10-27' }));
    expect(firstAttempts(p)[0]).toMatchObject({ correct: 4, total: 10, percent: 0.4 });
  });

  it('takes the first attempt of each form only', () => {
    const p = progressOf(
      mockLog({ sid: SID_A, form: 'A', qids: FORM, right: 5, day: '2026-10-27' }),
      mockLog({ sid: SID_B, form: 'B', qids: FORM, right: 8, day: '2026-10-31' }),
      mockLog({ sid: SID_A2, form: 'A', qids: FORM, right: 10, day: '2026-11-02' }),
    );
    expect(firstAttempts(p).map((a) => [a.form, a.sid, a.percent])).toEqual([
      ['A', SID_A, 0.5],
      ['B', SID_B, 0.8],
    ]);
    expect(mockHistory(p).map((a) => a.sid)).toEqual([SID_A2, SID_B, SID_A]);
  });

  it('never counts a remix as a first attempt', () => {
    const p = progressOf(mockLog({ sid: SID_R, form: REMIX, qids: FORM, right: 10, day: '2026-11-10' }));
    expect(firstAttempts(p)).toEqual([]);
    expect(mockHistory(p).map((a) => [a.form, a.percent])).toEqual([[REMIX, 1]]);
    expect(nextForm(p)).toBe('A');
  });

  it('waits for the submit before a started mock counts as an attempt', () => {
    const p = progressOf(
      mockLog({ sid: SID_A, form: 'A', qids: FORM, right: 3, day: '2026-10-27', submit: false }),
    );
    expect(firstAttempts(p)).toEqual([]);
    expect(mockHistory(p)).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('nextForm', () => {
  it('walks A to D as each form starts and gives null once all four are taken', () => {
    const logs: Item[][] = [];
    expect(nextForm(progressOf())).toBe('A');
    const sids = ['01JA00000000000000000000N1', '01JA00000000000000000000N2', '01JA00000000000000000000N3'];
    const days = ['2026-10-27', '2026-10-31', '2026-11-04'];
    for (const [i, form] of (['A', 'B', 'C'] as const).entries()) {
      logs.push(
        mockLog({ sid: sids[i] ?? '', form, qids: FORM, right: 1, day: days[i] ?? '', submit: form !== 'C' }),
      );
      expect(nextForm(progressOf(...logs))).toBe(FORMS[i + 1]);
    }
    logs.push(
      mockLog({ sid: '01JA00000000000000000000N4', form: 'D', qids: FORM, right: 1, day: '2026-11-09' }),
    );
    expect(nextForm(progressOf(...logs))).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('remixQids', () => {
  it('builds 65 from seen items with the 16, 19, 22 and 8 split', () => {
    const items = seenItems();
    const byId = new Map(items.map((item) => [item.id, item] as const));
    const qids = remixQids({ items, targets: TARGETS, seed: 11 });
    expect(qids).not.toBeNull();
    const picked = (qids ?? []).map((qid) => byId.get(qid));
    expect(picked).toHaveLength(65);
    expect(picked.every((item) => item !== undefined)).toBe(true);
    const count = (key: (item: FormItem) => string, only?: (item: FormItem) => boolean) => {
      const out: Record<string, number> = {};
      for (const item of picked)
        if (item && (only?.(item) ?? true)) out[key(item)] = (out[key(item)] ?? 0) + 1;
      return out;
    };
    expect(count((item) => domainOf(item.task))).toEqual({ '1': 16, '2': 19, '3': 22, '4': 8 });
    expect(count((item) => item.task)).toEqual(TARGETS.tasks);
    expect(
      count(
        (item) => domainOf(item.task),
        (item) => item.type === 'multi',
      ),
    ).toEqual(TARGETS.multi);
    expect(new Set(picked.map((item) => item?.concept)).size).toBe(65);
    expect(remixQids({ items, targets: TARGETS, seed: 11 })).toEqual(qids);
  });

  it('returns null when the seen items cannot fill a form', () => {
    const items = seenItems().filter((item) => item.task !== '4.2');
    expect(remixQids({ items, targets: TARGETS, seed: 11 })).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('domainBars', () => {
  it('splits a mock result by domain and sums to the result', () => {
    const qids = ['q-d1a', 'q-d1b', 'q-d2a', 'q-d3a', 'q-d3b', 'q-d3c', 'q-d4a'];
    const taskOf = new Map([
      ['q-d1a', '1.1'],
      ['q-d1b', '1.3'],
      ['q-d2a', '2.2'],
      ['q-d3a', '3.3'],
      ['q-d3b', '3.6'],
      ['q-d3c', '3.8'],
      ['q-d4a', '4.3'],
    ]);
    const p = progressOf(mockLog({ sid: SID_A, form: 'A', qids, right: 4, wrong: 2, day: '2026-10-27' }));
    const result = p.exams.get(SID_A);
    const session = p.sessions.get(SID_A);
    if (result === undefined || session === undefined) throw new Error('the mock did not replay');
    const bars = domainBars(result, session, taskOf);
    expect(bars).toEqual([
      { domain: '1', correct: 2, total: 2 },
      { domain: '2', correct: 1, total: 1 },
      { domain: '3', correct: 1, total: 3 },
      { domain: '4', correct: 0, total: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(bars.reduce((sum, bar) => sum + bar.correct, 0)).toBe(result.correct);
    expect(bars.reduce((sum, bar) => sum + bar.total, 0)).toBe(result.total);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the mock helpers**

Create `src/engine/mock.ts`:

```ts
import { DOMAIN_IDS, type DomainIdValue, FORM_IDS } from '../../schemas/ids.ts';
import { domainOf } from './quotas.ts';
import { type ExamResult, examsOf, type Progress, type SessionState, takenForms } from './replay.ts';
import { mulberry32, shuffle } from './rng.ts';

/**
 * The full mock (spec 4.3): a frozen form's live items in a seeded order, 90 minutes on the wall clock,
 * scored over the items it stored at the start; unanswered items count as wrong.
 * Only the first attempt of forms A to D is an unseen measurement; a remix (`src/engine/remix.ts`) never is.
 */
export const MOCK_MINUTES = 90;
/** Readiness reads first attempts submitted within this many days (spec 3.9). */
export const MOCK_WINDOW_DAYS = 21;
/** Unified Operations needs its latest mock at 80% within this many days (spec 3.4). */
export const RANK_MOCK_DAYS = 14;
export const FORMS = FORM_IDS;
/** The `form` of a remix mock's session events. */
export const REMIX = 'remix';

const isForm = (form: string | null): boolean => (FORMS as readonly string[]).includes(form ?? '');

/** The wall-clock deadline of a mock started at `start`, as an ISO time. */
export function mockDeadline(start: Date): string {
  return new Date(start.getTime() + MOCK_MINUTES * 60_000).toISOString();
}

/** The qids a mock stores at its start: the form's live items, in an order shuffled by the session seed. */
export function mockQids(form: readonly string[], live: ReadonlySet<string>, seed: number): string[] {
  return shuffle(
    form.filter((qid) => live.has(qid)),
    mulberry32(seed),
  );
}

/** One submitted mock: `percent` is the raw share right over the stored qids, and `day` the submit's study day. */
export interface MockAttempt {
  form: string;
  sid: string;
  percent: number;
  day: string;
  at: string;
  correct: number;
  total: number;
}

function attemptOf(result: ExamResult): MockAttempt {
  return {
    form: result.form ?? REMIX,
    sid: result.sid,
    percent: result.total === 0 ? 0 : result.correct / result.total,
    day: result.day,
    at: result.submittedAt,
    correct: result.correct,
    total: result.total,
  };
}

/** The first submitted session of each form A to D, in submit order; a remix never counts. */
export function firstAttempts(p: Progress): MockAttempt[] {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const out: MockAttempt[] = [];
  for (const result of examsOf(p, 'mock')) {
    if (result.form === null || !isForm(result.form) || seen.has(result.form)) continue;
    seen.add(result.form);
    out.push(attemptOf(result));
  }
  return out;
}

/** Every submitted mock, remixes included, newest first. */
export function mockHistory(p: Progress): MockAttempt[] {
  return examsOf(p, 'mock').map(attemptOf).reverse();
}

/** The first of forms A to D no mock has started yet, or null once all four are taken. */
export function nextForm(p: Progress): string | null {
  const taken = takenForms(p);
  return FORMS.find((form) => !taken.has(form)) ?? null;
}

/** A mock's score by domain, from its stored qids and first answers; `taskOf` maps each qid to its task. */
export function domainBars(
  result: ExamResult,
  session: SessionState,
  taskOf: ReadonlyMap<string, string>,
): { domain: DomainIdValue; correct: number; total: number }[] {
  if (result.sid !== session.sid) throw new Error(`result ${result.sid} is not session ${session.sid}`);
  const bars = DOMAIN_IDS.map((domain) => ({ domain, correct: 0, total: 0 }));
  for (const qid of session.qids) {
    const task = taskOf.get(qid);
    if (task === undefined) continue;
    const bar = bars.find((b) => b.domain === domainOf(task));
    if (bar === undefined) continue;
    bar.total++;
    if (session.answers.get(qid)?.correct === true) bar.correct++;
  }
  return bars;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the remix**

Create `src/engine/remix.ts`:

```ts
import { buildForms, type FormItem, type FormTargets } from './forms.ts';
import { mulberry32, seedFor, shuffle } from './rng.ts';

/**
 * A remix mock (spec 4.3): once forms A to D are used up, one form built from items Dani has already seen,
 * to the same per-form targets; it is practice only and never counts toward Ready or a rank.
 * This file is the app's only importer of `buildForms`, so the form builder loads with the exam screens alone.
 */
export function remixQids(args: {
  items: readonly FormItem[];
  targets: FormTargets;
  seed: number;
}): string[] | null {
  const built = buildForms({ items: args.items, targets: args.targets, count: 1, seed: args.seed });
  if (!built.ok) return null;
  const [form] = built.forms;
  return form === undefined ? null : shuffle(form, mulberry32(seedFor(args.seed, 'remix')));
}
```

## Task 13: Readiness and the go/no-go line

**Files:**
- Create: `src/engine/readiness.ts`, `tests/unit/readiness.test.ts`, `tests/property/readiness.property.test.ts`, `tests/fixtures/journeys/exam-crunch.readiness.json` (written once by its test)
- Test: `tests/unit/readiness.test.ts`, `tests/property/readiness.property.test.ts`

Design notes:
- The formulas are spec 3.9's, read at `t* = readinessTarget(memory.examDate, now, tz)`: the exam day's 04:00 when the exam date is after today, else `now` plus 7 days.
- `Q_d` is the mean over tested concepts tagged with any task of domain `d` of `conceptMastery(cm, t*)`, which is `recall(card, t*) * successGate(cm)` and 0 without a card; a concept tagged in two domains counts in both, and an empty domain reads 0.
- `Memory` is `0.24 Q_1 + 0.30 Q_2 + 0.34 Q_3 + 0.12 Q_4`, clamped to [0, 1] against rounding.
- `Mock` is the mean percent of the latest two first attempts with `daysBetween(day, today) <= 21`, or the one, or null; with null the value is `min(Memory, 0.70)` and `capped` is true.
- `value` is null while fewer than 40 tested concepts have a card (`evidenceCount`), and the label is then Building; the app shows `Math.floor(100 * value)`, so a shown 80 always means `value >= 0.80`.
- Ready needs `value >= 0.80`, the latest first attempt in the window at 0.80 or more, and every `Q_d >= 0.70`; Close needs `value >= 0.65`.
- `weakest` is the task with at least 3 tested concepts and the lowest mean, ties by task id, for the focus-quiz link.
- `goNoGo` is true from `examDate - 7` to `examDate - 2` while the label is not Ready, and false without an exam date.
- NOTE (Decisions): readiness may rise without an event when a low attempt ages out of the 21-day window, so the never-rise property covers Memory and every `Q_d` on any log, and the value only on logs with no mock submit.
- The exam-crunch reading is pinned by a golden file, `tests/fixtures/journeys/exam-crunch.readiness.json`, written once by its test from Task 9's journey (with its boss and Mock A) and then compared exactly; delete it and rerun the test whenever a journey changes on purpose, then review the diff.
- The test fixtures build `MemoryState` as `{ ...deriveMemory([], content), concepts, examDate }`, so they keep compiling whatever fields Tasks 9 and 10 add, and give concepts their tasks through Task 9's `ConceptSpec.tasks`.
- Covers P3-092, P3-093, P3-094, P3-095, P3-096, P3-097, P3-098, P3-100 (engine part), P3-102 and P3-105.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing unit tests**

Create `tests/unit/readiness.test.ts`:

```ts
import { existsSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { addDays, DAY_MS, dayStartAt } from '../../src/engine/day.ts';
import {
  type ConceptMemory,
  deriveMemory,
  type MemoryContent,
  type MemoryState,
} from '../../src/engine/memory.ts';
import { firstAttempts, type MockAttempt } from '../../src/engine/mock.ts';
import {
  CLOSE,
  DOMAIN_FLOOR,
  DOMAIN_WEIGHTS,
  EVIDENCE_MIN,
  evidenceCount,
  goNoGo,
  NO_MOCK_CAP,
  PROJECT_DAYS,
  READY,
  type Readiness,
  readiness,
  readinessTarget,
  WEAKEST_MIN_CONCEPTS,
} from '../../src/engine/readiness.ts';
import { derive, toPlain } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import { recall, review, type SrsCard } from '../../src/engine/srs.ts';
import { JOURNEY_CONTENT, journeyNow } from '../fixtures/journeys/content.ts';
import { examCrunch } from '../fixtures/journeys/journeys.ts';
import { type ConceptSpec, contentOf } from '../fixtures/memory.ts';

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-20T12:00:00.000Z');
const TODAY = '2026-10-20';
const PLUS7 = new Date(NOW.getTime() + 7 * DAY_MS);

/** A card last reviewed at `at` with the given stability; a huge stability keeps recall at about 1. */
const cardAt = (at: Date, stability: number): SrsCard => ({ ...review(null, 'good', at), stability });
const STRONG = cardAt(NOW, 1_000_000);

/** A concept's memory with a card and `successDays` distinct success days (the gate is min(1, days / 2)). */
const memoryOfCard = (card: SrsCard | null, successDays = 2): ConceptMemory => ({
  card,
  enteredOn: card === null ? null : TODAY,
  lastAnswerDay: card === null ? null : TODAY,
  fsrsDay: card === null ? null : TODAY,
  fsrsRating: card === null ? null : 'good',
  extraAgainDay: null,
  successDays: Array.from({ length: successDays }, (_, i) => addDays(TODAY, -i)),
  misconception: null,
});

/** `count` tested concepts tagged with `task`, named `<prefix>-NN`. */
const conceptsFor = (prefix: string, task: string, count: number): ConceptSpec[] =>
  Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => ({
    id: `${prefix}-${String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`,
    module: 1,
    tasks: [task],
  }));

/** Ten tested concepts per domain on tasks 1.1, 2.1, 3.1 and 4.1. */
const WORLD: ConceptSpec[] = [
  ...conceptsFor('d1', '1.1', 10),
  ...conceptsFor('d2', '2.1', 10),
  ...conceptsFor('d3', '3.1', 10),
  ...conceptsFor('d4', '4.1', 10),
];

/** A memory state over `content` whose concepts are exactly `entries`. */
function memoryWith(
  content: MemoryContent,
  entries: Iterable<readonly [string, ConceptMemory]>,
  examDate: string | null = null,
): MemoryState {
  return { ...deriveMemory([], content), concepts: new Map(entries), examDate };
}

/** Every listed concept strong, except the ids in `skip`. */
const strongAll = (specs: readonly ConceptSpec[], skip: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set()) =>
  specs.filter((s) => !skip.has(s.id)).map((s) => [s.id, memoryOfCard(STRONG)] as const);

const attempt = (form: string, day: string, percent: number): MockAttempt => ({
  form,
  sid: `01JA0000000000000000000M${form}0`,
  percent,
  day,
  at: `${day}T10:00:00.000Z`,
  correct: Math.round(percent * 100),
  total: 100,
});

function read(
  specs: readonly ConceptSpec[],
  args: {
    entries?: Iterable<readonly [string, ConceptMemory]>;
    attempts?: readonly MockAttempt[];
    now?: Date;
    examDate?: string | null;
  } = {},
): Readiness {
  const content = contentOf(specs);
  return readiness({
    memory: memoryWith(content, args.entries ?? strongAll(specs), args.examDate ?? null),
    content,
    attempts: args.attempts ?? [],
    now: args.now ?? NOW,
    tz: 0,
  });
}

describe('readiness constants (spec 3.9)', () => {
  it('weighs the domains 24, 30, 34 and 12 and keeps the spec thresholds', () => {
    expect(DOMAIN_WEIGHTS).toEqual({ '1': 0.24, '2': 0.3, '3': 0.34, '4': 0.12 });
    expect([EVIDENCE_MIN, NO_MOCK_CAP, READY, CLOSE, DOMAIN_FLOOR]).toEqual([40, 0.7, 0.8, 0.65, 0.7]);
    expect([WEAKEST_MIN_CONCEPTS, PROJECT_DAYS]).toEqual([3, 7]);
  });
});

describe('readiness', () => {
  it('stays within 0 and 1', () => {
    for (const r of [
      read(WORLD),
      read(WORLD, { entries: [] }),
      read(WORLD, { attempts: [attempt('A', TODAY, 1)] }),
      read(WORLD, { attempts: [attempt('A', TODAY, 0)], entries: [] }),
    ]) {
      for (const x of [r.memory, ...Object.values(r.domains), r.value ?? 0, r.mock ?? 0]) {
        expect(x).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
        expect(x).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
      }
    }
  });

  it('counts an unseen concept as 0', () => {
    const specs = [...WORLD, ...conceptsFor('unseen', '1.2', 10)];
    const r = read(specs, { entries: strongAll(WORLD) });
    expect(r.domains['1']).toBeCloseTo(0.5, 5);
    expect(r.domains['2']).toBeCloseTo(1, 5);
  });

  it('caps at 0.70 without a mock', () => {
    const r = read(WORLD);
    expect(r.memory).toBeGreaterThan(0.99);
    expect(r.value).toBe(0.7);
    expect(r.capped).toBe(true);
    expect(r.mock).toBeNull();
    expect(r.label).toBe('Close');
  });

  it('projects 7 days ahead without an exam date', () => {
    expect(readinessTarget(null, NOW, 0)).toEqual(PLUS7);
    expect(readinessTarget(TODAY, NOW, 0)).toEqual(PLUS7);
    expect(readinessTarget('2026-10-19', NOW, 0)).toEqual(PLUS7);
    const weak = cardAt(NOW, 5);
    const entries = strongAll(WORLD, new Set(['d4-01']));
    const r = read(WORLD, { entries: [...entries, ['d4-01', memoryOfCard(weak)]] });
    expect(r.at).toBe(PLUS7.toISOString());
    expect(r.domains['4']).toBeCloseTo((9 * recall(STRONG, PLUS7) + recall(weak, PLUS7)) / 10, 12);
  });

  it('reads at the exam day when the date is set and later than today', () => {
    expect(readinessTarget('2026-11-14', NOW, 0)).toEqual(new Date('2026-11-14T04:00:00.000Z'));
    expect(readinessTarget('2026-11-14', NOW, 60)).toEqual(dayStartAt('2026-11-14', 60));
    const weak = cardAt(NOW, 5);
    const entries = strongAll(WORLD, new Set(['d4-01']));
    const r = read(WORLD, { entries: [...entries, ['d4-01', memoryOfCard(weak)]], examDate: '2026-11-14' });
    const exam = new Date('2026-11-14T04:00:00.000Z');
    expect(r.at).toBe(exam.toISOString());
    expect(r.domains['4']).toBeCloseTo((9 * recall(STRONG, exam) + recall(weak, exam)) / 10, 12);
  });

  it('averages the latest two first attempts within 21 days', () => {
    const attempts = [
      attempt('A', '2026-09-28', 0.2),
      attempt('B', '2026-10-01', 0.6),
      attempt('C', '2026-10-15', 0.9),
      attempt('D', '2026-10-18', 0.7),
    ];
    const r = read(WORLD, { attempts });
    expect(r.mock).toBeCloseTo(0.8, 12);
    expect(r.capped).toBe(false);
    expect(r.value).toBeCloseTo(0.5 * r.memory + 0.4, 12);
    expect(read(WORLD, { attempts: attempts.slice(0, 2) }).mock).toBeCloseTo(0.6, 12);
  });

  it('may rise when a low mock attempt ages out of the 21-day window', () => {
    const attempts = [attempt('A', '2026-10-01', 0.5), attempt('B', '2026-10-06', 0.9)];
    const before = read(WORLD, { attempts, now: new Date('2026-10-22T12:00:00.000Z') });
    const after = read(WORLD, { attempts, now: new Date('2026-10-23T12:00:00.000Z') });
    expect(before.mock).toBeCloseTo(0.7, 12);
    expect(after.mock).toBeCloseTo(0.9, 12);
    expect(after.value ?? 0).toBeGreaterThan(before.value ?? 0);
  });

  it('needs every domain at 0.70 for Ready', () => {
    const recent = [attempt('A', '2026-10-18', 0.9)];
    const base = WORLD.filter((s) => !s.id.startsWith('d4-'));
    const world = (strong: number): Readiness => {
      const d4 = conceptsFor('d4', '4.1', 100);
      const specs = [...base, ...d4];
      return read(specs, { attempts: recent, entries: strongAll([...base, ...d4.slice(0, strong)]) });
    };
    const low = world(69);
    expect(low.domains['4']).toBeLessThan(0.7);
    expect(low.value ?? 0).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.8);
    expect(low.label).toBe('Close');
    const high = world(71);
    expect(high.domains['4']).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.7);
    expect(high.label).toBe('Ready');
  });

  it('needs the latest unseen mock at 80% for Ready', () => {
    const lowLast = read(WORLD, {
      attempts: [attempt('A', '2026-10-10', 0.95), attempt('B', '2026-10-18', 0.79)],
    });
    expect(lowLast.value ?? 0).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.8);
    expect(lowLast.label).toBe('Close');
    const highLast = read(WORLD, {
      attempts: [attempt('A', '2026-10-10', 0.79), attempt('B', '2026-10-18', 0.95)],
    });
    expect(highLast.label).toBe('Ready');
  });

  it('shows no number before 40 concepts have evidence', () => {
    const attempts = [attempt('A', TODAY, 1)];
    const few = read(WORLD, { attempts, entries: strongAll(WORLD, new Set(['d4-10'])) });
    expect(few.value).toBeNull();
    expect(few.label).toBe('Building');
    expect(few.memory).toBeGreaterThan(0.9);
    const content = contentOf(WORLD);
    expect(evidenceCount(memoryWith(content, strongAll(WORLD, new Set(['d4-10']))), content)).toBe(39);
    expect(read(WORLD, { attempts }).value).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('picks the weakest task with at least 3 concepts', () => {
    const halfGate = (specs: readonly ConceptSpec[]) =>
      specs.map((s) => [s.id, memoryOfCard(STRONG, 1)] as const);
    const twoUnseen = conceptsFor('t12', '1.2', 2);
    const t23 = conceptsFor('t23', '2.3', 3);
    const t24 = conceptsFor('t24', '2.4', 3);
    const r = read([...WORLD, ...twoUnseen, ...t23, ...t24], {
      entries: [...strongAll(WORLD), ...halfGate(t23), ...halfGate(t24)],
    });
    expect(r.weakest?.task).toBe('2.3');
    expect(r.weakest?.value).toBeCloseTo(0.5, 5);
    expect(read(WORLD).weakest?.task).toBe('1.1');
  });

  it('reads the exam-crunch journey to its committed value', () => {
    const events = examCrunch();
    const now = journeyNow('exam-crunch');
    const memory = deriveMemory(events, JOURNEY_CONTENT);
    const r = readiness({
      memory,
      content: JOURNEY_CONTENT,
      attempts: firstAttempts(derive(events, null)),
      now,
      tz: 0,
    });
    expect(r.value).not.toBeNull();
    const path = fileURLToPath(new URL('../fixtures/journeys/exam-crunch.readiness.json', import.meta.url));
    const actual = `${JSON.stringify(toPlain({ shown: Math.floor(100 * (r.value ?? 0)), readiness: r }), null, 2)}\n`;
    if (!existsSync(path)) writeFileSync(path, actual);
    expect(actual).toBe(readFileSync(path, 'utf8'));
  });
});

describe('goNoGo', () => {
  const close = read(WORLD);
  const ready = read(WORLD, { attempts: [attempt('A', '2026-10-18', 0.9)] });

  it('shows from 7 to 2 days before the exam while readiness is not Ready', () => {
    expect(ready.label).toBe('Ready');
    const at = (today: string, r: Readiness = close) =>
      goNoGo({ examDate: '2026-11-14', today, readiness: r });
    expect(at('2026-11-07')).toBe(true);
    expect(at('2026-11-12')).toBe(true);
    expect(at('2026-11-06')).toBe(false);
    expect(at('2026-11-13')).toBe(false);
    expect(at('2026-11-09', ready)).toBe(false);
    expect(goNoGo({ examDate: null, today: '2026-11-07', readiness: close })).toBe(false);
  });
});
```
