```ts
import fc from 'fast-check';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { NewEvent, StudyEvent } from '../../schemas/events.ts';
import { DOMAIN_IDS } from '../../schemas/ids.ts';
import { addDays } from '../../src/engine/day.ts';
import { deriveMemory } from '../../src/engine/memory.ts';
import { firstAttempts, mockDeadline } from '../../src/engine/mock.ts';
import { type Readiness, readiness } from '../../src/engine/readiness.ts';
import { derive } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import { timelineOf } from '../fixtures/events.ts';
import { answerOf, contentOf, examStartOf, submitOf } from '../fixtures/memory.ts';

/** Readiness over any log (spec 9): Memory and every Q_d never rise with time alone, and every value stays in [0, 1]. */
const SIZE = 44;
const CONTENT = contentOf([
  ...Array.from({ length: SIZE }, (_, i) => ({
    id: `bun-${i}`,
    module: 1 + (i % 13),
    tasks: [`${1 + (i % 4)}.${1 + (i % 3)}`],
  })),
  { id: 'menu', module: 2, tested: false, tasks: [] },
]);
const CONCEPTS = [...Array.from({ length: SIZE }, (_, i) => `bun-${i}`), 'menu', 'ghost'];
const START = '2026-10-01';

type Item = readonly [string, NewEvent];

/** An ISO time `minute` minutes after 04:00 UTC on day `n` from the start, so it stays in that study day. */
const timeOf = (n: number, minute: number): string =>
  new Date(Date.parse(`${addDays(START, n)}T04:00:00.000Z`) + minute * 60_000).toISOString();

const answerArb: fc.Arbitrary<Item[]> = fc
  .record({
    day: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 20 }),
    minute: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 1_300 }),
    concept: fc.constantFrom(...CONCEPTS),
    mode: fc.constantFrom('check' as const, 'quiz' as const, 'review' as const, 'focus' as const),
    correct: fc.boolean(),
    sure: fc.boolean(),
  })
  .map(({ day, minute, concept, mode, correct, sure }) => [
    [timeOf(day, minute), answerOf({ concept, qid: `q-${concept}-1`, mode, correct, sure })],
  ]);

const settingsArb: fc.Arbitrary<Item[]> = fc
  .record({ day: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 20 }), exam: fc.option(fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 40 })) })
  .map(({ day, exam }) => [
    [timeOf(day, 1_390), { t: 'settings', patch: { examDate: exam === null ? null : addDays(START, exam) } }],
  ]);

/** A submitted mock of form A, B or a remix over up to 6 concepts, its answers right or wrong. */
const mockArb = (sid: string): fc.Arbitrary<Item[]> =>
  fc
    .record({
      day: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 20 }),
      form: fc.constantFrom('A', 'B', 'remix'),
      picks: fc.array(fc.record({ concept: fc.constantFrom(...CONCEPTS), correct: fc.boolean() }), {
        minLength: 1,
        maxLength: 6,
      }),
    })
    .map(({ day, form, picks }) => {
      const start = timeOf(day, 1_000);
      const qids = picks.map((_, i) => `q-${sid}-${i}`);
      return [
        [start, examStartOf(sid, 'mock', qids, { form, deadline: mockDeadline(new Date(start)) })],
        ...picks.map(
          ({ concept, correct }, i): Item => [
            timeOf(day, 1_001 + i),
            answerOf({ concept, qid: qids[i] ?? '', mode: 'mock', sid, correct, sure: true }),
          ],
        ),
        [timeOf(day, 1_010), submitOf(sid, 'mock')],
      ];
    });

/** Optionally 40 concepts answered right on day 0, so readiness often shows a number. */
const warmArb: fc.Arbitrary<Item[]> = fc
  .boolean()
  .map((warm) =>
    warm
      ? Array.from(
          { length: 40 },
          (_, i): Item => [
            timeOf(0, i),
            answerOf({ concept: `bun-${i}`, qid: `q-bun-${i}-1`, mode: 'check', correct: true, sure: true }),
          ],
        )
      : [],
  );

const logArb = (mocks: boolean): fc.Arbitrary<StudyEvent[]> =>
  fc
    .tuple(
      warmArb,
      fc.array(answerArb, { maxLength: 60 }),
      fc.array(settingsArb, { maxLength: 2 }),
      mocks
        ? fc.tuple(mockArb('01JA0000000000000000000MK1'), mockArb('01JA0000000000000000000MK2'))
        : fc.constant([] as Item[][]),
    )
    .map(([warm, answers, settings, mockBlocks]) =>
      timelineOf(
        [...warm, ...answers.flat(), ...settings.flat(), ...mockBlocks.flat()].sort((a, b) =>
          a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : a[0] > b[0] ? 1 : 0,
        ),
      ),
    );

const gapArb = fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 60 * 24 * 40 });

function readAt(events: readonly StudyEvent[], minutesAfterLast: number): Readiness {
  const last = events.reduce((max, e) => (e.at > max ? e.at : max), timeOf(0, 0));
  return readiness({
    memory: deriveMemory(events, CONTENT),
    content: CONTENT,
    attempts: firstAttempts(derive(events, null)),
    now: new Date(Date.parse(last) + minutesAfterLast * 60_000),
    tz: 0,
  });
}

describe('readiness properties (spec 9)', () => {
  it('Memory and every Q_d never rise as time passes without a new event', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(logArb(true), gapArb, gapArb, (events, first, more) => {
        const before = readAt(events, first);
        const after = readAt(events, first + more);
        expect(after.memory).toBeLessThanOrEqual(before.memory + 1e-12);
        for (const d of DOMAIN_IDS) expect(after.domains[d]).toBeLessThanOrEqual(before.domains[d] + 1e-12);
      }),
      { numRuns: 150 },
    );
  });

  it('readiness never rises as time passes for a log with no mock submit', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(logArb(false), gapArb, gapArb, (events, first, more) => {
        const before = readAt(events, first);
        const after = readAt(events, first + more);
        expect(after.value === null).toBe(before.value === null);
        expect(after.value ?? 0).toBeLessThanOrEqual((before.value ?? 0) + 1e-12);
      }),
      { numRuns: 150 },
    );
  });

  it('stays within 0 and 1 for any log', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(logArb(true), gapArb, (events, gap) => {
        const r = readAt(events, gap);
        for (const x of [
          r.value ?? 0,
          r.memory,
          r.mock ?? 0,
          ...Object.values(r.domains),
          r.weakest?.value ?? 0,
        ]) {
          expect(x).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
          expect(x).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
        }
      }),
      { numRuns: 150 },
    );
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write readiness**

Create `src/engine/readiness.ts`:

```ts
import { DOMAIN_IDS, type DomainIdValue } from '../../schemas/ids.ts';
import { addDays, DAY_MS, dayStartAt, daysBetween, studyDayAt } from './day.ts';
import { conceptMastery } from './mastery.ts';
import type { MemoryContent, MemoryState } from './memory.ts';
import { MOCK_WINDOW_DAYS, type MockAttempt } from './mock.ts';
import { domainOf } from './quotas.ts';

/**
 * Readiness (spec 3.9): memory of the tested concepts read at the exam moment `t*`, blended with the latest unseen
 * mocks, and capped at 0.70 until a mock has been taken. It sits beside `deriveMemory` and is read for a moment.
 */
export const DOMAIN_WEIGHTS = { '1': 0.24, '2': 0.3, '3': 0.34, '4': 0.12 } as const;
/** Tested concepts with a card before readiness shows a number. */
export const EVIDENCE_MIN = 40;
export const NO_MOCK_CAP = 0.7;
export const READY = 0.8;
export const CLOSE = 0.65;
/** Every domain's `Q_d` must reach this for Ready. */
export const DOMAIN_FLOOR = 0.7;
/** A task needs this many tested concepts to be named the weakest. */
export const WEAKEST_MIN_CONCEPTS = 3;
/** Without an exam date after today, readiness reads this many days ahead. */
export const PROJECT_DAYS = 7;

export type ReadinessLabel = 'Ready' | 'Close' | 'Building';

export interface Readiness {
  /** In [0, 1], or null before `EVIDENCE_MIN` tested concepts have a card; shown as `Math.floor(100 * value)`. */
  value: number | null;
  label: ReadinessLabel;
  /** True when no first attempt sits in the window, so the value is capped at 0.70. */
  capped: boolean;
  memory: number;
  mock: number | null;
  domains: Record<DomainIdValue, number>;
  weakest: { task: string; value: number } | null;
  /** The moment `t*` readiness is read at, as an ISO time. */
  at: string;
}

/** `t*`: the exam day's 04:00 when the exam date is after today, else `now` plus 7 days. */
export function readinessTarget(examDate: string | null, now: Date, tz: number): Date {
  if (examDate !== null && examDate > studyDayAt(now, tz)) return dayStartAt(examDate, tz);
  return new Date(now.getTime() + PROJECT_DAYS * DAY_MS);
}

/** Tested concepts of the content whose memory has a card. */
export function evidenceCount(memory: MemoryState, content: MemoryContent): number {
  let count = 0;
  for (const [id, info] of content.concepts) {
    if (info.tested && (memory.concepts.get(id)?.card ?? null) !== null) count++;
  }
  return count;
}

const mean = (values: readonly number[]): number =>
  values.length === 0 ? 0 : values.reduce((sum, v) => sum + v, 0) / values.length;
const clamp01 = (x: number): number => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, x));
const byAt = (a: MockAttempt, b: MockAttempt): number => (a.at < b.at ? -1 : a.at > b.at ? 1 : 0);

export function readiness(args: {
  memory: MemoryState;
  content: MemoryContent;
  attempts: readonly MockAttempt[];
  now: Date;
  tz: number;
}): Readiness {
  const { memory, content, now, tz } = args;
  const today = studyDayAt(now, tz);
  const target = readinessTarget(memory.examDate, now, tz);

  const byDomain = new Map<DomainIdValue, number[]>(DOMAIN_IDS.map((d) => [d, []]));
  const byTask = new Map<string, number[]>();
  for (const [id, info] of content.concepts) {
    if (!info.tested) continue;
    const value = conceptMastery(memory.concepts.get(id), target);
    for (const domain of new Set(info.tasks.map(domainOf))) byDomain.get(domain)?.push(value);
    for (const task of new Set(info.tasks)) {
      const list = byTask.get(task) ?? [];
      list.push(value);
      byTask.set(task, list);
    }
  }
  const domains = {} as Record<DomainIdValue, number>;
  for (const d of DOMAIN_IDS) domains[d] = mean(byDomain.get(d) ?? []);
  const memoryValue = clamp01(DOMAIN_IDS.reduce((sum, d) => sum + DOMAIN_WEIGHTS[d] * domains[d], 0));

  const recent = args.attempts.filter((a) => daysBetween(a.day, today) <= MOCK_WINDOW_DAYS).sort(byAt);
  const latestTwo = recent.slice(-2);
  const mock = latestTwo.length === 0 ? null : mean(latestTwo.map((a) => a.percent));
  const blended = mock === null ? Math.min(memoryValue, NO_MOCK_CAP) : 0.5 * memoryValue + 0.5 * mock;
  const value = evidenceCount(memory, content) < EVIDENCE_MIN ? null : clamp01(blended);

  const latest = recent.at(-1);
  const ready =
    value !== null &&
    value >= READY &&
    latest !== undefined &&
    latest.percent >= READY &&
    DOMAIN_IDS.every((d) => domains[d] >= DOMAIN_FLOOR);
  const label: ReadinessLabel = ready ? 'Ready' : value !== null && value >= CLOSE ? 'Close' : 'Building';

  let weakest: { task: string; value: number } | null = null;
  for (const task of [...byTask.keys()].sort()) {
    const values = byTask.get(task) ?? [];
    if (values.length < WEAKEST_MIN_CONCEPTS) continue;
    const taskValue = mean(values);
    if (weakest === null || taskValue < weakest.value) weakest = { task, value: taskValue };
  }

  return {
    value,
    label,
    capped: mock === null,
    memory: memoryValue,
    mock,
    domains,
    weakest,
    at: target.toISOString(),
  };
}

/** The go/no-go line (spec 10.2): shown from 7 to 2 days before the exam while readiness is not Ready. */
export function goNoGo(args: { examDate: string | null; today: string; readiness: Readiness }): boolean {
  const { examDate, today } = args;
  if (examDate === null) return false;
  return addDays(examDate, -7) <= today && today <= addDays(examDate, -2) && args.readiness.label !== 'Ready';
}
```

## Task 14: Ranks and the extras line

**Files:**
- Create: `src/engine/ranks.ts`, `tests/unit/ranks.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/engine/next.ts`, `tests/unit/next.test.ts`, `tests/unit/anti-grind.test.ts` (after Tasks 9 and 11)
- Test: `tests/unit/ranks.test.ts`, `tests/unit/next.test.ts`, `tests/unit/anti-grind.test.ts`

Design notes:
- `RankDef` mirrors a `content/game/ranks.yaml` entry; the app maps the index's `RankView` to it, and the engine never reads the YAML.
- `rankFacts` turns the log into the facts the rules read: modules with a passed boss (`correct >= bossPassMark(total)` from Task 9, counted once per module), modules whose mastery is 60 or more, and `firstAttempts`; the caller passes `ready` (the readiness label is Ready) and `today`.
- `meets` checks every requirement at the same moment; `unseenMocksAt80` counts distinct forms whose first attempt is at least 0.80, and `latestMockWithinDays` needs the latest of those within that many days of `today`, so remixes never count.
- `computedRank` is the highest rank that meets its requirements now, `shownRank` the highest of that and every recorded `rank` event (`Progress.ranks` from Task 8), and `rankToRecord` the computed rank when it is above every recorded one, never Basic, so the app writes one `rank` event per promotion.
- `nextRankLine` caps each count at its requirement, prints `readiness <label>` for the Ready requirement (`-` while readiness has no number), and gives null at the top rank.
- The key-fix rescore conflict (P3-118) follows the built code: the survival test bumps `questionVersions` so the old answers turn stale and lose their cards, and the recorded rank still shows.
- The rank fixtures cross each threshold by exactly one event: the fifth boss submit, one more answer that gives Module 13 its second success day (its hex moves from about 39 to about 78), and the submit of the second unseen form at 80%.
- Boss and mock answers in these fixtures use concepts the content does not have, so Task 9's exam memory rules leave the hexes alone.
- `next.ts` makes `focus`, `boss` and `mock` resumable; an exam's answers wait in kv until the submit, so an open boss or mock always has questions left.
- `extras` returns at most two entries: the lowest module whose boss is ready and out of its cooldown, then the next form once 7 bosses are passed and the latest submitted mock (a remix included) is at least 3 days old; incident and lab entries wait for Phase 4.
- Covers P3-110, P3-111, P3-112, P3-113, P3-117, P3-118, P3-119, P3-135, P3-137 and P3-138.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing rank tests**

Create `tests/unit/ranks.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { NewEvent, StudyEvent } from '../../schemas/events.ts';
import { studyDayAt } from '../../src/engine/day.ts';
import { moduleMastery } from '../../src/engine/mastery.ts';
import { deriveMemory, type MemoryContent } from '../../src/engine/memory.ts';
import { mockDeadline } from '../../src/engine/mock.ts';
import {
  computedRank,
  meets,
  nextRankLine,
  type RankDef,
  type RankFacts,
  rankFacts,
  rankToRecord,
  shownRank,
} from '../../src/engine/ranks.ts';
import { derive, type Progress } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import { timelineOf } from '../fixtures/events.ts';
import { answerOf, at, contentOf, examStartOf, submitOf } from '../fixtures/memory.ts';

/** The spec 3.4 rank ladder with made-up facts, so these tests never depend on content/game/ranks.yaml. */
const RANKS: RankDef[] = [
  { id: 'basic', name: 'Basic', short: 'Basic', requires: {}, facts: [] },
  {
    id: 'business-support-plus',
    name: 'Business Support+',
    short: 'Business+',
    requires: { bosses: 5, hexesAt60: 5 },
    facts: ['A made-up first fact.', 'A made-up second fact.'],
  },
  {
    id: 'enterprise',
    name: 'Enterprise',
    short: 'Enterprise',
    requires: { bosses: 13, hexesAt60: 13 },
    facts: ['A made-up first fact.', 'A made-up second fact.'],
  },
  {
    id: 'unified-operations',
    name: 'Unified Operations',
    short: 'Unified Ops',
    requires: { unseenMocksAt80: 2, latestMockWithinDays: 14, ready: true },
    facts: ['A made-up first fact.', 'A made-up second fact.'],
  },
];
const [BASIC, PLUS, ENTERPRISE, UNIFIED] = RANKS as [RankDef, RankDef, RankDef, RankDef];

type Item = readonly [string, NewEvent];

const DAY1 = '2026-10-20';
const DAY2 = '2026-10-21';
const NOW = new Date(at(DAY2, 20));
const pad2 = (n: number): string => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const bun = (m: number): string => `bun-${pad2(m)}`;
/** One tested concept per module, so each hex is that concept's mastery. */
const SPECS = Array.from({ length: 13 }, (_, i) => ({ id: bun(i + 1), module: i + 1, tasks: ['1.1'] }));
const CONTENT = contentOf(SPECS);

/** A right answer on day 1 gives a hex near 40; a second success day on day 2 lifts it near 80. */
const studied = (m: number, days: 1 | 2): Item[] => [
  [
    at(DAY1, 9, m),
    answerOf({ concept: bun(m), qid: `q-${bun(m)}-a`, mode: 'quiz', correct: true, sure: true }),
  ],
  ...(days === 2
    ? [
        [
          at(DAY2, 9, m),
          answerOf({ concept: bun(m), qid: `q-${bun(m)}-b`, mode: 'quiz', correct: true, sure: true }),
        ] as Item,
      ]
    : []),
];

const bossSid = (m: number): string => `01JA0000000000000000000B${pad2(m)}`;
/** A passed one-item boss of module `m`; its concept is unknown to the content, so memory ignores it. */
const boss = (m: number): Item[] => {
  const start = at(DAY2, 10, 3 * m);
  return [
    [
      start,
      examStartOf(bossSid(m), 'boss', [`q-boss-${m}`], {
        scope: `m${pad2(m)}`,
        deadline: mockDeadline(new Date(start)),
      }),
    ],
    [
      at(DAY2, 10, 3 * m + 1),
      answerOf({
        concept: 'boss-ghost',
        qid: `q-boss-${m}`,
        mode: 'boss',
        sid: bossSid(m),
        correct: true,
        sure: true,
      }),
    ],
    [at(DAY2, 10, 3 * m + 2), submitOf(bossSid(m), 'boss')],
  ];
};

/** A mock of `form` with 10 items, `right` of them right; without `submit` it stops before the submit. */
const mock = (sid: string, form: string, right: number, hour: number, submit = true): Item[] => {
  const start = at(DAY2, hour);
  const qids = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => `q-${form}-${i}`);
  return [
    [start, examStartOf(sid, 'mock', qids, { form, deadline: mockDeadline(new Date(start)) })],
    ...qids.map(
      (qid, i): Item => [
        at(DAY2, hour, i + 1),
        answerOf({ concept: 'mock-ghost', qid, mode: 'mock', sid, correct: i < right, sure: true }),
      ],
    ),
    ...(submit ? [[at(DAY2, hour, 30), submitOf(sid, 'mock')] as Item] : []),
  ];
};

const rankEvent = (rank: string): Item => [at(DAY2, 21), { t: 'rank', rank }];

const logOf = (items: readonly Item[]): StudyEvent[] =>
  timelineOf([...items].sort((a, b) => (a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : a[0] > b[0] ? 1 : 0)));

function factsOf(
  events: readonly StudyEvent[],
  options: { now?: Date; ready?: boolean; content?: MemoryContent } = {},
): { facts: RankFacts; progress: Progress } {
  const now = options.now ?? NOW;
  const progress = derive(events, null);
  const mastery = moduleMastery(
    deriveMemory(events, options.content ?? CONTENT),
    options.content ?? CONTENT,
    now,
  );
  return {
    progress,
    facts: rankFacts({ progress, mastery, ready: options.ready ?? false, today: studyDayAt(now, 0) }),
  };
}

const range = (from: number, to: number): number[] =>
  Array.from({ length: to - from + 1 }, (_, i) => from + i);
const ENTERPRISE_LOG: Item[] = [...range(1, 13).flatMap((m) => studied(m, 2)), ...range(1, 13).flatMap(boss)];

describe('rank thresholds (spec 3.4), each crossed by exactly one event', () => {
  it('reaches Business Support+ with the fifth boss pass', () => {
    const base = [...range(1, 5).flatMap((m) => studied(m, 2)), ...range(1, 4).flatMap(boss)];
    const before = factsOf(logOf(base)).facts;
    expect([before.bossesPassed, before.hexesAt60]).toEqual([4, 5]);
    expect(computedRank(RANKS, before).id).toBe('basic');
    const after = factsOf(logOf([...base, ...boss(5)])).facts;
    expect(after.bossesPassed).toBe(5);
    expect(computedRank(RANKS, after).id).toBe('business-support-plus');
  });

  it('reaches Enterprise when one answer lifts the thirteenth hex to 60', () => {
    const base = [
      ...range(1, 12).flatMap((m) => studied(m, 2)),
      ...studied(13, 1),
      ...range(1, 13).flatMap(boss),
    ];
    const before = factsOf(logOf(base)).facts;
    expect([before.bossesPassed, before.hexesAt60]).toEqual([13, 12]);
    expect(computedRank(RANKS, before).id).toBe('business-support-plus');
    const after = factsOf(logOf(ENTERPRISE_LOG)).facts;
    expect(after.hexesAt60).toBe(13);
    expect(computedRank(RANKS, after).id).toBe('enterprise');
  });

  it('reaches Unified Operations with the second unseen form at 80% while readiness reads Ready', () => {
    const sidA = '01JA00000000000000000000UA';
    const sidB = '01JA00000000000000000000UB';
    const base = [...ENTERPRISE_LOG, ...mock(sidA, 'A', 9, 12), ...mock(sidB, 'B', 8, 14, false)];
    const before = factsOf(logOf(base), { ready: true }).facts;
    expect(computedRank(RANKS, before).id).toBe('enterprise');
    const after = factsOf(logOf([...base, [at(DAY2, 14, 30), submitOf(sidB, 'mock')]]), {
      ready: true,
    }).facts;
    expect(after.attempts.map((a) => [a.form, a.percent])).toEqual([
      ['A', 0.9],
      ['B', 0.8],
    ]);
    expect(computedRank(RANKS, after).id).toBe('unified-operations');
    expect(computedRank(RANKS, { ...after, ready: false }).id).toBe('enterprise');
  });

  it('needs the latest unseen mock at 80% within 14 days', () => {
    const facts: RankFacts = {
      bossesPassed: 0,
      hexesAt60: 0,
      attempts: [
        {
          form: 'A',
          sid: 's1',
          percent: 0.9,
          day: '2026-10-27',
          at: '2026-10-27T10:00:00.000Z',
          correct: 9,
          total: 10,
        },
        {
          form: 'B',
          sid: 's2',
          percent: 0.85,
          day: '2026-10-31',
          at: '2026-10-31T10:00:00.000Z',
          correct: 17,
          total: 20,
        },
      ],
      ready: true,
      today: '2026-11-14',
    };
    expect(meets(UNIFIED, facts)).toBe(true);
    expect(meets(UNIFIED, { ...facts, today: '2026-11-15' })).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('a rank is never taken away (spec 3.4)', () => {
  it('keeps the rank after decay lowers the hexes', () => {
    const events = logOf([...ENTERPRISE_LOG, rankEvent('enterprise')]);
    const later = new Date(NOW.getTime() + 60 * 86_400_000);
    const { facts, progress } = factsOf(events, { now: later });
    expect(facts.hexesAt60).toBeLessThan(13);
    expect(computedRank(RANKS, facts).id).not.toBe('enterprise');
    expect(shownRank(RANKS, facts, progress.ranks).id).toBe('enterprise');
  });

  it('keeps the rank after a questionVersions bump makes old answers stale', () => {
    const events = logOf([
      ...range(1, 5).flatMap((m) => studied(m, 2)),
      ...range(1, 5).flatMap(boss),
      rankEvent('business-support-plus'),
    ]);
    const bumped = contentOf(
      SPECS,
      new Map(range(1, 13).flatMap((m) => [[`q-${bun(m)}-a`, 2] as const, [`q-${bun(m)}-b`, 2] as const])),
    );
    const { facts, progress } = factsOf(events, { content: bumped });
    expect(facts.hexesAt60).toBe(0);
    expect(computedRank(RANKS, facts).id).toBe('basic');
    expect(shownRank(RANKS, facts, progress.ranks).id).toBe('business-support-plus');
  });

  it('keeps the rank after an ENGINE_VERSION replay', () => {
    const events = logOf([...ENTERPRISE_LOG, rankEvent('enterprise')]);
    const stored = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(events)) as StudyEvent[];
    const replayed = derive(stored, null);
    const { facts } = factsOf(stored);
    const lowered: RankFacts = { ...facts, bossesPassed: 0, hexesAt60: 0 };
    expect(computedRank(RANKS, lowered).id).toBe('basic');
    expect(shownRank(RANKS, lowered, replayed.ranks).id).toBe('enterprise');
  });

  it('never needs XP', () => {
    const lessons: Item[] = range(1, 1_000).map((n) => [
      new Date(Date.parse(at(DAY1, 5)) + n * 1_000).toISOString(),
      { t: 'lesson.done', note: `m01/lesson-${n}` },
    ]);
    const events = logOf(lessons);
    expect(deriveMemory(events, CONTENT).xp).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(10_000);
    const { facts } = factsOf(events, { ready: true });
    expect(computedRank(RANKS, facts).id).toBe('basic');
  });
});

describe('rankToRecord', () => {
  const facts = (bossesPassed: number, hexesAt60: number): RankFacts => ({
    bossesPassed,
    hexesAt60,
    attempts: [],
    ready: false,
    today: DAY2,
  });

  it('records a computed rank above every recorded one and never Basic', () => {
    expect(rankToRecord(RANKS, facts(0, 0), new Set())).toBeNull();
    expect(rankToRecord(RANKS, facts(5, 5), new Set())?.id).toBe('business-support-plus');
    expect(rankToRecord(RANKS, facts(5, 5), new Set(['business-support-plus']))).toBeNull();
    expect(rankToRecord(RANKS, facts(5, 5), new Set(['enterprise']))).toBeNull();
    expect(rankToRecord(RANKS, facts(13, 13), new Set(['business-support-plus']))?.id).toBe('enterprise');
  });
});

describe('nextRankLine', () => {
  const facts: RankFacts = { bossesPassed: 3, hexesAt60: 4, attempts: [], ready: false, today: DAY2 };

  it('names the next rank and how far each requirement is', () => {
    expect(nextRankLine(RANKS, BASIC, facts, 'Close')).toBe('Business Support+: 3/5 bosses, 4/5 hexes at 60');
    expect(nextRankLine(RANKS, PLUS, { ...facts, bossesPassed: 9, hexesAt60: 8 }, 'Close')).toBe(
      'Enterprise: 9/13 bosses, 8/13 hexes at 60',
    );
    const oneForm: RankFacts = {
      ...facts,
      attempts: [
        { form: 'A', sid: 's1', percent: 0.85, day: DAY2, at: at(DAY2, 12), correct: 17, total: 20 },
        { form: 'B', sid: 's2', percent: 0.7, day: DAY2, at: at(DAY2, 13), correct: 14, total: 20 },
      ],
    };
    expect(nextRankLine(RANKS, ENTERPRISE, oneForm, 'Close')).toBe(
      'Unified Operations: 1/2 unseen mocks at 80, readiness Close',
    );
    expect(nextRankLine(RANKS, ENTERPRISE, oneForm, '-')).toBe(
      'Unified Operations: 1/2 unseen mocks at 80, readiness -',
    );
  });

  it('gives no line at the top rank', () => {
    expect(nextRankLine(RANKS, UNIFIED, facts, 'Ready')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('extras (spec 4.6)', () => {
  const status = (over: Partial<BossStatus> = {}): BossStatus => ({
    unlocked: true,
    passed: false,
    passedAt: null,
    ready: false,
    cooldownUntil: null,
    open: null,
    attempts: 0,
    ...over,
  });
  const NOW = new Date(at('2026-10-10', 12));
  const passedUpTo = (n: number): [number, BossStatus][] =>
    Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => [i + 1, status({ passed: true, passedAt: at('2026-10-09') })]);
  const extrasOf = (
    bosses: [number, BossStatus][],
    log: readonly (readonly [string, NewEvent])[] = [],
    today = '2026-10-10',
  ) => extras({ bosses: new Map(bosses), progress: derive(timelineOf(log), null), today, now: NOW });
  const mockOn = (sid: string, form: string, day: string) => {
    const start = at(day, 9);
    return [
      [start, examStartOf(sid, 'mock', ['q-a'], { form, deadline: mockDeadline(new Date(start)) })],
      [at(day, 10), submitOf(sid, 'mock')],
    ] as const;
  };

  it('offers the lowest ready boss first, then the next mock', () => {
    expect(MOCK_AFTER_BOSSES).toBe(7);
    expect(MOCK_GAP_DAYS).toBe(3);

      number,
      BossStatus,
    ][];
    expect(extrasOf(bosses)).toEqual([
      { kind: 'boss', module: 8 },
      { kind: 'mock', form: 'A' },
    ]);
  });

  it('skips a boss in its cooldown and offers the next ready one', () => {

        [3, status({ ready: true })],
      ]),
    ).toEqual([{ kind: 'boss', module: 3 }]);
    expect(extrasOf([[2, over]])).toEqual([{ kind: 'boss', module: 2 }]);
    expect(extrasOf([[2, cooling]])).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('offers a mock only after 7 passed bosses', () => {
    expect(extrasOf(passedUpTo(6))).toEqual([]);
    expect(extrasOf(passedUpTo(7))).toEqual([{ kind: 'mock', form: 'A' }]);
  });

  it('waits 3 days after the latest mock, a remix included', () => {
    const afterA = mockOn('01JA00000000000000000000MA', 'A', '2026-10-08');
    expect(extrasOf(passedUpTo(7), afterA, '2026-10-10')).toEqual([]);
    expect(extrasOf(passedUpTo(7), afterA, '2026-10-11')).toEqual([{ kind: 'mock', form: 'B' }]);
    const remix = mockOn('01JA00000000000000000000MR', 'remix', '2026-10-10');
    expect(extrasOf(passedUpTo(7), [...afterA, ...remix], '2026-10-11')).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('offers no mock once forms A to D are taken', () => {
    const all = [
      ...mockOn('01JA00000000000000000000M1', 'A', '2026-09-20'),
      ...mockOn('01JA00000000000000000000M2', 'B', '2026-09-24'),

      ...mockOn('01JA00000000000000000000M4', 'D', '2026-10-02'),
    ];
    expect(extrasOf(passedUpTo(13), all)).toEqual([]);

});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the ranks**

Create `src/engine/ranks.ts`:

```ts
import { daysBetween } from './day.ts';
import { bossPassMark } from './memory.ts';
import { firstAttempts, type MockAttempt } from './mock.ts';
import { examsOf, type Progress } from './replay.ts';

/**
 * Ranks (spec 3.4): the Support plans of `content/game/ranks.yaml`, earned by bosses, hexes and unseen mocks,
 * never by XP. The app records each promotion as a `rank` event, so the shown rank never drops.
 */
export interface RankDef {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  short: string;
  requires: {
    bosses?: number;
    hexesAt60?: number;
    unseenMocksAt80?: number;
    latestMockWithinDays?: number;
    ready?: boolean;
  };
  facts: readonly string[];
}

/** What the rank rules read at one moment. */
export interface RankFacts {
  bossesPassed: number;
  hexesAt60: number;
  attempts: readonly MockAttempt[];
  ready: boolean;
  today: string;
}

/** An unseen mock counts toward a rank from this share right. */
export const UNSEEN_MOCK_MARK = 0.8;
/** A hex counts toward a rank from this module mastery. */
export const HEX_MARK = 60;

/** The first attempts at 80% or more, one per form, oldest first. */
function atEighty(attempts: readonly MockAttempt[]): MockAttempt[] {
  const byForm = new Map<string, MockAttempt>();
  for (const a of attempts) if (a.percent >= UNSEEN_MOCK_MARK && !byForm.has(a.form)) byForm.set(a.form, a);
  return [...byForm.values()].sort((a, b) => (a.at < b.at ? -1 : a.at > b.at ? 1 : 0));
}

/** The facts of the log at one moment: modules with a passed boss, module masteries at 60 or more, first attempts. */
export function rankFacts(args: {
  progress: Progress;
  mastery: ReadonlyMap<number, number>;
  ready: boolean;
  today: string;
}): RankFacts {
  const passed = new Set(
    examsOf(args.progress, 'boss')
      .filter((r) => r.total > 0 && r.correct >= bossPassMark(r.total))
      .map((r) => r.scope),
  );
  return {
    bossesPassed: passed.size,
    hexesAt60: [...args.mastery.values()].filter((m) => m >= HEX_MARK).length,
    attempts: firstAttempts(args.progress),
    ready: args.ready,
    today: args.today,
  };
}

/** True when every requirement of the rank holds at the same moment. */
export function meets(rank: RankDef, facts: RankFacts): boolean {
  const r = rank.requires;
  if (r.bosses !== undefined && facts.bossesPassed < r.bosses) return false;
  if (r.hexesAt60 !== undefined && facts.hexesAt60 < r.hexesAt60) return false;
  const good = atEighty(facts.attempts);
  if (r.unseenMocksAt80 !== undefined && good.length < r.unseenMocksAt80) return false;
  if (r.latestMockWithinDays !== undefined) {
    const latest = good.at(-1);
    if (latest === undefined || daysBetween(latest.day, facts.today) > r.latestMockWithinDays) return false;
  }
  if (r.ready === true && !facts.ready) return false;
  return true;
}

/** The highest rank whose every requirement holds now; the first rank (Basic) always does. */
export function computedRank(ranks: readonly RankDef[], facts: RankFacts): RankDef {
  for (let i = ranks.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const rank = ranks[i];
    if (rank !== undefined && meets(rank, facts)) return rank;
  }
  const [basic] = ranks;
  if (basic === undefined) throw new Error('ranks.yaml lists no rank');
  return basic;
}

const indexOf = (ranks: readonly RankDef[], id: string): number => ranks.findIndex((r) => r.id === id);

/** The highest of the computed rank and every recorded one, so decay or a replay never lowers it. */
export function shownRank(
  ranks: readonly RankDef[],
  facts: RankFacts,
  recorded: ReadonlySet<string>,
): RankDef {
  const computed = computedRank(ranks, facts);
  let best = indexOf(ranks, computed.id);
  for (const id of recorded) best = Math.max(best, indexOf(ranks, id));
  return ranks[best] ?? computed;
}

/** The computed rank when it is above every recorded one, for the app to record; never Basic. */
export function rankToRecord(
  ranks: readonly RankDef[],
  facts: RankFacts,
  recorded: ReadonlySet<string>,
): RankDef | null {
  const computed = computedRank(ranks, facts);
  const at = indexOf(ranks, computed.id);
  if (at <= 0) return null;
  for (const id of recorded) if (indexOf(ranks, id) >= at) return null;
  return computed;
}

/**
 * Home's next-rank line: `Enterprise: 9/13 bosses, 8/13 hexes at 60`, or for a mock rank
 * `Unified Operations: 1/2 unseen mocks at 80, readiness Close`; `label` is the readiness label, or `-`
 * while readiness has no number. Null at the top rank.
 */
export function nextRankLine(
  ranks: readonly RankDef[],
  shown: RankDef,
  facts: RankFacts,
  label: string,
): string | null {
  const at = indexOf(ranks, shown.id);
  const next = at < 0 ? undefined : ranks[at + 1];
  if (next === undefined) return null;
  const r = next.requires;
  const parts: string[] = [];
  const of = (have: number, need: number, what: string) => `${Math.min(have, need)}/${need} ${what}`;
  if (r.bosses !== undefined) parts.push(of(facts.bossesPassed, r.bosses, 'bosses'));
  if (r.hexesAt60 !== undefined) parts.push(of(facts.hexesAt60, r.hexesAt60, 'hexes at 60'));
  if (r.unseenMocksAt80 !== undefined) {
    parts.push(of(atEighty(facts.attempts).length, r.unseenMocksAt80, 'unseen mocks at 80'));
  }
  if (r.ready === true) parts.push(`readiness ${label}`);
  return `${next.name}: ${parts.join(', ')}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Resume exams and add the extras line**

Replace `src/engine/next.ts` with:

```ts
import type { BossStatus } from './boss.ts';
import { daysBetween, studyDayAt } from './day.ts';
import { mockHistory, nextForm } from './mock.ts';
import { CHUNK_SIZE, ITEM_SECONDS, type ReviewQueue } from './queue.ts';
import type { Progress, SessionState } from './replay.ts';

/**
 * The Next action (spec 4.6): the one thing Home suggests, the first that applies, and the optional extras line.
 * Mission safety waits for missions (Phase 6); incident and lab extras wait for Phase 4.
 */

/** A lesson in course order; `first` marks a module's first lesson. */
export interface LessonRef {
  note: string;
  module: number;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  first: boolean;
  minutes: number;
  checkConcepts: number;
}

export interface NextInput {
  lessons: readonly LessonRef[];
  progress: Progress;
  queue: ReviewQueue;
  currentLesson: string | null;
  today: string;
  tz: number;
}

export type NextAction =
  | { kind: 'resume'; session: SessionState; left: number }
  | { kind: 'review'; count: number }
  | { kind: 'continue'; lesson: LessonRef }
  | { kind: 'check'; module: number; concepts: number; lesson: LessonRef }
  | { kind: 'lesson'; lesson: LessonRef }
  | { kind: 'done' };

const RESUMABLE = new Set(['check', 'quiz', 'focus', 'review', 'boss', 'mock']);

/** The newest unfinished session that still has a question to answer; a review must be today's. */
function resumable(input: NextInput): { session: SessionState; left: number } | null {
  let best: { session: SessionState; left: number } | null = null;
  for (const s of input.progress.sessions.values()) {
    if (!RESUMABLE.has(s.mode) || s.submittedAt !== null || s.abandoned) continue;
    if (s.mode === 'review' && studyDayAt(new Date(s.startedAt), input.tz) !== input.today) continue;
    const left = s.qids.filter((qid) => !s.answers.has(qid)).length;
    if (left === 0) continue;
    const newer =
      !best ||
      s.startedAt > best.session.startedAt ||
      (s.startedAt === best.session.startedAt && s.sid > best.session.sid);
    if (newer) best = { session: s, left };
  }
  return best;
}

export function nextAction(input: NextInput): NextAction {
  const { lessons, progress, queue, currentLesson } = input;
  const resume = resumable(input);
  if (resume) return { kind: 'resume', ...resume };
  if (queue.items.length > 0) return { kind: 'review', count: Math.min(queue.items.length, CHUNK_SIZE) };
  const current = lessons.find((l) => l.note === currentLesson);
  if (current && !progress.lessonsDone.has(current.note)) return { kind: 'continue', lesson: current };
  if (!queue.lessonsAllowed) return { kind: 'done' };
  const unchecked = lessons.filter(
    (l) => progress.lessonsDone.has(l.note) && l.checkConcepts > 0 && !progress.checkScores.has(l.note),
  );
  const first = unchecked[0];
  if (first) {
    const group = unchecked.filter((l) => l.module === first.module);
    const concepts = group.reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.checkConcepts, 0);
    return { kind: 'check', module: first.module, concepts, lesson: first };
  }
  const lesson = lessons.find((l) => !progress.lessonsDone.has(l.note));
  if (lesson) return { kind: 'lesson', lesson };
  return { kind: 'done' };
}

/** The time estimate in whole minutes: 30 seconds per review or check item, a lesson's own minutes. */
export function nextMinutes(action: NextAction): number | null {
  const items = (count: number) => Math.ceil((count * ITEM_SECONDS) / 60);
  switch (action.kind) {
    case 'resume':
      return items(action.left);
    case 'review':
      return items(action.count);
    case 'check':
      return items(action.concepts);
    case 'continue':
    case 'lesson':
      return action.lesson.minutes;
    case 'done':
      return null;
  }
}

/** One entry of the extras line under Done for today. */
export type Extra = { kind: 'boss'; module: number } | { kind: 'mock'; form: string };

/** The mock extra needs this many passed bosses (spec 4.3). */
export const MOCK_AFTER_BOSSES = 7;
/** And at least this many days since the latest submitted mock (spec 10.2). */
export const MOCK_GAP_DAYS = 3;

/** The extras line (spec 4.6), at most two entries: the lowest ready boss out of its cooldown, then the next mock. */
export function extras(args: {
  bosses: ReadonlyMap<number, BossStatus>;
  progress: Progress;
  today: string;
  now: Date;
}): Extra[] {
  const out: Extra[] = [];
  const cooling = (status: BossStatus): boolean =>
    status.cooldownUntil !== null && Date.parse(status.cooldownUntil) > args.now.getTime();
  const [module] = [...args.bosses]
    .filter(([, status]) => status.ready && !cooling(status))
    .map(([m]) => m)
    .sort((a, b) => a - b);
  if (module !== undefined) out.push({ kind: 'boss', module });
  const passed = [...args.bosses.values()].filter((status) => status.passed).length;
  const [latest] = mockHistory(args.progress);
  const form = nextForm(args.progress);
  const rested = latest === undefined || daysBetween(latest.day, args.today) >= MOCK_GAP_DAYS;
  if (passed >= MOCK_AFTER_BOSSES && rested && form !== null) out.push({ kind: 'mock', form });
  return out;
}
```

## Task 15: Index fields, per-form exam chunks, budgets and loaders

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/fixtures/index.ts`, `tests/unit/exam-forms.test.ts`
- Modify: `schemas/chunks.ts`, `scripts/content/build.ts`, `scripts/check-budgets.ts`, `src/app/content.ts`, `src/app/progress.ts`, `tests/unit/build.test.ts`, `tests/unit/build-real.test.ts`, `tests/unit/sw-manifest.test.ts`, `tests/unit/home.test.ts` (after Task 9), `tests/unit/next-ready.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/build.test.ts`, `tests/unit/build-real.test.ts`, `tests/unit/sw-manifest.test.ts`, `tests/unit/exam-forms.test.ts`

Design notes:
- `buildContent` emits one chunk per form, `content/exam-a.<hash8>.json` to `exam-d`, through its local `emit`, each holding that form's live items as `QuestionView` in form order; retired items are skipped, and pool items in no form never ship (Decisions: one exam chunk per form, a deliberate deviation from spec 2.10's single `exam.<hash>.json`).
- `index.exam.items` lists the shipped form items with version, concept, task, form and type, never their text, so replay, stale detection, domain scores and Even Keel need no form chunk; module chunks, `questionVersions` and search stay practice only.
- A remix builds to the forms' shared task and multi-response counts, which Task 18's `remixTargets` reads off `index.exam.items`, so the index carries no separate targets field and keeps the brief's shape.
- `index.blueprint`, `index.ranks` and `index.badges` come from the context Task 2 loads (spec 2.10); a rank's `requires` keeps only the keys it sets, and a badge's missing `arrives` becomes null.
- Build errors about forms name ids and paths only, because exam-pool text must never reach tool output.
- `ensureExamForm(id)` returns a promise of the chunk, or null for an unknown form or a failed fetch, and loads each form at most once; it never sets `loadError`, so a mock start can await it and record nothing when the chunk cannot load (Decisions); `examForms` keeps every loaded form for the learn pool and results.
- `progress.ts` merges the form items' versions into the replay refs, so a mock answer never counts as unknown.
- `check-budgets.ts` requires 0 or 4 exam chunks, puts each under `CHUNK_BUDGET`, checks that `sw.js` precaches each one (a mock works offline on a phone, P3-047), and also budgets every lazy JavaScript chunk, that is any `dist/assets/*.js` the page does not load at start; the `budgets:` line now prints the largest lazy chunk and the largest exam chunk beside the initial JS.
- Only `tests/unit/home.test.ts` and `tests/unit/next-ready.test.ts` hold typed `ContentIndex` literals, so only they spread `emptyIndexFields()`; `vite-plugin.test.ts` builds its index through `buildContent` and needs no change.
- The build tests write a made-up pool file and `forms.yaml` into the fixture tree (bakery text, ids `q-exa001` and so on), and every assertion message names ids only.
- Covers P3-043, P3-044, P3-045, P3-047, P3-085 (chunk part) and P3-189.

```ts
/** The Phase 3 index fields, empty, for typed `ContentIndex` literals in tests that build no exam. */
export function emptyIndexFields(): Pick<ContentIndex, 'blueprint' | 'ranks' | 'badges' | 'exam'> {

    ranks: [],
```

```ts
      ? MULTI.replace('q-9q4r5s', id)
          .replace('version: 3', 'version: 1')

    pool: exam

    concept: shared-kitchen

    options:

      - { id: b, text: Buy a second oven for the branch, why: A second oven idles on quiet days. }
      - { id: c, text: Close the branch at noon, why: Closing turns customers away. }
      - { id: d, text: Bake every loaf the night before, why: Baking early adds no oven time. }
    evidence:

    takeaway: Borrow ovens for a busy day.

      `questions:${examItem('q-exa001')}${examItem('q-exa002', 'retired')}${examItem('q-exb001', 'live', true)}${examItem('q-exc001')}${examItem('q-exd001')}${examItem('q-exs001')}`,
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add the index types**

In `schemas/chunks.ts`, insert these types just above the `ContentIndex` doc comment, and add the four new fields at the end of `ContentIndex`, so the block from `DomainView` to the end of the file reads:

```ts
/** A blueprint domain for Progress and readiness (spec 2.10). */
export interface DomainView {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  weight: number;
}

/** A blueprint task statement; `short` names it on one Home line, for example `access management`. */
export interface TaskView {
  id: string;
  domain: string;
  text: string;
  short: string;
}

/** A rank of content/game/ranks.yaml; Basic has no requirements and no facts. */
export interface RankView {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  short: string;
  requires: Record<string, number | boolean>;
  facts: string[];
}

/** A badge of content/game/badges.yaml; `arrives` names the phase content a badge waits for. */
export interface BadgeView {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  rule: string;
  arrives: 'labs' | 'missions' | 'capstone' | null;
}

/** What replay, stale detection and domain scores need of a form item, never its text. */
export interface ExamItemRef {
  version: number;
  concept: string;
  task: string;
  form: string;
  type: 'single' | 'multi';
}

/** One mock form: `chunk` is content/exam-a.<hash8>.json, and `size` counts the live items it ships. */
export interface ExamFormMeta {
  id: string;
  chunk: string;
  frozen: boolean;
  size: number;
}

/** content/exam-<form>.<hash>.json: one form's live items in form order, loaded only by its mock or once it is taken. */
export interface ExamChunk {
  form: string;
  questions: QuestionView[];
}

/** content/index.<hash>.json, whose name the build injects into index.html. */
export interface ContentIndex {
  contentVersion: string;
  modules: ModuleMeta[];
  concepts: ConceptMeta[];
  glossary: GlossaryItem[];
  missions: MissionMeta[];
  /** Every live practice question id to its version, so replay can count events about unknown questions. */
  questionVersions: Record<string, number>;
  /** Path of the search chunk from the site root. */
  search: string;
  blueprint: { domains: DomainView[]; tasks: TaskView[] };
  ranks: RankView[];
  badges: BadgeView[];
  /** The mock forms and their item refs; form items never appear in `questionVersions`, a module chunk or search. */
  exam: { forms: ExamFormMeta[]; items: Record<string, ExamItemRef> };
}
```

In `scripts/content/build.ts`, replace the imports from `import type {` through `} from './lib/context.ts';` with:

```ts
  DomainView,

  ExamFormMeta,
  ExamItemRef,
  GlossaryItem,
  LessonChunk,
  LessonMeta,
  MissionMeta,
  ModuleChunk,
  ModuleMeta,
  QuestionView,
  RankView,
  SearchChunk,
  SearchEntry,
  TaskView,
} from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import type { Concept } from '../../schemas/concept.ts';
import { normalize } from '../../src/app/search.ts';
import {
  CONCEPTS_FILE,
  type Context,
  type LessonNote,
  type ModuleNote,
  type Note,
  POOL_DIR,
  type QuestionEntry,
} from './lib/context.ts';
```

Insert this function just above `const sha256 = (text: string): string =>`:

```ts
/**
 * The mock forms (spec 4.3): one chunk per form, `content/exam-a.<hash8>.json` to `exam-d`, holding that form's live
 * items in form order, so an untaken form's text never loads outside its own mock. Pool items in no form never ship.
 * Errors name ids only, because exam-pool text must never reach tool output.
 */
function formChunks(
  ctx: Context,
  notesById: ReadonlyMap<string, Note>,
  emit: (stem: string, data: unknown) => string,
): { forms: ExamFormMeta[]; items: Record<string, ExamItemRef> } {
  const forms: ExamFormMeta[] = [];
  const items: Record<string, ExamItemRef> = {};
  if (ctx.forms === null) return { forms, items };
  const pool = new Map(
    ctx.questions.filter(({ q }) => q.pool === 'exam').map((entry) => [entry.q.id, entry] as const),
  );
  for (const form of ctx.forms.forms) {
    const questions: QuestionView[] = [];
    for (const qid of form.qids) {
      const entry = pool.get(qid);
      if (entry === undefined) throw new Error(`form ${form.id} lists ${qid}, which is not in ${POOL_DIR}/`);
      if (entry.q.status !== 'live') continue;
      if (entry.q.type === 'match') throw new Error(`${qid} in form ${form.id} is a match item`);
      const taken = items[qid];
      if (taken !== undefined) throw new Error(`${qid} sits in forms ${taken.form} and ${form.id}`);
      const note = notesById.get(entry.noteId);
      if (note === undefined) throw new Error(`${entry.path}: ${entry.noteId} is not a note`);
      questions.push(questionView(entry, note));
      items[qid] = {
        version: entry.q.version,
        concept: entry.q.concept,
        task: entry.q.task,
        form: form.id,
        type: entry.q.type,
      };
    }
    const chunk: ExamChunk = { form: form.id, questions };
    forms.push({
      id: form.id,
      chunk: emit(`exam-${form.id.toLowerCase()}`, chunk),
      frozen: form.frozen,
      size: questions.length,
    });
  }
  return { forms, items };
}
```

In `buildContent`, replace the lines from `const searchChunk: SearchChunk = { entries: search };` to the end of `const body = { ... };` with:

```ts
  const searchChunk: SearchChunk = { entries: search };
  const domains: DomainView[] = (ctx.blueprint?.domains ?? []).map(({ id, name, weight }) => ({
    id,
    name,
    weight,
  }));
  const tasks: TaskView[] = (ctx.blueprint?.tasks ?? []).map(({ id, text, short }) => ({
    id,
    domain: id.charAt(0),
    text,
    short,
  }));
  const ranks: RankView[] = (ctx.ranks ?? []).map((rank) => {
    const requires: Record<string, number | boolean> = {};
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(rank.requires)) if (value !== undefined) requires[key] = value;
    return { id: rank.id, name: rank.name, short: rank.short, requires, facts: [...(rank.facts ?? [])] };
  });
  const badges: BadgeView[] = (ctx.badges ?? []).map((badge) => ({
    id: badge.id,
    name: badge.name,
    rule: badge.rule,
    arrives: badge.arrives ?? null,
  }));
  const body = {
    modules,
    concepts,
    glossary,
    missions,
    questionVersions,
    search: emit('search', searchChunk),
    blueprint: { domains, tasks },
    ranks,
    badges,
    exam: formChunks(ctx, notesById, emit),
  };
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Load forms in the app and know their items in replay**

In `src/app/content.ts`, add `ExamChunk` to the type import from `../../schemas/chunks.ts`, add this signal just above the `searchEntries` signal:

```ts
/** Mock form chunks loaded so far, by form id (`A` to `D`). */
export const examForms = signal<ReadonlyMap<string, ExamChunk>>(new Map());
```

and append this at the end of the file:

```ts
const examLoads = new Map<string, Promise<ExamChunk | null>>();

/**
 * Loads one mock form's chunk at most once (spec 4.3); only its mock, and once the form is taken the learn screens
 * and results, ask for it. It resolves to the chunk, or to null for an unknown form or a failed fetch, which a later
 * call retries; it never sets `loadError`, so a mock start that cannot load its form records nothing and says so.
 */
export function ensureExamForm(id: string): Promise<ExamChunk | null> {
  const loaded = examForms.value.get(id);
  if (loaded !== undefined) return Promise.resolve(loaded);
  const meta = index.value?.exam.forms.find((f) => f.id === id);
  if (meta === undefined) return Promise.resolve(null);
  const pending = examLoads.get(id);
  if (pending !== undefined) return pending;
  const load = fetch(meta.chunk)
    .then((response) => {
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
      return response.json() as Promise<ExamChunk>;
    })
    .then((chunk) => {
      examForms.value = new Map(examForms.value).set(id, chunk);
      return chunk;
    })
    .catch(() => null)
    .finally(() => examLoads.delete(id));
  examLoads.set(id, load);
  return load;
}
```

```ts
});
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Budget the exam and lazy chunks**

Replace `scripts/check-budgets.ts` with:

```ts
/**
 * Fails the build when the initial JavaScript, a lazy JavaScript chunk, a module or exam-form content chunk or the
 * service worker exceeds its gzip budget (spec 1.1), or when the worker does not precache the page, every module
 * chunk and every exam-form chunk (spec 1.4), so a mock works offline on a phone.
 */
import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { gzipSync } from 'node:zlib';
import { SW_PLACEHOLDER } from './lib/sw-manifest.ts';

const KB = 1024;
const INITIAL_JS_BUDGET = 50 * KB;
const CHUNK_BUDGET = 120 * KB;
const SW_BUDGET = 4 * KB;
const MODULE_CHUNK = /^m\d{2}\.[0-9a-f]{8}\.json$/;
const EXAM_CHUNK = /^exam-[a-d]\.[0-9a-f]{8}\.json$/;

const dist = resolve(import.meta.dirname, '..', 'dist');
const gzipped = (file: string): number => gzipSync(readFileSync(join(dist, file)), { level: 9 }).length;
const kb = (bytes: number): string => `${(bytes / KB).toFixed(1)} KB`;
const breaches: string[] = [];

const html = readFileSync(join(dist, 'index.html'), 'utf8');
const scripts = [
  ...new Set([...html.matchAll(/(?:src|href)="\/?(assets\/[^"]+\.js)"/g)].map((m) => m[1] ?? '')),
];
const initialJs = scripts.reduce((total, file) => total + gzipped(file), 0);
if (scripts.length === 0) breaches.push('dist/index.html references no script under assets/');
if (initialJs > INITIAL_JS_BUDGET) {
  breaches.push(`initial JS is ${kb(initialJs)} gzipped, over ${kb(INITIAL_JS_BUDGET)}`);
}

/** The largest of `files` by gzip size, each checked against the chunk budget. */
function budgeted(files: readonly string[]): { name: string; size: number } {
  let largest = { name: '-', size: 0 };
  for (const file of files) {
    const size = gzipped(file);
    if (size > largest.size) largest = { name: file, size };
    if (size > CHUNK_BUDGET) breaches.push(`${file} is ${kb(size)} gzipped, over ${kb(CHUNK_BUDGET)}`);
  }
  return largest;
}

const lazyJs = readdirSync(join(dist, 'assets'))
  .map((name) => `assets/${name}`)
  .filter((file) => file.endsWith('.js') && !scripts.includes(file))
  .sort();
const largestLazy = budgeted(lazyJs);

const contentFiles = readdirSync(join(dist, 'content')).sort();
const chunks = contentFiles.filter((name) => MODULE_CHUNK.test(name)).map((name) => `content/${name}`);
if (chunks.length !== 13) breaches.push(`dist/content holds ${chunks.length} module chunks, not 13`);
const largest = budgeted(chunks);
const examChunks = contentFiles.filter((name) => EXAM_CHUNK.test(name)).map((name) => `content/${name}`);
if (examChunks.length !== 0 && examChunks.length !== 4) {
  breaches.push(`dist/content holds ${examChunks.length} exam chunks, not 0 or 4`);
}
const largestExam = budgeted(examChunks);

let worker = 0;
if (existsSync(join(dist, 'sw.js'))) {
  const code = readFileSync(join(dist, 'sw.js'), 'utf8');
  worker = gzipped('sw.js');
  if (code.includes(SW_PLACEHOLDER)) breaches.push('dist/sw.js still holds the manifest placeholder');
  for (const file of ['index.html', ...chunks, ...examChunks]) {
    if (!code.includes(`"/${file}"`)) breaches.push(`dist/sw.js does not precache /${file}`);
  }
  if (worker > SW_BUDGET) breaches.push(`sw.js is ${kb(worker)} gzipped, over ${kb(SW_BUDGET)}`);
} else {
  breaches.push('dist/sw.js is missing');
}

console.log(
  `budgets: initial JS ${kb(initialJs)} of ${kb(INITIAL_JS_BUDGET)} in ${scripts.length} script(s); ` +
    `largest lazy JS ${largestLazy.name} ${kb(largestLazy.size)} of ${kb(CHUNK_BUDGET)} across ${lazyJs.length} chunks; ` +
    `largest module chunk ${largest.name} ${kb(largest.size)} of ${kb(CHUNK_BUDGET)} across ${chunks.length} chunks; ` +
    `largest exam chunk ${largestExam.name} ${kb(largestExam.size)} of ${kb(CHUNK_BUDGET)} across ${examChunks.length} chunks; ` +
    `sw.js ${kb(worker)} of ${kb(SW_BUDGET)}`,
);
for (const breach of breaches) console.error(`budget breach: ${breach}`);
process.exit(breaches.length > 0 ? 1 : 0);
```

## Task 16: Routes, exam keys, kv keys and Help rows

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/exam.ts`, `src/app/screens/Lazy.tsx`, `src/app/screens/Pending.tsx`, `tests/unit/exam.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/app/router.ts`, `src/app/nav.ts`, `src/app/keys.ts`, `src/app/keyboard.ts`, `src/app/screens/Screen.tsx`, `src/app/screens/Help.tsx`, `src/store/kv.ts`, `tests/unit/router.test.ts`, `tests/unit/nav.test.ts`, `tests/unit/keys.test.ts`, `tests/unit/migrations.test.ts`, `tests/unit/backup.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/reader.spec.ts` (the Help test; Task 23 later appends to its `SCREENS`)
- Test: `tests/unit/router.test.ts`, `tests/unit/nav.test.ts`, `tests/unit/keys.test.ts`, `tests/unit/exam.test.ts`, `tests/unit/migrations.test.ts`, `tests/unit/backup.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/reader.spec.ts`

Design notes:
- The routes follow spec 7.3's runner shape `#/s/<mode>/<scope>` and results `#/r/<sid>`; a mock route is lower case (`#/s/mock/a`) while session events store the form in upper case (Decisions).
- `#/s/focus/<scope>` accepts only the 19 blueprint tasks (1.1 to 1.4, 2.1 to 2.4, 3.1 to 3.8, 4.1 to 4.3) and the modules `m01` to `m13`, and `#/r/<sid>` only a ULID, so a typo lands on Not found.
- During an exam the global keys keep only `?` and Escape, and Escape only closes an open sheet (the deliberate deviation from spec 7.4 in Decisions); `keyCommand` returns the new `close-sheet` command for it, so closing Help never depends on the dialog's own cancel handling.
- `KeyContext.exam` is computed here from the route (`boss` or `mock`); Task 17 switches it to the `exam` signal, which is set only while an exam is open.
- `examKey` reuses `runnerKey` for the digits, so `1` to `6` read the same in both shells; arrows, `f`, `g` and `Ctrl+Enter` are the exam's own, and nothing fires while typing, while the confirmation is open, or with Alt.
- `formatClock` rounds up to the whole second, so `0:00` shows only once the time is up.
- `examBarText` is the focus bar's exact text; Task 18 renders the bar so that its `textContent` equals it.
- The exam screens must stay out of the 38.8 KB initial bundle, so `Screen.tsx` loads them through `lazyScreen`, a small `import()` wrapper in `Lazy.tsx`; until Tasks 18, 19 and 21 land, every new route shows the lazy `Pending` placeholder.
- After Task 19 only the focus route still uses `Pending`, so Task 21, when it routes `focus` to the runner, must also delete the `FocusScreen` constant and its comment line from `Screen.tsx` and delete `src/app/screens/Pending.tsx`; an unused `FocusScreen` makes Biome warn `lint/correctness/noUnusedVariables`.
- `rankSeen` and `examSession` are plain kv keys, so a backup carries them and `mergeKv` keeps the newer `updatedAt` per key with no schema change (spec 8.1).
- Covers P3-109, P3-115 (the kv key), P3-145, P3-146 (the keys), P3-147, P3-150 (the kv key) and P3-169 (the kv merge).

```ts
  const MOCK: KeyContext['route'] = { kind: 'mock', form: 'a' };
  const exam = (more: Partial<KeyContext> = {}): KeyContext => ctx({ route: MOCK, exam: true, ...more });

    expect(keyCommand(key('k', { meta: true }), exam())).toBeNull();
    expect(keyCommand(key('K', { ctrl: true }), exam())).toBeNull();
```

```ts
import { type ExamKeyState, examBarText, examKey, formatClock } from '../../src/app/exam.ts';
import type { RunnerKeyInput } from '../../src/app/runner-keys.ts';

const key = (k: string, more: Partial<RunnerKeyInput> = {}): RunnerKeyInput => ({

  typing: false,
  control: false,

  ...more,
});

});

describe('examKey (spec 7.4)', () => {

    ['1', {}, { kind: 'choose', n: 1 }],
    ['4', {}, { kind: 'choose', n: 4 }],

    ['G', {}, { kind: 'grid' }],

    expect(examKey(key('f', { meta: true }), state())).toBeNull();
    expect(examKey(key('1', { alt: true }), state())).toBeNull();

    expect(examKey(key('Enter', { ctrl: true, typing: true }), state())).toBeNull();
```

Replace `src/app/router.ts` with:

```ts
/** Hash routes (spec 7.3); pure, so the tests run without a browser. */
export type Route =
  | { kind: 'home' }
  | { kind: 'module'; module: number }
  | { kind: 'lesson'; module: number; slug: string; anchor: string | null }
  | { kind: 'missions' }
  | { kind: 'mission'; id: string }
  | { kind: 'check'; module: number; slug: string }
  | { kind: 'quiz'; module: number }
  | { kind: 'review' }
  | { kind: 'boss'; module: number }
  | { kind: 'mocks' }
  | { kind: 'mock'; form: MockRoute }
  | { kind: 'focus'; scope: string }
  | { kind: 'results'; sid: string }
  | { kind: 'not-found'; hash: string };

/** A mock's route segment: a form in lower case, or the remix. */
export type MockRoute = 'a' | 'b' | 'c' | 'd' | 'remix';

const MODULE = '(0[1-9]|1[0-3])';
const SLUG = '([a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*)';
const MODULE_PATH = new RegExp(`^/m/${MODULE}$`);
const LESSON_PATH = new RegExp(`^/m/${MODULE}/l/${SLUG}(?:/([a-z0-9-]+))?$`);
const MISSION_PATH = /^\/missions\/(mission-\d{2})$/;
const CHECK_PATH = new RegExp(`^/s/check/m${MODULE}/${SLUG}$`);
const QUIZ_PATH = new RegExp(`^/s/quiz/m${MODULE}$`);
const BOSS_PATH = new RegExp(`^/s/boss/m${MODULE}$`);
const MOCK_PATH = /^\/s\/mock\/(a|b|c|d|remix)$/;
/** A blueprint task (1.1 to 4.3) or a module (`m01` to `m13`). */
const FOCUS_PATH = new RegExp(`^/s/focus/(1\\.[1-4]|2\\.[1-4]|3\\.[1-8]|4\\.[1-3]|m${MODULE})$`);
const RESULTS_PATH = /^\/r\/([0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26})$/;

export function pad2(n: number): string {
  return String(n).padStart(2, '0');
}

/** Reads `location.hash`; anything unknown becomes a not-found route that keeps the hash. */
export function parseHash(hash: string): Route {
  const path = hash.startsWith('#') ? hash.slice(1) : hash;
  if (path === '' || path === '/') return { kind: 'home' };
  if (path === '/missions') return { kind: 'missions' };
  if (path === '/s/review') return { kind: 'review' };
  if (path === '/s/mock') return { kind: 'mocks' };
  const module = MODULE_PATH.exec(path);
  if (module) return { kind: 'module', module: Number(module[1]) };
  const lesson = LESSON_PATH.exec(path);
  if (lesson) {
    return { kind: 'lesson', module: Number(lesson[1]), slug: lesson[2] ?? '', anchor: lesson[3] ?? null };
  }
  const mission = MISSION_PATH.exec(path);
  if (mission) return { kind: 'mission', id: mission[1] ?? '' };
  const check = CHECK_PATH.exec(path);
  if (check) return { kind: 'check', module: Number(check[1]), slug: check[2] ?? '' };
  const quiz = QUIZ_PATH.exec(path);
  if (quiz) return { kind: 'quiz', module: Number(quiz[1]) };
  const boss = BOSS_PATH.exec(path);
  if (boss) return { kind: 'boss', module: Number(boss[1]) };
  const mock = MOCK_PATH.exec(path);
  if (mock) return { kind: 'mock', form: (mock[1] ?? 'a') as MockRoute };
  const focus = FOCUS_PATH.exec(path);
  if (focus) return { kind: 'focus', scope: focus[1] ?? '' };
  const results = RESULTS_PATH.exec(path);
  if (results) return { kind: 'results', sid: results[1] ?? '' };
  return { kind: 'not-found', hash };
}

export function toHash(route: Route): string {
  switch (route.kind) {
    case 'home':
      return '#/';
    case 'module':
      return `#/m/${pad2(route.module)}`;
    case 'lesson':
      return `#/m/${pad2(route.module)}/l/${route.slug}${route.anchor === null ? '' : `/${route.anchor}`}`;
    case 'missions':
      return '#/missions';
    case 'mission':
      return `#/missions/${route.id}`;
    case 'check':
      return `#/s/check/m${pad2(route.module)}/${route.slug}`;
    case 'quiz':
      return `#/s/quiz/m${pad2(route.module)}`;
    case 'review':
      return '#/s/review';
    case 'boss':
      return `#/s/boss/m${pad2(route.module)}`;
    case 'mocks':
      return '#/s/mock';
    case 'mock':
      return `#/s/mock/${route.form}`;
    case 'focus':
      return `#/s/focus/${route.scope}`;
    case 'results':
      return `#/r/${route.sid}`;
    case 'not-found':
      return route.hash;
  }
}
```

In `src/app/nav.ts`, replace `up` with:

```ts
/** Where Escape goes: one level up, or null at Home. */
export function up(route: Route): Route | null {
  switch (route.kind) {
    case 'lesson':
      return { kind: 'module', module: route.module };
    case 'module':
    case 'missions':
    case 'review':
    case 'not-found':
      return { kind: 'home' };
    case 'mission':
      return { kind: 'missions' };
    case 'check':
      return { kind: 'lesson', module: route.module, slug: route.slug, anchor: null };
    case 'quiz':
    case 'boss':
      return { kind: 'module', module: route.module };
    case 'focus':
      return route.scope.startsWith('m')
        ? { kind: 'module', module: Number(route.scope.slice(1)) }
        : { kind: 'home' };
    case 'mocks':
    case 'results':
      return { kind: 'home' };
    case 'mock':
      return { kind: 'mocks' };
    case 'home':
      return null;
  }
}
```

In `src/app/keys.ts`, replace `KeyContext` and `KeyCommand` with:

```ts
export interface KeyContext {
  route: Route;
  modules: readonly ModuleMeta[];
  overlayOpen: boolean;
  termOpen: boolean;
  /** When `g` was pressed, if it was the previous key. */
  gAt: number | null;
  /** True while a boss or mock is open: search, the `g` sequences, `j` and `k` are off, and Escape never leaves. */
  exam: boolean;
}

export type KeyCommand =
  | { kind: 'palette' }
  | { kind: 'help' }
  | { kind: 'progress' }
  | { kind: 'close-term' }
  /** Close the open sheet; only an exam asks for this, because Escape there never goes up a level. */
  | { kind: 'close-sheet' }
  | { kind: 'g'; at: number }
  | { kind: 'navigate'; hash: string }
  /** Follow the Next button (`Enter` on Home). */
  | { kind: 'next' }
  /** Focus the next or previous `[data-nav-item]` (`j` and `k` on Home and module pages). */
  | { kind: 'move'; delta: 1 | -1 };
```

Add `examCommand` above `keyCommand`, and replace `keyCommand` with this version, which calls it right after it reads the key:

```ts
/**
 * The global keys during an exam: only `?` opens Help, and Escape only closes an open sheet, because the note
 * search would let a mock peek at the notes and Escape must never leave a timed exam (spec 7.4, deliberate deviation).
 */
function examCommand(input: KeyInput, ctx: KeyContext, key: string): KeyCommand | null {
  if (input.meta || input.ctrl || input.alt) return null;
  if (key === 'Escape') return ctx.overlayOpen ? { kind: 'close-sheet' } : null;
  if (ctx.overlayOpen || input.typing) return null;
  return key === '?' ? { kind: 'help' } : null;
}

/** The global shortcuts of spec 7.4 built so far; null means the key is not ours. */
export function keyCommand(input: KeyInput, ctx: KeyContext): KeyCommand | null {
  const key = input.key.length === 1 ? input.key.toLowerCase() : input.key;
  if (ctx.exam) return examCommand(input, ctx, key);
  if ((input.meta || input.ctrl) && !input.alt && key === 'k') return { kind: 'palette' };
  if (input.meta || input.ctrl || input.alt || ctx.overlayOpen) return null;
  if (key === 'Escape' && ctx.termOpen) return { kind: 'close-term' };
  if (input.typing) return null;
  switch (key) {
    case '/':
      return { kind: 'palette' };
    case '?':
      return { kind: 'help' };
    case 'g':
      return { kind: 'g', at: input.at };
    case 'h':
      return gPending(input, ctx) ? { kind: 'navigate', hash: '#/' } : null;
    case 'p':
      return gPending(input, ctx) ? { kind: 'progress' } : null;
    case 'r':
      return gPending(input, ctx) ? { kind: 'navigate', hash: toHash({ kind: 'review' }) } : null;
    case 'Enter':
      return ctx.route.kind === 'home' && !input.control ? { kind: 'next' } : null;
    case 'j':
    case 'k':
      return ctx.route.kind === 'home' || ctx.route.kind === 'module'
        ? { kind: 'move', delta: key === 'j' ? 1 : -1 }
        : null;
    case 'c':
      return ctx.route.kind === 'lesson'
        ? {
            kind: 'navigate',
            hash: toHash({ kind: 'check', module: ctx.route.module, slug: ctx.route.slug }),
          }
        : null;
    case 'Escape': {
      const parent = up(ctx.route);
      return parent === null ? null : { kind: 'navigate', hash: toHash(parent) };
    }
    case '[':
    case ']': {
      if (ctx.route.kind !== 'lesson') return null;
      const { prev, next } = neighbors(ctx.modules, ctx.route.module, ctx.route.slug);
      const target = key === '[' ? prev : next;
      return target === null
        ? null
        : {
            kind: 'navigate',
            hash: toHash({ kind: 'lesson', module: target.module, slug: target.slug, anchor: null }),
          };
    }
    default:
      return null;
  }
}
```

In `src/app/keyboard.ts`, replace `useKeyboard` with this version, which passes `exam` and handles `close-sheet`:

```ts
/** The one window keydown listener for the global shortcuts (spec 7.4); dialogs handle their own keys. */
export function useKeyboard(): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    let gAt: number | null = null;
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent): void => {
      const step = keyStep(
        {
          key: event.key,
          meta: event.metaKey,
          ctrl: event.ctrlKey,
          alt: event.altKey,
          typing: isTyping(event.target),
          control: isControl(event.target),
          at: event.timeStamp,
        },
        {
          route: route.value,
          modules: index.value?.modules ?? [],
          overlayOpen: overlay.value !== null,
          termOpen: openTerm.value !== null,
          gAt,
          exam: route.value.kind === 'boss' || route.value.kind === 'mock',
        },
      );
      gAt = step.gAt;
      const command = step.command;
      if (command === null) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      switch (command.kind) {
        case 'palette':
          overlay.value = 'palette';
          break;
        case 'help':
          overlay.value = 'help';
          break;
        case 'progress':
          overlay.value = 'progress';
          break;
        case 'close-term':
          closeTerm();
          break;
        case 'close-sheet':
          overlay.value = null;
          break;
        case 'navigate':
          navigate(command.hash);
          break;
        case 'next':
          followNext();
          break;
        case 'move':
          moveFocus(command.delta);
          break;
        case 'g':
          break;
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, []);
}
```

Create `src/app/exam.ts`:

```ts
/** The exam shell's keys and its focus bar text (spec 7.2 and 7.4), pure so the tests run without a browser. */
import { type RunnerKeyInput, runnerKey } from './runner-keys.ts';

export type ExamCommand =
  | { kind: 'choose'; n: number }
  | { kind: 'prev' }
  | { kind: 'next' }
  | { kind: 'flag' }
  | { kind: 'grid' }
  | { kind: 'submit' };

export interface ExamKeyState {
  optionCount: number;
  /** True while the submit confirmation is open; its own buttons and Escape answer then. */
  confirmOpen: boolean;
  /** True while the question grid covers the question. */
  gridOpen: boolean;
}

/** One keydown in the exam shell; null means the key is not the exam's. */
export function examKey(input: RunnerKeyInput, state: ExamKeyState): ExamCommand | null {
  if (input.typing || input.alt || state.confirmOpen) return null;
  const key = input.key.length === 1 ? input.key.toLowerCase() : input.key;
  if (input.ctrl || input.meta) return key === 'Enter' ? { kind: 'submit' } : null;
  if (key === 'g') return { kind: 'grid' };
  if (state.gridOpen) return null;
  if (key === 'ArrowLeft') return { kind: 'prev' };
  if (key === 'ArrowRight') return { kind: 'next' };
  if (key === 'f') return { kind: 'flag' };
  // The digits read exactly as the runner reads them, for one question that is never answered yet.
  const command = runnerKey(input, {
    type: 'single',
    answered: false,
    optionCount: state.optionCount,
    leftCount: 0,
    reportOpen: false,
  });
  return command?.kind === 'choose' ? { kind: 'choose', n: command.n } : null;
}

/** Time left as `m:ss` with unpadded minutes, rounded up to the second; `0:00` once the time is up. */
export function formatClock(ms: number): string {
  if (ms <= 0) return '0:00';
  const seconds = Math.ceil(ms / 1000);
  return `${Math.floor(seconds / 60)}:${String(seconds % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

/** The gap between the focus bar's parts, as the spec 7.2 mockup writes it. */
export const BAR_GAP = '   ';

/** The focus bar's text: `Mock A   23/65   61:12   Flag (f)   Grid (g)`. */
export function examBarText(args: {
  label: string;
  position: number;
  total: number;
  leftMs: number;
}): string {
  return [
    args.label,
    `${args.position}/${args.total}`,
    formatClock(args.leftMs),
    'Flag (f)',
    'Grid (g)',
  ].join(BAR_GAP);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Route the new screens through lazy placeholders**

Create `src/app/screens/Lazy.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ComponentType } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'preact/hooks';

/** The line a lazy screen shows when its code cannot load, which offline means the service worker lacks it. */
export const LAZY_ERROR = 'This screen could not load. Check your connection and reload.';

/**
 * A screen whose code loads on first use through `import()`, so exam code stays out of the initial bundle
 * (the 50 KB budget of spec 2.10); it shows `Loading` until then.
 */
export function lazyScreen<P extends object>(load: () => Promise<ComponentType<P>>): ComponentType<P> {
  let loaded: ComponentType<P> | null = null;
  let pending: Promise<ComponentType<P>> | null = null;
  return function LazyScreen(props: P) {
    const [, setReady] = useState(0);
    const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
    useEffect(() => {
      if (loaded !== null) return;
      pending ??= load();
      let live = true;
      pending.then(
        (component) => {
          loaded = component;
          if (live) setReady((n) => n + 1);
        },
        () => {
          pending = null;
          if (live) setFailed(true);
        },
      );
      return () => {
        live = false;
      };
    }, []);
    if (loaded !== null) {
      const Loaded = loaded;
      return <Loaded {...props} />;
    }
    return failed ? <p role="alert">{LAZY_ERROR}</p> : <p class="muted">Loading</p>;
  };
}
```

```tsx
import type { ContentIndex } from '../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { pad2, type Route } from '../router.ts';
import { Home } from './Home.tsx';
import { lazyScreen } from './Lazy.tsx';
import { LessonPage } from './LessonPage.tsx';
import { MissionPage } from './MissionPage.tsx';
import { MissionsPage } from './MissionsPage.tsx';
import { ModulePage } from './ModulePage.tsx';
import { NotFound } from './NotFound.tsx';
import { Runner } from './Runner.tsx';

// Exam screens load on first use; Tasks 18, 19 and 21 replace these placeholders with the real screens.
const BossScreen = lazyScreen(() => import('./Pending.tsx').then((m) => m.Pending));
const MockScreen = lazyScreen(() => import('./Pending.tsx').then((m) => m.Pending));
const MocksScreen = lazyScreen(() => import('./Pending.tsx').then((m) => m.Pending));
const ResultsScreen = lazyScreen(() => import('./Pending.tsx').then((m) => m.Pending));
const FocusScreen = lazyScreen(() => import('./Pending.tsx').then((m) => m.Pending));

/** The screen for a route; the content index is always loaded by the time this renders. */
export function Screen({ route, idx }: { route: Route; idx: ContentIndex }) {
  switch (route.kind) {
    case 'home':
      return <Home idx={idx} />;
    case 'module':
      return <ModulePage idx={idx} module={route.module} />;
    case 'lesson':
      return <LessonPage idx={idx} module={route.module} slug={route.slug} anchor={route.anchor} />;
    case 'missions':
      return <MissionsPage idx={idx} />;
    case 'mission':
      return <MissionPage idx={idx} id={route.id} />;
    // Keyed by the route object, which every arrival replaces, so "Another quiz" on the quiz route starts afresh.
    case 'check':
      return <Runner key={route} idx={idx} mode="check" scope={`m${pad2(route.module)}/${route.slug}`} />;
    case 'quiz':
      return <Runner key={route} idx={idx} mode="quiz" scope={`m${pad2(route.module)}`} />;
    case 'review':
      return <Runner key={route} idx={idx} mode="review" scope="review" />;
    case 'boss':
      return <BossScreen key={route} title={`Boss: Module ${route.module}`} />;
    case 'mocks':
      return <MocksScreen title="Mock exams" />;
    case 'mock':
      return <MockScreen key={route} title={`Mock ${route.form.toUpperCase()}`} />;
    case 'focus':
      return <FocusScreen key={route} title="Focus quiz" />;
    case 'results':
      return <ResultsScreen key={route} title="Results" />;
    case 'not-found':
      return <NotFound hash={route.hash} />;
  }
}
```

## Task 17: App signals for exams, readiness, ranks, badges and extras

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/learn-pool.ts`, `tests/unit/learn-pool.test.ts`, `tests/unit/watch-ranks.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/app/memory.ts`, `src/app/home.ts`, `src/app/state.ts`, `src/app/keyboard.ts`, `src/app/screens/Home.tsx`, `tests/unit/home.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/home.test.ts`, `tests/unit/learn-pool.test.ts`, `tests/unit/watch-ranks.test.ts`

Design notes:
- This task needs Tasks 8 to 16 on `master`: it reads `takenForms` (Task 8), `MemoryContent.examTasks` and `MemoryState.badges` (Tasks 9 and 10), `learnPool` and `bossStatus` (Task 11), `firstAttempts` (Task 12), `readiness` (Task 13), `rankFacts`, `shownRank`, `rankToRecord` and `extras` (Task 14), and `examForms`, `ensureExamForm` and `index.exam` (Task 15).
- `memoryContentOf` now fills `examTasks` from `index.exam.items` and adds each exam item's version to `questionVersions`, so Task 9's stale rule also catches an exam answer to an older item version.
- `learnPoolNow` is the one door from the exam pool into learn screens: it asks for every taken form's chunk and adds the loaded items of taken forms only; a chunk still on its way joins on the next call, and a boss start (Task 18) awaits the chunks first.
- `practicePoolOf` is the pure twin of the runner's private `poolOf` (which Task 11 gave `type` and `module`), so the boss start in Task 18 builds the same pool from module chunks; Task 21 may switch `poolOf` to it.
- `rankUp` treats a missing `rankSeen` as Basic, so a fresh log never shows a promotion line, and compares positions in `ranks.yaml` order.
- `watchRanks` is an effect that records one `rank` event per promotion once the log has loaded (spec 3.4); it guards against a second write while the first is in flight, and Task 18 starts it from `App`.
- `watch-ranks.test.ts` needs Task 14's real `rankToRecord`: a rank with no requirement above Basic is due at once, so the first replay after `storeReady` records it, and a later replay records nothing more.
- `nextLabel` takes the moment, because a resumed boss or mock shows the whole minutes left to its deadline (`Resume: Mock exam A (61 min left)`, rounded up, never below 0) instead of the questions left; its `minutes` is the same number.
- The `exam` signal in `state.ts` is set only while an exam shell is on screen (Task 18), so the global keys (Task 16) switch on it rather than on the route: a mock's start screen still lets Escape go up to the mock list.
- Covers P3-063 (the boss-passed feed), P3-079 (the learn-pool helper), P3-111, P3-135 and P3-165.
