```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { ContentIndex, ExamChunk, ExamItemRef, QuestionView } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { examForms, index } from '../../src/app/content.ts';
import { examPoolOf, learnPoolNow } from '../../src/app/learn-pool.ts';
import { events } from '../../src/app/progress.ts';
import { derive, takenForms } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import { learnPool, type PoolQuestion } from '../../src/engine/select.ts';
import { eventsOf, SID } from '../fixtures/events.ts';
import { emptyIndexFields } from '../fixtures/index.ts';

/** A made-up bakery exam item; only its id, note and type matter here. */
const view = (id: string, note: string): QuestionView => ({
  id,
  version: 1,
  type: 'single',
  concept: 'sourdough-starter',
  note,
  task: '1.1',
  level: 1,
  stem: 'Which jar holds the starter?',
  select: null,
  options: [],
  pairs: [],
  takeaway: 'The starter lives in the jar.',
  read: { route: '#/m/01/l/starters', text: 'The starter lives in the jar.' },
});
const ref = (form: string): ExamItemRef => ({
  version: 1,
  concept: 'sourdough-starter',
  task: '1.1',
  form,
  type: 'single',
});
const ITEMS: Record<string, ExamItemRef> = { 'x-aaaaa1': ref('A'), 'x-bbbbb1': ref('B') };
const FORMS = new Map<string, ExamChunk>([
  ['A', { form: 'A', questions: [view('x-aaaaa1', 'm01/starters')] }],
  ['B', { form: 'B', questions: [view('x-bbbbb1', 'm02/ovens')] }],
]);
const PRACTICE: PoolQuestion[] = [
  { id: 'q-ppppp1', concept: 'sourdough-starter', note: 'm01/starters', type: 'single', module: 1 },
];
const MOCK_A_STARTED = eventsOf({
  t: 'session',
  sid: SID,
  mode: 'mock',
  phase: 'start',
  seed: 7,
  form: 'A',
  qids: ['x-aaaaa1'],
  deadline: '2026-10-10T09:30:00.000Z',
});
const IDX: ContentIndex = {
  contentVersion: 'test',
  modules: [],
  concepts: [],
  glossary: [],
  missions: [],
  questionVersions: { 'q-ppppp1': 1 },
  search: 'content/search.json',
  ...emptyIndexFields(),
  exam: {
    forms: [
      { id: 'A', chunk: 'content/exam-a.0000aaaa.json', frozen: true, size: 1 },
      { id: 'B', chunk: 'content/exam-b.0000bbbb.json', frozen: true, size: 1 },
    ],
    items: ITEMS,
  },
};
const ids = (pool: readonly PoolQuestion[]): string[] => pool.map((q) => q.id).sort();

afterEach(() => {
  events.value = [];
  examForms.value = new Map();
  index.value = null;
});

describe('examPoolOf', () => {
  it('tags each loaded form item with its form and module, and skips items the index does not list', () => {
    expect(examPoolOf(FORMS, ITEMS)).toEqual([
      {
        id: 'x-aaaaa1',
        concept: 'sourdough-starter',
        note: 'm01/starters',
        type: 'single',
        module: 1,
        form: 'A',
      },
      {
        id: 'x-bbbbb1',
        concept: 'sourdough-starter',
        note: 'm02/ovens',
        type: 'single',
        module: 2,
        form: 'B',
      },
    ]);
    expect(examPoolOf(FORMS, { 'x-aaaaa1': ref('A') }).map((q) => q.id)).toEqual(['x-aaaaa1']);
  });
});

describe('the learn pool (spec 4.3)', () => {
  it('a boss pool built after Mock A starts can hold a Form A item', () => {
    const taken = takenForms(derive(MOCK_A_STARTED, null));
    expect(ids(learnPool(PRACTICE, examPoolOf(FORMS, ITEMS), taken))).toEqual(['q-ppppp1', 'x-aaaaa1']);
  });

  it('holds no item of an untaken form', () => {
    expect(ids(learnPool(PRACTICE, examPoolOf(FORMS, ITEMS), new Set()))).toEqual(['q-ppppp1']);
    const taken = takenForms(derive(MOCK_A_STARTED, null));
    expect(ids(learnPool(PRACTICE, examPoolOf(FORMS, ITEMS), taken))).not.toContain('x-bbbbb1');
  });

  it('learnPoolNow adds the loaded items of taken forms only', () => {
    index.value = IDX;
    examForms.value = FORMS;
    expect(ids(learnPoolNow(PRACTICE))).toEqual(['q-ppppp1']);
    events.value = MOCK_A_STARTED;
    expect(ids(learnPoolNow(PRACTICE))).toEqual(['q-ppppp1', 'x-aaaaa1']);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/unit/watch-ranks.test.ts`, the guard of the record side of P3-111:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { ContentIndex, RankView } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { index } from '../../src/app/content.ts';
import { rank, rankUp, watchRanks } from '../../src/app/memory.ts';
import { events, storeReady } from '../../src/app/progress.ts';
import { emptyIndexFields } from '../fixtures/index.ts';

/** Basic, then a made-up rank with no requirement, so the first replay already promotes. */
const RANKS: RankView[] = [
  { id: 'basic', name: 'Basic', short: 'Basic', requires: {}, facts: [] },
  { id: 'apprentice-baker', name: 'Apprentice Baker', short: 'Apprentice', requires: {}, facts: [] },
];
const IDX: ContentIndex = {
  contentVersion: 'test',
  modules: [],
  concepts: [],
  glossary: [],
  missions: [],
  questionVersions: {},
  search: 'content/search.json',
  ...emptyIndexFields(),
  ranks: RANKS,
};
const rankEvents = (): string[] => events.value.flatMap((e) => (e.t === 'rank' ? [e.rank] : []));

let stop: (() => void) | null = null;
afterEach(() => {
  stop?.();
  stop = null;
  events.value = [];
  storeReady.value = false;
  index.value = null;
});

describe('watchRanks (spec 3.4)', () => {
  it('records one rank event per promotion, and only once the log has loaded', async () => {
    index.value = IDX;
    storeReady.value = false;
    stop = watchRanks();
    expect(rankEvents()).toEqual([]);
    storeReady.value = true;
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(rankEvents()).toEqual(['apprentice-baker']));
    events.value = [...events.value];
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 10));
    expect(rankEvents()).toEqual(['apprentice-baker']);
  });

  it('shows no rank and records nothing until the index lists the ranks', async () => {
    storeReady.value = true;
    stop = watchRanks();
    expect(rank.value).toBeNull();
    expect(rankUp.value).toBeNull();
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 10));
    expect(rankEvents()).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

Replace `memoryContentOf`, the version Task 9 left with its doc comment, with:

```ts
/**
 * What the memory replay needs from the index: concepts with their module and tasks, aliases, question versions
 * (practice and exam, so a stale exam answer is caught too), and each exam item's task.
 */
export function memoryContentOf(idx: ContentIndex): MemoryContent {
  const concepts = new Map<string, ConceptInfo>();
  const aliases = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const c of idx.concepts) {
    concepts.set(c.id, {
      module: moduleOf(c.home),
      tested: c.tested,
      confusableWith: c.confusableWith,
      splitFrom: c.splitFrom,
      tasks: c.tasks,
    });
    for (const alias of c.aliases) aliases.set(alias, c.id);
  }
  const questionVersions = new Map(Object.entries(idx.questionVersions));
  const examTasks = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const [qid, item] of Object.entries(idx.exam.items)) {
    questionVersions.set(qid, item.version);
    examTasks.set(qid, item.task);
  }
  return {
    concepts,
    aliases,
    questionVersions,
    examTasks,
    coreLabs: new Map(),
    keptMissions: new Set(idx.missions.filter((m) => m.keep).map((m) => m.id)),
  };
}
```

Replace `resumeOf` with this version, followed by the new `minutesLeft`:

```ts
/** Where a session in progress lives, and what it is called. */
function resumeOf(action: Extract<NextAction, { kind: 'resume' }>, idx: ContentIndex): [string, string] {
  const { mode, scope, form } = action.session;
  if (mode === 'review') return ['Review', toHash({ kind: 'review' })];
  if (mode === 'check') {
    const [module = '', slug = ''] = scope.split('/');
    return [
      `Lesson check: ${lessonTitle(idx, scope)}`,
      toHash({ kind: 'check', module: Number(module.slice(1)), slug }),
    ];
  }
  if (mode === 'mock') {
    const remix = form === null || form === 'remix';
    const route = remix ? 'remix' : (form.toLowerCase() as MockRoute);
    return [remix ? 'Remix mock' : `Mock exam ${form}`, toHash({ kind: 'mock', form: route })];
  }
  if (mode === 'focus') return ['Focus quiz', toHash({ kind: 'focus', scope })];
  const module = Number(scope.slice(1));
  const short = idx.modules.find((m) => m.n === module)?.short ?? '';
  if (mode === 'boss') return [`Boss: ${short}`, toHash({ kind: 'boss', module })];
  return [`Module quiz: ${short}`, toHash({ kind: 'quiz', module })];
}

/** Whole minutes until an exam's deadline, rounded up and never below 0. */
const minutesLeft = (deadline: string, now: Date): number =>
  Math.max(0, Math.ceil((Date.parse(deadline) - now.getTime()) / MINUTE_MS));
```

Replace `nextLabel` with:

```ts
/** The Next button's words, link and time estimate (spec 4.6); `done` has neither link nor time. */
export function nextLabel(
  action: NextAction,
  idx: ContentIndex,
  now: Date,
): { label: string; href: string | null; minutes: number | null } {
  const minutes = nextMinutes(action);
  switch (action.kind) {
    case 'resume': {
      const [what, href] = resumeOf(action, idx);
      const { deadline } = action.session;
      if (deadline !== null) {
        const left = minutesLeft(deadline, now);
        return { label: `Resume: ${what} (${left} min left)`, href, minutes: left };
      }
      return { label: `Resume: ${what} (${action.left} left)`, href, minutes };
    }
    case 'review':
      return {
        label: `Review ${plural(action.count, 'concept', 'concepts')}`,
        href: toHash({ kind: 'review' }),
        minutes,
      };
    case 'continue':
      return { label: `Continue: ${action.lesson.title}`, href: lessonHash(action.lesson), minutes };
    case 'check':
      return {
        label: `Check yourself: Module ${action.module} (${plural(action.concepts, 'concept', 'concepts')})`,
        href: toHash({ kind: 'check', module: action.lesson.module, slug: action.lesson.slug }),
        minutes,
      };
    case 'lesson':
      return {
        label: action.lesson.first
          ? `Start Module ${action.lesson.module}: ${action.lesson.title}`
          : `Next lesson: ${action.lesson.title}`,
        href: lessonHash(action.lesson),
        minutes,
      };
    case 'done':
      return { label: 'Done for today', href: null, minutes: null };
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the learn pool**

Create `src/app/learn-pool.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The one place learn screens get their question pool (spec 4.3): the practice items of the loaded module chunks,
 * plus the items of every form whose mock has started, so an untaken form never reaches a learn-mode selector.
 */
import type { ExamChunk, ExamItemRef, ModuleChunk } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { type ExamPoolQuestion, learnPool, type PoolQuestion } from '../engine/select.ts';
import { ensureExamForm, examForms, index } from './content.ts';
import { taken } from './memory.ts';

/** The module of a note id such as `m03/aws-lambda`. */
const moduleOf = (note: string): number => Number(note.slice(1, 3));

/** The practice pool of some module chunks, with each concept as memory keys it (aliases resolved). */
export function practicePoolOf(
  chunks: readonly ModuleChunk[],
  conceptOf: (concept: string) => string,
): PoolQuestion[] {
  return chunks.flatMap((c) =>
    c.questions.map((q) => ({
      id: q.id,
      concept: conceptOf(q.concept),
      note: q.note,
      type: q.type,
      module: c.module,
    })),
  );
}

/** The exam items of the loaded form chunks, tagged with their form; items the index does not list are left out. */
export function examPoolOf(
  forms: ReadonlyMap<string, ExamChunk>,
  items: Readonly<Record<string, ExamItemRef>>,
): ExamPoolQuestion[] {
  const pool: ExamPoolQuestion[] = [];
  for (const chunk of forms.values()) {
    for (const q of chunk.questions) {
      const item = items[q.id];
      if (item === undefined) continue;
      pool.push({
        id: q.id,
        concept: item.concept,
        note: q.note,
        type: item.type,
        module: moduleOf(q.note),
        form: item.form,
      });
    }
  }
  return pool;
}

/**
 * A learn screen's pool now: asks for every taken form's chunk (a form not loaded yet joins on a later call),
 * then adds the loaded items of taken forms to the practice items.
 */
export function learnPoolNow(practice: readonly PoolQuestion[]): PoolQuestion[] {
  const forms = taken.peek();
  for (const form of forms) void ensureExamForm(form);
  return learnPool(practice, examPoolOf(examForms.peek(), index.peek()?.exam.items ?? {}), forms);
}
```

import { computed, effect } from '@preact/signals';
import type { BadgeEarned, BadgeId } from '../engine/badges.ts';
import { type BossStatus, bossStatus } from '../engine/boss.ts';
import { studyDay, tzOffset } from '../engine/day.ts';
import { moduleMastery } from '../engine/mastery.ts';
import { deriveMemory } from '../engine/memory.ts';
import { firstAttempts } from '../engine/mock.ts';

import {
  type RankDef,
  type RankFacts,
  rankFacts as factsOf,
  rankToRecord,
  shownRank,
} from '../engine/ranks.ts';
import { readiness } from '../engine/readiness.ts';
import { takenForms } from '../engine/replay.ts';
import { streakOf } from '../engine/streak.ts';
import { KV_KEYS } from '../store/kv.ts';
import { now } from './clock.ts';
import { index } from './content.ts';

import { pad2 } from './router.ts';

and append to the end of the file:

```ts
/** The ranks of `content/game/ranks.yaml` in order, Basic first. */
export const ranks = computed<readonly RankDef[]>(() =>
  (index.value?.ranks ?? []).map((r) => ({ ...r, requires: r.requires as RankDef['requires'] })),
);

/** The forms A to D whose mock has started, so their items may join practice (spec 4.3). */
export const taken = computed(() => takenForms(progress.value));

/** Each module's boss: unlocked, ready, passed, cooling down or open (spec 4.2). */
export const bosses = computed<ReadonlyMap<number, BossStatus>>(() => {
  const idx = index.value;
  const masteries = mastery.value;
  const out = new Map<number, BossStatus>();
  if (idx === null || masteries === null) return out;
  const p = progress.value;
  for (const m of idx.modules) {
    out.set(
      m.n,
      bossStatus({
        module: m.n,
        progress: p,
        lessonsRead: m.lessons.every((l) => p.lessonsDone.has(`m${pad2(m.n)}/${l.slug}`)),
        mastery: masteries.get(m.n) ?? 0,
        now: now.value,
      }),
    );
  }
  return out;
});

/** The first submitted attempt of each form A to D. */
export const attempts = computed(() => firstAttempts(progress.value));

/** Readiness at the minute clock (spec 3.9); null until the content index has loaded. */
export const readinessNow = computed(() => {
  const m = memory.value;
  const content = memoryContent.value;
  if (m === null || content === null) return null;
  const at = now.value;
  return readiness({ memory: m, content, attempts: attempts.value, now: at, tz: tzOffset(at) });
});

/** The facts the rank rules read (spec 3.4), counted once by the engine's `rankFacts`. */
export const rankFacts = computed<RankFacts>(() =>
  factsOf({
    progress: progress.value,
    mastery: mastery.value ?? new Map(),
    ready: readinessNow.value?.label === 'Ready',
    today: studyDay(now.value),
  }),
);

/** Modules whose boss has been passed at least once. */
export const bossesPassed = computed(() => rankFacts.value.bossesPassed);

/** Modules whose mastery is 60 or more. */
export const hexesAt60 = computed(() => rankFacts.value.hexesAt60);

/**
 * The rank shown: the highest of the computed and the recorded ranks, so it never drops (spec 3.4); null until the
 * index lists the ranks, because the engine's rank helpers need at least Basic.
 */
export const rank = computed<RankDef | null>(() =>
  ranks.value.length === 0 ? null : shownRank(ranks.value, rankFacts.value, progress.value.ranks),
);

/** The rank whose promotion line was dismissed, from kv. */
export const rankSeen = computed(() => {
  const value = kvRecords.value[KV_KEYS.rankSeen]?.value;
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : null;
});

/** The shown rank while it is above the one last dismissed, else null; Basic never shows a promotion line. */
export const rankUp = computed<RankDef | null>(() => {
  const shown = rank.value;
  if (!storeReady.value || shown === null) return null;
  const list = ranks.value;
  const seen = Math.max(
    0,
    list.findIndex((r) => r.id === rankSeen.value),
  );
  return list.findIndex((r) => r.id === shown.id) > seen ? shown : null;
});

/** The extras line's entries (spec 4.6): a ready boss and a due mock, at most two. */
export const extraItems = computed(() => {
  const at = now.value;
  return extras({ bosses: bosses.value, progress: progress.value, today: studyDay(at), now: at });
});

/** Badges earned, each with the event that earned it. */
export const badges = computed<ReadonlyMap<BadgeId, BadgeEarned>>(() => memory.value?.badges ?? new Map());

/** Records one `rank` event per promotion once the log has loaded (spec 3.4); returns the cleanup. */
export function watchRanks(): () => void {
  const writing = new Set<string>();
  return effect(() => {
    if (!storeReady.value || ranks.value.length === 0) return;
    const due = rankToRecord(ranks.value, rankFacts.value, progress.value.ranks);
    if (due === null || writing.has(due.id)) return;
    writing.add(due.id);
    void record({ t: 'rank', rank: due.id });
  });
}
```

## Task 18: The exam shell in focus mode

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/screens/Exam.tsx`, `src/app/exam-session.ts`, `src/app/exam-settle.ts`, `tests/unit/exam-session.test.ts`, `tests/unit/exam-settle.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/app/exam.ts`, `src/app/screens/Screen.tsx`, `src/app/screens/TopBar.tsx`, `src/app/App.tsx`, `src/ui/base.css`, `tests/unit/exam.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/exam-session.test.ts`, `tests/unit/exam-settle.test.ts`, `tests/unit/exam.test.ts`

Design notes:
- While the timer runs, the exam lives in the kv draft `examSession` (`{ sid, picks, flagged, changes, ms, current }`), written on every change; a reload restores the position, picks and flags from it, and the deadline comes from the session's start event, so the clock survives reloads and cannot pause (spec 4.3).
- `pick` logs the previous non-empty pick in `changes` whenever the pick differs (a multi pick compares as a set), which feeds Task 19's `Changed right to wrong` tag and Task 10's badges.
- At submit, `submitEvents` writes one `answer` per qid in session order (unanswered ones with `picked: []` and `correct: false`), every one `sure: false`, `flagged: true` and `changes` only when present, then the `session` submit (Decisions); an item whose text failed to load is left out and still scores as wrong, because the score is over the stored qids.
- `settleExam` is the only caller of `submitEvents`: it keeps an in-flight set of sids, loads the session's question text, re-reads the session from the log right before recording (by sid, which is stricter than `openSession` by mode and scope), records only while it is still open, clears the draft and returns whether it recorded.
- The App ticker runs whenever any boss or mock is open, wherever the route is: it ticks `examTick` every second for the focus bar and calls `settleExam` once the deadline has passed, which also settles an exam already past its deadline when the log loads.
- The exam screen itself moves to `#/r/<sid>` as soon as the session it showed is submitted, whoever settled it (Submit, the ticker or another tab), so the brief's "the ticker navigates when the route is that exam" holds without the ticker reading the route.
- Only one exam is open at a time: every start screen shows `Resume` for the open one instead of its start button, and a start that finds an exam opened meanwhile records nothing.
- A boss start awaits the module chunks of its module and every earlier one and the chunks of taken forms, builds its pool with `learnPoolNow(practicePoolOf(...))` and draws with `bossDraw`; `lessons` are the module's lesson note ids (`m05/<slug>`).
- A mock start awaits `ensureExamForm`, which Task 15 makes resolve with the chunk, or with null on a failed load; on null it shows `This mock could not load. Check your connection and try again.` and records nothing, so a failed offline fetch never burns a form.
- A mock stores only the form's live qids (those `index.exam.items` lists for the form) in an order shuffled by the session seed (`mockQids`), and its draft is written to kv before the start event, so the shell never opens without one.
- A remix loads every taken form, imports `src/engine/remix.ts` lazily (the only app importer of the form builder) and builds 65 items to the shared form targets, which `remixTargets` reads off `index.exam.items` as the largest count per task and per domain over the forms, because every form shares the same counts (spec 4.3) and a retired item must not shrink a remix; it records `form: 'remix'`.
- The focus bar replaces the whole top bar while `exam` is set, so XP, the rank, due counts and the streak are out of sight (spec 7.2); its `textContent` equals `examBarText`, because the three-space gaps are whitespace-only text nodes that a flex container does not render, and `gap` spaces the parts instead.
- `span.exam-clock` has `role="timer"` and `aria-live="off"`, so a screen reader never announces every second.
- Each question renders in its own component keyed by qid, so focus moves to the stem on every new question (spec 7.6); options reuse the runner's `fieldset.options` and full-width `label.option`, which are at least 44 px tall.
- The grid marks answered cells solid and unanswered ones dashed, and a flagged cell carries an `F`, while each button's name says `Question 3, answered, flagged`, so no state rests on color.
- The submit confirmation is a `dialog.confirm` opened with `showModal()`, the only confirmation modal (spec 7.6); `Keep working` has `autofocus`, so a stray Enter never submits, and Escape closes it as `Keep working` does.
- New copy beyond the brief: the boss start screen says `Opens after you read every lesson of Module <n>.` while locked and `The next boss opens in <h> h.` while cooling down; the mock start screen says `<n> questions, 90 minutes. The timer cannot pause.` with `n` the form's live items (65 for a frozen form), `This mock is not ready yet.` without a form, and the remix says `Opens after you take all four mocks.` until all four are taken and offers `Start the remix`; an open exam elsewhere shows `<label> is open. One exam runs at a time.` beside `Resume`; failures say `This exam could not load. Check your connection and try again.` and `The exam could not be submitted. Check your connection and try again.`; a boss with an empty draw says `No questions for this boss yet.`, and a remix the forms cannot fill says `The remix could not be built from these forms.`.
- `ensureExamForm(id): Promise<ExamChunk | null>` (Task 15) resolves at once with an already loaded chunk, fetches it once otherwise, and resolves null without setting `loadError` when the fetch fails, so the mock start can await it as the binding decision says.
- `remixTargets` imports `Domain` from `src/engine/quotas.ts`, where Task 3 defines it; `src/engine/forms.ts` imports it without re-exporting it.
- `src/app/topbar.ts` needs no change: the focus bar's words come from `examBarText` and `formatClock` in `src/app/exam.ts`.
- Covers P3-049, P3-075, P3-077, P3-078, P3-088 (the record side), P3-144, P3-146, P3-148, P3-149, P3-150 and P3-163.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { ExamItemRef, QuestionView } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';

  addTime,

/** A made-up bakery item: option `a` is right for a single, `a` and `b` for a multi. */
const item = (id: string, type: 'single' | 'multi' = 'single', version = 1): QuestionView => ({
  id,
  version,
  type,
  concept: `concept-${id}`,
  note: 'm01/starters',
  task: '1.1',
  level: 1,
  stem: 'Which jar holds the starter?',
  select: type === 'multi' ? 2 : null,
  options: ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'].map((o) => ({
    id: o,
    text: `Jar ${o}`,
    correct: o === 'a' || (type === 'multi' && o === 'b'),
    why: 'Because of the label.',
  })),
  pairs: [],
  takeaway: 'Label the jars.',
  read: { route: '#/m/01/l/starters', text: 'Label the jars.' },
});
const QUESTIONS = new Map([
  ['x-one', item('x-one')],
  ['x-two', item('x-two', 'multi', 2)],
  ['x-three', item('x-three')],
]);
const SESSION: SessionState = (() => {
  const p = derive(
    eventsOf({
      t: 'session',
      sid: SID,
      mode: 'mock',
      phase: 'start',
      seed: 42,
      form: 'A',
      qids: ['x-one', 'x-two', 'x-three'],
      deadline: '2026-10-10T09:30:00.000Z',
    }),
    null,
  );
  const s = p.sessions.get(SID);
  if (s === undefined) throw new Error('no session');
  return s;
})();

describe('pick', () => {
  it('logs the previous non-empty pick when it changes, and nothing for a first pick or the same pick', () => {
    let d = pick(newDraft(SID), 'x-one', ['a']);
    expect(d.changes).toEqual({});
    d = pick(d, 'x-one', ['a']);
    expect(d.changes).toEqual({});
    d = pick(d, 'x-one', ['c']);
    d = pick(d, 'x-one', ['d']);
    expect(d.picks['x-one']).toEqual(['d']);
    expect(d.changes).toEqual({ 'x-one': [['a'], ['c']] });
  });

  it('treats a multi pick as a set', () => {
    const d = pick(pick(newDraft(SID), 'x-two', ['a', 'b']), 'x-two', ['b', 'a']);
    expect(d.changes).toEqual({});
  });
});

describe('toggleFlag and addTime', () => {

    expect(d.flagged).toEqual(['x-two']);
    expect(addTime(addTime(d, 'x-two', 1500.4), 'x-two', 2000).ms).toEqual({ 'x-two': 3500 });
    expect(addTime(d, 'x-two', -5)).toBe(d);

describe('submitEvents', () => {

    expect(events.map((e) => (e.t === 'answer' ? e.qid : e.t))).toEqual(['x-one', 'session']);

});

describe('examDraftOf', () => {
  it('reads back a draft written for this session', () => {
    const d = toggleFlag(pick(newDraft(SID), 'x-one', ['a']), 'x-one');
    expect(examDraftOf(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify({ ...d, current: 2 })), SID)).toEqual({ ...d, current: 2 });
  });

  it('rejects a draft for another session or a malformed value', () => {
    expect(examDraftOf(newDraft(SID2), SID)).toBeNull();
    expect(examDraftOf(null, SID)).toBeNull();
    expect(examDraftOf('draft', SID)).toBeNull();
    expect(examDraftOf({ ...newDraft(SID), picks: { 'x-one': 'a' } }, SID)).toBeNull();
    expect(examDraftOf({ ...newDraft(SID), flagged: [1] }, SID)).toBeNull();
    expect(examDraftOf({ ...newDraft(SID), changes: { 'x-one': ['a'] } }, SID)).toBeNull();
    expect(examDraftOf({ ...newDraft(SID), ms: { 'x-one': -1 } }, SID)).toBeNull();
    expect(examDraftOf({ ...newDraft(SID), current: 1.5 }, SID)).toBeNull();
    expect(examDraftOf({ sid: SID }, SID)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('the grid and the counts', () => {
  it('names each grid button by its number, whether it is answered, and whether it is flagged', () => {
    expect(gridLabel(3, true, true)).toBe('Question 3, answered, flagged');
    expect(gridLabel(4, false, false)).toBe('Question 4, unanswered');
    expect(gridLabel(5, false, true)).toBe('Question 5, unanswered, flagged');
  });

  it('counts unanswered and flagged items', () => {
    expect(countsLine(3, 2)).toBe('3 unanswered, 2 flagged.');
    expect(countsLine(0, 0)).toBe('0 unanswered, 0 flagged.');
  });
});

describe('remixTargets', () => {
  it('takes each task and multi-response count as the largest over the forms', () => {
    const ref = (form: string, task: string, type: 'single' | 'multi'): ExamItemRef => ({
      version: 1,
      concept: `c-${task}`,
      task,
      form,
      type,
    });
    const items = {
      'x-1': ref('A', '1.1', 'single'),
      'x-2': ref('A', '1.1', 'multi'),
      'x-3': ref('A', '3.2', 'multi'),
      'x-4': ref('B', '1.1', 'single'),
      'x-5': ref('B', '3.2', 'multi'),
      'x-6': ref('B', '2.2', 'multi'),
    };
    expect(remixTargets(items)).toEqual({
      tasks: { '1.1': 2, '2.2': 1, '3.2': 1 },
      multi: { '1': 1, '2': 1, '3': 1, '4': 0 },
    });
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the exam draft**

Create `src/app/exam-session.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The exam draft (spec 4.3 and 8.1): while a boss or mock runs, picks, flags, answer changes and time per item live
 * in kv under `examSession`, and only at submit do they become answer events. Pure, so the tests run without a browser.
 */
import type { ExamItemRef, QuestionView } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import type { NewEvent } from '../../schemas/events.ts';
import { DOMAIN_IDS } from '../../schemas/ids.ts';
import type { FormTargets } from '../engine/forms.ts';
import type { Domain } from '../engine/quotas.ts';
import type { SessionState } from '../engine/replay.ts';
import { isCorrect } from './runner.ts';

export interface ExamDraft {
  sid: string;
  /** The current pick per question id; an empty or missing pick is unanswered. */
  picks: Record<string, string[]>;
  flagged: string[];
  /** Earlier picks per question id, oldest first, for the `Changed right to wrong` tag (spec 4.4). */
  changes: Record<string, string[][]>;
  /** Milliseconds each question was on screen. */
  ms: Record<string, number>;
  /** The 0-based index of the question on screen. */
  current: number;
}

export function newDraft(sid: string): ExamDraft {
  return { sid, picks: {}, flagged: [], changes: {}, ms: {}, current: 0 };
}

const isStrings = (value: unknown): value is string[] =>
  Array.isArray(value) && value.every((x) => typeof x === 'string');

const isRecordOf = <T>(value: unknown, item: (x: unknown) => x is T): value is Record<string, T> =>
  typeof value === 'object' &&
  value !== null &&
  !Array.isArray(value) &&
  Object.values(value).every((x) => item(x));

const isPickList = (value: unknown): value is string[][] => Array.isArray(value) && value.every(isStrings);
const isMs = (value: unknown): value is number =>
  typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0;

/** The kv draft of session `sid`, or null when the value is another session's or is malformed. */
export function examDraftOf(value: unknown, sid: string): ExamDraft | null {
  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) return null;
  const v = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (v.sid !== sid) return null;
  if (!isRecordOf(v.picks, isStrings) || !isStrings(v.flagged) || !isRecordOf(v.changes, isPickList))
    return null;
  if (
    !isRecordOf(v.ms, isMs) ||
    typeof v.current !== 'number' ||
    !Number.isInteger(v.current) ||
    v.current < 0
  )
    return null;
  return { sid, picks: v.picks, flagged: v.flagged, changes: v.changes, ms: v.ms, current: v.current };
}

const samePick = (a: readonly string[], b: readonly string[]): boolean =>
  a.length === b.length && [...a].sort().join('\n') === [...b].sort().join('\n');

/** Sets the pick of one question, logging the previous non-empty pick in `changes` when it differs. */
export function pick(draft: ExamDraft, qid: string, picked: string[]): ExamDraft {
  const before = draft.picks[qid] ?? [];
  if (samePick(before, picked)) return draft;
  const changes =
    before.length > 0 ? { ...draft.changes, [qid]: [...(draft.changes[qid] ?? []), before] } : draft.changes;
  return { ...draft, picks: { ...draft.picks, [qid]: picked }, changes };
}

export function toggleFlag(draft: ExamDraft, qid: string): ExamDraft {
  const flagged = draft.flagged.includes(qid)
    ? draft.flagged.filter((x) => x !== qid)
    : [...draft.flagged, qid];
  return { ...draft, flagged };
}

/** Adds time on screen to one question. */
export function addTime(draft: ExamDraft, qid: string, ms: number): ExamDraft {
  if (ms <= 0) return draft;
  return { ...draft, ms: { ...draft.ms, [qid]: (draft.ms[qid] ?? 0) + Math.round(ms) } };
}

export const isAnswered = (draft: ExamDraft, qid: string): boolean => (draft.picks[qid]?.length ?? 0) > 0;

/**
 * The events of a submit: one answer per question in session order, an unanswered one with `picked: []` and
 * `correct: false` (spec 4.3), every one `sure: false` (spec 3.1), then the session submit.
 * A question whose text is not loaded is left out, and still counts as wrong because the score is over `qids`.
 */
export function submitEvents(args: {
  draft: ExamDraft;
  session: SessionState;
  questions: ReadonlyMap<string, QuestionView>;
  mode: 'boss' | 'mock';
}): NewEvent[] {
  const { draft, session, questions, mode } = args;
  const out: NewEvent[] = [];
  for (const qid of session.qids) {
    const q = questions.get(qid);
    if (q === undefined) continue;
    const picked = draft.picks[qid] ?? [];
    const changes = draft.changes[qid] ?? [];
    out.push({
      t: 'answer',
      sid: session.sid,
      mode,
      qid,
      qv: q.version,
      concept: q.concept,
      picked,
      correct: picked.length > 0 && isCorrect(q, picked),
      sure: false,
      ms: draft.ms[qid] ?? 0,
      ...(draft.flagged.includes(qid) ? { flagged: true } : {}),
      ...(changes.length > 0 ? { changes } : {}),
    });
  }
  out.push({ t: 'session', sid: session.sid, mode, phase: 'submit', seed: session.seed });
  return out;
}

/** A grid button's accessible name: `Question 3, answered, flagged`. */
export function gridLabel(n: number, answered: boolean, flagged: boolean): string {
  return [`Question ${n}`, answered ? 'answered' : 'unanswered', ...(flagged ? ['flagged'] : [])].join(', ');
}

/** The review page's and the confirmation's count line: `3 unanswered, 2 flagged.` */
export function countsLine(unanswered: number, flagged: number): string {
  return `${unanswered} unanswered, ${flagged} flagged.`;
}

/**
 * The per-form targets a remix must meet, read off the frozen forms' live items in the index: each task count and each
 * multi-response count per domain is the largest over the forms, which all share the same counts (spec 4.3), so an
 * item retired from one form never shrinks or skews a remix.
 */
export function remixTargets(items: Readonly<Record<string, ExamItemRef>>): FormTargets {
  const perForm = new Map<string, { tasks: Record<string, number>; multi: Record<string, number> }>();
  for (const item of Object.values(items)) {
    const counts = perForm.get(item.form) ?? { tasks: {}, multi: {} };
    perForm.set(item.form, counts);
    counts.tasks[item.task] = (counts.tasks[item.task] ?? 0) + 1;
    const domain = item.task.split('.')[0] as string;
    if (item.type === 'multi') counts.multi[domain] = (counts.multi[domain] ?? 0) + 1;
  }
  const tasks: Record<string, number> = {};
  const multi = Object.fromEntries(DOMAIN_IDS.map((d) => [d, 0])) as Record<Domain, number>;
  for (const counts of perForm.values()) {
    for (const [task, n] of Object.entries(counts.tasks)) tasks[task] = Math.max(tasks[task] ?? 0, n);
    for (const d of DOMAIN_IDS) multi[d] = Math.max(multi[d], counts.multi[d] ?? 0);
  }
  return { tasks, multi };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the single settling writer**

Create `src/app/exam-settle.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Settling an exam: the single writer that turns the kv draft of a boss or mock into answer events and the submit,
 * whether the Submit button, the deadline or a later visit asks first (spec 4.3).
 */
import { effect, signal } from '@preact/signals';
import type { ModuleChunk, QuestionView } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import type { Progress, SessionState } from '../engine/replay.ts';
import { KV_KEYS } from '../store/kv.ts';
import { ensureExamForm, ensureModule, index, loadError, modules } from './content.ts';
import { examDraftOf, newDraft, submitEvents } from './exam-session.ts';
import { kvRecords, progress, record, writeKv } from './progress.ts';

/** The wall clock in milliseconds, ticked every second by App while an exam is open; the focus bar reads it. */
export const examTick = signal(Date.now());

/** What the focus bar's Flag and Grid buttons do, set by the exam shell while it shows a question. */
export const examControls = signal<{
  flag: () => void;
  grid: () => void;
  flagged: boolean;
  gridOpen: boolean;
} | null>(null);

/** The open boss or mock, if any; only one may be open at a time. */
export function openExam(p: Progress): SessionState | null {
  let best: SessionState | null = null;
  for (const s of p.sessions.values()) {
    if ((s.mode !== 'boss' && s.mode !== 'mock') || s.submittedAt !== null || s.abandoned) continue;
    if (best === null || s.startedAt > best.startedAt) best = s;
  }
  return best;
}

/** Resolves once every module chunk asked for has loaded, or with null when a load fails. */
export function loadModules(ns: readonly number[]): Promise<ModuleChunk[] | null> {
  for (const n of ns) ensureModule(n);
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    let stop: (() => void) | null = null;
    let done = false;
    stop = effect(() => {
      const loaded = ns.map((n) => modules.value.get(n));
      const failed = loadError.value !== null;
      if (!failed && loaded.some((c) => c === undefined)) return;
      done = true;
      resolve(failed ? null : (loaded as ModuleChunk[]));
      stop?.();
    });
    if (done) stop();
  });
}

/**
 * The question text of a session's items: the form chunks of its exam items and, for a boss, the module chunks of
 * its module and every earlier one; null when a chunk cannot load.
 */
export async function examQuestions(session: SessionState): Promise<Map<string, QuestionView> | null> {
  const items = index.peek()?.exam.items ?? {};
  const forms = new Set<string>();
  for (const qid of session.qids) {
    const item = items[qid];
    if (item !== undefined) forms.add(item.form);
  }
  const chunks = await Promise.all([...forms].map((f) => ensureExamForm(f)));
  if (chunks.some((c) => c === null)) return null;
  const module = session.mode === 'boss' ? Number(session.scope.slice(1)) : 0;
  const practice = module > 0 ? await loadModules(Array.from({ length: module }, (_, i) => i + 1)) : [];
  if (practice === null) return null;
  const wanted = new Set(session.qids);
  const out = new Map<string, QuestionView>();
  for (const q of [...chunks.flatMap((c) => c?.questions ?? []), ...practice.flatMap((c) => c.questions)]) {
    if (wanted.has(q.id)) out.set(q.id, q);
  }
  return out;
}

const settling = new Set<string>();

/**
 * Records the answers and the submit of an open exam, then clears its draft; true when it recorded.
 * It is the only caller of `submitEvents`, ignores a second call while one is in flight, and re-reads the session
 * right before recording, so a duplicate submit can never reach the log.
 */
export async function settleExam(sid: string, now: Date): Promise<boolean> {
  if (settling.has(sid)) return false;
  settling.add(sid);
  try {
    const first = progress.peek().sessions.get(sid);
    if (first === undefined || first.submittedAt !== null || first.abandoned) return false;
    if (first.mode !== 'boss' && first.mode !== 'mock') return false;
    const questions = await examQuestions(first);
    if (questions === null) return false;
    const session = progress.peek().sessions.get(sid);
    if (session === undefined || session.submittedAt !== null || session.abandoned) return false;
    const draft = examDraftOf(kvRecords.peek()[KV_KEYS.examSession]?.value, sid) ?? newDraft(sid);
    for (const e of submitEvents({ draft, session, questions, mode: first.mode })) await record(e, now);
    await writeKv(KV_KEYS.examSession, null, now);
    return true;
  } finally {
    settling.delete(sid);
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add the exam route and name helpers**

Replace `src/app/exam.ts` with:

```ts
/** The exam shell's keys and its focus bar text (spec 7.2 and 7.4), pure so the tests run without a browser. */
import type { ContentIndex } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import type { SessionState } from '../engine/replay.ts';
import { type MockRoute, toHash } from './router.ts';
import { type RunnerKeyInput, runnerKey } from './runner-keys.ts';

/** The `form` a remix mock's session events carry; forms A to D are upper case (spec 4.3). */
export const REMIX_FORM = 'remix';

export type ExamCommand =
  | { kind: 'choose'; n: number }
  | { kind: 'prev' }
  | { kind: 'next' }
  | { kind: 'flag' }
  | { kind: 'grid' }
  | { kind: 'submit' };

export interface ExamKeyState {
  optionCount: number;
  /** True while the submit confirmation is open; its own buttons and Escape answer then. */
  confirmOpen: boolean;
  /** True while the question grid covers the question. */
  gridOpen: boolean;
}

/** One keydown in the exam shell; null means the key is not the exam's. */
export function examKey(input: RunnerKeyInput, state: ExamKeyState): ExamCommand | null {
  if (input.typing || input.alt || state.confirmOpen) return null;
  const key = input.key.length === 1 ? input.key.toLowerCase() : input.key;
  if (input.ctrl || input.meta) return key === 'Enter' ? { kind: 'submit' } : null;
  if (key === 'g') return { kind: 'grid' };
  if (state.gridOpen) return null;
  if (key === 'ArrowLeft') return { kind: 'prev' };
  if (key === 'ArrowRight') return { kind: 'next' };
  if (key === 'f') return { kind: 'flag' };
  // The digits read exactly as the runner reads them, for one question that is never answered yet.
  const command = runnerKey(input, {
    type: 'single',
    answered: false,
    optionCount: state.optionCount,
    leftCount: 0,
    reportOpen: false,
  });
  return command?.kind === 'choose' ? { kind: 'choose', n: command.n } : null;
}

/** Time left as `m:ss` with unpadded minutes, rounded up to the second; `0:00` once the time is up. */
export function formatClock(ms: number): string {
  if (ms <= 0) return '0:00';
  const seconds = Math.ceil(ms / 1000);
  return `${Math.floor(seconds / 60)}:${String(seconds % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

/** The gap between the focus bar's parts, as the spec 7.2 mockup writes it. */
export const BAR_GAP = '   ';

/** The focus bar's text: `Mock A   23/65   61:12   Flag (f)   Grid (g)`. */
export function examBarText(args: {
  label: string;
  position: number;
  total: number;
  leftMs: number;
}): string {
  return [
    args.label,
    `${args.position}/${args.total}`,
    formatClock(args.leftMs),
    'Flag (f)',
    'Grid (g)',
  ].join(BAR_GAP);
}

/** The route of an exam session: `#/s/boss/m05`, `#/s/mock/a` or `#/s/mock/remix`. */
export function examHashOf(session: Pick<SessionState, 'mode' | 'scope' | 'form'>): string {
  if (session.mode === 'boss') return toHash({ kind: 'boss', module: Number(session.scope.slice(1)) });
  const form = session.form === null || session.form === REMIX_FORM ? 'remix' : session.form.toLowerCase();
  return toHash({ kind: 'mock', form: form as MockRoute });
}

/** The name of an exam session: `Boss: <short>`, `Mock A` or `Remix mock`. */
export function examLabel(session: Pick<SessionState, 'mode' | 'scope' | 'form'>, idx: ContentIndex): string {
  if (session.mode === 'boss') {
    const module = Number(session.scope.slice(1));
    return `Boss: ${idx.modules.find((m) => m.n === module)?.short ?? `Module ${module}`}`;
  }
  return session.form === null || session.form === REMIX_FORM ? 'Remix mock' : `Mock ${session.form}`;
}
```

```tsx
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { ContentIndex, QuestionView } from '../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { bossDeadline, bossDraw } from '../../engine/boss.ts';
import { resolveConcept } from '../../engine/memory.ts';
import { mockDeadline, mockQids } from '../../engine/mock.ts';
import type { SessionState } from '../../engine/replay.ts';
import { recall } from '../../engine/srs.ts';
import { KV_KEYS } from '../../store/kv.ts';
import { ulid } from '../../store/ulid.ts';
import { useTitle } from '../../ui/useTitle.ts';
import { now } from '../clock.ts';
import { ensureExamForm } from '../content.ts';
import { type ExamCommand, examHashOf, examKey, examLabel, REMIX_FORM } from '../exam.ts';
import {
  addTime,
  countsLine,
  type ExamDraft,
  examDraftOf,
  gridLabel,
  isAnswered,
  newDraft,
  pick,
  remixTargets,
  toggleFlag,
} from '../exam-session.ts';
import { examControls, examQuestions, loadModules, openExam, settleExam } from '../exam-settle.ts';
import { isControl, isTyping } from '../keyboard.ts';
import { learnPoolNow, practicePoolOf } from '../learn-pool.ts';
import { bosses, memory, memoryContent, taken } from '../memory.ts';
import { kvRecords, newSessionSeed, progress, record, storeReady, writeKv } from '../progress.ts';
import { pad2, type Route, toHash } from '../router.ts';
import { displayOrder } from '../runner.ts';
import { exam, examPosition, navigate, overlay } from '../state.ts';
import { NotFound } from './NotFound.tsx';

export type ExamRoute = Extract<Route, { kind: 'boss' } | { kind: 'mock' }>;

export const BOSS_LEDE = '15 questions, 21 minutes. The timer cannot pause.';
export const MOCK_LOAD_ERROR = 'This mock could not load. Check your connection and try again.';
export const REMIX_LEDE =
  '65 questions from forms you have taken. No XP, and it does not count toward readiness or rank.';
const START_ERROR = 'This exam could not load. Check your connection and try again.';
const SUBMIT_ERROR = 'The exam could not be submitted. Check your connection and try again.';

/** The concept a question tests, as memory keys it (aliases resolved); the raw id when memory does not know it. */
function memoryConcept(concept: string): string {
  const content = memoryContent.peek();
  return (content === null ? null : resolveConcept(content, concept)) ?? concept;
}

const cardOf = (concept: string) => memory.peek()?.concepts.get(memoryConcept(concept))?.card ?? null;

/** The boss `#/s/boss/mNN` and the mocks `#/s/mock/a` to `d` and `remix`: a start screen, then the exam shell. */
export function Exam({ idx, route }: { idx: ContentIndex; route: ExamRoute }) {
  const shown = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const ready = storeReady.value;
  const p = progress.value;
  const open = ready ? openExam(p) : null;
  const mine = open !== null && examHashOf(open) === toHash(route) ? open : null;
  const done = mine === null && shown.current !== null ? p.sessions.get(shown.current) : undefined;
  const finished = done !== undefined && done.submittedAt !== null;
  if (mine !== null) shown.current = mine.sid;
  // Whoever settled the exam (Submit, the deadline, another tab), its screen moves on to the results.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (finished && done !== undefined) navigate(toHash({ kind: 'results', sid: done.sid }));
  }, [finished, done]);
  if (route.kind === 'boss' && !idx.modules.some((m) => m.n === route.module)) {
    return <NotFound hash={toHash(route)} />;
  }
  if (!ready || finished) return <p class="muted">Loading</p>;
  if (mine !== null) return <ExamRun key={mine.sid} idx={idx} session={mine} />;
  return <ExamStart idx={idx} route={route} open={open} />;
}

function ExamStart({ idx, route, open }: { idx: ContentIndex; route: ExamRoute; open: SessionState | null }) {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const meta = route.kind === 'boss' ? idx.modules.find((m) => m.n === route.module) : undefined;
  const form = route.kind === 'mock' && route.form !== 'remix' ? route.form.toUpperCase() : null;
  const title =
    route.kind === 'boss' ? `Boss: ${meta?.short ?? ''}` : form === null ? 'Remix mock' : `Mock ${form}`;
  useTitle(title);
  const size = Object.values(idx.exam.items).filter((i) => i.form === form).length;
  const lede =
    route.kind === 'boss'
      ? BOSS_LEDE
      : form === null
        ? REMIX_LEDE
        : `${size} questions, 90 minutes. The timer cannot pause.`;
  const blocked = blockedLine(idx, route, form);

  async function start(): Promise<void> {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    const problem = await startExam(idx, route, form);
    if (problem !== null) {
      setError(problem);
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <section class="exam-start" aria-labelledby="exam-title">
      <h1 id="exam-title">{title}</h1>
      <p class="lede">{lede}</p>
      {open !== null ? (
        <p>
          <a class="button-link" href={examHashOf(open)}>
            Resume
          </a>{' '}
          <span class="muted">{examLabel(open, idx)} is open. One exam runs at a time.</span>
        </p>
      ) : blocked !== null ? (
        <p class="muted">{blocked}</p>
      ) : (
        <p>
          <button class="button" type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void start()}>
            {route.kind === 'boss'
              ? 'Start the boss'
              : form === null
                ? 'Start the remix'
                : `Start Mock ${form}`}
          </button>
        </p>
      )}
      {error !== null && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}

/** Why this exam cannot start now, or null. */
function blockedLine(idx: ContentIndex, route: ExamRoute, form: string | null): string | null {
  if (route.kind === 'boss') {
    const status = bosses.value.get(route.module);
    if (status === undefined || !status.unlocked) {
      return `Opens after you read every lesson of Module ${route.module}.`;
    }
    if (status.cooldownUntil !== null) {
      const hours = Math.max(
        1,
        Math.ceil((Date.parse(status.cooldownUntil) - now.value.getTime()) / 3_600_000),
      );
      return `The next boss opens in ${hours} h.`;
    }
    return null;
  }
  if (form === null) return taken.value.size < 4 ? 'Opens after you take all four mocks.' : null;
  return idx.exam.forms.some((f) => f.id === form) ? null : 'This mock is not ready yet.';
}

/** Draws and records the start of a boss, a mock or a remix; returns the line to show when it cannot. */
async function startExam(idx: ContentIndex, route: ExamRoute, form: string | null): Promise<string | null> {
  const seed = newSessionSeed();
  let start: Parameters<typeof record>[0];
  const sid = ulid();
  if (route.kind === 'boss') {
    const n = route.module;
    const chunks = await loadModules(Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => i + 1));
    if (chunks === null) return START_ERROR;
    await Promise.all([...taken.peek()].map((f) => ensureExamForm(f)));
    const at = new Date();
    const qids = bossDraw({
      module: n,
      pool: learnPoolNow(practicePoolOf(chunks, memoryConcept)),
      lessons: (idx.modules.find((m) => m.n === n)?.lessons ?? []).map((l) => `m${pad2(n)}/${l.slug}`),
      progress: progress.peek(),
      recall: (concept) => {
        const card = cardOf(concept);
        return card === null ? 0 : recall(card, at);
      },
      studied: (concept) => cardOf(concept) !== null,
      now: at,
      seed,
    });
    if (qids.length === 0) return 'No questions for this boss yet.';
    start = {
      t: 'session',
      sid,
      mode: 'boss',
      phase: 'start',
      scope: `m${pad2(n)}`,
      seed,
      qids,
      deadline: bossDeadline(at),
    };
  } else if (form !== null) {
    const chunk = await ensureExamForm(form);
    if (chunk === null) return MOCK_LOAD_ERROR;
    const live = new Set(Object.keys(idx.exam.items).filter((id) => idx.exam.items[id]?.form === form));
    const qids = mockQids(
      chunk.questions.map((q) => q.id),
      live,
      seed,
    );
    start = {
      t: 'session',
      sid,
      mode: 'mock',
      phase: 'start',
      seed,
      form,
      qids,
      deadline: mockDeadline(new Date()),
    };
  } else {
    const forms = [...taken.peek()];
    const chunks = await Promise.all(forms.map((f) => ensureExamForm(f)));
    if (chunks.some((c) => c === null)) return MOCK_LOAD_ERROR;
    const { remixQids } = await import('../../engine/remix.ts');
    const items = Object.entries(idx.exam.items)
      .filter(([, i]) => forms.includes(i.form))
      .map(([id, i]) => ({ id, concept: i.concept, task: i.task, type: i.type }));
    const qids = remixQids({ items, targets: remixTargets(idx.exam.items), seed });
    if (qids === null) return 'The remix could not be built from these forms.';
    start = {
      t: 'session',
      sid,
      mode: 'mock',
      phase: 'start',
      seed,
      form: REMIX_FORM,
      qids,
      deadline: mockDeadline(new Date()),
    };
  }
  // Another tab or screen may have opened an exam while the chunks loaded; one exam runs at a time.
  if (openExam(progress.peek()) !== null) return null;
  await writeKv(KV_KEYS.examSession, newDraft(sid));
  await record(start);
  return null;
}

type View = 'question' | 'grid' | 'review';

function ExamRun({ idx, session }: { idx: ContentIndex; session: SessionState }) {
  const label = examLabel(session, idx);
  useTitle(label);
  const qids = session.qids;
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<ReadonlyMap<string, QuestionView> | null>(null);
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<ExamDraft>(
    () => examDraftOf(kvRecords.peek()[KV_KEYS.examSession]?.value, session.sid) ?? newDraft(session.sid),
  );
  const [view, setView] = useState<View>('question');
  const [confirmOpen, setConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [submitError, setSubmitError] = useState(false);
  const shownAt = useRef(Date.now());
  const confirm = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);
  const current = Math.min(draft.current, Math.max(0, qids.length - 1));
  const qid = qids[current] ?? '';
  const q = questions?.get(qid);
  const order = q === undefined ? [] : displayOrder(q, session.seed);

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    void examQuestions(session).then((loaded) => {
      if (!live) return;
      if (loaded === null) setFailed(true);
      else setQuestions(loaded);
    });
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, [session]);

  useEffect(() => {
    exam.value = { sid: session.sid, label, total: qids.length, deadline: session.deadline ?? '' };
    return () => {
      exam.value = null;
    };
  }, [session.sid, label, qids.length, session.deadline]);

  useEffect(() => {
    examPosition.value = current + 1;
  }, [current]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = confirm.current;
    if (el === null) return;
    if (confirmOpen && !el.open) el.showModal();
    if (!confirmOpen && el.open) el.close();
  }, [confirmOpen]);

  /** Writes the draft to kv on every change, so a reload restores the position, picks and flags. */
  const save = (next: ExamDraft): void => {
    setDraft(next);
    void writeKv(KV_KEYS.examSession, next);
  };
  /** The draft with the time on the current question added, and the clock restarted for the next one. */
  const timed = (d: ExamDraft): ExamDraft => {
    const at = Date.now();
    const next = addTime(d, qid, at - shownAt.current);
    shownAt.current = at;
    return next;
  };
  const go = (index: number): void => {
    save({ ...timed(draft), current: Math.min(Math.max(0, index), qids.length - 1) });
    setView('question');
  };
  const choose = (id: string): void => {
    if (q === undefined) return;
    const before = draft.picks[qid] ?? [];
    let picked: string[];
    if (q.type === 'single') picked = [id];
    else if (before.includes(id)) picked = before.filter((x) => x !== id);
    else if (before.length >= (q.select ?? 0)) return;
    else picked = [...before, id];
    save(pick(draft, qid, picked));
  };
  const flag = (): void => save(toggleFlag(draft, qid));
  const toggleGrid = (): void => setView(view === 'grid' ? 'question' : 'grid');
  async function submit(): Promise<void> {
    if (submitting) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    setSubmitError(false);
    save(timed(draft));
    const recorded = await settleExam(session.sid, new Date());
    setConfirmOpen(false);
    if (!recorded && progress.peek().sessions.get(session.sid)?.submittedAt === null) {
      setSubmitError(true);
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  const run = (command: ExamCommand): void => {
    switch (command.kind) {
      case 'choose': {
        const id = order[command.n - 1];
        if (id !== undefined && view === 'question') choose(id);
        break;
      }
      case 'prev':
        if (view === 'question') go(current - 1);
        break;
      case 'next':
        if (view === 'question') go(current + 1);
        break;
      case 'flag':
        flag();
        break;
      case 'grid':
        toggleGrid();
        break;
      case 'submit':
        setConfirmOpen(true);
        break;
    }
  };
  const latest = useRef({ run, optionCount: order.length, confirmOpen, gridOpen: view === 'grid' });
  latest.current = { run, optionCount: order.length, confirmOpen, gridOpen: view === 'grid' };

  useEffect(() => {
    examControls.value = {
      flag,
      grid: toggleGrid,
      flagged: draft.flagged.includes(qid),
      gridOpen: view === 'grid',
    };
  });
  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      examControls.value = null;
    },
    [],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent): void => {
      if (overlay.value !== null || event.defaultPrevented) return;
      const state = latest.current;
      const command = examKey(
        {
          key: event.key,
          meta: event.metaKey,
          ctrl: event.ctrlKey,
          alt: event.altKey,
          shift: event.shiftKey,
          typing: isTyping(event.target),
          control: isControl(event.target),
          at: event.timeStamp,
        },
        { optionCount: state.optionCount, confirmOpen: state.confirmOpen, gridOpen: state.gridOpen },
      );
      if (command === null) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      state.run(command);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, []);

  const unanswered = qids.filter((id) => !isAnswered(draft, id)).length;
  const counts = countsLine(unanswered, draft.flagged.length);
  const grid = (
    <nav class="exam-grid" aria-label="Questions">
      <ol class="exam-grid-list">
        {qids.map((id, i) => {
          const answered = isAnswered(draft, id);
          const flagged = draft.flagged.includes(id);
          return (
            <li key={id}>
              <button
                type="button"
                class={`grid-cell${answered ? ' is-answered' : ''}${flagged ? ' is-flagged' : ''}`}
                aria-label={gridLabel(i + 1, answered, flagged)}
                aria-current={i === current ? 'true' : undefined}
                onClick={() => go(i)}
              >
                {i + 1}
                {flagged && (
                  <span class="grid-flag" aria-hidden="true">
                    F
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>
    </nav>
  );

  let body = <p class="muted">Loading</p>;
  if (failed) body = <p role="alert">{START_ERROR}</p>;
  else if (view === 'grid') body = grid;
  else if (view === 'review') {
    body = (
      <section class="exam-review" aria-labelledby="review-title">
        <h2 id="review-title">Review</h2>
        {grid}
        <p class="exam-counts">{counts}</p>
        <p>
          <button class="button" type="button" onClick={() => setConfirmOpen(true)}>
            Submit
          </button>
        </p>
      </section>
    );
  } else if (questions !== null) {
    body = (
      <ExamQuestion
        key={qid}
        qid={qid}
        q={q}
        order={order}
        chosen={draft.picks[qid] ?? []}
        flagged={draft.flagged.includes(qid)}
        first={current === 0}
        last={current === qids.length - 1}
        onChoose={choose}
        onPrev={() => go(current - 1)}
        onNext={() => go(current + 1)}
        onFlag={flag}
        onReview={() => {
          save(timed(draft));
          setView('review');
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <h1 class="visually-hidden">{label}</h1>
      {body}
      {submitError && <p role="alert">{SUBMIT_ERROR}</p>}
      <dialog
        ref={confirm}
        class="confirm"
        aria-labelledby="confirm-title"
        onClose={() => setConfirmOpen(false)}
      >
        <div class="sheet-body">
          <h2 id="confirm-title">Submit the exam?</h2>
          <p>{counts}</p>
          <p class="sheet-actions">
            <button class="button button-quiet" type="button" autofocus onClick={() => setConfirmOpen(false)}>
              Keep working
            </button>
            <button class="button" type="button" disabled={submitting} onClick={() => void submit()}>
              Submit
            </button>
          </p>
        </div>
      </dialog>
    </>
  );
}

interface ExamQuestionProps {
  qid: string;
  q: QuestionView | undefined;
  order: readonly string[];
  chosen: readonly string[];
  flagged: boolean;
  first: boolean;
  last: boolean;
  onChoose: (id: string) => void;
  onPrev: () => void;
  onNext: () => void;
  onFlag: () => void;
  onReview: () => void;
}

/** One exam question; keyed by its id, so focus moves to the stem on every new question (spec 7.6). */
function ExamQuestion(props: ExamQuestionProps) {
  const { qid, q, order, chosen, flagged } = props;
  const stem = useRef<HTMLParagraphElement>(null);
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    stem.current?.focus();
  }, []);
  return (
    <section class="exam" data-qid={qid} aria-labelledby="stem">
      <p id="stem" class="stem" tabIndex={-1} ref={stem}>
        {q?.stem ?? 'This question is not in this version of the content.'}
      </p>
      {q !== undefined && (
        <fieldset class="options" aria-labelledby="stem">
          {order.map((id, i) => {
            const option = q.options.find((o) => o.id === id);
            if (option === undefined) return null;
            return (
              <label key={id} class={`option${chosen.includes(id) ? ' chosen' : ''}`}>
                <input
                  type={q.type === 'single' ? 'radio' : 'checkbox'}
                  name={`q-${q.id}`}
                  value={id}
                  checked={chosen.includes(id)}
                  onChange={() => props.onChoose(id)}
                />
                <span class="option-n" aria-hidden="true">
                  {i + 1}
                </span>
                <span class="option-text">{option.text}</span>
              </label>
            );
          })}
        </fieldset>
      )}
      <div class="exam-nav">
        <button class="button button-quiet" type="button" disabled={props.first} onClick={props.onPrev}>
          Previous
        </button>
        <button class="button button-quiet" type="button" aria-pressed={flagged} onClick={props.onFlag}>
          Flag
        </button>
        {props.last ? (
          <button class="button" type="button" onClick={props.onReview}>
            Review and submit
          </button>
        ) : (
          <button class="button" type="button" onClick={props.onNext}>
            Next
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

Replace `src/app/screens/Screen.tsx` with:

```tsx
import type { ContentIndex } from '../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { pad2, type Route } from '../router.ts';
import { Home } from './Home.tsx';
import { lazyScreen } from './Lazy.tsx';
import { LessonPage } from './LessonPage.tsx';
import { MissionPage } from './MissionPage.tsx';
import { MissionsPage } from './MissionsPage.tsx';
import { ModulePage } from './ModulePage.tsx';
import { NotFound } from './NotFound.tsx';
import { Runner } from './Runner.tsx';

// Exam screens load on first use; Tasks 19 and 21 replace the placeholders that are left.
const ExamScreen = lazyScreen(() => import('./Exam.tsx').then((m) => m.Exam));
const MocksScreen = lazyScreen(() => import('./Pending.tsx').then((m) => m.Pending));
const ResultsScreen = lazyScreen(() => import('./Pending.tsx').then((m) => m.Pending));
const FocusScreen = lazyScreen(() => import('./Pending.tsx').then((m) => m.Pending));

/** The screen for a route; the content index is always loaded by the time this renders. */
export function Screen({ route, idx }: { route: Route; idx: ContentIndex }) {
  switch (route.kind) {
    case 'home':
      return <Home idx={idx} />;
    case 'module':
      return <ModulePage idx={idx} module={route.module} />;
    case 'lesson':
      return <LessonPage idx={idx} module={route.module} slug={route.slug} anchor={route.anchor} />;
    case 'missions':
      return <MissionsPage idx={idx} />;
    case 'mission':
      return <MissionPage idx={idx} id={route.id} />;
    // Keyed by the route object, which every arrival replaces, so "Another quiz" on the quiz route starts afresh.
    case 'check':
      return <Runner key={route} idx={idx} mode="check" scope={`m${pad2(route.module)}/${route.slug}`} />;
    case 'quiz':
      return <Runner key={route} idx={idx} mode="quiz" scope={`m${pad2(route.module)}`} />;
    case 'review':
      return <Runner key={route} idx={idx} mode="review" scope="review" />;
    case 'boss':
      return <ExamScreen key={route} idx={idx} route={route} />;
    case 'mocks':
      return <MocksScreen title="Mock exams" />;
    case 'mock':
      return <ExamScreen key={route} idx={idx} route={route} />;
    case 'focus':
      return <FocusScreen key={route} title="Focus quiz" />;
    case 'results':
      return <ResultsScreen key={route} title="Results" />;
    case 'not-found':
      return <NotFound hash={route.hash} />;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Swap in the focus bar and run the exam clock**

Replace `src/app/screens/TopBar.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { levelProgress } from '../../engine/level.ts';
import { BAR_GAP, formatClock } from '../exam.ts';
import { examControls, examTick } from '../exam-settle.ts';
import { memory, queue, streak } from '../memory.ts';
import { storeReady } from '../progress.ts';
import { toHash } from '../router.ts';
import { exam, examPosition, navigate, overlay } from '../state.ts';
import { dueLabel, FLASH_MS, streakLabel, xpGain } from '../topbar.ts';

const openProgress = (): void => {
  overlay.value = 'progress';
};

/** Shows each XP gain for 600 ms; the first value after the log loads never flashes. */
function useXpFlash(xp: number | null): { gain: number; key: number } | null {
  const before = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const [flash, setFlash] = useState<{ gain: number; key: number } | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (xp === null) return;
    const gain = xpGain(before.current, xp);
    before.current = xp;
    if (gain !== null) setFlash((shown) => ({ gain, key: (shown?.key ?? 0) + 1 }));
  }, [xp]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (flash === null) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setFlash(null), FLASH_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [flash]);
  return flash;
}

/** The 44 px top bar (spec 7.2): the due count, the streak, Progress and Search, over the 2 px level line. */
export function TopBar() {
  const open = exam.value;
  return open === null ? <SiteBar /> : <FocusBar info={open} />;
}

/**
 * The focus bar of a boss or mock (spec 7.2): `Mock A   23/65   61:12   Flag (f)   Grid (g)`, and nothing else, so
 * XP, the rank, due counts and the streak stay out of sight. Its text equals `examBarText`; the gaps are text nodes
 * that the flex layout does not render, so the bar spaces its parts with `gap` instead.
 */
function FocusBar({ info }: { info: NonNullable<typeof exam.value> }) {
  const controls = examControls.value;
  return (
    <header class="topbar focus">
      <div class="topbar-inner focus-bar">
        <span class="exam-label">{info.label}</span>
        {BAR_GAP}
        <span class="exam-pos">
          {examPosition.value}/{info.total}
        </span>
        {BAR_GAP}
        <span class="exam-clock" role="timer" aria-live="off">
          {formatClock(Date.parse(info.deadline) - examTick.value)}
        </span>
        {BAR_GAP}
        <button
          class="topbar-button"
          type="button"
          aria-pressed={controls?.flagged ?? false}
          onClick={() => controls?.flag()}
        >
          Flag (f)
        </button>
        {BAR_GAP}
        <button
          class="topbar-button"
          type="button"
          aria-expanded={controls?.gridOpen ?? false}
          onClick={() => controls?.grid()}
        >
          Grid (g)
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

/** The site bar: links, the due count, the streak, Progress and Search, over the level line. */
function SiteBar() {
  const ready = storeReady.value;
  const due = ready ? (queue.value?.items.length ?? 0) : 0;
  const days = ready ? (streak.value?.current ?? 0) : 0;
  const xp = ready ? (memory.value?.xp ?? null) : null;
  const flash = useXpFlash(xp);
  return (
    <header class="topbar">
      <nav class="topbar-inner" aria-label="Site">
        <a class="brand" href="#/">
          CCP
        </a>
        <a href="#/missions">Missions</a>
        {(due > 0 || days > 0) && (
          <span class="topbar-stats">
            {due > 0 && (
              <button
                class="topbar-button"
                type="button"
                aria-label={dueLabel(due)}
                onClick={() => navigate(toHash({ kind: 'review' }))}
              >
                {due}
                <span class="topbar-word"> due</span>
              </button>
            )}
            {due > 0 && days > 0 && (
              <span class="topbar-sep" aria-hidden="true">
                ·
              </span>
            )}
            {days > 0 && (
              <button
                class="topbar-button"
                type="button"
                aria-label={streakLabel(days)}
                onClick={openProgress}
              >
                {days}d
              </button>
            )}
          </span>
        )}
        <button class="topbar-button" type="button" onClick={openProgress}>
          Progress
        </button>
        <button
          class="search"
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            overlay.value = 'palette';
          }}
        >
          Search <kbd>/</kbd>
        </button>
        {flash !== null && (
          <span key={flash.key} class="xp-flash" aria-hidden="true">
            +{flash.gain}
          </span>
        )}
      </nav>
      <div class="level-line" aria-hidden="true">
        <span class="level-fill" style={{ width: `${xp === null ? 0 : levelProgress(xp) * 100}%` }} />
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

Replace `src/app/App.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { effect } from '@preact/signals';
import { useEffect } from 'preact/hooks';
import { ENGINE_VERSION } from '../engine/replay.ts';
import { KV_KEYS } from '../store/kv.ts';
import { startClock } from './clock.ts';
import { index, loadError, loadIndex } from './content.ts';
import { examTick, openExam, settleExam } from './exam-settle.ts';
import { useKeyboard } from './keyboard.ts';
import { watchRanks } from './memory.ts';
import { events, initStore, kvRecords, progress, saveError, storeReady, writeKv } from './progress.ts';
import { Help } from './screens/Help.tsx';
import { Palette } from './screens/Palette.tsx';
import { Progress } from './screens/Progress.tsx';
import { Screen } from './screens/Screen.tsx';
import { TopBar } from './screens/TopBar.tsx';
import { overlay, route } from './state.ts';
import { rulesLine } from './topbar.ts';

/** The shell: the top bar, the screen for the route once the content index has loaded, and the open dialog. */
export function App() {
  useEffect(() => {
    loadIndex();
    void initStore();
    return startClock();
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => watchRanks(), []);
  useExamTicker();
  useKeyboard();
  return (
    <>
      <TopBar />
      <SaveError />
      <RulesUpdated />
      <main id="main" class="page" tabIndex={-1}>
        <Body />
      </main>
      {overlay.value === 'palette' && <Palette />}
      {overlay.value === 'help' && <Help />}
      {overlay.value === 'progress' && <Progress />}
    </>
  );
}

/**
 * The exam clock: while any boss or mock is open, wherever the route is, ticks `examTick` every second and settles the
 * exam once its deadline has passed, also for an exam already past it when the log loads (spec 4.3).
 */
function useExamTicker(): void {
  useEffect(
    () =>
      effect(() => {
        if (!storeReady.value) return;
        const open = openExam(progress.value);
        if (open === null || open.deadline === null) return;
        const { sid } = open;
        const deadline = Date.parse(open.deadline);
        const tick = (): void => {
          const at = Date.now();
          examTick.value = at;
          if (at >= deadline) void settleExam(sid, new Date(at));
        };
        tick();
        const timer = setInterval(tick, 1000);
        return () => clearInterval(timer);
      }),
    [],
  );
}

const openProgress = (): void => {
  overlay.value = 'progress';
};

/** The banner of spec 8.1 while writes fail; the log still lives in memory, so an export saves it. */
function SaveError() {
  const message = saveError.value;
  if (message === null) return null;
  return (
    <div class="banner" role="alert">
      <span>{message}</span>
      <button class="button" type="button" onClick={openProgress}>
        Export
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

const stampEngine = (): void => {
  void writeKv(KV_KEYS.engineVersion, ENGINE_VERSION);
};

/** The one line of spec 8.2 after `ENGINE_VERSION` changes; an empty log takes the new version silently. */
function RulesUpdated() {
  const state = rulesLine({
    ready: storeReady.value,
    stored: kvRecords.value[KV_KEYS.engineVersion]?.value,
    events: events.value.length,
  });
  useEffect(() => {
    if (state === 'stamp') stampEngine();
  }, [state]);
  if (state !== 'show') return null;
  return (
    <div class="banner banner-info" role="status">
      <span>Rules updated, progress recalculated.</span>
      <button class="link-button" type="button" onClick={stampEngine}>
        Dismiss
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function Body() {
  const error = loadError.value;
  const idx = index.value;
  if (error !== null) return <p role="alert">{error}</p>;
  if (idx === null) return <p class="muted">Loading</p>;
  return <Screen route={route.value} idx={idx} />;
}
```

## Task 19: Results, error review and the mock list

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/screens/Results.tsx`, `src/app/screens/Mocks.tsx`, `src/app/results.ts`, `src/app/dates.ts`, `tests/unit/results.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/app/screens/Screen.tsx`, `src/ui/base.css`
- Test: `tests/unit/results.test.ts`

Design notes:
- Results `#/r/<sid>` show raw percentages only, never a scaled score (spec 3.4 K9); `scoreOf` rounds the percentage down, as readiness does, so a shown 80 always means at least 80 percent.
- A mock shows how many items were scored (a frozen form scores only its live items, spec 4.3) and that the real exam adds 15 unscored items, `Time ran out.` when the deadline settled it, `+150 XP` on the first attempt of a form (read from `xpByEvent` on the submit event's id), and one domain bar per domain in `--d1` to `--d4`.
- A boss shows `bossLine`: `Passed. +100 XP` on the first pass of the module, `Passed.` on a later pass, and otherwise the pass mark and the 20 h cooldown (spec 4.2).
- `p.badge-line` names the badges whose earning event is one of the session's events (its answers or its submit), so a mock that earns Pass Mark says `Badge: Pass Mark` (spec 3.10).
- Error review lists every item not answered right, unanswered ones included, with confident errors (not flagged) first and flagged ones after, each in session order (spec 4.4); each shows the stem, the picked and the right options with `Your answer` and `Correct answer` tags and their why, the auto tag `Changed right to wrong` when an earlier pick in `changes` was right, and three `aria-pressed` cause buttons that record a `cause` event, the latest of which replay keeps (Task 8).
- The cause buttons sit in a `fieldset` with a visually hidden legend `Why was this wrong?`, the accessible group Biome asks for.
- The page loads its question text with `examQuestions` (Task 18): the form chunk of every qid listed in `index.exam.items` through `ensureExamForm`, and for a boss the module chunks of its module and every earlier one; exam item text shows only here, after the exam was submitted.
- An unknown sid, or a sid that is not a submitted boss or mock, shows `This result is not in this browser.` with a link `Back to Home`.
- The mock list shows one row per form the index lists (`Not taken`, `In progress` while that form's mock is open, or `Taken Oct 27 · 52/65 (80%)` linking to its results), the empty-state line `4 exam forms. Best after about 7 bosses.` until any mock starts, and the `Remix mock` row (`Practice only`) once all four forms have a first attempt.
- `shortDate` moves to `src/app/dates.ts` and reads a study day in UTC (`Oct 14`), in the en-US style of the formatter `Progress.tsx` has today; Task 22 moves Progress onto it.
- After this task only the focus routes use `Pending`; Task 21 points them at the runner and deletes `src/app/screens/Pending.tsx`.
- `MOCKS_EMPTY` lives in `results.ts`, so the empty-state line of spec 7.7 is matched by a unit test (P3-139).
- The `result` builder of `results.test.ts` carries `day`, which Task 8 makes a required field of `ExamResult`.
- New copy beyond the brief: `In progress` on the mock list, `Not answered.` under an unanswered wrong item, and `This question is not in this version of the content.` for an item whose text no longer ships.
- Covers P3-073, P3-074, P3-079 (the results part), P3-086, P3-087, P3-088, P3-090, P3-091, P3-133 (the results line), P3-139 and P3-188.

const answer = (picked: string[], changes: string[][]): AnswerRecord => ({

describe('error review (spec 4.4)', () => {

});

    expect(bossLine(result(15, 15), false, 0)).toBe('Passed.');

      ['pass-mark', { day: '2026-10-27', eventId: '01JA00000000000000000000T1' }],
      ['first-brew', { day: '2026-10-10', eventId: '01JA00000000000000000000B1' }],
      ['full-dress-rehearsal', { day: '2026-10-27', eventId: '01JA00000000000000000000T1' }],
    ]);
    const inside = new Set(['01JA00000000000000000000A1', '01JA00000000000000000000T1']);
    expect(badgesIn(badges, inside).sort()).toEqual(['full-dress-rehearsal', 'pass-mark']);
    expect(badgesIn(badges, new Set())).toEqual([]);

});

describe('dates and the mock list', () => {

    expect(shortDate('2026-11-01')).toBe('Nov 1');

        correct: 52,
        total: 65,
      }),
    ).toBe('Taken Oct 27 · 52/65 (80%)');
  });
});
