- [ ] **Step 3: Write the date and results helpers**

Create `src/app/dates.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The short date of lists and lines (`Oct 14`), read in UTC so a study day never shifts with the time zone.
 * The other style is `examDateLabel` (`Sat 14 Nov`) in home.ts; no third style exists.
 */
export function shortDate(day: string): string {
  return new Date(`${day}T00:00:00.000Z`).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
    month: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
    timeZone: 'UTC',
  });
}
```

Create `src/app/results.ts`:

```ts
/** Pure copy and ordering for the results page and the mock list (spec 4.3 and 4.4), tested without a browser. */
import type { QuestionView } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import type { BadgeEarned } from '../engine/badges.ts';
import { bossPassMark } from '../engine/memory.ts';
import type { MockAttempt } from '../engine/mock.ts';
import type { AnswerRecord, ExamResult } from '../engine/replay.ts';
import { shortDate } from './dates.ts';
import { isCorrect } from './runner.ts';

/** The three cause taps of error review, in the order of the `cause` event's values (spec 4.4). */
export const CAUSE_LABELS = {
  unknown: "Didn't know",
  confused: 'Confused it with another',
  misread: 'Misread',
} as const;

/** Wrong items with confident errors first: unflagged, then flagged, each in session order (spec 4.4). */
export function wrongOrder(items: readonly { qid: string; flagged: boolean }[]): string[] {
  return [...items.filter((i) => !i.flagged), ...items.filter((i) => i.flagged)].map((i) => i.qid);
}

/** True when an earlier pick of this answer was right and the final one is wrong. */
export function changedRightToWrong(record: AnswerRecord, q: QuestionView): boolean {
  return !record.correct && record.changes.some((earlier) => isCorrect(q, earlier));
}

/** A raw score, `52/65 (80%)`, with the percentage rounded down so 80 always means at least 80 (spec 3.9). */
export function scoreOf(correct: number, total: number): string {
  return `${correct}/${total} (${total === 0 ? 0 : Math.floor((100 * correct) / total)}%)`;
}

export function scoreLine(r: ExamResult): string {
  return `Score ${scoreOf(r.correct, r.total)}`;
}

/** The boss verdict: XP only on the first pass of a module (spec 3.2); a fail names the pass mark and the cooldown. */
export function bossLine(r: ExamResult, firstPass: boolean, xp: number): string {
  if (r.correct >= bossPassMark(r.total)) return firstPass && xp > 0 ? `Passed. +${xp} XP` : 'Passed.';
  return `Not passed: ${bossPassMark(r.total)}/${r.total} passes. The next boss opens in 20 h.`;
}

/** How many items a mock scored, and the real exam's unscored items (spec 4.3). */
export function scoredLine(total: number): string {
  return `${total} items scored. The real exam adds 15 unscored items you cannot tell apart.`;
}

export function domainLine(name: string, correct: number, total: number): string {
  return `${name} ${scoreOf(correct, total)}`;
}

/** The line under a session's end for badges earned inside it: `Badge: First Brew · Pass Mark`. */
export function badgeEarnedLine(names: readonly string[]): string {
  return `Badge: ${names.join(' · ')}`;
}

/** The badges whose earning event is one of the session's events, in the order they were earned. */
export function badgesIn(badges: ReadonlyMap<string, BadgeEarned>, eventIds: ReadonlySet<string>): string[] {
  return [...badges.entries()]
    .filter(([, earned]) => eventIds.has(earned.eventId))
    .sort(([, a], [, b]) => (a.eventId < b.eventId ? -1 : a.eventId > b.eventId ? 1 : 0))
    .map(([id]) => id);
}

/** The mock list's line until the first mock starts (spec 7.7). */
export const MOCKS_EMPTY = '4 exam forms. Best after about 7 bosses.';

/** A taken form's row on the mock list: `Taken Oct 27 · 52/65 (80%)`. */
export function takenLine(attempt: MockAttempt): string {
  return `Taken ${shortDate(attempt.day)} · ${scoreOf(attempt.correct, attempt.total)}`;
}
```

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { ContentIndex, QuestionView } from '../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { bossPassMark } from '../../engine/memory.ts';
import { domainBars } from '../../engine/mock.ts';
import { type Cause, type ExamResult, examsOf, type SessionState } from '../../engine/replay.ts';
import { useTitle } from '../../ui/useTitle.ts';
import { examLabel } from '../exam.ts';
import { examQuestions } from '../exam-settle.ts';
import { badges, memory, memoryContent } from '../memory.ts';
import { progress, record, storeReady } from '../progress.ts';
import {
  badgeEarnedLine,
  badgesIn,
  bossLine,
  CAUSE_LABELS,
  changedRightToWrong,
  domainLine,
  scoredLine,
  scoreLine,
  wrongOrder,
} from '../results.ts';
import { toHash } from '../router.ts';

export const NO_RESULT = 'This result is not in this browser.';

/** The results of a boss or mock `#/r/<sid>`: score, verdict or domain bars, then error review (spec 4.3 and 4.4). */
export function Results({ idx, sid }: { idx: ContentIndex; sid: string }) {
  const p = progress.value;
  const session = p.sessions.get(sid);
  const result = p.exams.get(sid);
  const title = session === undefined ? 'Results' : `${examLabel(session, idx)} results`;
  useTitle(title);
  if (!storeReady.value) return <p class="muted">Loading</p>;
  if (session === undefined || result === undefined) {
    return (
      <>
        <h1>Results</h1>
        <p>{NO_RESULT}</p>
        <p>
          <a href="#/">Back to Home</a>
        </p>
      </>
    );
  }
  return <ResultsBody idx={idx} session={session} result={result} title={title} />;
}

function ResultsBody({
  idx,
  session,
  result,
  title,
}: {
  idx: ContentIndex;
  session: SessionState;
  result: ExamResult;
  title: string;
}) {
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<ReadonlyMap<string, QuestionView> | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    void examQuestions(session).then((loaded) => {
      if (live) setQuestions(loaded ?? new Map());
    });
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, [session]);
  const xp = memory.value?.xpByEvent.get(result.submitId) ?? 0;
  const eventIds = new Set([result.submitId, ...[...session.answers.values()].map((a) => a.id)]);
  const earned = badgesIn(badges.value, eventIds).map(
    (id) => idx.badges.find((b) => b.id === id)?.name ?? id,
  );
  const module = Number(session.scope.slice(1));
  const firstPass =
    result.mode === 'boss' &&
    examsOf(progress.value, 'boss', session.scope).find((r) => r.correct >= bossPassMark(r.total))?.sid ===
      session.sid;
  const wrong = wrongOrder(
    session.qids
      .filter((qid) => session.answers.get(qid)?.correct !== true)
      .map((qid) => ({ qid, flagged: session.answers.get(qid)?.flagged === true })),
  );

  return (
    <>
      <h1>{title}</h1>
      <p class="score-line">{scoreLine(result)}</p>
      {result.mode === 'mock' ? (
        <MockSummary idx={idx} session={session} result={result} xp={xp} />
      ) : (
        <p>{bossLine(result, firstPass, xp)}</p>
      )}
      {earned.length > 0 && <p class="badge-line">{badgeEarnedLine(earned)}</p>}
      <h2>Wrong answers</h2>
      {wrong.length === 0 ? (
        <p>No wrong answers.</p>
      ) : questions === null ? (
        <p class="muted">Loading</p>
      ) : (
        <ol class="wrong-list">
          {wrong.map((qid) => (
            <WrongItem key={qid} sid={session.sid} qid={qid} q={questions.get(qid)} session={session} />
          ))}
        </ol>
      )}
      <ul class="end-links">
        <li>
          <a href="#/">Back to Home</a>
        </li>
        {result.mode === 'boss' && (
          <li>
            <a href={toHash({ kind: 'module', module })}>Back to Module {module}</a>
          </li>
        )}
      </ul>
    </>
  );
}

/** A mock's scored-items note, the timeout line, first-attempt XP and the domain bars (raw percentages only). */
function MockSummary({
  idx,
  session,
  result,
  xp,
}: {
  idx: ContentIndex;
  session: SessionState;
  result: ExamResult;
  xp: number;
}) {
  const taskOf = memoryContent.value?.examTasks ?? new Map<string, string>();
  return (
    <>
      <p class="muted">{scoredLine(result.total)}</p>
      {result.timedOut && <p>Time ran out.</p>}
      {xp > 0 && <p>+{xp} XP</p>}
      <div class="domain-bars">
        {domainBars(result, session, taskOf).map((row) => {
          const name = idx.blueprint.domains.find((d) => d.id === row.domain)?.name ?? `Domain ${row.domain}`;
          const width = row.total === 0 ? 0 : (100 * row.correct) / row.total;
          return (
            <div key={row.domain} class="domain-bar">
              <span class="domain-text">{domainLine(name, row.correct, row.total)}</span>
              <span class="domain-track" aria-hidden="true">
                <span
                  class="domain-fill"
                  style={{ width: `${width}%`, background: `var(--d${row.domain})` }}
                />
              </span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

function WrongItem({
  sid,
  qid,
  q,
  session,
}: {
  sid: string;
  qid: string;
  q: QuestionView | undefined;
  session: SessionState;
}) {
  const answer = session.answers.get(qid);
  const cause = progress.value.causes.get(`${sid} ${qid}`) ?? null;
  const picked = (answer?.picked ?? []) as string[];
  const choose = (value: Cause): void => {
    void record({ t: 'cause', sid, qid, cause: value });
  };
  return (
    <li class="wrong-item" data-qid={qid}>
      <p class="stem">{q?.stem ?? 'This question is not in this version of the content.'}</p>
      {q !== undefined && (
        <ul class="review">
          {q.options
            .filter((o) => o.correct || picked.includes(o.id))
            .map((o) => (
              <li key={o.id} class={`review-item ${o.correct ? 'is-correct' : 'is-wrong'}`}>
                <p class="review-line">
                  <span class="option-text">{o.text}</span>
                </p>
                <p class="tags">
                  {picked.includes(o.id) && (
                    <span class={`tag ${o.correct ? 'ok' : 'bad'}`}>Your answer</span>
                  )}
                  {o.correct && <span class="tag ok">Correct answer</span>}
                </p>
                <p class="why">{o.why}</p>
              </li>
            ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {picked.length === 0 && <p class="muted">Not answered.</p>}
      {answer !== undefined && q !== undefined && changedRightToWrong(answer, q) && (
        <p class="tags">
          <span class="tag auto">Changed right to wrong</span>
        </p>
      )}
      <fieldset class="causes">
        <legend class="visually-hidden">Why was this wrong?</legend>
        {(Object.keys(CAUSE_LABELS) as Cause[]).map((key) => (
          <button
            key={key}
            type="button"
            class="button button-quiet cause"
            aria-pressed={cause === key}
            onClick={() => choose(key)}
          >
            {CAUSE_LABELS[key]}
          </button>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
    </li>
  );
}
```

Create `src/app/screens/Mocks.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ContentIndex } from '../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { FORM_IDS } from '../../../schemas/ids.ts';
import { useTitle } from '../../ui/useTitle.ts';
import { openExam } from '../exam-settle.ts';
import { attempts, taken } from '../memory.ts';
import { progress, storeReady } from '../progress.ts';
import { MOCKS_EMPTY, takenLine } from '../results.ts';
import { type MockRoute, toHash } from '../router.ts';

/** The mock list `#/s/mock` (spec 7.3): one row per form, then the remix once all four are taken. */
export function Mocks({ idx }: { idx: ContentIndex }) {
  useTitle('Mock exams');
  const firsts = attempts.value;
  const started = taken.value;
  const open = storeReady.value ? openExam(progress.value) : null;
  const rows = FORM_IDS.filter((f) => idx.exam.forms.some((m) => m.id === f)).map((form) => {
    const route = toHash({ kind: 'mock', form: form.toLowerCase() as MockRoute });
    const first = firsts.find((a) => a.form === form);
    if (first !== undefined)
      return { form, href: toHash({ kind: 'results', sid: first.sid }), meta: takenLine(first) };
    const running = open !== null && open.mode === 'mock' && open.form === form;
    return { form, href: route, meta: running ? 'In progress' : 'Not taken' };
  });
  return (
    <>
      <h1>Mock exams</h1>
      <p class="lede">65 questions in 90 minutes, scored like the real exam.</p>
      {started.size === 0 && <p class="muted">{MOCKS_EMPTY}</p>}
      <ul class="rows">
        {rows.map((r) => (
          <li key={r.form}>
            <a class="row" href={r.href} data-nav-item>
              <span class="row-title">Mock {r.form}</span>
              <span class="row-meta">{r.meta}</span>
            </a>
          </li>
        ))}
        {firsts.length === FORM_IDS.length && (
          <li>
            <a class="row" href={toHash({ kind: 'mock', form: 'remix' })} data-nav-item>
              <span class="row-title">Remix mock</span>
              <span class="row-meta">Practice only</span>
            </a>
          </li>
        )}
      </ul>
    </>
  );
}
```

Replace `src/app/screens/Screen.tsx` with:

```tsx
import type { ContentIndex } from '../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { pad2, type Route } from '../router.ts';
import { Home } from './Home.tsx';
import { lazyScreen } from './Lazy.tsx';
import { LessonPage } from './LessonPage.tsx';
import { MissionPage } from './MissionPage.tsx';
import { MissionsPage } from './MissionsPage.tsx';
import { ModulePage } from './ModulePage.tsx';
import { NotFound } from './NotFound.tsx';
import { Runner } from './Runner.tsx';

// Exam screens load on first use; Task 21 replaces the focus quiz placeholder with the runner.
const ExamScreen = lazyScreen(() => import('./Exam.tsx').then((m) => m.Exam));
const MocksScreen = lazyScreen(() => import('./Mocks.tsx').then((m) => m.Mocks));
const ResultsScreen = lazyScreen(() => import('./Results.tsx').then((m) => m.Results));
const FocusScreen = lazyScreen(() => import('./Pending.tsx').then((m) => m.Pending));

/** The screen for a route; the content index is always loaded by the time this renders. */
export function Screen({ route, idx }: { route: Route; idx: ContentIndex }) {
  switch (route.kind) {
    case 'home':
      return <Home idx={idx} />;
    case 'module':
      return <ModulePage idx={idx} module={route.module} />;
    case 'lesson':
      return <LessonPage idx={idx} module={route.module} slug={route.slug} anchor={route.anchor} />;
    case 'missions':
      return <MissionsPage idx={idx} />;
    case 'mission':
      return <MissionPage idx={idx} id={route.id} />;
    // Keyed by the route object, which every arrival replaces, so "Another quiz" on the quiz route starts afresh.
    case 'check':
      return <Runner key={route} idx={idx} mode="check" scope={`m${pad2(route.module)}/${route.slug}`} />;
    case 'quiz':
      return <Runner key={route} idx={idx} mode="quiz" scope={`m${pad2(route.module)}`} />;
    case 'review':
      return <Runner key={route} idx={idx} mode="review" scope="review" />;
    case 'boss':
      return <ExamScreen key={route} idx={idx} route={route} />;
    case 'mocks':
      return <MocksScreen idx={idx} />;
    case 'mock':
      return <ExamScreen key={route} idx={idx} route={route} />;
    case 'focus':
      return <FocusScreen key={route} title="Focus quiz" />;
    case 'results':
      return <ResultsScreen key={route} idx={idx} sid={route.sid} />;
    case 'not-found':
      return <NotFound hash={route.hash} />;
  }
}
```

## Task 20: Home lines, hexes and the rank in the top bar

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/app/home.ts`, `src/app/screens/Home.tsx`, `src/ui/HexMap.tsx`, `src/app/topbar.ts`, `src/app/screens/TopBar.tsx`, `src/app/App.tsx`, `src/ui/base.css`, `tests/unit/home.test.ts`, `tests/unit/topbar.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/practice.spec.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/home.test.ts`, `tests/unit/topbar.test.ts`

Design notes:
- Home order (spec 7.2): `h1`, the Next block, the streak reset line, the hex map, the readiness line with the weakest line, the next-rank line, the extras line (only when Next is `done`), one reminder line, the Missions link, then `Mock exams`.
- The reminder priority becomes go/no-go, then the exam date question, then the backup reminder, then the first-run line (spec 4.6 and 7.5), decided by the pure `reminderOf` so the order is tested without a browser.
- `readinessLine` floors `100 * value`, so a shown 80 always means `value >= 0.80` (Decisions); the two spaces before the label are kept on screen by `white-space: pre-wrap`.
- A passed boss draws `polygon.hex-ring`, a thin graphite ring 4 units outside the hex, in the gap between hexes, that never goes away (spec 3.8), and a ready boss draws `span.hex-dot`; both say the same in the hex's accessible name, so no state lives in color alone.
- The rank button sits inside `span.topbar-stats` after the due count and the streak, as the spec 7.2 bar reads, and shows the plan name; its accessible name starts with the full name (WCAG 2.5.3).
- `rank` and `rankFacts` (Task 17) are never null, so Home and the top bar only guard on `storeReady` and on `ranks` being non-empty.
- Under 480 px the rank shows its `short` name and stands in for the `Progress` button it opens, and the `Missions` link waits on Home, so the phone bar reads `18 · 9d · Business+` (spec 7.2 Mobile) and never runs past the right gutter.
- The rank-up line uses the existing `.banner.banner-info` and hides during an exam, like every other top bar extra.
- This task reads the Task 17 signals `bosses`, `readinessNow`, `rank`, `rankFacts`, `ranks`, `rankUp` and `extraItems`, the Task 13 `goNoGo`, the Task 14 `nextRankLine` and `Extra`, and `exam` from `state.ts`.

```ts
const readinessOf = (over: Partial<Readiness>): Readiness => ({
  value: 0.62,
  label: 'Close',
  capped: false,
  memory: 0.6,
  mock: 0.64,
  domains: { '1': 0.6, '2': 0.6, '3': 0.6, '4': 0.6 },
  weakest: null,
  at: '2026-11-14T04:00:00.000Z',
  ...over,
});

describe('readinessLine', () => {

    expect(readinessLine(readinessOf({}))).toBe('Readiness 62  Close');
    expect(readinessLine(readinessOf({ value: 0.85, label: 'Ready' }))).toBe('Readiness 85  Ready');
  });

  it('shows 79 for 0.799 and 80 for 0.80', () => {
    expect(readinessLine(readinessOf({ value: 0.799 }))).toBe('Readiness 79  Close');
    expect(readinessLine(readinessOf({ value: 0.8 }))).toBe('Readiness 80  Close');
  });

});

describe('Home lines', () => {

    expect(weakestLine({ id: '2.3', short: 'access management' })).toBe(
      'Weakest: 2.3 access management (focus quiz)',
    );

  it('writes the go/no-go line with the exam date', () => {

      'Exam Sat 14 Nov, readiness Close. Rescheduling closes 24 h before the appointment.',
    );
    expect(goNoGoLine('2026-11-14', 'Building')).toBe(
      'Exam Sat 14 Nov, readiness Building. Rescheduling closes 24 h before the appointment.',
    );
  });

  it('joins the extras with a bar', () => {
    expect(extrasLine([{ label: 'Boss: Networking' }, { label: 'Mock B' }])).toBe(
      'Optional today: Boss: Networking  |  Mock B',
    );
    expect(extrasLine([{ label: 'Mock A' }])).toBe('Optional today: Mock A');
    expect(`${EXTRAS_LEAD}Boss: Networking${EXTRAS_JOIN}Mock B`).toBe(
      'Optional today: Boss: Networking  |  Mock B',
    );
  });

  it('links a boss extra to its boss and a mock extra to its form', () => {
    const modules = [{ n: 5, short: 'Networking' }];
    expect(extraLink({ kind: 'boss', module: 5 }, modules)).toEqual({
      label: 'Boss: Networking',
      href: '#/s/boss/m05',
    });
    expect(extraLink({ kind: 'mock', form: 'B' }, modules)).toEqual({ label: 'Mock B', href: '#/s/mock/b' });
  });
});

describe('reminderOf', () => {
  const none = { 'go-no-go': false, 'exam-ask': false, backup: false, 'first-run': false };

  it('puts go/no-go first, then the exam date question, the backup reminder and the first-run line', () => {
    expect(reminderOf({ 'go-no-go': true, 'exam-ask': true, backup: true, 'first-run': true })).toBe(
      'go-no-go',
    );
    expect(reminderOf({ ...none, 'exam-ask': true, backup: true, 'first-run': true })).toBe('exam-ask');
    expect(reminderOf({ ...none, backup: true, 'first-run': true })).toBe('backup');
    expect(reminderOf({ ...none, 'first-run': true })).toBe('first-run');
  });

  it('shows nothing when no line is due', () => {
    expect(reminderOf(none)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

Replace `hexLabel` with:

```ts
/** The accessible name of a hex (spec 7.3), which also names a passed or ready boss, so the ring and dot are not the only signal. */
export function hexLabel(
  module: number,
  short: string,
  mastery: number,
  band: HexBand,
  boss: 'passed' | 'ready' | null,
): string {
  const base = `Module ${module} ${short}, mastery ${mastery}, ${BAND_LABEL[band]}`;
  return boss === null ? base : `${base}, boss ${boss}`;
}
```

Append:

```ts
/** `Readiness 62  Close` (spec 7.2); the number is floored, so a shown 80 always means at least 0.80. */
export function readinessLine(r: Readiness): string {
  if (r.value === null) return 'Readiness -';
  const shown = Math.floor(100 * r.value);
  return r.capped
    ? `Readiness ${shown}  capped until your first unseen mock`
    : `Readiness ${shown}  ${r.label}`;
}

/** The link under the readiness line to a focus quiz on the weakest task statement (spec 3.9). */
export function weakestLine(task: { id: string; short: string }): string {
  return `Weakest: ${task.id} ${task.short} (focus quiz)`;
}

/** The go/no-go reminder (spec 3.9), with the date as `examDateLabel` writes it. */
export function goNoGoLine(examDate: string, label: ReadinessLabel): string {
  return `Exam ${examDateLabel(examDate)}, readiness ${label}. Rescheduling closes 24 h before the appointment.`;
}

/** How the extras line starts, and what stands between two extras; Home renders the same text with links. */
export const EXTRAS_LEAD = 'Optional today: ';
export const EXTRAS_JOIN = '  |  ';

/** The optional extras under "Done for today" (spec 4.6), at most two. */
export function extrasLine(items: readonly { label: string }[]): string {
  return `${EXTRAS_LEAD}${items.map((item) => item.label).join(EXTRAS_JOIN)}`;
}

/** The label and link of one extra: a ready boss or the next unseen mock form. */
export function extraLink(
  extra: Extra,
  modules: readonly { n: number; short: string }[],
): { label: string; href: string } {
  if (extra.kind === 'boss') {
    const short = modules.find((m) => m.n === extra.module)?.short ?? '';
    return { label: `Boss: ${short}`, href: `#/s/boss/m${pad2(extra.module)}` };
  }
  return { label: `Mock ${extra.form}`, href: `#/s/mock/${extra.form.toLowerCase()}` };
}

/** The reminders Home may show, highest priority first; at most one shows (spec 4.6). */
export type Reminder = 'go-no-go' | 'exam-ask' | 'backup' | 'first-run';
const REMINDERS: readonly Reminder[] = ['go-no-go', 'exam-ask', 'backup', 'first-run'];

/** The one reminder line to show, or null when none is due. */
export function reminderOf(due: Readonly<Record<Reminder, boolean>>): Reminder | null {
  return REMINDERS.find((r) => due[r]) ?? null;
}
```

```ts
/** The rank button's name: the visible plan name first (WCAG 2.5.3), then what it does. */
export function rankLabel(name: string): string {
  return `${name}: rank, open Progress`;
}

/** The one dismissible promotion line under the top bar (spec 3.4): the plan name and its two facts. */
export function rankUpLine(rank: { name: string; facts: readonly string[] }): string {
  return `${rank.name}: ${rank.facts.join(' ')}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Draw the boss ring and dot on the hexes**

Replace `src/ui/HexMap.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { hexLabel } from '../app/home.ts';
import { pad2 } from '../app/router.ts';
import type { HexBand } from '../engine/mastery.ts';

export interface Hex {
  module: number;
  short: string;
  mastery: number;
  band: HexBand;
  /** A passed boss rings the hex for good; a ready boss puts a dot on it (spec 3.8). */
  boss: 'passed' | 'ready' | null;
}

/** A pointy-top hexagon in a 100 by 115.47 box, inset by half its stroke. */
const HEXAGON = '50,2 98,29.7 98,85.8 50,113.5 2,85.8 2,29.7';
/** The same hexagon grown 4 units outward on every side, so a passed boss's thin ring sits in the gap around the hex. */
const RING = '50,-2.6 102,27.4 102,88.1 50,118.1 -2,88.1 -2,27.4';
/** The honeycomb of spec 7.3: rows of 4, 5 and 4 modules. */
const ROWS = [4, 5, 4] as const;

/** The 13 module hexes (spec 3.8 and 7.3), filled in graphite by mastery band; each opens its module. */
export function HexMap({ hexes }: { hexes: readonly Hex[] }) {
  const rows: Hex[][] = [];
  let start = 0;
  for (const size of ROWS) {
    rows.push(hexes.slice(start, start + size));
    start += size;
  }
  return (
    <nav class="hexes" aria-label="Modules">
      {rows.map((row) => (
        <div key={row[0]?.module ?? 0} class="hex-row">
          {row.map((h) => (
            <a
              key={h.module}
              class={`hex hex-${h.band}`}
              href={`#/m/${pad2(h.module)}`}
              data-nav-item
              aria-label={hexLabel(h.module, h.short, h.mastery, h.band, h.boss)}
            >
              <svg viewBox="0 0 100 115.47" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
                <polygon points={HEXAGON} />
                {h.boss === 'passed' && <polygon class="hex-ring" points={RING} />}
              </svg>
              <span class="hex-n">{h.module}</span>
              {h.boss === 'ready' && <span class="hex-dot" aria-hidden="true" />}
            </a>
          ))}
        </div>
      ))}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Rebuild Home**

Replace `src/app/screens/Home.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { ContentIndex } from '../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { studyDay } from '../../engine/day.ts';
import { hexBand } from '../../engine/mastery.ts';
import { nextRankLine } from '../../engine/ranks.ts';
import { goNoGo } from '../../engine/readiness.ts';
import { reminderDue } from '../../store/backup.ts';
import { KV_KEYS } from '../../store/kv.ts';
import { HexMap } from '../../ui/HexMap.tsx';
import { useTitle } from '../../ui/useTitle.ts';
import { now } from '../clock.ts';
import { TARGET_EXAM_DATE } from '../config.ts';
import {
  examAskDue,
  examDateLabel,
  extraLink,
  EXTRAS_JOIN,
  EXTRAS_LEAD,
  goNoGoLine,
  nextLabel,
  nothingDueLine,
  readinessLine,
  reminderOf,
  streakLine,
  weakestLine,
} from '../home.ts';
import {
  bosses,
  examAsk,
  extraItems,
  mastery,
  memory,
  nextUp,
  queue,
  rank,
  rankFacts,
  ranks,
  readinessNow,
  streak,
} from '../memory.ts';
import { events, progress, record, storeReady, writeKv } from '../progress.ts';
import { toHash } from '../router.ts';
import { overlay } from '../state.ts';
import { lastExportAt } from './Progress.tsx';

const DAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
const ISO_DAY = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

/**
 * Home `#/` (spec 7.2): the Next button, the streak reset line, the 13 hexes, readiness, the next rank, the
 * extras, at most one reminder line, and the ways to Missions and the mocks.
 */
export function Home({ idx }: { idx: ContentIndex }) {
  useTitle('Home');
  const byModule = mastery.value;
  const statuses = storeReady.value ? bosses.value : null;
  const hexes = idx.modules.map((m) => {
    const value = byModule?.get(m.n) ?? 0;
    const status = statuses?.get(m.n);
    const passed = status?.passed === true;
    return {
      module: m.n,
      short: m.short,
      mastery: value,
      band: hexBand(value, passed),
      boss: passed ? ('passed' as const) : status?.ready === true ? ('ready' as const) : null,
    };
  });
  return (
    <>
      <h1>AWS Cloud Practitioner Essentials</h1>
      <NextBlock idx={idx} />
      <StreakReset />
      <HexMap hexes={hexes} />
      <ReadinessLines idx={idx} />
      <NextRank />
      <Extras idx={idx} />
      <HomeLine idx={idx} />
      <p>
        <a href="#/missions">Missions: real AWS, done by hand</a>
      </p>
      <p>
        <a href="#/s/mock">Mock exams</a>
      </p>
    </>
  );
}

/** The Next button (spec 4.6), or "Done for today" with when the next reviews fall due. */
function NextBlock({ idx }: { idx: ContentIndex }) {
  // nextUp waits for the stored log, so a returning reader never sees the first-run lesson flash past.
  const action = nextUp.value;
  if (action === null) return <div class="next-block" />;
  const { label, href, minutes } = nextLabel(action, idx, now.value);
  if (href === null) {
    const q = queue.value;
    return (
      <div class="next-block">
        <p class="next-done">{label}</p>
        {q !== null && <p class="muted">{nothingDueLine(q, now.value)}</p>}
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div class="next-block">
      <a class="next" href={href} data-next>
        <span class="next-label">{label}</span>
        <span class="next-meta">
          {minutes !== null && <span>~{minutes} min</span>}
          <kbd class="next-key">Enter</kbd>
        </span>
      </a>
    </div>
  );
}

function StreakReset() {
  const s = storeReady.value ? streak.value : null;
  const line = s === null ? null : streakLine(s);
  return line === null ? null : <p class="muted streak-line">{line}</p>;
}

/** `Readiness 62  Close` and, when a task has at least 3 concepts, the weakest one as a focus quiz link (spec 3.9). */
function ReadinessLines({ idx }: { idx: ContentIndex }) {
  const r = storeReady.value ? readinessNow.value : null;
  if (r === null) return null;
  const weakest = r.weakest;
  const task = weakest === null ? undefined : idx.blueprint.tasks.find((t) => t.id === weakest.task);
  return (
    <>
      <p class="readiness-line">{readinessLine(r)}</p>
      {task !== undefined && (
        <p class="weakest-line">
          <a href={toHash({ kind: 'focus', scope: task.id })}>{weakestLine(task)}</a>
        </p>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

/** What the next rank needs (spec 3.4); nothing at the top rank. */
function NextRank() {
  const shown = storeReady.value && ranks.value.length > 0 ? rank.value : null;
  if (shown === null) return null;
  const r = readinessNow.value;
  const label = r === null || r.value === null ? '-' : r.label;
  const line = nextRankLine(ranks.value, shown, rankFacts.value, label);
  return line === null ? null : <p class="muted rank-line">{line}</p>;
}

/** Optional extras once Next is done for today (spec 4.6): a ready boss, then the next mock. */
function Extras({ idx }: { idx: ContentIndex }) {
  const action = nextUp.value;
  const items = extraItems.value;
  if (action === null || action.kind !== 'done' || items.length === 0) return null;
  const links = items.map((e) => extraLink(e, idx.modules));
  // The visible text equals `extrasLine(links)`, so its tested copy is the copy on screen.
  return (
    <p class="extras-line">
      {EXTRAS_LEAD}
      {links.map((link, i) => (
        <span key={link.href}>
          {i > 0 && <span aria-hidden="true">{EXTRAS_JOIN}</span>}
          <a href={link.href}>{link.label}</a>
        </span>
      ))}
    </p>
  );
}

const openProgress = (): void => {
  overlay.value = 'progress';
};

/**
 * At most one line (spec 4.6): the go/no-go reminder, the exam date question, the backup reminder, or the
 * first-run line, in that order.
 */
function HomeLine({ idx }: { idx: ContentIndex }) {
  if (!storeReady.value) return null;
  const examDate = memory.value?.examDate ?? null;
  const r = readinessNow.value;
  const at = now.value;
  const last = lastExportAt();
  const empty = events.value.length === 0;
  const which = reminderOf({
    'go-no-go': examDate !== null && r !== null && goNoGo({ examDate, today: studyDay(at), readiness: r }),
    'exam-ask': examAskDue({
      idx,
      lessonsDone: progress.value.lessonsDone,
      examDate,
      examAsk: examAsk.value,
    }),
    backup: !empty && reminderDue(events.value, last, at),
    'first-run': empty,
  });
  switch (which) {
    case 'go-no-go':
      return examDate === null || r === null ? null : (
        <p class="home-line go-no-go">{goNoGoLine(examDate, r.label)}</p>
      );
    case 'exam-ask':
      return <ExamQuestion />;
    case 'backup': {
      const days = last === null ? 0 : Math.floor((at.getTime() - new Date(last).getTime()) / DAY_MS);
      return (
        <p class="home-line">
          {last === null ? 'No backup yet.' : `Last backup ${days} days ago.`}{' '}
          <button class="link-button" type="button" onClick={openProgress}>
            Export a backup
          </button>
        </p>
      );
    }
    case 'first-run':
      return (
        <p class="home-line">
          Progress lives in this browser.{' '}
          <button class="link-button" type="button" onClick={openProgress}>
            Import a backup
          </button>
        </p>
      );
    case null:
      return null;
  }
}

/** The one-time exam date question (spec 7.5); Save writes a settings event, Not now a kv mark. */
function ExamQuestion() {
  const [date, setDate] = useState(TARGET_EXAM_DATE);
  const valid = ISO_DAY.test(date);
  return (
    <form
      class="home-line exam-ask"
      onSubmit={(event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (valid) void record({ t: 'settings', patch: { examDate: date } });
      }}
    >
      <p class="exam-ask-question">
        No exam date yet. The plan targets{' '}
        <span class="exam-ask-date">{examDateLabel(TARGET_EXAM_DATE)}.</span>
      </p>
      <div class="exam-ask-row">
        <label class="date-field">
          Exam date
          <input type="date" value={date} required onInput={(event) => setDate(event.currentTarget.value)} />
        </label>
        <button class="button" type="submit" disabled={!valid}>
          Save
        </button>
        <button class="link-button" type="button" onClick={() => void writeKv(KV_KEYS.examAsk, 'dismissed')}>
          Not now
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```css
/* Phase 3 Home (spec 7.2): readiness, the next rank, the extras, the go/no-go line, boss rings and dots. */
.readiness-line {

  font-weight: 600;

.extras-line {
  margin: 4px 0;

  stroke-width: 2;

  .topbar-stats:has(.topbar-rank) ~ .topbar-progress {

}
```

## Task 21: Module page, recall dots and the focus quiz

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/module.ts`, `tests/unit/module.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/app/screens/ModulePage.tsx`, `src/app/screens/LessonPage.tsx`, `src/app/screens/Runner.tsx`, `src/app/screens/Screen.tsx`, `src/app/runner.ts`, `src/ui/base.css`, `tests/unit/runner.test.ts`
- Delete: `src/app/screens/Pending.tsx`
- Test: `tests/unit/module.test.ts`, `tests/unit/runner.test.ts`

Design notes:
- The module page follows spec 7.3: the mastery line, the lessons, the tested concepts weakest first with their next-due study day, the practice links with the focus quiz, then the boss with its cooldown.
- `weakestConcepts` and `bossLineOf` read the zone from `now` with `tzOffset`, as the rest of `src/app` does, because a due date is a study day and that needs the 04:00 boundary in the reader's zone.
- `bossLineOf` adds one line the brief left open, for an unlocked boss with no cooldown: `15 questions in 21 minutes; 12 right to pass.`, so the Boss section always says something before its button.
- A passed boss that is cooling down after a retry shows both brief lines, `Passed on Oct 13. Next try in 18 h.`, so the hidden Start button always has its reason.
- Recall bands follow spec 3.6: no card is `not studied`, below 0.70 `weak`, below 0.90 `fading`, else `strong`; each band has its own fill (empty, quarter, half, full), so the dot never relies on color (spec 7.6).
- The dot goes right after its `h3[id]`, never inside it, so the heading's accessible name and anchor stay as they were.
- A focus quiz is a 10-item quiz (spec 4.1) over a task's tested concepts (`#/s/focus/2.3`) or a module's 10 weakest tested concepts (`#/s/focus/m05`); it counts like a quiz and pays the bet.
- Every learn pool is Task 17's `practicePoolOf` (the pure twin of the old `poolOf`, with `type` and `module` from Task 11) passed through `learnPoolNow`, and `shippedOf` adds the loaded chunks of taken forms, so a taken-form item that a selector picks also renders (spec 4.3, P3-079).
- A module quiz keeps to its module: exam items of other modules are filtered out by `PoolQuestion.module` (Task 11).
- The runner's end line gains `p.badge-line` (`Badge: First Brew`) when `badgesIn` (Task 19) finds a badge earned by an event of the session (spec 3.10).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing module tests**

Create `tests/unit/module.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  bossLineOf,
  conceptMeta,
  masteryLine,
  RECALL_LABEL,
  recallBand,
  weakestConcepts,
} from '../../src/app/module.ts';
import type { BossStatus } from '../../src/engine/boss.ts';
import type { MemoryState } from '../../src/engine/memory.ts';
import type { SrsCard } from '../../src/engine/srs.ts';

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-14T10:00:00.000Z');

const card = (due: string, stability: number): SrsCard => ({
  due,
  stability,
  difficulty: 5,
  elapsedDays: 0,
  scheduledDays: 1,
  learningSteps: 0,
  reps: 1,
  lapses: 0,
  state: 2,
  lastReview: '2026-10-10T10:00:00.000Z',
});

/** A memory with just the concept map filled; the other fields are never read by these helpers. */
const memoryWith = (concepts: [string, SrsCard | null, number][]): MemoryState =>
  ({
    concepts: new Map(
      concepts.map(([id, c, successDays]) => [
        id,
        {
          card: c,
          enteredOn: '2026-10-10',
          lastAnswerDay: '2026-10-10',
          fsrsDay: '2026-10-10',
          fsrsRating: null,
          extraAgainDay: null,
          misconception: null,
          successDays: Array.from({ length: successDays }, (_, i) => `2026-10-0${i + 1}`),
        },
      ]),
    ),
  }) as unknown as MemoryState;

const status = (over: Partial<BossStatus>): BossStatus => ({
  unlocked: true,
  passed: false,
  passedAt: null,
  ready: false,
  cooldownUntil: null,
  open: null,
  attempts: 0,
  ...over,
});

describe('weakestConcepts', () => {
  it('orders by mastery, weakest first, then by id, and gives each its next-due study day', () => {
    const memory = memoryWith([
      ['c-strong', card('2026-10-30T14:00:00.000Z', 40), 3],
      ['c-weak', card('2026-10-14T14:00:00.000Z', 1), 3],
    ]);
    const names = new Map([
      ['c-strong', 'Strong one'],
      ['c-weak', 'Weak one'],
      ['c-b-new', 'New b'],
      ['c-a-new', 'New a'],
    ]);
    const rows = weakestConcepts(['c-strong', 'c-weak', 'c-b-new', 'c-a-new'], names, memory, NOW);
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(['c-a-new', 'c-b-new', 'c-weak', 'c-strong']);
    expect(rows[0]).toEqual({ id: 'c-a-new', name: 'New a', mastery: 0, due: null });
    expect(rows[2]?.due).toBe('2026-10-14');
    expect(rows[3]?.due).toBe('2026-10-30');
    expect(rows[3]?.mastery ?? 0).toBeGreaterThan(rows[2]?.mastery ?? 100);
  });
});

describe('conceptMeta', () => {
  it('says not studied, due today, or the due date', () => {
    expect(conceptMeta({ id: 'c', name: 'C', mastery: 0, due: null }, '2026-10-14')).toBe('not studied');
    expect(conceptMeta({ id: 'c', name: 'C', mastery: 32, due: '2026-10-14' }, '2026-10-14')).toBe(
      'mastery 32 · due today',
    );
    expect(conceptMeta({ id: 'c', name: 'C', mastery: 32, due: '2026-10-02' }, '2026-10-14')).toBe(
      'mastery 32 · due today',
    );
    expect(conceptMeta({ id: 'c', name: 'C', mastery: 32, due: '2026-10-20' }, '2026-10-14')).toBe(
      'mastery 32 · due Oct 20',
    );
  });
});

describe('bossLineOf', () => {
  it('says what opens the boss', () => {
    expect(bossLineOf(status({ unlocked: false }), NOW)).toBe(
      'Opens after you read every lesson of this module.',
    );
  });

  it('rounds the cooldown up to whole hours', () => {
    expect(bossLineOf(status({ cooldownUntil: '2026-10-14T14:01:00.000Z' }), NOW)).toBe('Next try in 5 h.');
    expect(bossLineOf(status({ cooldownUntil: '2026-10-15T06:00:00.000Z' }), NOW)).toBe('Next try in 20 h.');
  });

  it('names the day of the pass', () => {
    expect(bossLineOf(status({ passed: true, passedAt: '2026-10-12T15:00:00.000Z' }), NOW)).toBe(
      'Passed on Oct 12.',
    );
  });

  it('gives a passed boss that is cooling down both lines', () => {
    expect(
      bossLineOf(
        status({
          passed: true,
          passedAt: '2026-10-13T14:00:00.000Z',
          cooldownUntil: '2026-10-15T04:00:00.000Z',
        }),
        NOW,
      ),
    ).toBe('Passed on Oct 13. Next try in 18 h.');
  });

  it('gives the size, time and pass mark of an open boss', () => {
    expect(bossLineOf(status({ ready: true }), NOW)).toBe('15 questions in 21 minutes; 12 right to pass.');
  });
});

describe('masteryLine', () => {
  it('shows the mastery and its band, and a passed boss', () => {
    expect(masteryLine(64, 'solid', false)).toBe('Mastery 64 · solid');
    expect(masteryLine(85, 'mastered', true)).toBe('Mastery 85 · mastered · boss passed');
  });
});

describe('recallBand', () => {
  it('cuts at 0.70 and 0.90, and has no band without a card', () => {
    expect(recallBand(null)).toBe('none');
    expect(recallBand(0.69)).toBe('weak');
    expect(recallBand(0.7)).toBe('fading');
    expect(recallBand(0.899)).toBe('fading');
    expect(recallBand(0.9)).toBe('strong');
    expect(RECALL_LABEL).toEqual({ none: 'not studied', weak: 'weak', fading: 'fading', strong: 'strong' });
  });
});
```

The `card` builder fills every field of `SrsCard` and `memoryWith` every field of `ConceptMemory` as `src/engine/srs.ts` and `src/engine/memory.ts` define them at HEAD; Phase 3 adds no field to either.
The due times sit at 14:00 UTC, so their study day is the same calendar day in every zone from UTC-10 to UTC+13, whatever zone the unit tests run in.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the module page helpers**

Create `src/app/module.ts`:

```ts
/** Pure helpers for the module page and the lesson recall dots (spec 7.3), so the tests run without a browser. */
import { BOSS_MINUTES, BOSS_SIZE, type BossStatus } from '../engine/boss.ts';
import { studyDayAt, tzOffset } from '../engine/day.ts';
import { BAND_LABEL, conceptMastery, type HexBand } from '../engine/mastery.ts';
import { bossPassMark, type MemoryState } from '../engine/memory.ts';
import { shortDate } from './dates.ts';

const HOUR_MS = 3_600_000;

export interface ConceptRow {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  /** Concept mastery now, 0 to 100. */
  mastery: number;
  /** The study day the concept is next due, or null without a card. */
  due: string | null;
}

/** Tested concepts weakest first, then by id, each with its next-due study day in the reader's zone. */
export function weakestConcepts(
  ids: readonly string[],
  names: ReadonlyMap<string, string>,
  memory: MemoryState,
  now: Date,
): ConceptRow[] {
  const tz = tzOffset(now);
  return ids
    .map((id) => {
      const cm = memory.concepts.get(id);
      const card = cm?.card ?? null;
      return {
        id,
        name: names.get(id) ?? id,
        mastery: Math.round(100 * conceptMastery(cm, now)),
        due: card === null ? null : studyDayAt(new Date(card.due), tz),
      };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => a.mastery - b.mastery || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0));
}

/** `not studied`, `mastery 32 · due today` or `mastery 32 · due Oct 14` (date by `shortDate`). */
export function conceptMeta(row: ConceptRow, today: string): string {
  if (row.due === null) return 'not studied';
  return `mastery ${row.mastery} · due ${row.due <= today ? 'today' : shortDate(row.due)}`;
}

/** The Boss section's line (spec 3.11 and 4.2): locked, passed, cooling down, or open. */
export function bossLineOf(s: BossStatus, now: Date): string {
  if (!s.unlocked) return 'Opens after you read every lesson of this module.';
  const passed =
    s.passed && s.passedAt !== null
      ? `Passed on ${shortDate(studyDayAt(new Date(s.passedAt), tzOffset(now)))}.`
      : null;
  const cooling =
    s.cooldownUntil === null
      ? null
      : `Next try in ${Math.max(1, Math.ceil((Date.parse(s.cooldownUntil) - now.getTime()) / HOUR_MS))} h.`;
  // A passed boss that is cooling down says both, so the missing Start button always has its reason.
  if (passed !== null || cooling !== null) return [passed, cooling].filter((x) => x !== null).join(' ');
  return `${BOSS_SIZE} questions in ${BOSS_MINUTES} minutes; ${bossPassMark(BOSS_SIZE)} right to pass.`;
}

/** `Mastery 64 · solid`, with ` · boss passed` once the boss is passed (spec 7.3). */
export function masteryLine(mastery: number, band: HexBand, bossPassed: boolean): string {
  return `Mastery ${mastery} · ${BAND_LABEL[band]}${bossPassed ? ' · boss passed' : ''}`;
}

/** Recall bands for the lesson dots (spec 3.6): weak below 0.70, fading below 0.90, else strong. */
export type RecallBand = 'none' | 'weak' | 'fading' | 'strong';

export function recallBand(r: number | null): RecallBand {
  if (r === null) return 'none';
  if (r < 0.7) return 'weak';
  if (r < 0.9) return 'fading';
  return 'strong';
}

export const RECALL_LABEL: Record<RecallBand, string> = {
  none: 'not studied',
  weak: 'weak',
  fading: 'fading',
  strong: 'strong',
};
```

```ts
/** `Focus quiz: 2.3 access management` for a task scope, `Focus quiz: Module 5` for a module scope (spec 4.1). */
export function focusTitle(scope: string, tasks: readonly TaskView[]): string {
  if (scope.startsWith('m')) return `${modeLabel('focus')}: Module ${Number(scope.slice(1))}`;
  const task = tasks.find((t) => t.id === scope);
  return `${modeLabel('focus')}: ${scope}${task === undefined ? '' : ` ${task.short}`}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Rebuild the module page**

Replace `src/app/screens/ModulePage.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { useEffect } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { ContentIndex } from '../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { studyDay } from '../../engine/day.ts';
import { hexBand } from '../../engine/mastery.ts';
import { Breadcrumbs } from '../../ui/Breadcrumbs.tsx';
import { Html } from '../../ui/Html.tsx';
import { useTitle } from '../../ui/useTitle.ts';
import { now } from '../clock.ts';
import { ensureModule, modules } from '../content.ts';
import { bosses, mastery, memory } from '../memory.ts';
import { bossLineOf, conceptMeta, masteryLine, weakestConcepts } from '../module.ts';
import { progress, storeReady } from '../progress.ts';
import { pad2 } from '../router.ts';
import { quizAvailability } from '../runner.ts';
import { NotFound } from './NotFound.tsx';

/**
 * Module `#/m/NN` (spec 7.3): the mastery line, the lessons, the tested concepts weakest first, practice with the
 * focus quiz, the boss with its cooldown, then the module README without its title and lesson list.
 */
export function ModulePage({ idx, module }: { idx: ContentIndex; module: number }) {
  const meta = idx.modules.find((m) => m.n === module);
  useTitle(meta === undefined ? 'Not found' : `Module ${module}: ${meta.short}`);
  useEffect(() => ensureModule(module), [module]);
  if (meta === undefined) return <NotFound hash={`#/m/${pad2(module)}`} />;
  const chunk = modules.value.get(module);
  const { lessonsDone, checkScores } = progress.value;
  const noteId = (slug: string): string => `m${pad2(module)}/${slug}`;
  const quiz = quizAvailability({
    anyLessonDone: meta.lessons.some((l) => lessonsDone.has(noteId(l.slug))),
    questionCount: chunk?.questions.length ?? 0,
  });
  const ready = storeReady.value;
  const value = mastery.value?.get(module) ?? 0;
  const boss = ready ? bosses.value.get(module) : undefined;
  const passed = boss?.passed === true;
  return (
    <>
      <Breadcrumbs
        items={[
          { label: 'Home', href: '#/' },
          { label: `Module ${module}: ${meta.short}`, href: `#/m/${pad2(module)}` },
        ]}
      />
      <h1>{meta.short}</h1>
      <p class="muted lede">
        Module {module}, {meta.lessons.length === 1 ? '1 lesson' : `${meta.lessons.length} lessons`}
      </p>
      {ready && <p class="mastery-line">{masteryLine(value, hexBand(value, passed), passed)}</p>}
      <h2>Lessons</h2>
      <ul class="rows">
        {meta.lessons.map((l) => {
          const read = lessonsDone.has(noteId(l.slug));
          const score = checkScores.get(noteId(l.slug));
          return (
            <li key={l.slug}>
              <a class="row" href={`#/m/${pad2(module)}/l/${l.slug}`} data-nav-item>
                <span class="row-n">{l.n}</span>
                <span class="row-title">{l.title}</span>
                {(read || score !== undefined) && (
                  <span class="row-meta row-marks">
                    {read && (
                      <span class="mark">
                        <svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="14" height="14" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
                          <path d="M3 8.5l3 3 7-7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" />
                        </svg>
                        Read
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {score !== undefined && (
                      <span class="mark">
                        Check {score.correct}/{score.total}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </span>
                )}
              </a>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
      <Concepts idx={idx} module={module} />
      <h2>Practice</h2>
      {chunk === undefined ? (
        <p class="muted">Loading</p>
      ) : quiz === 'open' ? (
        <p class="practice-links">
          <a class="button-link" href={`#/s/quiz/m${pad2(module)}`}>
            Start a module quiz
          </a>
          <a class="button-link" href={`#/s/focus/m${pad2(module)}`}>
            Focus quiz: 10 weakest concepts
          </a>
        </p>
      ) : (
        <p class="muted">
          {quiz === 'locked'
            ? 'Opens after you finish one lesson of this module.'
            : 'No practice questions for this module yet.'}
        </p>
      )}
      <h2>Boss</h2>
      {boss === undefined ? (
        <p class="muted">Loading</p>
      ) : (
        <>
          <p class="muted boss-line">{bossLineOf(boss, now.value)}</p>
          {boss.unlocked && (boss.open !== null || boss.cooldownUntil === null) && (
            <p>
              <a class="button-link" href={`#/s/boss/m${pad2(module)}`}>
                {boss.open !== null ? 'Resume the boss' : 'Start the boss (15 questions, 21 min)'}
              </a>
            </p>
          )}
        </>
      )}
      {chunk === undefined ? (
        <p class="muted">Loading</p>
      ) : (
        chunk.readme
          .filter((s) => s.id !== '' && s.id !== 'lessons')
          .map((s) => <Html key={s.id} className="prose" html={s.html} />)
      )}
    </>
  );
}

/** The module's tested concepts, weakest first, each with its mastery and next-due day (spec 7.3). */
function Concepts({ idx, module }: { idx: ContentIndex; module: number }) {
  const m = storeReady.value ? memory.value : null;
  if (m === null) return null;
  const prefix = `m${pad2(module)}/`;
  const tested = idx.concepts.filter((c) => c.tested && c.home.startsWith(prefix));
  if (tested.length === 0) return null;
  const at = now.value;
  const rows = weakestConcepts(
    tested.map((c) => c.id),
    new Map(tested.map((c) => [c.id, c.name] as const)),
    m,
    at,
  );
  const today = studyDay(at);
  return (
    <>
      <h2>Concepts</h2>
      <ul class="rows">
        {rows.map((row) => {
          const c = tested.find((x) => x.id === row.id);
          return (
            <li key={row.id}>
              <a
                class="row"
                href={
                  c === undefined
                    ? `#/m/${pad2(module)}`
                    : `#/m/${pad2(module)}/l/${c.home.slice(4)}/${c.anchor}`
                }
                data-nav-item
              >
                <span class="row-title">{row.name}</span>
                <span class="row-meta">{conceptMeta(row, today)}</span>
              </a>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { recall } from '../../engine/srs.ts';
import { now } from '../clock.ts';
```

and add this effect right after the evidence-highlight `useLayoutEffect`:

```tsx
  // Recall dots (spec 7.3): one right after each H3 that anchors a tested concept homed in this note, by band.
  const noteId = `m${pad2(module)}/${slug}`;
  const memoryNow = storeReady.value ? memory.value : null;
  const at = now.value;
  // biome-ignore lint/correctness/useExhaustiveDependencies: Biome takes a signal's value for a constant import; it changes, so it is a dependency
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const root = body.current;
    if (root === null || lesson === undefined || memoryNow === null) return;
    const dots: HTMLElement[] = [];
    for (const c of idx.concepts) {
      if (!c.tested || c.home !== noteId) continue;
      const heading = root.querySelector(`h3[id="${c.anchor}"]`);
      if (heading === null) continue;
      const card = memoryNow.concepts.get(c.id)?.card ?? null;
      const band = recallBand(card === null ? null : recall(card, at));
      const dot = document.createElement('span');
      dot.className = `recall-dot recall-${band}`;
      dot.setAttribute('role', 'img');
      dot.setAttribute('aria-label', `Recall: ${RECALL_LABEL[band]}`);
      heading.after(dot);
      dots.push(dot);
    }
    return () => {
      for (const dot of dots) dot.remove();
    };
  }, [lesson, memoryNow, at, idx, noteId]);
```

In `src/app/screens/Runner.tsx`:

1. Extend the imports:


(merge each into the existing import line of the same module, so `../content.ts`, `../memory.ts`, `../progress.ts`, `../runner.ts` and `preact/hooks` keep one import each).

2. Change `type RunnerMode` to:

```tsx
type RunnerMode = 'check' | 'quiz' | 'focus' | 'review';
```

and its `scope` comment to `` `m03/aws-lambda` for a lesson check, `m03` for a module quiz, `2.3` or `m03` for a focus quiz, `review` for a review. ``.

3. Replace `poolOf` and `startOrResume` with:

```tsx
/** The learn pool of some chunks plus the items of taken forms (spec 4.3), with each concept as memory keys it. */
function poolOf(chunks: readonly ModuleChunk[]): PoolQuestion[] {
  return learnPoolNow(practicePoolOf(chunks, memoryConcept));
}

/** Every question a session may show: the loaded chunks' and the loaded chunks of taken forms. */
function shippedOf(chunks: readonly ModuleChunk[]): Map<string, QuestionView> {
  const out = new Map<string, QuestionView>();
  for (const c of chunks) for (const q of c.questions) out.set(q.id, q);
  for (const form of taken.value) {
    for (const q of examForms.value.get(form)?.questions ?? []) out.set(q.id, q);
  }
  return out;
}

/** Starts the session of this mode and scope, or finds the one in progress; null when nothing can be asked. */
function startOrResume(
  idx: ContentIndex,
  chunk: ModuleChunk,
  mode: 'check' | 'quiz',
  scope: string,
): string | null {
  const open = openSession(progress.peek(), mode, scope);
  if (open !== null) return open.sid;
  const seed = newSessionSeed();
  const module = Number(scope.slice(1, 3));
  const pool = poolOf([chunk]).filter((q) => q.module === module);
  const slug = scope.split('/')[1] ?? '';
  const qids =
    mode === 'check'
      ? selectCheck({
          concepts: homedConcepts(idx, chunk, scope, slug),
          pool: pool.filter((q) => q.note === scope),
          progress: progress.peek(),
          seed,
        })
      : selectQuiz({ pool, progress: progress.peek(), seed, recall: conceptRecall });
  if (qids.length === 0) return null;
  const sid = ulid();
  void record({ t: 'session', sid, mode, phase: 'start', scope, seed, qids });
  return sid;
}

/** The size of a focus quiz (spec 4.1). */
const FOCUS_SIZE = 10;

/** The concepts a focus quiz covers: a task statement's tested concepts, or a module's 10 weakest. */
function focusConcepts(idx: ContentIndex, scope: string): string[] {
  if (scope.startsWith('m')) {
    const ids = idx.concepts.filter((c) => c.tested && c.home.startsWith(`${scope}/`)).map((c) => c.id);
    const m = memory.peek();
    if (m === null) return ids.slice(0, FOCUS_SIZE);
    return weakestConcepts(ids, new Map(), m, now.peek())
      .slice(0, FOCUS_SIZE)
      .map((row) => row.id);
  }
  return idx.concepts.filter((c) => c.tested && c.tasks.includes(scope)).map((c) => c.id);
}
```

4. Replace `Runner` with:

```tsx
/** The runner `#/s/check/mNN/<slug>`, `#/s/quiz/mNN`, `#/s/focus/<scope>` and `#/s/review`. */
export function Runner({ idx, mode, scope }: Props) {
  if (mode === 'review') return <ReviewRunner idx={idx} />;
  if (mode === 'focus') return <FocusRunner idx={idx} scope={scope} />;
  return <ModuleRunner idx={idx} mode={mode} scope={scope} />;
}

/** A focus quiz (spec 4.1): 10 items over a task's concepts or a module's weakest, loading every module it needs. */
function FocusRunner({ idx, scope }: { idx: ContentIndex; scope: string }) {
  const title = focusTitle(scope, idx.blueprint.tasks);
  useTitle(title);
  const module = scope.startsWith('m') ? Number(scope.slice(1)) : null;
  const known =
    module === null
      ? idx.blueprint.tasks.some((t) => t.id === scope)
      : idx.modules.some((m) => m.n === module);
  const ready = storeReady.value;
  const [concepts, setConcepts] = useState<readonly string[] | null>(null);
  const [sid, setSid] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [empty, setEmpty] = useState(false);
  const loaded = modules.value;

  // biome-ignore lint/correctness/useExhaustiveDependencies: Biome takes a signal's value for a constant import; it changes, so it is a dependency
  useEffect(() => {
    if (known && ready && concepts === null) setConcepts(focusConcepts(idx, scope));
  }, [known, ready, concepts, idx, scope]);
  const wanted = useMemo(() => (concepts === null ? [] : modulesOf(concepts)), [concepts]);
  useEffect(() => {
    for (const n of wanted) ensureModule(n);
  }, [wanted]);
  const chunks = wanted.map((n) => loaded.get(n)).filter((c): c is ModuleChunk => c !== undefined);
  const allLoaded = concepts !== null && chunks.length === wanted.length;

  // biome-ignore lint/correctness/useExhaustiveDependencies: Biome takes a signal's value for a constant import; it changes, so it is a dependency
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!allLoaded || concepts === null || sid !== null || empty) return;
    const open = openSession(progress.peek(), 'focus', scope);
    if (open !== null) {
      setSid(open.sid);
      return;
    }
    const want = new Set(concepts);
    const pool = poolOf(chunks).filter((q) => want.has(q.concept));
    const seed = newSessionSeed();
    const qids = selectQuiz({
      pool,
      progress: progress.peek(),
      seed,
      size: FOCUS_SIZE,
      recall: conceptRecall,
    });
    if (qids.length === 0) {
      setEmpty(true);
      return;
    }
    const started = ulid();
    void record({ t: 'session', sid: started, mode: 'focus', phase: 'start', scope, seed, qids });
    setSid(started);
  }, [allLoaded, concepts, sid, empty, scope, loaded]);

  if (!known) return <NotFound hash={location.hash} />;
  const short = module === null ? undefined : idx.modules.find((m) => m.n === module)?.short;
  const crumbs = [
    { label: 'Home', href: '#/' },
    ...(module === null ? [] : [{ label: `Module ${module}: ${short ?? ''}`, href: `#/m/${pad2(module)}` }]),
    { label: modeLabel('focus'), href: location.hash },
  ];
  const session = sid === null ? undefined : progress.value.sessions.get(sid);
  return (
    <>
      <Breadcrumbs items={crumbs} />
      <h1>{title}</h1>
      {empty ? (
        <p class="muted">No practice questions for this focus quiz yet.</p>
      ) : !allLoaded || session === undefined ? (
        <p class="muted">Loading</p>
      ) : (
        <Session
          idx={idx}
          shipped={shippedOf(chunks)}
          pool={[]}
          session={session}
          mode="focus"
          module={module}
          slug={null}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

5. In `ModuleRunner`, pass `shipped={shippedOf([chunk])}` instead of `shipped={new Map(chunk.questions.map((q) => [q.id, q] as const))}`; in `ReviewRunner`, replace the line that builds `shipped` from `loaded` with `const shipped = shippedOf([...loaded.values()]);`.

6. In `EndLine`, render `<BadgeLine idx={idx} session={session} />` right after each `p.end-line`, and insert this branch before the `module !== null` branch of the `ul.end-links` ternary:

```tsx
        ) : mode === 'focus' ? (
          <>
            <li>
              <a href={module === null ? '#/' : `#/m/${pad2(module)}`}>
                {module === null ? 'Back to Home' : `Back to Module ${module}`}
              </a>
            </li>
            <li>
              <a href={toHash({ kind: 'focus', scope: session.scope })}>Another focus quiz</a>
            </li>
          </>
```

7. Add the badge line component after `EndLine`:

```tsx
/** `Badge: First Brew` under the end line when an event of this session earned a badge (spec 3.10). */
function BadgeLine({ idx, session }: { idx: ContentIndex; session: SessionState }) {
  const earned = memory.value?.badges;
  if (earned === undefined) return null;
  const ids = new Set(events.value.filter((e) => 'sid' in e && e.sid === session.sid).map((e) => e.id));
  const names = badgesIn(earned, ids).map((id) => idx.badges.find((b) => b.id === id)?.name ?? id);
  return names.length === 0 ? null : <p class="badge-line">{badgeEarnedLine(names)}</p>;
}
```

## Task 22: The Progress overlay

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/progress-lines.ts`, `tests/unit/progress-lines.test.ts`, `src/app/screens/progress/RankSection.tsx`, `src/app/screens/progress/ReadinessSection.tsx`, `src/app/screens/progress/DayStrip.tsx`, `src/app/screens/progress/Calibration.tsx`, `src/app/screens/progress/Causes.tsx`, `src/app/screens/progress/BadgeList.tsx`, `src/app/screens/progress/MockHistory.tsx`, `src/app/screens/progress/ResetSection.tsx`
- Modify: `src/app/screens/Progress.tsx`, `src/app/progress.ts`, `src/ui/base.css`
- Test: `tests/unit/progress-lines.test.ts`

Design notes:
- The overlay follows the Progress row of spec 7.3: after Level and Streak come the rank, readiness with its domain bars and the go/no-go line, the last 14 days, calibration, error causes with the answer-change verdict (spec 4.4), badges and mock history; Backup, Exam date, Diagnostics and Versions stay as built, and a typed reset closes the sheet.
- Every percentage in these lines is floored, like readiness, so a shown 80% always means at least 0.80.
- The 14-day strip is an SVG with an `aria-label` that totals the XP, and the same numbers sit in a visually hidden table, so the chart never relies on sight.
- The domain bars use `--d1` to `--d4`, the only place besides results where those colors appear (spec 7.6), and each bar carries its number as text.
- The change verdict counts a changed exam answer as `wrong to right` when its first pick was wrong and its final pick right, and as `right to wrong` by `changedRightToWrong` (Task 19), so Results and Progress agree on every item.
- `Causes` loads the module chunks of changed boss items and the form chunks of changed mock items before it counts, and shows `Loading` until they are in.
- `resetAll` empties both stores through `replaceStores` and then the signals, so a failed write still leaves this tab empty, as `replaceAll` does.
- `Progress.tsx` drops its local en-US formatter for `shortDate` (Task 19); `Last backup` now names the study day of the export.
- The sheet opens with focus on its title, as the runner does with its stem, so it starts at the rank and readiness rather than scrolled down to its first button.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the Progress lines**

Create `src/app/progress-lines.ts`:

```ts
/** Pure copy for the Progress overlay (spec 7.3 and 4.4), so the tests run without a browser. */
import type { BadgeView, QuestionView } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import type { BadgeEarned, Calibration } from '../engine/badges.ts';
import { addDays } from '../engine/day.ts';
import type { MockAttempt } from '../engine/mock.ts';
import type { AnswerRecord, Cause } from '../engine/replay.ts';
import { shortDate } from './dates.ts';
import { examDateLabel } from './home.ts';
import { CAUSE_LABELS, changedRightToWrong } from './results.ts';
import { isCorrect } from './runner.ts';

/** How many study days the XP strip shows. */
const STRIP_DAYS = 14;

/** Floored, so a shown 80 always means at least 0.80. */
const percent = (value: number): number => Math.floor(100 * value);

/** XP per study day for the 14 days ending today, oldest first. */
export function dayStrip(xpByDay: ReadonlyMap<string, number>, today: string): { day: string; xp: number }[] {
  return Array.from({ length: STRIP_DAYS }, (_, i) => {
    const day = addDays(today, i - (STRIP_DAYS - 1));
    return { day, xp: xpByDay.get(day) ?? 0 };
  });
}

const side = (label: string, s: { right: number; total: number }): string =>
  s.total === 0 ? `${label}: none yet` : `${label}: ${percent(s.right / s.total)}% right (${s.total})`;

/** Sure and Not sure accuracy over the badge window (spec 3.10 Well Calibrated). */
export function calibrationLine(c: Calibration): string {
  if (c.sure.total === 0 && c.notSure.total === 0) return 'No counted answers yet.';
  return `${side('Sure', c.sure)} · ${side('Not sure', c.notSure)}`;
}

/** The cause counts of spec 4.4, in the order the results page offers them. */
export function causeLine(counts: Readonly<Record<Cause, number>>): string {
  if (counts.unknown + counts.confused + counts.misread === 0) return 'No error causes yet.';
  return (['unknown', 'confused', 'misread'] as const)
    .map((cause) => `${CAUSE_LABELS[cause]} ${counts[cause]}`)
    .join(' · ');
}

/** Changed exam answers: first pick wrong and final right, or right to wrong as the results page tags it. */
export function changeCounts(
  records: readonly AnswerRecord[],
  questions: ReadonlyMap<string, QuestionView>,
): { wrongToRight: number; rightToWrong: number } {
  let wrongToRight = 0;
  let rightToWrong = 0;
  for (const r of records) {
    const q = questions.get(r.qid);
    const first = r.changes[0];
    if (q === undefined || first === undefined) continue;
    if (changedRightToWrong(r, q)) rightToWrong += 1;
    else if (r.correct && !isCorrect(q, first)) wrongToRight += 1;
  }
  return { wrongToRight, rightToWrong };
}

/** Whether changing answers has helped so far (spec 4.4). */
export function changeVerdict(wrongToRight: number, rightToWrong: number): string {
  if (wrongToRight + rightToWrong === 0) return 'No changed answers yet.';
  const verdict =
    wrongToRight > rightToWrong ? 'helped' : wrongToRight < rightToWrong ? 'hurt' : 'made no difference';
  return `Changing answers: ${wrongToRight} wrong to right, ${rightToWrong} right to wrong. So far it has ${verdict}.`;
}

const ARRIVES: Record<NonNullable<BadgeView['arrives']>, string> = {
  labs: 'labs',
  missions: 'missions',
  capstone: 'the capstone',
};

/** `First Brew · earned Oct 12` (date by `shortDate`), its rule, or when a later phase makes it earnable. */
export function badgeLine(b: BadgeView, earned: BadgeEarned | undefined): string {
  if (earned !== undefined) return `${b.name} · earned ${shortDate(earned.day)}`;
  if (b.arrives !== null) return `${b.name} · arrives with ${ARRIVES[b.arrives]}`;
  return `${b.name} · ${b.rule}`;
}

/** `Mock A · Tue 27 Oct · 52/65 (80%)` (date by `examDateLabel`); a remix reads `Remix`. */
export function mockLine(a: MockAttempt): string {
  const name = a.form === 'remix' ? 'Remix' : `Mock ${a.form}`;
  return `${name} · ${examDateLabel(a.day)} · ${a.correct}/${a.total} (${percent(a.percent)}%)`;
}

/** `Rank Business Support+`. */
export function rankLine(name: string): string {
  return `Rank ${name}`;
}

/** `Memory 58 · Mock 66`, with `Mock -` before the first unseen mock (spec 3.9). */
export function memoryMockLine(memory: number, mock: number | null): string {
  return `Memory ${percent(memory)} · Mock ${mock === null ? '-' : percent(mock)}`;
}

/** One domain bar's text: `Cloud Concepts 62`. */
export function domainValueLine(name: string, value: number): string {
  return `${name} ${percent(value)}`;
}

/** The word to type before deleting every event and setting. */
export const RESET_WORD = 'RESET';
```

```ts
/** Deletes every event and kv record in this browser (spec 7.3, the typed reset), then empties the signals. */
export async function resetAll(): Promise<void> {
  if (db !== null) {
    try {
      await replaceStores(db, [], {});
    } catch {
      failed();
    }
  }
  events.value = [];
  kvRecords.value = {};
}
```

Create `src/app/screens/progress/RankSection.tsx`:

```tsx
import { nextRankLine } from '../../../engine/ranks.ts';
import { rank, rankFacts, ranks, readinessNow } from '../../memory.ts';
import { storeReady } from '../../progress.ts';
import { rankLine } from '../../progress-lines.ts';

/** The shown rank and what the next one needs (spec 3.4). */
export function RankSection() {
  const shown = rank.value;
  if (!storeReady.value || shown === null) return null;
  const r = readinessNow.value;
  const label = r === null || r.value === null ? '-' : r.label;
  const next = nextRankLine(ranks.value, shown, rankFacts.value, label);
  return (
    <>
      <h3>Rank</h3>
      <p class="progress-line">{rankLine(shown.name)}</p>
      {next !== null && <p class="muted">{next}</p>}
    </>
  );
}
```

Create `src/app/screens/progress/ReadinessSection.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ContentIndex } from '../../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { studyDay } from '../../../engine/day.ts';
import { goNoGo } from '../../../engine/readiness.ts';
import { now } from '../../clock.ts';
import { goNoGoLine, readinessLine } from '../../home.ts';
import { memory, readinessNow } from '../../memory.ts';
import { domainValueLine, memoryMockLine } from '../../progress-lines.ts';

/** Readiness with its parts, one bar per exam domain, and the go/no-go line in its window (spec 3.9). */
export function ReadinessSection({ idx }: { idx: ContentIndex }) {
  const r = readinessNow.value;
  if (r === null) return null;
  const examDate = memory.value?.examDate ?? null;
  const due = examDate !== null && goNoGo({ examDate, today: studyDay(now.value), readiness: r });
  return (
    <>
      <h3>Readiness</h3>
      <p class="progress-line readiness-line">{readinessLine(r)}</p>
      <p class="muted">{memoryMockLine(r.memory, r.mock)}</p>
      <ul class="readiness-bars">
        {idx.blueprint.domains.map((d) => {
          const value = r.domains[d.id as '1' | '2' | '3' | '4'] ?? 0;
          return (
            <li key={d.id} class="bar-row">
              <span class="bar-label">{domainValueLine(d.name, value)}</span>
              <span class="bar-track" aria-hidden="true">
                <span
                  class="bar-fill"
                  style={{ width: `${Math.floor(100 * value)}%`, background: `var(--d${d.id})` }}
                />
              </span>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
      {due && examDate !== null && <p class="go-no-go">{goNoGoLine(examDate, r.label)}</p>}
    </>
  );
}
```

Create `src/app/screens/progress/DayStrip.tsx`:

```tsx
import { studyDay } from '../../../engine/day.ts';
import { now } from '../../clock.ts';
import { shortDate } from '../../dates.ts';
import { memory } from '../../memory.ts';
import { dayStrip } from '../../progress-lines.ts';

const BAR = 16;
const GAP = 4;
const HEIGHT = 48;

/** XP per study day for the last 14 days (spec 7.3): an SVG for sight and a hidden table for everyone else. */
export function DayStrip() {
  const m = memory.value;
  if (m === null) return null;
  const days = dayStrip(m.xpByDay, studyDay(now.value));
  const top = Math.max(1, ...days.map((d) => d.xp));
  const total = days.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.xp, 0);
  return (
    <>
      <h3>Last 14 days</h3>
      <svg
        class="day-strip"
        viewBox={`0 0 ${days.length * (BAR + GAP) - GAP} ${HEIGHT}`}
        role="img"
        aria-label={`XP per day for the last 14 days, ${total} XP in all`}
      >
        {days.map((d, i) => {
          const h = d.xp === 0 ? 1 : Math.max(2, Math.round((HEIGHT * d.xp) / top));
          return <rect key={d.day} x={i * (BAR + GAP)} y={HEIGHT - h} width={BAR} height={h} />;
        })}
      </svg>
      <table class="visually-hidden">
        <caption>XP per day</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th scope="col">Day</th>
            <th scope="col">XP</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {days.map((d) => (
            <tr key={d.day}>
              <td>{shortDate(d.day)}</td>
              <td>{d.xp}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </>
  );
}
```

Create `src/app/screens/progress/Causes.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { QuestionView } from '../../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { resolveConcept } from '../../../engine/memory.ts';
import type { AnswerRecord } from '../../../engine/replay.ts';
import { ensureExamForm, ensureModule, examForms, index, modules } from '../../content.ts';
import { memoryContent } from '../../memory.ts';
import { progress } from '../../progress.ts';
import { causeLine, changeCounts, changeVerdict } from '../../progress-lines.ts';

/** Error-cause counts and whether changing answers has helped so far (spec 4.4). */
export function Causes() {
  const p = progress.value;
  const counts = { unknown: 0, confused: 0, misread: 0 };
  for (const cause of p.causes.values()) counts[cause] += 1;
  const changed: AnswerRecord[] = [...p.answers.values()]
    .flat()
    .filter((r) => (r.mode === 'boss' || r.mode === 'mock') && r.changes.length > 0);
  const items = index.value?.exam.items ?? {};
  const content = memoryContent.value;
  const forms = [
    ...new Set(changed.flatMap((r) => (items[r.qid] === undefined ? [] : [items[r.qid]?.form ?? '']))),
  ];
  const wanted = [
    ...new Set(
      changed.flatMap((r) => {
        if (items[r.qid] !== undefined || content === null) return [];
        const concept = resolveConcept(content, r.concept);
        const module = concept === null ? undefined : content.concepts.get(concept)?.module;
        return module === undefined ? [] : [module];
      }),
    ),
  ];
  const key = `${forms.join(',')}|${wanted.join(',')}`;
  // biome-ignore lint/correctness/useExhaustiveDependencies: the key names every form and module the effect loads
  useEffect(() => {
    for (const f of forms) void ensureExamForm(f);
    for (const n of wanted) ensureModule(n);
  }, [key]);
  const questions = new Map<string, QuestionView>();
  for (const n of wanted) for (const q of modules.value.get(n)?.questions ?? []) questions.set(q.id, q);
  for (const f of forms) for (const q of examForms.value.get(f)?.questions ?? []) questions.set(q.id, q);
  const loaded = changed.every((r) => questions.has(r.qid));
  const { wrongToRight, rightToWrong } = changeCounts(changed, questions);
  return (
    <>
      <h3>Error causes</h3>
      <p class="progress-line">{causeLine(counts)}</p>
      <p class="muted">{loaded ? changeVerdict(wrongToRight, rightToWrong) : 'Loading'}</p>
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { ContentIndex } from '../../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { memory } from '../../memory.ts';
import { badgeLine } from '../../progress-lines.ts';

/** The 12 badges (spec 3.10): earned ones with their day, the rest with their rule or the phase that brings them. */
export function BadgeList({ idx }: { idx: ContentIndex }) {
  const earned = memory.value?.badges;
  if (earned === undefined) return null;
  return (
    <>
      <h3>Badges</h3>
      <ul class="badges">
        {idx.badges.map((b) => {
          const got = earned.get(b.id as Parameters<typeof earned.get>[0]);
          return (
            <li key={b.id} class={got === undefined ? 'badge' : 'badge badge-earned'}>
              {got !== undefined && (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="14" height="14" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
                  <path d="M3 8.5l3 3 7-7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" />
                </svg>
              )}
              {badgeLine(b, got)}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { resetAll } from '../../progress.ts';
import { RESET_WORD } from '../../progress-lines.ts';

/** Deletes all progress after the reset word is typed (spec 7.3 settings); no modal, the confirmation is inline. */
export function ResetSection() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [typed, setTyped] = useState('');
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  return (
    <>
      <h3>Reset</h3>
      {done ? (
        <p class="import-done" role="status">
          Deleted. This browser has no progress now.
        </p>
      ) : !open ? (
        <p class="sheet-actions">
          <button class="button button-quiet" type="button" onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
            Reset all progress
          </button>
        </p>
      ) : (
        <div class="reset">
          <p>Type {RESET_WORD} to delete every event and setting in this browser.</p>
          <label class="date-field">
            Reset word
            <input
              type="text"
              value={typed}
              autocomplete="off"
              spellcheck={false}
              onInput={(event) => setTyped(event.currentTarget.value)}
            />
          </label>
          <p class="sheet-actions">
            <button
              class="button"
              type="button"
              disabled={typed !== RESET_WORD}
              onClick={() => {
                void resetAll().then(() => setDone(true));
              }}
            >
              Delete everything
            </button>
            <button class="link-button" type="button" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
              Cancel
            </button>
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```
