## Task 23: End-to-end tests for bosses, mocks, go/no-go and the keyboard

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/fixtures/exam-e2e.ts`, `tests/fixtures/ready-module.ts`, `tests/unit/ready-module.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/exam-helpers.ts`, `tests/e2e/exam.spec.ts`
- Modify: `tests/e2e/helpers.ts`, `tests/e2e/reader.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/memory.spec.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/ready-module.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/exam.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/memory.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/reader.spec.ts`

Design notes:
- No exam test renders a real exam item: `useSyntheticExam` rewrites the index's `exam` field with four made-up forms and serves their chunks through `page.route`, so the real `exam-?.<hash8>.json` files are never fetched (the config already blocks service workers), and this task never waits for Task 7.
- The synthetic items carry made-up text (`Made-up exam item 1`, `Option 1` to `Option 4`, the key always `Option 1`) on real tested concepts and tasks from the built index, so replay, readiness and domain bars see ordinary concepts.
- Boss items are practice items, whose text may appear, so `answerBoss` finds the shown item in the module chunks with `chunkFor` and answers with `pickRight` or `pickWrong`.
- `exam.spec.ts` turns `trace`, `screenshot` and `video` off, and every axe scan of the exam shell, its grid and the confirmation uses `{ redact: true }`, which prints rule ids, help text and node targets and never node HTML.
- The clock is installed at `2026-10-20T09:00:00Z` and keeps running, because Preact runs effects on timers and a paused page clock leaves the app on `Loading`.
  This departs from the brief, which asked for `page.clock.pauseAt(time)` after `install`: Playwright's clock also fakes `requestAnimationFrame` and `setTimeout`, which Preact's `useEffect` waits on, so a paused clock never loads the index, and the mock reload test can only bound the bar's time instead of reading exactly `80:00`.
- The deadline and cooldown tests jump with `fastForward`, which fires the due timers, and the go/no-go test moves the date with `setSystemTime` and a reload.
- The mock reload test fast-forwards 10:00 and accepts `80:00` down to `79:00`, because the real seconds the test takes also pass.
- The keyboard test presses `Alt+Tab` on WebKit, which, like Safari, tabs to buttons only with Option held.
- Exam copy that no unit test renders is matched here: the boss and mock start ledes, `Submit the exam?`, `Time ran out.` on a mock that hits its deadline, the remix lede with its lock line, and `This result is not in this browser.` with its `Back to Home` link.
- `readyModule` seeds `lesson.done` for every lesson of the module and three right quiz answers per concept on days 21, 17 and 9 before the clock, and `tests/unit/ready-module.test.ts` pins that this gives module mastery of at least 60 under the engine, so the e2e setup never drifts silently.
- `seedKv` stamps `engineVersion`, so the "Rules updated" line never sits above the screens under test.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the fixtures**

Create `tests/fixtures/ready-module.ts`:

```ts
/** Events that make one module boss-ready (spec 3.8 and 4.2): every lesson read and every concept answered well. */
import type { NewEvent, StudyEvent } from '../../schemas/events.ts';
import { timelineOf } from './events.ts';
import { answerOf, startOf, submitOf } from './memory.ts';

/** The moment every exam end-to-end test starts at. */
export const E2E_NOW = '2026-10-20T09:00:00.000Z';
/** Days before the clock on which each concept gets one right answer: three study days, well spaced. */
export const READY_DAYS = [21, 17, 9] as const;

const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;

export function readyModuleEvents(args: {
  module: number;
  /** Lesson slugs of the module. */
  lessons: readonly string[];
  /** Live questions of the module; the first of each concept is answered. */
  questions: readonly { id: string; version: number; concept: string }[];
  now: string;
}): StudyEvent[] {
  const now = Date.parse(args.now);
  const scope = `m${String(args.module).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  const first = new Map<string, { id: string; version: number; concept: string }>();
  for (const q of args.questions) if (!first.has(q.concept)) first.set(q.concept, q);
  const picks = [...first.values()];
  const iso = (ms: number): string => new Date(ms).toISOString();
  const items: (readonly [string, NewEvent])[] = [];
  let t = now - 22 * DAY_MS;
  for (const slug of args.lessons) {
    items.push([iso(t), { t: 'lesson.done', note: `${scope}/${slug}` }]);
    t += 1000;
  }
  for (const [i, days] of READY_DAYS.entries()) {
    if (picks.length === 0) break;
    const sid = `01JA00000000000000000000R${i + 1}`;
    let s = now - days * DAY_MS;
    items.push([
      iso(s),
      startOf(
        sid,
        'quiz',
        picks.map((q) => q.id),
        scope,
      ),
    ]);
    for (const q of picks) {
      s += 1000;
      items.push([
        iso(s),
        answerOf({
          concept: q.concept,
          qid: q.id,
          qv: q.version,
          correct: true,
          sure: true,
          mode: 'quiz',
          sid,
        }),
      ]);
    }
    items.push([iso(s + 1000), submitOf(sid, 'quiz')]);
  }
  return timelineOf(items);
}
```

Create `tests/fixtures/exam-e2e.ts`:

```ts
/** A made-up exam for end-to-end tests: four forms of 65 items with made-up text and known keys. */
import type { ExamChunk, ExamFormMeta, ExamItemRef, QuestionView } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';

export const EXAM_FORMS = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'] as const;
export const EXAM_FORM_SIZE = 65;

export interface SyntheticExam {
  forms: ExamFormMeta[];
  items: Record<string, ExamItemRef>;
  /** Each form's chunk by its file name, as the index names it. */
  chunks: Record<string, ExamChunk>;
}

/** Builds the forms over real tested concepts, so replay and readiness see ordinary concepts and tasks. */
export function syntheticExam(
  concepts: readonly { id: string; home: string; tasks: readonly string[] }[],
): SyntheticExam {
  const usable = concepts.filter((c) => c.tasks.length > 0);
  if (usable.length === 0)
    throw new Error('the synthetic exam needs at least one tested concept with a task');
  const out: SyntheticExam = { forms: [], items: {}, chunks: {} };
  let n = 0;
  for (const form of EXAM_FORMS) {
    const questions: QuestionView[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < EXAM_FORM_SIZE; i += 1) {
      const c = usable[n % usable.length];
      n += 1;
      if (c === undefined) continue;
      const id = `q-zz${String(n).padStart(4, '0')}`;
      const task = c.tasks[0] ?? '';
      questions.push({
        id,
        version: 1,
        type: 'single',
        concept: c.id,
        note: c.home,
        task,
        level: 1,
        stem: `Made-up exam item ${n}`,
        select: null,
        options: [1, 2, 3, 4].map((k) => ({
          id: `o${k}`,
          text: `Option ${k}`,
          correct: k === 1,
          why: `Made-up reason ${k}.`,
        })),
        pairs: [],
        takeaway: 'Made-up takeaway.',
        read: { route: '#/', text: 'Made-up evidence.' },
      });
      out.items[id] = { version: 1, concept: c.id, task, form, type: 'single' };
    }
    const file = `exam-${form.toLowerCase()}.e2e00000.json`;
    out.forms.push({ id: form, chunk: `content/${file}`, frozen: true, size: EXAM_FORM_SIZE });
    out.chunks[file] = { form, questions };
  }
  return out;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add `seedKv` and the redacting axe check**

In `tests/e2e/helpers.ts`, replace `expectNoAxeViolations` with:

```ts
/**
 * Scans the settled page with axe. With `redact`, each finding names only the rule, its help text and the node
 * targets, never node HTML, so an exam item's text can never reach test output.
 */
export async function expectNoAxeViolations(page: Page, options: { redact?: boolean } = {}): Promise<void> {
  // Scan the settled page: mid fade-in, a dialog's text is still partly transparent and fails color-contrast.
  await page.evaluate(() => Promise.all(document.getAnimations().map((animation) => animation.finished)));
  const results = await new AxeBuilder({ page }).withTags(AXE_TAGS).analyze();
  const violations = results.violations.map((v) =>
    options.redact === true
      ? `${v.id} (${v.nodes.length} nodes): ${v.help}; targets: ${v.nodes.map((n) => n.target.join(' ')).join(', ')}`
      : `${v.id} (${v.nodes.length} nodes): ${v.help}; first: ${v.nodes[0]?.html ?? ''}`,
  );
  expect(violations).toEqual([]);
}
```

and append:

```ts
/**
 * Writes kv records into the page's IndexedDB the way `writeKv` stores them; they take effect at the next reload.
 * Call it once the app has opened its database, for example after Home's h1 shows.
 */
export async function seedKv(page: Page, records: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>): Promise<void> {
  await page.evaluate(
    async ({ name, version, store, list }) => {
      const db = await new Promise<IDBDatabase>((resolve, reject) => {
        const open = indexedDB.open(name, version);
        open.onsuccess = () => resolve(open.result);
        open.onerror = () => reject(open.error);
      });
      await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        const tx = db.transaction(store, 'readwrite');
        for (const [key, value] of list)
          tx.objectStore(store).put({ key, value, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
        tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
        tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
        tx.onabort = () => reject(tx.error);
      });
      db.close();
    },
    { name: DB_NAME, version: SCHEMA_VERSION, store: STORES.kv, list: Object.entries(records) },
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the exam helpers**

Create `tests/e2e/exam-helpers.ts`:

```ts
/** Helpers for the exam end-to-end tests; every exam item they serve is made up. */
import { expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import type { ContentIndex, QuestionView } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { ENGINE_VERSION } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import { KV_KEYS } from '../../src/store/kv.ts';
import { syntheticExam } from '../fixtures/exam-e2e.ts';
import { E2E_NOW, readyModuleEvents } from '../fixtures/ready-module.ts';
import { h1, seedEvents, seedKv } from './helpers.ts';
import { chunkFor, lessonsOf, pickRight, pickWrong } from './questions.ts';

export const HOME_TITLE = 'AWS Cloud Practitioner Essentials';

/**
 * Serves a made-up exam in place of the real one: the index's `exam` field lists the synthetic forms and items, and
 * every exam chunk request gets the synthetic chunk, so no real exam item is fetched or shown.
 */
export async function useSyntheticExam(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  let chunks: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  await page.route('**/content/index.*.json', async (route) => {
    const response = await route.fetch();
    const index = (await response.json()) as ContentIndex;
    const exam = syntheticExam(index.concepts.filter((c) => c.tested));
    chunks = exam.chunks;
    await route.fulfill({ response, json: { ...index, exam: { forms: exam.forms, items: exam.items } } });
  });
  await page.route('**/content/exam-*.json', async (route) => {
    const file = new URL(route.request().url()).pathname.split('/').pop() ?? '';
    const chunk = chunks[file];
    if (chunk === undefined) await route.fulfill({ status: 404, body: '' });
    else await route.fulfill({ json: chunk });
  });
}

/** Seeds the events that make a module boss-ready at `E2E_NOW`, with the engine version stamped. */
export async function readyModule(page: Page, module: number): Promise<void> {
  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(h1(page, HOME_TITLE)).toBeVisible();
  const questions = await chunkFor(page, module);
  const lessons = await lessonsOf(page, module);
  await seedKv(page, { [KV_KEYS.engineVersion]: ENGINE_VERSION });
  await seedEvents(page, readyModuleEvents({ module, lessons, questions, now: E2E_NOW }));
  await expect(h1(page, HOME_TITLE)).toBeVisible();
}

/** The practice items a boss of this module can draw: its own chunk and every earlier module's. */
export async function bossQuestions(page: Page, module: number): Promise<QuestionView[]> {
  const out: QuestionView[] = [];
  for (let n = 1; n <= module; n += 1) out.push(...(await chunkFor(page, n)));
  return out;
}

/** Answers the boss item on screen right or wrong, found by its `data-qid` among practice items. */
export async function answerBoss(
  page: Page,
  questions: readonly QuestionView[],
  right: boolean,
): Promise<void> {
  const exam = page.locator('section.exam');
  await expect(exam).toBeVisible();
  const qid = await exam.getAttribute('data-qid');
  const q = questions.find((x) => x.id === qid);
  if (q === undefined) throw new Error(`boss item ${qid} is not a practice item of the modules loaded`);
  if (right) await pickRight(page, q);
  else await pickWrong(page, q);
}

/** Picks the made-up key (`Option 1`) or a made-up wrong option on the mock item on screen. */
export async function answerMock(page: Page, right: boolean): Promise<void> {
  await page.getByRole('radio', { name: right ? 'Option 1' : 'Option 2', exact: true }).check();
}

/** Starts the boss of a module from its start screen. */
export async function startBoss(page: Page, module: number): Promise<void> {
  await page.goto(`/#/s/boss/m${String(module).padStart(2, '0')}`);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start the boss', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.locator('section.exam')).toBeVisible();
}

/** Answers every boss item (right when `right(i)` says so), then submits through the review page and the dialog. */
export async function finishBoss(page: Page, module: number, right: (i: number) => boolean): Promise<void> {
  const questions = await bossQuestions(page, module);
  for (let i = 0; ; i += 1) {
    await answerBoss(page, questions, right(i));
    const review = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Review and submit', exact: true });
    if (await review.isVisible()) {
      await review.click();
      break;
    }
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next', exact: true }).click();
  }
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Submit', exact: true }).click();
  await page.locator('dialog.confirm').getByRole('button', { name: 'Submit', exact: true }).click();
}
```

```ts
import { expect, type Page, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { timelineOf } from '../fixtures/events.ts';
import { E2E_NOW } from '../fixtures/ready-module.ts';
import {
  answerBoss,
  answerMock,
  bossQuestions,
  finishBoss,
  HOME_TITLE,
  readyModule,
  startBoss,
  useSyntheticExam,
} from './exam-helpers.ts';
import {
  expectNoAxeViolations,
  expectNoSidewaysScroll,
  failOnPageProblems,
  h1,
  seedEvents,
} from './helpers.ts';

failOnPageProblems();
// UTC keeps the study day fixed; nothing of an exam may land in a trace, a screenshot or a video.
test.use({ timezoneId: 'UTC', trace: 'off', screenshot: 'off', video: 'off' });

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await useSyntheticExam(page);
  await page.clock.install({ time: new Date(E2E_NOW) });
});

const resultsTitle = (page: Page) => page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: /^Boss: .+ results$/ });
const nextBlock = (page: Page) => page.locator('.next-block');

    page.getByText('15 questions, 21 minutes. The timer cannot pause.', { exact: true }),
  ).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start the boss', exact: true }).click();

  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) {
    await answerBoss(page, questions, i !== 1);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next', exact: true }).click();
  }
  await page.clock.fastForward('21:00');
  await page.clock.runFor(1000);
  await expect(resultsTitle(page)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText(/^Not passed: /)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: 'Wrong answers' })).toBeVisible();
  const misread = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Misread', exact: true }).first();
  await expect(misread).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
  await misread.click();
  await expect(misread).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
});
```

```markdown
- The index lists form item ids, concepts, tasks and versions (`exam.items`), never their text.
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Document Phase 3 in the README**

In `README.md`, insert this section right before `## Course text stays out`:

```markdown
## Exams

The exam pool lives in `content/exam/pool/`, one file per module, written by `agents/exam-writer.md`, which never reads the practice questions.
`pnpm content:quotas` writes `content/exam/quotas.yaml` from the registry and the blueprint, and `pnpm content:forms` splits the live pool into forms A to D in `content/exam/forms.yaml`.
`pnpm content:forms --freeze` freezes the forms once the pool meets its quotas, and rule R12 then keeps them as they are.
The check rounds run over the pool with `--pool exam`, and every tool prints exam items by id and count only.
Honor system: never open `content/exam/` or an `exam-?.<hash>.json` chunk before you have taken that form.
Each module has a boss of 15 questions in 21 minutes, which opens once every lesson of the module is read; 12 right passes, and a new try waits 20 hours.
A mock is 65 questions in 90 minutes on a wall-clock timer that cannot pause, scored over its live items with raw percentages only.
Readiness blends memory and the latest unseen mocks, caps at 70 without a mock, and names the weakest task statement; from 7 to 2 days before the exam date, Home shows the go/no-go line while readiness is below Ready.
Ranks follow the Support plans in `content/game/ranks.yaml` and never drop, and the 12 badges in `content/game/badges.yaml` are derived from the log.
Rule R10 also checks the exam quotas (`examQuotas: true`), and rule R12 guards the frozen forms.
```

### Spec coverage

| Spec 10.1, Phase 3 | Tasks |

### Deliberate deviations

- Quota math counts a tested concept once in each task it is tagged with, because spec 4.3 says "in proportion to concept counts" and a concept tagged with a task is a concept of that task (Tasks 3 and 4).
- One exam chunk per form instead of spec 2.10's single `exam.<hash>.json`, so an untaken form's text never loads outside its own mock and leakage is structural (Task 15).
- During an exam `/`, `Cmd+K`, the `g` sequences and `j`/`k` are off and Escape only closes an open sheet, beyond spec 7.4's `g` sequences, so the palette cannot peek at notes and Help still closes by keyboard (Tasks 16 and 17).
- A mock start stores only the form's live qids and scores over them, so replay never depends on `forms.yaml` (Task 12).
- Boss and mock answers stay out of `COUNTING_MODES` and apply on submit through `EXAM_MODES` with 0 XP per item, following spec 3.1's gate and Phase 2's extra Again rule (Task 9).
- Badges are derived as the first event at which each condition held, so a badge never disappears even when its condition later fails (Task 10).
- The key-fix rescore conflict (P3-118) follows the built code as in Phase 2: older-version answers keep XP and leave FSRS alone (Task 14).
- Readiness, ranks and badges sit beside `deriveMemory`, not in one `derive(content, events, now)`, and there is still no snapshot cache (P3-164, P3-165; Tasks 9, 13, 14 and 17).
- Check batches keep "different lessons" as the soft rule the built `makeBatches` applies, against spec 2.7's wording, because a hard lesson rule can make 26-batch deals impossible (Task 5).
- Readiness may rise without a new event when a mock attempt ages out of the 21-day window; the never-rise property holds for Memory, every `Q_d`, and readiness on logs with no mock submit (Task 13).
- `ensureExamForm` resolves with the chunk or null instead of the brief's `void`, so a mock start can show `This mock could not load. Check your connection and try again.` and burn no form when offline (Tasks 15 and 18).
- A remix builds to the largest count per task and per domain over the four forms, read off `index.exam.items`, so an item retired from Form A never shrinks or skews a remix, and the index carries no separate targets field (Tasks 15 and 18).
- `rank` is null until the index lists the ranks, because the engine's rank helpers need at least Basic and the store can be ready before the index loads (Tasks 17, 20 and 22).
- The exam tests never pause the page clock, against the brief's `pauseAt`, because Playwright's clock also fakes the timers Preact's effects wait on; the mock reload test accepts `80:00` down to `79:00` (Task 23).
- Under 480 px the rank button stands in for `Progress` and the `Missions` link waits on Home, so the phone bar reads `18 · 9d · Business+` and the phone overflow checks stay green; `practice.spec.ts` opens Progress through the rank (Task 20).
- `bossLineOf` gains a line for an open boss, `15 questions in 21 minutes; 12 right to pass.`, because the Boss section needs a line before its button (Task 21).
- `rankLine`, `memoryMockLine`, `domainValueLine` and `changeCounts` join `progress-lines.ts`, so the Progress copy is tested and Results and Progress agree on changed answers (Task 22).
- The R2 frozen-form tests fake HEAD the way the existing R2 tests do instead of building a git repository, and `validate.ts` redaction is covered through the Vite plugin test, because the script runs only against the repository root (Task 2).
- `short` names keep their case (`AI/ML and analytics`), and Task 3's form search is two-phase (multi-response seats first) rather than seat by seat (Tasks 1 and 3).
- The fan-out keeps the write-stage drafts uncommitted in `<scratchpad>/p3/wt-exam` until the check stage ends, and a drain entry keeps its `drain` tag and seed 331, as the brief's commit list describes (Task 6).
- Pass Mark, Full Dress Rehearsal and the first-attempt measures read a form's first submitted mock; no Phase 3 screen abandons a mock, and if one ever does, both parts must switch to the form's first started session (Tasks 10 and 12).
- A taken form's start screen still offers `Start Mock A`, so a form can be retaken, and only its first attempt counts for XP, readiness and badges; the remix start screen unlocks once four forms are started, while the mock list shows the Remix row once four have a submitted first attempt (Tasks 18 and 19).
- Exam copy that no unit test renders (`Time ran out.`, `Submit the exam?`, the ledes, `This result is not in this browser.`) is matched in Task 23's end-to-end tests; `Changed right to wrong` and `This mock could not load. Check your connection and try again.` are matched only by their unit-tested helpers and constants (Tasks 19 and 23).
- Visual screenshot baselines of the exam shell and results wait for Phase 6 (P3-180).
